# 人民检察院刑事诉讼规则

**分类**：司法解释  
**法律部门**：诉讼与非诉讼程序法  
**公布日期**：2019-12-30  
**生效日期**：2019-12-30

> 第二十八次会议通过，自2019年12月30日起施行） 目  录 TOC \o "1-2" \n  \h \u  HYPERLINK \l _Toc997014327 第一章  通  则 HYPERLINK \l _Toc1672895894 第二章  管  辖 HYPERLINK \l _Toc1711271915 第三章  回  避 HYPERLINK \l _Toc1634192631 第四章  辩护与代理 HYPERLINK \l _Toc144836122 第五章  证  据 HYPERLINK \l _Toc1699679102 第六章  强制措施 HYPERLINK \l _Toc1088465533 第一节  拘  传 HYPERLINK \l _Toc2006246154 第二节  取保候审 HYPERLINK \l _Toc533193304 第三节  监视居住 HYPERLINK \l _Toc2134568551 第四节  拘  留 HYPERLINK \l _Toc686425348 第五节  逮  捕 HYPERLINK \l _Toc1101097709 第六节  监察机关移送案件的强制措施 HYPERLINK \l _Toc1293741975 第七节  其他规定

### 第一章 通 则

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条  为保证人民检察院在刑事诉讼中严格依照法定程序办案，正确履行职权，实现惩罚犯罪与保障人权的统一，根据《中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法》《中华人民共和国人民检察院组织法》和有关法律规定，结合人民检察院工作实际，制定本规则。

**Article 1** These Rules are formulated in accordance with the Criminal Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China, the Organic Law of the People's Procuratorates of the People's Republic of China, and relevant legal provisions, and in light of the actual work of the People's Procuratorates, to ensure that the People's Procuratorates strictly comply with statutory procedures, correctly perform their functions and powers, and achieve the unity of punishing crimes and protecting human rights in criminal proceedings.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条  人民检察院在刑事诉讼中的任务，是立案侦查直接受理的案件、审查逮捕、审查起诉和提起公诉、对刑事诉讼实行法律监督，保证准确、及时查明犯罪事实，正确应用法律，惩罚犯罪分子，保障无罪的人不受刑事追究，保障刑事法律的统一正确实施，维护社会主义法制，尊重和保障人权，保护公民的人身权利、财产权利、民主权利和其他权利，保障社会主义建设事业的顺利进行。

**Article 2** The tasks of the People's Procuratorates in criminal proceedings are to place cases on file for investigation of cases directly accepted, to examine arrests, to examine prosecutions and to initiate public prosecutions, to exercise legal supervision over criminal proceedings, to ensure the accurate and timely ascertainment of criminal facts, the correct application of law, the punishment of criminals, the protection of innocent persons from criminal prosecution, the uniform and correct implementation of criminal law, the safeguarding of the socialist legal system, the respect and protection of human rights, the protection of citizens' personal rights, property rights, democratic rights and other rights, and the smooth progress of socialist construction.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条  人民检察院办理刑事案件，应当严格遵守《中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法》以及其他法律的有关规定，秉持客观公正的立场，尊重和保障人权，既要追诉犯罪，也要保障无罪的人不受刑事追究。

**Article 3** When handling criminal cases, the People's Procuratorates shall strictly observe the relevant provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China and other laws, uphold an objective and impartial stance, respect and protect human rights, and shall not only prosecute crimes but also ensure that innocent persons are not subjected to criminal prosecution.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条  人民检察院办理刑事案件，由检察官、检察长、检察委员会在各自职权范围内对办案事项作出决定，并依照规定承担相应司法责任。  
检察官在检察长领导下开展工作。重大办案事项，由检察长决定。检察长可以根据案件情况，提交检察委员会讨论决定。其他办案事项，检察长可以自行决定，也可以委托检察官决定。  
本规则对应当由检察长或者检察委员会决定的重大办案事项有明确规定的，依照本规则的规定。本规则没有明确规定的，省级人民检察院可以制定有关规定，报最高人民检察院批准。  
以人民检察院名义制发的法律文书，由检察长签发；属于检察官职权范围内决定事项的，检察长可以授权检察官签发。  
重大、疑难、复杂或者有社会影响的案件，应当向检察长报告。

**Article 4** When handling criminal cases, the People's Procuratorates shall have the prosecutor, the chief procurator, and the procuratorial committee make decisions on case-handling matters within their respective functions and powers, and bear corresponding judicial responsibilities in accordance with the provisions.  
Prosecutors shall carry out work under the leadership of the chief procurator. Major case-handling matters shall be decided by the chief procurator. The chief procurator may, based on the circumstances of the case, submit the matter to the procuratorial committee for discussion and decision. For other case-handling matters, the chief procurator may decide on his/her own or entrust a prosecutor to decide.  
If these Rules have clear provisions on major case-handling matters that shall be decided by the chief procurator or the procuratorial committee, such provisions shall be followed. If these Rules have no clear provisions, the provincial people's procuratorate may formulate relevant provisions and report them to the Supreme People's Procuratorate for approval.  
Legal documents issued in the name of the People's Procuratorate shall be signed and issued by the chief procurator; for matters within the functions and powers of a prosecutor to decide, the chief procurator may authorize the prosecutor to sign and issue.  
Cases that are major, difficult, complex, or have social impact shall be reported to the chief procurator.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条  人民检察院办理刑事案件，根据案件情况，可以由一名检察官独任办理，也可以由两名以上检察官组成办案组办理。由检察官办案组办理的，检察长应当指定一名检察官担任主办检察官，组织、指挥办案组办理案件。  
检察官办理案件，可以根据需要配备检察官助理、书记员、司法警察、检察技术人员等检察辅助人员。检察辅助人员依照法律规定承担相应的检察辅助事务。

**Article 5** When handling criminal cases, the People's Procuratorate may, based on the circumstances of the case, have a single prosecutor handle the case independently, or have two or more prosecutors form a case-handling team to handle the case. When a case is handled by a prosecutor case-handling team, the chief procurator shall designate one prosecutor as the lead prosecutor to organize and direct the team in handling the case.  
When handling a case, a prosecutor may be provided with prosecutorial assistants such as assistant prosecutors, clerks, judicial police, and prosecutorial technical personnel as needed. Prosecutorial assistants shall undertake corresponding prosecutorial assistance matters in accordance with legal provisions.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条  人民检察院根据检察工作需要设置业务机构，在刑事诉讼中按照分工履行职责。  
业务机构负责人对本部门的办案活动进行监督管理。需要报请检察长决定的事项和需要向检察长报告的案件，应当先由业务机构负责人审核。业务机构负责人可以主持召开检察官联席会议进行讨论，也可以直接报请检察长决定或者向检察长报告。

**Article 6** The People's Procuratorates shall set up operational departments based on the needs of procuratorial work, and perform their duties according to the division of labor in criminal proceedings.  
The head of an operational department shall supervise and manage the case-handling activities of that department. Matters that need to be submitted to the chief procurator for decision and cases that need to be reported to the chief procurator shall first be reviewed by the head of the operational department. The head of the operational department may convene and preside over a joint meeting of prosecutors for discussion, or may directly submit the matter to the chief procurator for decision or report the case to the chief procurator.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条  检察长不同意检察官处理意见的，可以要求检察官复核，也可以直接作出决定，或者提请检察委员会讨论决定。  
检察官执行检察长决定时，认为决定错误的，应当书面提出意见。检察长不改变原决定的，检察官应当执行。

**Article 7** If the chief procurator disagrees with a prosecutor's handling opinion, the chief procurator may require the prosecutor to review the matter, or may directly make a decision, or submit the matter to the procuratorial committee for discussion and decision.  
When a prosecutor executes a decision of the chief procurator and deems the decision erroneous, the prosecutor shall submit a written opinion. If the chief procurator does not change the original decision, the prosecutor shall execute it.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条  对同一刑事案件的审查逮捕、审查起诉、出庭支持公诉和立案监督、侦查监督、审判监督等工作，由同一检察官或者检察官办案组负责，但是审查逮捕、审查起诉由不同人民检察院管辖，或者依照法律、有关规定应当另行指派检察官或者检察官办案组办理的除外。  
人民检察院履行审查逮捕和审查起诉职责的办案部门，本规则中统称为负责捕诉的部门。

**Article 8** The same prosecutor or prosecutor case-handling team shall be responsible for the examination of arrest, examination of prosecution, appearance in court to support public prosecution, supervision over case filing, supervision over investigation, and supervision over adjudication in relation to the same criminal case, except where the examination of arrest and examination of prosecution are under the jurisdiction of different People's Procuratorates, or where a different prosecutor or prosecutor case-handling team shall be designated to handle the case in accordance with the law or relevant provisions.  
The case-handling departments of the People's Procuratorate that perform the duties of examining arrests and examining prosecutions are collectively referred to in these Rules as the departments responsible for arrest and prosecution.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条  最高人民检察院领导地方各级人民检察院和专门人民检察院的工作，上级人民检察院领导下级人民检察院的工作。检察长统一领导人民检察院的工作。  
上级人民检察院可以依法统一调用辖区的检察人员办理案件，调用的决定应当以书面形式作出。被调用的检察官可以代表办理案件的人民检察院履行出庭支持公诉等各项检察职责。

**Article 9** The Supreme People's Procuratorate shall lead the work of local People's Procuratorates at all levels and special People's Procuratorates, and a People's Procuratorate at a higher level shall lead the work of a People's Procuratorate at a lower level. The chief procurator shall uniformly lead the work of the People's Procuratorate.  
A People's Procuratorate at a higher level may, according to law, uniformly transfer procuratorial personnel within its jurisdiction to handle cases, and the decision on such transfer shall be made in writing. The transferred prosecutor may represent the People's Procuratorate handling the case in performing various procuratorial duties, including appearing in court to support public prosecution.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条  上级人民检察院对下级人民检察院作出的决定，有权予以撤销或者变更；发现下级人民检察院办理的案件有错误的，有权指令下级人民检察院予以纠正。  
下级人民检察院对上级人民检察院的决定应当执行。如果认为有错误的，应当在执行的同时向上级人民检察院报告。

**Article 10** A People's Procuratorate at a higher level shall have the authority to revoke or amend a decision made by a People's Procuratorate at a lower level; if it discovers errors in a case handled by a People's Procuratorate at a lower level, it shall have the authority to instruct the lower-level People's Procuratorate to make corrections.  
A People's Procuratorate at a lower level shall execute a decision made by a People's Procuratorate at a higher level. If it considers the decision erroneous, it shall, while executing it, also report the matter to the higher-level People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条  犯罪嫌疑人、被告人自愿如实供述自己的罪行，承认指控的犯罪事实，愿意接受处罚的，可以依法从宽处理。  
认罪认罚从宽制度适用于所有刑事案件。人民检察院办理刑事案件的各个诉讼环节，都应当做好认罪认罚的相关工作。

**Article 11** If a criminal suspect or defendant voluntarily and truthfully confesses his/her own crimes, admits the alleged criminal facts, and is willing to accept punishment, he/she may be dealt with leniently according to law.  
The system of leniency for pleading guilty and accepting punishment shall apply to all criminal cases. At every litigation stage of handling criminal cases, the People's Procuratorates shall carry out the relevant work on pleading guilty and accepting punishment.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条  人民检察院办理刑事案件的活动依照规定接受人民监督员监督。

**Article 12** The activities of the People's Procuratorates in handling criminal cases shall, in accordance with the provisions, be subject to supervision by people's supervisors.

### 第二章 管 辖

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条  人民检察院在对诉讼活动实行法律监督中发现的司法工作人员利用职权实施的非法拘禁、刑讯逼供、非法搜查等侵犯公民权利、损害司法公正的犯罪，可以由人民检察院立案侦查。  
对于公安机关管辖的国家机关工作人员利用职权实施的重大犯罪案件，需要由人民检察院直接受理的，经省级以上人民检察院决定，可以由人民检察院立案侦查。

**Article 13** For crimes such as illegal detention, extortion of confessions by torture, and illegal search committed by judicial personnel by taking advantage of their functions and powers, which violate citizens' rights and impair judicial justice, discovered by the People's Procuratorate in the course of exercising legal supervision over litigation activities, the People's Procuratorate may place the case on file for investigation.  
For major criminal cases committed by state functionaries by taking advantage of their functions and powers that fall within the jurisdiction of public security organs, if the case needs to be directly accepted by the People's Procuratorate, upon a decision by a People's Procuratorate at or above the provincial level, the People's Procuratorate may place the case on file for investigation.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，由设区的市级人民检察院立案侦查。基层人民检察院发现犯罪线索的，应当报设区的市级人民检察院决定立案侦查。  
设区的市级人民检察院根据案件情况也可以将案件交由基层人民检察院立案侦查，或者要求基层人民检察院协助侦查。对于刑事执行派出检察院辖区内与刑事执行活动有关的犯罪线索，可以交由刑事执行派出检察院立案侦查。  
最高人民检察院、省级人民检察院发现犯罪线索的，可以自行立案侦查，也可以将犯罪线索交由指定的省级人民检察院或者设区的市级人民检察院立案侦查。

**Article 14** For cases directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate, the People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts shall place the case on file for investigation. If a basic-level People's Procuratorate discovers clues to a crime, it shall report the matter to the People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts for a decision on filing the case for investigation.  
The People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts may, based on the circumstances of the case, assign the case to a basic-level People's Procuratorate for filing and investigation, or require the basic-level People's Procuratorate to assist in the investigation. For clues to crimes related to criminal execution activities within the jurisdiction of a detached procuratorate for criminal execution, the matter may be assigned to the detached procuratorate for criminal execution for filing and investigation.  
If the Supreme People's Procuratorate or a provincial People's Procuratorate discovers clues to a crime, it may itself place the case on file for investigation, or assign the clues to a designated provincial People's Procuratorate or a People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts for filing and investigation.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条  对[本规则第十三条](#art-13)第二款规定的案件，人民检察院需要直接立案侦查的，应当层报省级人民检察院决定。  
报请省级人民检察院决定立案侦查的案件，应当制作提请批准直接受理书，写明案件情况以及需要由人民检察院立案侦查的理由，并附有关材料。  
省级人民检察院应当在收到提请批准直接受理书后十日以内作出是否立案侦查的决定。省级人民检察院可以决定由设区的市级人民检察院立案侦查，也可以自行立案侦查。

**Article 15** For cases specified in the second paragraph of Article 13 of these Rules, if the People's Procuratorate needs to place the case on file for investigation directly, it shall report level by level to the provincial People's Procuratorate for a decision.  
For a case reported to the provincial People's Procuratorate for a decision on filing and investigation, a request for approval of direct acceptance shall be prepared, stating the circumstances of the case and the reasons why the case needs to be filed for investigation by the People's Procuratorate, with relevant materials attached.  
The provincial People's Procuratorate shall, within ten days after receiving the request for approval of direct acceptance, make a decision on whether to file the case for investigation. The provincial People's Procuratorate may decide that the People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts shall file the case for investigation, or may itself file the case for investigation.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条  上级人民检察院在必要的时候，可以直接立案侦查或者组织、指挥、参与侦查下级人民检察院管辖的案件。下级人民检察院认为案情重大、复杂，需要由上级人民检察院立案侦查的案件，可以请求移送上级人民检察院立案侦查。

**Article 16** A People's Procuratorate at a higher level may, when necessary, directly place a case on file for investigation, or organize, direct, or participate in the investigation of a case under the jurisdiction of a People's Procuratorate at a lower level. If a People's Procuratorate at a lower level considers a case to be major and complex and needs to be filed for investigation by the People's Procuratorate at a higher level, it may request that the case be transferred to the higher-level People's Procuratorate for filing and investigation.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，发现犯罪嫌疑人同时涉嫌监察机关管辖的职务犯罪线索的，应当及时与同级监察机关沟通。  
经沟通，认为全案由监察机关管辖更为适宜的，人民检察院应当将案件和相应职务犯罪线索一并移送监察机关；认为由监察机关和人民检察院分别管辖更为适宜的，人民检察院应当将监察机关管辖的相应职务犯罪线索移送监察机关，对依法由人民检察院管辖的犯罪案件继续侦查。  
人民检察院应当及时将沟通情况报告上一级人民检察院。沟通期间不得停止对案件的侦查。

**Article 17** When handling a case directly accepted for investigation, if the People's Procuratorate discovers that the criminal suspect is also suspected of duty-related crimes under the jurisdiction of a supervisory organ, it shall promptly communicate with the supervisory organ at the same level.  
If, after communication, it is considered more appropriate for the supervisory organ to have jurisdiction over the entire case, the People's Procuratorate shall transfer the case and the corresponding duty-related crime clues to the supervisory organ; if it is considered more appropriate for the supervisory organ and the People's Procuratorate to have separate jurisdiction, the People's Procuratorate shall transfer the corresponding duty-related crime clues under the jurisdiction of the supervisory organ to the supervisory organ, and continue the investigation of the criminal case under the jurisdiction of the People's Procuratorate according to law.  
The People's Procuratorate shall promptly report the communication situation to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level. The investigation of the case shall not be suspended during the communication period.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件涉及公安机关管辖的刑事案件，应当将属于公安机关管辖的刑事案件移送公安机关。如果涉嫌的主罪属于公安机关管辖，由公安机关为主侦查，人民检察院予以配合；如果涉嫌的主罪属于人民检察院管辖，由人民检察院为主侦查，公安机关予以配合。  
对于一人犯数罪、共同犯罪、共同犯罪的犯罪嫌疑人还实施其他犯罪、多个犯罪嫌疑人实施的犯罪存在关联，并案处理有利于查明案件事实和诉讼进行的，人民检察院可以在职责范围内对相关犯罪案件并案处理。

**Article 18** If a case directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate involves a criminal case under the jurisdiction of a public security organ, the People's Procuratorate shall transfer the criminal case under the jurisdiction of the public security organ to that organ. If the suspected principal crime is under the jurisdiction of the public security organ, the public security organ shall take the lead in the investigation, and the People's Procuratorate shall provide cooperation; if the suspected principal crime is under the jurisdiction of the People's Procuratorate, the People's Procuratorate shall take the lead in the investigation, and the public security organ shall provide cooperation.  
For cases where one person commits multiple crimes, joint crimes, where a criminal suspect in a joint crime also commits other crimes, or where crimes committed by multiple criminal suspects are related, and consolidated handling is conducive to ascertaining the facts of the case and the progress of the proceedings, the People's Procuratorate may, within its functions and powers, consolidate the relevant criminal cases for handling.

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条  [本规则第十三条](#art-13)规定的案件，由犯罪嫌疑人工作单位所在地的人民检察院管辖。如果由其他人民检察院管辖更为适宜的，可以由其他人民检察院管辖。

**Article 19** The cases specified in Article 13 of these Rules shall be under the jurisdiction of the People's Procuratorate of the place where the criminal suspect's work unit is located. If it is more appropriate for another People's Procuratorate to have jurisdiction, such other People's Procuratorate may have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条  对管辖不明确的案件，可以由有关人民检察院协商确定管辖。

**Article 20** For cases where jurisdiction is unclear, the relevant People's Procuratorates may determine jurisdiction through consultation.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条  几个人民检察院都有权管辖的案件，由最初受理的人民检察院管辖。必要时，可以由主要犯罪地的人民检察院管辖。

**Article 21** For a case over which several People's Procuratorates have jurisdiction, the People's Procuratorate that initially accepted the case shall have jurisdiction. If necessary, the case may be placed under the jurisdiction of the People's Procuratorate at the place where the principal crime was committed.

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条  对于下列案件，上级人民检察院可以指定管辖：  
（一）管辖有争议的案件；  
（二）需要改变管辖的案件；  
（三）需要集中管辖的特定类型的案件；  
（四）其他需要指定管辖的案件。  
对前款案件的审查起诉指定管辖的，人民检察院应当与相应的人民法院协商一致。对前款第三项案件的审查逮捕指定管辖的，人民检察院应当与相应的公安机关协商一致。

**Article 22** For the following cases, a People's Procuratorate at a higher level may designate jurisdiction:  
(1) Cases over which jurisdiction is disputed;  
(2) Cases for which jurisdiction needs to be changed;  
(3) Specific types of cases for which centralized jurisdiction is needed;  
(4) Other cases for which jurisdiction needs to be designated.  
Where the review and prosecution of a case referred to in the preceding paragraph is designated for jurisdiction, the People's Procuratorate shall reach an agreement with the corresponding People's Court. Where the review and arrest of a case referred to in item (3) of the preceding paragraph is designated for jurisdiction, the People's Procuratorate shall reach an agreement with the corresponding public security organ.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条  军事检察院等专门人民检察院的管辖以及军队与地方互涉刑事案件的管辖，按照有关规定执行。

**Article 23** The jurisdiction of special People's Procuratorates such as the Military Procuratorate and the jurisdiction of criminal cases involving both the military and local authorities shall be exercised in accordance with the relevant provisions.

### 第三章 回 避

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条  检察人员在受理举报和办理案件过程中，发现有[刑事诉讼法第二十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-29)或者第三十条规定的情形之一的，应当自行提出回避；没有自行提出回避的，人民检察院应当决定其回避，当事人及其法定代理人有权要求其回避。

**Article 24** Where, in the course of receiving reports and handling cases, a procurator discovers that he/she is in any of the circumstances provided for in Article 29 or Article 30 of the Criminal Procedure Law, he/she shall voluntarily request withdrawal; if he/she fails to voluntarily request withdrawal, the People's Procuratorate shall decide on his/her withdrawal, and the party and his/her statutory agent have the right to request his/her withdrawal.

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条  检察人员自行回避的，应当书面或者口头提出，并说明理由。口头提出的，应当记录在案。

**Article 25** Where a procurator withdraws voluntarily, he/she shall submit the request in writing or orally and state the reasons. If the request is made orally, it shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条  人民检察院应当告知当事人及其法定代理人有依法申请回避的权利，并告知办理相关案件的检察人员、书记员等人员的姓名、职务等有关情况。

**Article 26** The People's Procuratorate shall inform the party and his/her statutory agent of their right to apply for withdrawal according to law, and shall inform them of the names, positions and other relevant information of the procurators, clerks and other personnel handling the relevant case.

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条  当事人及其法定代理人要求检察人员回避的，应当书面或者口头向人民检察院提出，并说明理由。口头提出的，应当记录在案。根据[刑事诉讼法第三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-30)的规定要求检察人员回避的，应当提供有关证明材料。  
人民检察院经过审查或者调查，认为检察人员符合回避条件的，应当作出回避决定；不符合回避条件的，应当驳回申请。

**Article 27** Where a party or his/her statutory agent requests the withdrawal of a procurator, he/she shall submit the request in writing or orally to the People's Procuratorate and state the reasons. If the request is made orally, it shall be recorded in the case file. If the request for withdrawal of a procurator is made pursuant to Article 30 of the Criminal Procedure Law, relevant evidentiary materials shall be provided.  
After examination or investigation, if the People's Procuratorate deems that the conditions for withdrawal are met, it shall make a decision of withdrawal; if the conditions are not met, it shall reject the application.

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条  在开庭审理过程中，当事人及其法定代理人向法庭申请出庭的检察人员回避的，在收到人民法院通知后，人民检察院应当作出回避或者驳回申请的决定。不属于[刑事诉讼法第二十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-29)、第三十条规定情形的回避申请，出席法庭的检察人员应当建议法庭当庭驳回。

**Article 28** During court trial, where the party or his/her statutory agent applies to the court for the withdrawal of the procurator appearing in court, upon receiving the notice from the People's Court, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision to withdraw or reject the application. For a withdrawal application that does not fall under the circumstances provided for in Article 29 or Article 30 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the procurator appearing in court shall propose to the court to reject it on the spot.

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条  检察长的回避，由检察委员会讨论决定。检察委员会讨论检察长回避问题时，由副检察长主持，检察长不得参加。  
其他检察人员的回避，由检察长决定。

**Article 29** The withdrawal of the chief procurator shall be discussed and decided by the procuratorial committee. When the procuratorial committee discusses the withdrawal of the chief procurator, the meeting shall be presided over by the deputy chief procurator, and the chief procurator shall not participate.  
The withdrawal of other procurators shall be decided by the chief procurator.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条  当事人及其法定代理人要求公安机关负责人回避，向同级人民检察院提出，或者向公安机关提出后，公安机关移送同级人民检察院的，由检察长提交检察委员会讨论决定。

**Article 30** Where a party or his/her statutory agent requests the withdrawal of the head of a public security organ and submits the request to the People's Procuratorate at the same level, or submits it to the public security organ and the public security organ transfers it to the People's Procuratorate at the same level, the request shall be submitted by the chief procurator to the procuratorial committee for discussion and decision.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条  检察长应当回避，本人没有自行回避，当事人及其法定代理人也没有申请其回避的，检察委员会应当决定其回避。  
其他检察人员有前款规定情形的，检察长应当决定其回避。

**Article 31** Where the chief procurator should withdraw but fails to voluntarily withdraw, and the party and his/her statutory agent also fail to apply for his/her withdrawal, the procuratorial committee shall decide on his/her withdrawal.  
Where other procurators fall under the circumstances provided for in the preceding paragraph, the chief procurator shall decide on their withdrawal.

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条  人民检察院作出驳回申请回避的决定后，应当告知当事人及其法定代理人如不服本决定，有权在收到驳回申请回避的决定书后五日以内向原决定机关申请复议一次。

**Article 32** After making a decision to reject an application for withdrawal, the People's Procuratorate shall inform the party and his/her statutory agent that, if they are not satisfied with the decision, they have the right to apply to the original decision-making organ for reconsideration once within five days after receiving the written decision on rejection of the application for withdrawal.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条  当事人及其法定代理人对驳回申请回避的决定不服申请复议的，决定机关应当在三日以内作出复议决定并书面通知申请人。

**Article 33** Where the party or his/her statutory agent applies for reconsideration of the decision to reject the application for withdrawal, the decision-making organ shall make a reconsideration decision within three days and notify the applicant in writing.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条  对人民检察院直接受理的案件进行侦查的人员或者进行补充侦查的人员在回避决定作出以前和复议期间，不得停止对案件的侦查。

**Article 34** Where a person conducting investigation of a case directly accepted by the People's Procuratorate or a person conducting supplementary investigation, before the withdrawal decision is made and during the reconsideration period, shall not suspend the investigation of the case.

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条  参加过同一案件侦查的人员，不得承办该案的审查逮捕、审查起诉、出庭支持公诉和诉讼监督工作，但在审查起诉阶段参加自行补充侦查的人员除外。

**Article 35** Persons who have participated in the investigation of the same case shall not undertake the work of review and arrest, review and prosecution, appearing in court to support public prosecution, and litigation supervision for that case, except for persons who participate in self-conducted supplementary investigation at the stage of review and prosecution.

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条  被决定回避的检察长在回避决定作出以前所取得的证据和进行的诉讼行为是否有效，由检察委员会根据案件具体情况决定。  
被决定回避的其他检察人员在回避决定作出以前所取得的证据和进行的诉讼行为是否有效，由检察长根据案件具体情况决定。  
被决定回避的公安机关负责人在回避决定作出以前所进行的诉讼行为是否有效，由作出决定的人民检察院检察委员会根据案件具体情况决定。

**Article 36** Whether the evidence obtained and the procedural acts performed before the withdrawal decision is made are valid for the chief procurator for whom withdrawal has been decided shall be decided by the procuratorial committee based on the specific circumstances of the case.  
Whether the evidence obtained and the procedural acts performed before the withdrawal decision is made are valid for other procurators for whom withdrawal has been decided shall be decided by the chief procurator based on the specific circumstances of the case.  
Whether the procedural acts performed before the withdrawal decision is made are valid for the head of a public security organ for whom withdrawal has been decided shall be decided by the procuratorial committee of the People's Procuratorate that made the decision based on the specific circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条  本规则关于回避的规定，适用于书记员、司法警察和人民检察院聘请或者指派的翻译人员、鉴定人。  
书记员、司法警察和人民检察院聘请或者指派的翻译人员、鉴定人的回避由检察长决定。  
辩护人、诉讼代理人可以依照刑事诉讼法及本规则关于回避的规定要求回避、申请复议。

**Article 37** The provisions of these Rules on withdrawal apply to clerks, judicial police officers, and interpreters and expert evaluators engaged or appointed by the People's Procuratorate.  
The withdrawal of clerks, judicial police officers, and interpreters and expert evaluators engaged or appointed by the People's Procuratorate shall be decided by the chief procurator.  
Defense counsels and litigation agents may request withdrawal and apply for reconsideration in accordance with the provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law and these Rules on withdrawal.

### 第四章 辩护与代理

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条  人民检察院在办案过程中，应当依法保障犯罪嫌疑人行使辩护权利。

**Article 38** In the process of handling cases, the People's Procuratorate shall safeguard the right of criminal suspects to exercise the right to defense according to law.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条  辩护人、诉讼代理人向人民检察院提出有关申请、要求或者提交有关书面材料的，负责案件管理的部门应当接收并及时移送办案部门或者与办案部门联系，具体业务由办案部门负责办理，本规则另有规定的除外。

**Article 39** Where a defense counsel or litigation agent submits relevant applications, requests or written materials to the People's Procuratorate, the department responsible for case management shall receive them and promptly transfer them to the case-handling department or contact the case-handling department; the specific business shall be handled by the case-handling department, except as otherwise provided in these Rules.

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条  人民检察院负责侦查的部门在第一次讯问犯罪嫌疑人或者对其采取强制措施时，应当告知犯罪嫌疑人有权委托辩护人，并告知其如果因经济困难或者其他原因没有委托辩护人的，可以申请法律援助。属于[刑事诉讼法第三十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-35)规定情形的，应当告知犯罪嫌疑人有权获得法律援助。  
人民检察院自收到移送起诉案卷材料之日起三日以内，应当告知犯罪嫌疑人有权委托辩护人，并告知其如果因经济困难或者其他原因没有委托辩护人的，可以申请法律援助。属于[刑事诉讼法第三十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-35)规定情形的，应当告知犯罪嫌疑人有权获得法律援助。  
当面口头告知的，应当记入笔录，由被告知人签名；电话告知的，应当记录在案；书面告知的，应当将送达回执入卷。

**Article 40** When the department responsible for investigation of the People's Procuratorate interrogates a criminal suspect for the first time or adopts compulsory measures against him/her, it shall inform the criminal suspect of his/her right to entrust a defense counsel, and inform him/her that if he/she fails to entrust a defense counsel due to economic difficulties or other reasons, he/she may apply for legal aid. Where this falls under the circumstances provided for in Article 35 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the criminal suspect shall be informed of his/her right to obtain legal aid.  
Within three days from the date of receiving the transferred case file materials for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall inform the criminal suspect of his/her right to entrust a defense counsel, and inform him/her that if he/she fails to entrust a defense counsel due to economic difficulties or other reasons, he/she may apply for legal aid. Where this falls under the circumstances provided for in Article 35 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the criminal suspect shall be informed of his/her right to obtain legal aid.  
Where the information is given orally in person, it shall be recorded in the transcript and signed by the person informed; where the information is given by telephone, it shall be recorded in the case file; where the information is given in writing, the receipt of service shall be placed in the case file.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条  在押或者被指定居所监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人向人民检察院提出委托辩护人要求的，人民检察院应当及时向其监护人、近亲属或者其指定的人员转达要求，并记录在案。

**Article 41** Where a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance at a designated residence submits a request to the People's Procuratorate to retain a defender, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly convey the request to his or her guardian, near relative, or designated person, and shall record it in the case file.

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查案件和审查起诉案件，发现犯罪嫌疑人是盲、聋、哑人或者是尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人，或者可能被判处无期徒刑、死刑，没有委托辩护人的，应当自发现之日起三日以内书面通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供辩护。

**Article 42** Where, in handling cases directly accepted for investigation and cases subject to review for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate discovers that the criminal suspect is blind, deaf, or mute, or is a mental patient who has not completely lost the ability to recognize or control his or her own conduct, or may be sentenced to life imprisonment or death, and has not retained a defender, it shall, within three days from the date of discovery, notify in writing the legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide defense for the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条  人民检察院收到在押或者被指定居所监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人提出的法律援助申请，应当在二十四小时以内将申请材料转交法律援助机构，并通知犯罪嫌疑人的监护人、近亲属或者其委托的其他人员协助提供有关证件、证明等材料。

**Article 43** Where the People's Procuratorate receives a legal aid application filed by a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance at a designated residence, it shall, within twenty-four hours, transfer the application materials to the legal aid institution and notify the criminal suspect's guardian, near relative, or other person authorized by him or her to assist in providing relevant certificates, documents, and other materials.

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条  属于应当提供法律援助的情形，犯罪嫌疑人拒绝法律援助机构指派的律师作为辩护人的，人民检察院应当查明拒绝的原因。有正当理由的，予以准许，但犯罪嫌疑人需另行委托辩护人；犯罪嫌疑人未另行委托辩护人的，应当书面通知法律援助机构另行指派律师为其提供辩护。

**Article 44** In circumstances where legal aid shall be provided, if a criminal suspect refuses the lawyer assigned by the legal aid institution to act as a defender, the People's Procuratorate shall ascertain the reason for the refusal. If there is a justifiable reason, the refusal shall be permitted, provided that the criminal suspect shall retain another defender; if the criminal suspect fails to retain another defender, the People's Procuratorate shall notify in writing the legal aid institution to assign another lawyer to provide defense for him or her.

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条  辩护人接受委托后告知人民检察院，或者法律援助机构指派律师后通知人民检察院的，人民检察院负责案件管理的部门应当及时登记辩护人的相关信息，并将有关情况和材料及时通知、移交办案部门。  
负责案件管理的部门对办理业务的辩护律师，应当查验其律师执业证书、律师事务所证明和授权委托书或者法律援助公函。对其他辩护人、诉讼代理人，应当查验其身份证明和授权委托书。

**Article 45** Where a defender, after accepting the appointment, informs the People's Procuratorate, or a legal aid institution, after assigning a lawyer, notifies the People's Procuratorate, the case management department of the People's Procuratorate shall promptly register the relevant information of the defender and notify and transfer the relevant circumstances and materials to the case-handling department in a timely manner.  
The case management department shall examine the lawyer's practice certificate, law firm certificate, and power of attorney or legal aid letter for the defense lawyer handling the matter. For other defenders or litigation agents, it shall examine their identity certificates and powers of attorney.

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条  人民检察院负责案件管理的部门应当依照法律规定对辩护人、诉讼代理人的资格进行审查，办案部门应当予以协助。

**Article 46** The case management department of the People's Procuratorate shall, in accordance with the provisions of laws, examine the qualifications of defenders and litigation agents, and the case-handling department shall provide assistance.

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条  自人民检察院对案件审查起诉之日起，应当允许辩护律师查阅、摘抄、复制本案的案卷材料。案卷材料包括案件的诉讼文书和证据材料。  
人民检察院直接受理侦查案件移送起诉，审查起诉案件退回补充侦查、改变管辖、提起公诉的，应当及时告知辩护律师。

**Article 47** From the date on which the People's Procuratorate reviews the case for prosecution, the defense lawyer shall be permitted to consult, extract, and duplicate the case file materials of the case. The case file materials include the litigation documents and evidence materials of the case.  
Where a case directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate is transferred for prosecution, or a case under review for prosecution is returned for supplementary investigation, the jurisdiction is changed, or a public prosecution is initiated, the defense lawyer shall be promptly informed.

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条  自人民检察院对案件审查起诉之日起，律师以外的辩护人向人民检察院申请查阅、摘抄、复制本案的案卷材料或者申请同在押、被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人会见和通信的，由人民检察院负责捕诉的部门进行审查并作出是否许可的决定，在三日以内书面通知申请人。  
人民检察院许可律师以外的辩护人同在押或者被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人通信的，可以要求看守所或者公安机关将书信送交人民检察院进行检查。  
律师以外的辩护人申请查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料或者申请同在押、被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人会见和通信，具有下列情形之一的，人民检察院可以不予许可：  
（一）同案犯罪嫌疑人在逃的；  
（二）案件事实不清，证据不足，或者遗漏罪行、遗漏同案犯罪嫌疑人需要补充侦查的；  
（三）涉及国家秘密或者商业秘密的；  
（四）有事实表明存在串供、毁灭、伪造证据或者危害证人人身安全可能的。

**Article 48** From the date on which the People's Procuratorate reviews the case for prosecution, if a defender other than a lawyer applies to the People's Procuratorate to consult, extract, or duplicate the case file materials, or to meet and correspond with the criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of the People's Procuratorate shall review the application and decide whether to grant permission, and shall notify the applicant in writing within three days.  
If the People's Procuratorate permits a defender other than a lawyer to correspond with a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance, it may request the detention house or the public security organ to submit the correspondence to the People's Procuratorate for inspection.  
Where a defender other than a lawyer applies to consult, extract, or duplicate case file materials, or to meet and correspond with a criminal suspect in custody or under residential surveillance, the People's Procuratorate may refuse permission if any of the following circumstances exists:  
(1) the co-suspect in the same case is at large;  
(2) the facts of the case are unclear, the evidence is insufficient, or there are omitted crimes or omitted co-suspects that require supplementary investigation;  
(3) the case involves state secrets or trade secrets;  
(4) there are facts indicating the possibility of collusion, destruction or fabrication of evidence, or endangering the personal safety of a witness.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条  辩护律师或者经过许可的其他辩护人到人民检察院查阅、摘抄、复制本案的案卷材料，由负责案件管理的部门及时安排，由办案部门提供案卷材料。因办案部门工作等原因无法及时安排的，应当向辩护人说明，并自即日起三个工作日以内安排辩护人阅卷，办案部门应当予以配合。  
人民检察院应当为辩护人查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料设置专门的场所或者电子卷宗阅卷终端设备。必要时，人民检察院可以派员在场协助。  
辩护人复制案卷材料可以采取复印、拍照、扫描、刻录等方式，人民检察院不收取费用。

**Article 49** Where a defense lawyer or another defender who has obtained permission comes to the People's Procuratorate to consult, extract, or duplicate the case file materials, the case management department shall arrange it promptly, and the case-handling department shall provide the case file materials. If arrangement cannot be made promptly due to the work of the case-handling department or other reasons, an explanation shall be given to the defender, and the defender shall be arranged to review the case file within three working days from that date, and the case-handling department shall cooperate.  
The People's Procuratorate shall set up special venues or electronic case file review terminals for defenders to consult, extract, and duplicate case file materials. When necessary, the People's Procuratorate may send personnel to be present to assist.  
A defender may duplicate case file materials by means of photocopying, photographing, scanning, burning, etc., and the People's Procuratorate shall not charge any fees.

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条  案件提请批准逮捕或者移送起诉后，辩护人认为公安机关在侦查期间收集的证明犯罪嫌疑人无罪或者罪轻的证据材料未提交，申请人民检察院向公安机关调取的，人民检察院负责捕诉的部门应当及时审查。经审查，认为辩护人申请调取的证据已收集并且与案件事实有联系的，应当予以调取；认为辩护人申请调取的证据未收集或者与案件事实没有联系的，应当决定不予调取并向辩护人说明理由。公安机关移送相关证据材料的，人民检察院应当在三日以内告知辩护人。  
人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，适用前款规定。

**Article 50** After a case is submitted for approval of arrest or transferred for prosecution, if a defender believes that the public security organ has not submitted the evidence materials collected during investigation that prove the criminal suspect is innocent or has a lesser crime, and applies to the People's Procuratorate to obtain such evidence from the public security organ, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of the People's Procuratorate shall promptly review the application. Upon review, if it considers that the evidence applied for by the defender has been collected and is related to the facts of the case, it shall obtain such evidence; if it considers that the evidence applied for has not been collected or is not related to the facts of the case, it shall decide not to obtain it and shall explain the reasons to the defender. Where the public security organ transfers the relevant evidence materials, the People's Procuratorate shall inform the defender within three days.  
Where the People's Procuratorate handles a case directly accepted for investigation, the preceding paragraph shall apply.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条  在人民检察院侦查、审查逮捕、审查起诉过程中，辩护人收集的有关犯罪嫌疑人不在犯罪现场、未达到刑事责任年龄、属于依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的证据，告知人民检察院的，人民检察院应当及时审查。

**Article 51** During the process of investigation, review of arrest, and review for prosecution by the People's Procuratorate, if a defender informs the People's Procuratorate of evidence collected showing that the criminal suspect was not at the crime scene, did not reach the age of criminal responsibility, or is a mental patient who shall not bear criminal responsibility according to law, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly review the evidence.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条  案件移送起诉后，辩护律师依据[刑事诉讼法第四十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-43)第一款的规定申请人民检察院收集、调取证据的，人民检察院负责捕诉的部门应当及时审查。经审查，认为需要收集、调取证据的，应当决定收集、调取并制作笔录附卷；决定不予收集、调取的，应当书面说明理由。  
人民检察院根据辩护律师的申请收集、调取证据时，辩护律师可以在场。

**Article 52** After a case is transferred for prosecution, if a defense lawyer, pursuant to the provisions of Article 43, Paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, applies to the People's Procuratorate to collect or obtain evidence, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of the People's Procuratorate shall promptly review the application. Upon review, if it considers that evidence needs to be collected or obtained, it shall decide to collect or obtain the evidence and shall make a record to be attached to the case file; if it decides not to collect or obtain the evidence, it shall provide written reasons for the decision.  
Where the People's Procuratorate collects or obtains evidence upon the application of a defense lawyer, the defense lawyer may be present.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条  辩护律师申请人民检察院许可其向被害人或者其近亲属、被害人提供的证人收集与本案有关材料的，人民检察院负责捕诉的部门应当及时进行审查。人民检察院应当在五日以内作出是否许可的决定，通知辩护律师；不予许可的，应当书面说明理由。

**Article 53** Where a defense lawyer applies to the People's Procuratorate for permission to collect materials related to the case from the victim or his or her near relatives, or from a witness produced by the victim, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of the People's Procuratorate shall promptly conduct a review. The People's Procuratorate shall, within five days, decide whether to grant permission and notify the defense lawyer; if permission is not granted, it shall provide written reasons.

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条  在人民检察院侦查、审查逮捕、审查起诉过程中，辩护人要求听取其意见的，办案部门应当及时安排。辩护人提出书面意见的，办案部门应当接收并登记。  
听取辩护人意见应当制作笔录或者记录在案，辩护人提出的书面意见应当附卷。  
辩护人提交案件相关材料的，办案部门应当将辩护人提交材料的目的、来源及内容等情况记录在案，一并附卷。

**Article 54** During the process of investigation, review of arrest, and review for prosecution by the People's Procuratorate, if a defender requests to present his or her opinions, the case-handling department shall promptly arrange for it. If the defender submits written opinions, the case-handling department shall receive and register them.  
When hearing the defender's opinions, a record shall be made or noted in the case file, and any written opinions submitted by the defender shall be attached to the case file.  
Where the defender submits materials related to the case, the case-handling department shall record in the case file the purpose, source, and content of the materials submitted by the defender, and attach them to the case file.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条  人民检察院自收到移送起诉案卷材料之日起三日以内，应当告知被害人及其法定代理人或者其近亲属、附带民事诉讼的当事人及其法定代理人有权委托诉讼代理人。被害人及其法定代理人、近亲属因经济困难没有委托诉讼代理人的，应当告知其可以申请法律援助。  
当面口头告知的，应当记入笔录，由被告知人签名；电话告知的，应当记录在案；书面告知的，应当将送达回执入卷。被害人众多或者不确定，无法以上述方式逐一告知的，可以公告告知。无法告知的，应当记录在案。  
被害人有法定代理人的，应当告知其法定代理人；没有法定代理人的，应当告知其近亲属。  
法定代理人或者近亲属为二人以上的，可以告知其中一人。告知时应当按照[刑事诉讼法第一百零八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-108)第三项、第六项列举的顺序择先进行。  
当事人及其法定代理人、近亲属委托诉讼代理人的，参照[刑事诉讼法第三十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-33)等法律规定执行。

**Article 55** Within three days from the date of receiving the case file materials transferred for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall inform the victim and his or her legal representative or near relatives, and the parties to an incidental civil action and their legal representatives, of their right to retain a litigation agent. If the victim, his or her legal representative, or near relatives have not retained a litigation agent due to financial difficulties, they shall be informed that they may apply for legal aid.  
If the notification is made orally in person, it shall be recorded in the record, which shall be signed by the person notified; if it is made by telephone, it shall be noted in the case file; if it is made in writing, the receipt of service shall be placed in the case file. If there are many victims or the victims are uncertain and individual notification by the above methods is impossible, notification may be made by public announcement. If notification is impossible, it shall be noted in the case file.  
If the victim has a legal representative, the legal representative shall be notified; if there is no legal representative, the near relatives shall be notified.  
If there are two or more legal representatives or near relatives, one of them may be notified. When notifying, the order shall follow the sequence listed in items (3) and (6) of Article 108 of the Criminal Procedure Law, giving priority to the first.  
Where a party, his or her legal representative, or near relatives retain a litigation agent, the provisions of Article 33 of the Criminal Procedure Law and other legal provisions shall be referred to for implementation.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条  经人民检察院许可，诉讼代理人查阅、摘抄、复制本案案卷材料的，参照[本规则第四十九条](#art-49)的规定办理。  
律师担任诉讼代理人，需要申请人民检察院收集、调取证据的，参照[本规则第五十二条](#art-52)的规定办理。

**Article 56** Where a litigation agent, with the permission of the People's Procuratorate, consults, extracts, or duplicates the case file materials of the case, the provisions of Article 49 of these Rules shall be applied mutatis mutandis.  
Where a lawyer serving as a litigation agent needs to apply to the People's Procuratorate to collect or obtain evidence, the provisions of Article 52 of these Rules shall be applied mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条  辩护人、诉讼代理人认为公安机关、人民检察院、人民法院及其工作人员具有下列阻碍其依法行使诉讼权利行为之一，向同级或者上一级人民检察院申诉或者控告的，人民检察院负责控告申诉检察的部门应当接受并依法办理，其他办案部门应当予以配合：  
（一）违反规定，对辩护人、诉讼代理人提出的回避要求不予受理或者对不予回避决定不服的复议申请不予受理的；  
（二）未依法告知犯罪嫌疑人、被告人有权委托辩护人的；  
（三）未转达在押或者被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人委托辩护人的要求或者未转交其申请法律援助材料的；  
（四）应当通知而不通知法律援助机构为符合条件的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者被申请强制医疗的人指派律师提供辩护或者法律援助的；  
（五）在规定时间内不受理、不答复辩护人提出的变更强制措施申请或者解除强制措施要求的；  
（六）未依法告知辩护律师犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌的罪名和案件有关情况的；  
（七）违法限制辩护律师同在押、被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人会见和通信的；  
（八）违法不允许辩护律师查阅、摘抄、复制本案的案卷材料的；  
（九）违法限制辩护律师收集、核实有关证据材料的；  
（十）没有正当理由不同意辩护律师收集、调取证据或者通知证人出庭作证的申请，或者不答复、不说明理由的；  
（十一）未依法提交证明犯罪嫌疑人、被告人无罪或者罪轻的证据材料的；  
（十二）未依法听取辩护人、诉讼代理人意见的；  
（十三）未依法将开庭的时间、地点及时通知辩护人、诉讼代理人的；  
（十四）未依法向辩护人、诉讼代理人及时送达本案的法律文书或者及时告知案件移送情况的；  
（十五）阻碍辩护人、诉讼代理人在法庭审理过程中依法行使诉讼权利的；  
（十六）其他阻碍辩护人、诉讼代理人依法行使诉讼权利的。  
对于直接向上一级人民检察院申诉或者控告的，上一级人民检察院可以交下级人民检察院办理，也可以直接办理。  
辩护人、诉讼代理人认为看守所及其工作人员有阻碍其依法行使诉讼权利的行为，向人民检察院申诉或者控告的，由负责刑事执行检察的部门接受并依法办理；其他办案部门收到申诉或者控告的，应当及时移送负责刑事执行检察的部门。

**Article 57** Where a defender or litigation agent believes that a public security organ, the People's Procuratorate, the People's Court, or their staff members have committed any of the following acts that obstruct the defender or litigation agent from lawfully exercising their litigation rights, and files a petition or complaint with the People's Procuratorate at the same level or the next higher level, the department responsible for petition and prosecution of the People's Procuratorate shall accept and handle it according to law, and other case-handling departments shall cooperate:  
(1) failing, in violation of provisions, to accept a request for withdrawal filed by a defender or litigation agent, or failing to accept a review application regarding a decision not to withdraw;  
(2) failing to inform, according to law, the criminal suspect or defendant of the right to retain a defender;  
(3) failing to convey the request of a criminal suspect or defendant in custody or under residential surveillance for retaining a defender, or failing to forward his or her application for legal aid materials;  
(4) failing, when obligated, to notify a legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide defense or legal aid for a criminal suspect or defendant who meets the conditions, or for a person who is subject to an application for compulsory medical treatment;  
(5) failing, within the prescribed time, to accept or respond to a defender's application for modification of compulsory measures or request for removal of compulsory measures;  
(6) failing to inform the defense lawyer, according to law, of the crime suspected of the criminal suspect and the relevant information of the case;  
(7) illegally restricting the defense lawyer from meeting and corresponding with the criminal suspect or defendant in custody or under residential surveillance;  
(8) illegally refusing to allow the defense lawyer to consult, extract, or duplicate the case file materials of the case;  
(9) illegally restricting the defense lawyer from collecting or verifying relevant evidence materials;  
(10) without justifiable reasons, refusing to agree to the defense lawyer's application to collect or obtain evidence or to notify a witness to testify in court, or failing to reply or explain the reasons;  
(11) failing to submit, according to law, evidence materials proving that the criminal suspect or defendant is innocent or has a lesser crime;  
(12) failing to hear, according to law, the opinions of the defender or litigation agent;  
(13) failing to notify the defender or litigation agent of the time and place of the court session in a timely manner, according to law;  
(14) failing to serve promptly, according to law, the legal documents of the case to the defender or litigation agent, or failing to inform them promptly of the transfer of the case;  
(15) obstructing the defender or litigation agent from exercising their litigation rights according to law during the court trial;  
(16) other acts obstructing the defender or litigation agent from lawfully exercising their litigation rights.  
Where a petition or complaint is filed directly with the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level, the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level may refer it to the lower-level People's Procuratorate for handling, or may handle it directly.  
Where a defender or litigation agent believes that a detention house or its staff members have committed acts obstructing them from lawfully exercising their litigation rights and files a petition or complaint with the People's Procuratorate, the department responsible for criminal execution inspection shall accept and handle it according to law; if other case-handling departments receive the petition or complaint, they shall promptly transfer it to the department responsible for criminal execution inspection.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条  辩护人、诉讼代理人认为其依法行使诉讼权利受到阻碍向人民检察院申诉或者控告的，人民检察院应当及时受理并调查核实，在十日以内办结并书面答复。情况属实的，通知有关机关或者本院有关部门、下级人民检察院予以纠正。

**Article 58** Where a defender or litigation agent believes that his or her lawful exercise of litigation rights has been obstructed and files a petition or complaint with the People's Procuratorate, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly accept the case, investigate and verify the matter, and complete the handling and provide a written reply within ten days. If the situation is confirmed, the People's Procuratorate shall notify the relevant organ or the relevant department of the People's Procuratorate itself, or a lower-level People's Procuratorate, to take corrective action.

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条  辩护律师告知人民检察院其委托人或者其他人员准备实施、正在实施危害国家安全、危害公共安全以及严重危及他人人身安全犯罪的，人民检察院应当接受并立即移送有关机关依法处理。  
人民检察院应当为反映情况的辩护律师保密。

**Article 59** Where a defense lawyer informs the People's Procuratorate that his or her client or another person is preparing to commit or is committing a crime that endangers national security, endangers public safety, or seriously endangers the personal safety of others, the People's Procuratorate shall accept the information and immediately transfer it to the relevant organ for handling according to law.  
The People's Procuratorate shall keep confidential the defense lawyer who reported the information.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条  人民检察院发现辩护人有帮助犯罪嫌疑人、被告人隐匿、毁灭、伪造证据、串供，或者威胁、引诱证人作伪证以及其他干扰司法机关诉讼活动的行为，可能涉嫌犯罪的，应当将涉嫌犯罪的线索或者证据材料移送有管辖权的机关依法处理。  
人民检察院发现辩护律师在刑事诉讼中违反法律、法规或者执业纪律的，应当及时向其所在的律师事务所、所属的律师协会以及司法行政机关通报。

**Article 60** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that a defender has, by assisting a criminal suspect or defendant in concealing, destroying, or fabricating evidence, colluding in statements, or threatening or inducing a witness to give false testimony or other acts that interfere with the judicial authorities' litigation activities, which may constitute a crime, it shall transfer the clues or evidence materials of the suspected crime to the organ with jurisdiction for handling according to law.  
Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that a defense lawyer has violated laws, regulations, or professional ethics in criminal proceedings, it shall promptly report the matter to the law firm where the lawyer practices, the lawyers association to which the lawyer belongs, and the judicial administration organ.

### 第五章 证 据

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条  人民检察院认定案件事实，应当以证据为根据。  
公诉案件中被告人有罪的举证责任由人民检察院承担。人民检察院在提起公诉指控犯罪时，应当提出确实、充分的证据，并运用证据加以证明。  
人民检察院提起公诉，应当秉持客观公正立场，对被告人有罪、罪重、罪轻的证据都应当向人民法院提出。

**Article 61** When determining the facts of a case, the People's Procuratorate shall base its determination on evidence.  
The burden of adducing evidence to prove the guilt of the defendant in a public prosecution case shall be borne by the People's Procuratorate. When instituting a public prosecution and charging a crime, the People's Procuratorate shall present truthful and sufficient evidence and use that evidence to prove the charges.  
When instituting a public prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall uphold an objective and impartial position, and shall present to the People's Court all evidence concerning the defendant's guilt, the gravity of the offense, and any mitigating circumstances.

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条  证据的审查认定，应当结合案件的具体情况，从证据与待证事实的关联程度、各证据之间的联系、是否依照法定程序收集等方面进行综合审查判断。

**Article 62** The examination and determination of evidence shall be conducted in light of the specific circumstances of the case, through a comprehensive examination and judgment from the perspectives of the relevance of the evidence to the facts to be proved, the connections among the various items of evidence, and whether the evidence was collected in accordance with legally prescribed procedures.

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条  人民检察院侦查终结或者提起公诉的案件，证据应当确实、充分。证据确实、充分，应当符合以下条件：  
（一）定罪量刑的事实都有证据证明；  
（二）据以定案的证据均经法定程序查证属实；  
（三）综合全案证据，对所认定事实已排除合理怀疑。

**Article 63** For cases where the People's Procuratorate concludes an investigation or institutes a public prosecution, the evidence shall be truthful and sufficient. Evidence being truthful and sufficient shall satisfy the following conditions:  
(1) All facts concerning conviction and sentencing are supported by evidence;  
(2) All evidence on which the case is determined has been verified as true through legally prescribed procedures;  
(3) Comprehensive evaluation of all evidence in the case has eliminated any reasonable doubt regarding the facts determined.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条  行政机关在行政执法和查办案件过程中收集的物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据等证据材料，经人民检察院审查符合法定要求的，可以作为证据使用。  
行政机关在行政执法和查办案件过程中收集的鉴定意见、勘验、检查笔录，经人民检察院审查符合法定要求的，可以作为证据使用。

**Article 64** Evidence materials, such as physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, and electronic data, collected by administrative organs in the course of administrative law enforcement and case investigation, may be used as evidence after being examined by the People's Procuratorate and found to meet the statutory requirements.  
Expert evaluation opinions, inspection records, and examination records collected by administrative organs in the course of administrative law enforcement and case investigation may be used as evidence after being examined by the People's Procuratorate and found to meet the statutory requirements.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条  监察机关依照法律规定收集的物证、书证、证人证言、被调查人供述和辩解、视听资料、电子数据等证据材料，在刑事诉讼中可以作为证据使用。

**Article 65** Evidence materials, such as physical evidence, documentary evidence, witness testimony, statements and explanations of the person under investigation, audio-visual materials, and electronic data, collected by supervisory organs in accordance with legal provisions may be used as evidence in criminal proceedings.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条  对采用刑讯逼供等非法方法收集的犯罪嫌疑人供述和采用暴力、威胁等非法方法收集的证人证言、被害人陈述，应当依法排除，不得作为移送审查逮捕、批准或者决定逮捕、移送起诉以及提起公诉的依据。

**Article 66** Confessions of a criminal suspect obtained through illegal methods such as extorting confessions by torture, and witness testimony and victim statements obtained through illegal methods such as violence or threats, shall be excluded according to law and shall not be used as the basis for referral for review of arrest, approval or decision of arrest, referral for prosecution, or institution of a public prosecution.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条  对采用下列方法收集的犯罪嫌疑人供述，应当予以排除：  
（一）采用殴打、违法使用戒具等暴力方法或者变相肉刑的恶劣手段，使犯罪嫌疑人遭受难以忍受的痛苦而违背意愿作出的供述；  
（二）采用以暴力或者严重损害本人及其近亲属合法权益等进行威胁的方法，使犯罪嫌疑人遭受难以忍受的痛苦而违背意愿作出的供述；  
（三）采用非法拘禁等非法限制人身自由的方法收集的供述。

**Article 67** Confessions of a criminal suspect obtained through the following methods shall be excluded:  
(1) Confessions made against the suspect's will by causing unbearable pain through violent methods such as beating, unlawful use of restraint devices, or other means of disguised corporal punishment;  
(2) Confessions made against the suspect's will by causing unbearable pain through threats of violence or serious harm to the lawful rights and interests of the suspect or their close relatives;  
(3) Confessions obtained through illegal methods of restricting personal freedom, such as unlawful detention.

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条  对采用刑讯逼供方法使犯罪嫌疑人作出供述，之后犯罪嫌疑人受该刑讯逼供行为影响而作出的与该供述相同的重复性供述，应当一并排除，但下列情形除外：  
（一）侦查期间，根据控告、举报或者自己发现等，公安机关确认或者不能排除以非法方法收集证据而更换侦查人员，其他侦查人员再次讯问时告知诉讼权利和认罪认罚的法律规定，犯罪嫌疑人自愿供述的；  
（二）审查逮捕、审查起诉期间，检察人员讯问时告知诉讼权利和认罪认罚的法律规定，犯罪嫌疑人自愿供述的。

**Article 68** Where a criminal suspect is made to give a confession by means of extorting confessions by torture, subsequent repeated confessions that are identical to that confession and are made under the influence of such torture shall also be excluded, except in the following circumstances:  
(1) During the investigation period, where, based on complaints, reports, or self-discovery, the public security organ confirms that evidence was obtained by illegal means or cannot exclude such possibility, and the investigators are replaced; when other investigators conduct another interrogation, they inform the suspect of his litigation rights and the legal provisions on admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, and the criminal suspect voluntarily makes a confession;  
(2) During the period of review of arrest or review of prosecution, where the prosecutor, during interrogation, informs the criminal suspect of his litigation rights and the legal provisions on admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, and the criminal suspect voluntarily makes a confession.

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条  采用暴力、威胁以及非法限制人身自由等非法方法收集的证人证言、被害人陈述，应当予以排除。

**Article 69** Witness testimony and victim statements obtained through illegal methods such as violence, threats, or unlawful restriction of personal freedom shall be excluded.

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条  收集物证、书证不符合法定程序，可能严重影响司法公正的，人民检察院应当及时要求公安机关补正或者作出书面解释；不能补正或者无法作出合理解释的，对该证据应当予以排除。  
对公安机关的补正或者解释，人民检察院应当予以审查。经补正或者作出合理解释的，可以作为批准或者决定逮捕、提起公诉的依据。

**Article 70** Where the collection of physical evidence or documentary evidence does not conform to legally prescribed procedures and may seriously affect judicial fairness, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly request the public security organ to make corrections or provide a written explanation; if corrections cannot be made or a reasonable explanation cannot be provided, such evidence shall be excluded.  
The People's Procuratorate shall review the corrections or explanations provided by the public security organ. Where corrections have been made or a reasonable explanation has been provided, such evidence may be used as the basis for approving or deciding an arrest or for instituting a public prosecution.

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条  对重大案件，人民检察院驻看守所检察人员在侦查终结前应当对讯问合法性进行核查并全程同步录音、录像，核查情况应当及时通知本院负责捕诉的部门。  
负责捕诉的部门认为确有刑讯逼供等非法取证情形的，应当要求公安机关依法排除非法证据，不得作为提请批准逮捕、移送起诉的依据。

**Article 71** For major cases, the procuratorial personnel stationed in the detention center by the People's Procuratorate shall, before the conclusion of the investigation, verify the legality of interrogations and make synchronous audio and video recordings throughout the entire process; the results of the verification shall be promptly notified to the department of the same procuratorate responsible for arrest and prosecution.  
If the department responsible for arrest and prosecution finds that there are indeed illegal evidence collection practices such as extorting confessions by torture, it shall request the public security organ to exclude the illegal evidence according to law, and such evidence shall not be used as the basis for requesting approval of arrest or for referral for prosecution.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条  人民检察院发现侦查人员以非法方法收集证据的，应当及时进行调查核实。  
当事人及其辩护人或者值班律师、诉讼代理人报案、控告、举报侦查人员采用刑讯逼供等非法方法收集证据，并提供涉嫌非法取证的人员、时间、地点、方式和内容等材料或者线索的，人民检察院应当受理并进行审查。根据现有材料无法证明证据收集合法性的，应当及时进行调查核实。  
上一级人民检察院接到对侦查人员采用刑讯逼供等非法方法收集证据的报案、控告、举报，可以直接进行调查核实，也可以交由下级人民检察院调查核实。交由下级人民检察院调查核实的，下级人民检察院应当及时将调查结果报告上一级人民检察院。  
人民检察院决定调查核实的，应当及时通知公安机关。

**Article 72** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that investigators have collected evidence by illegal means, it shall promptly conduct investigation and verification.  
Where a party, his defender, duty lawyer, or litigation agent reports, complains, or informs against investigators for collecting evidence by illegal methods such as extorting confessions by torture, and provides materials or clues concerning the persons suspected of illegal evidence collection, the time, place, method, and content thereof, the People's Procuratorate shall accept the case and conduct a review. If the legality of the evidence collection cannot be proven based on the existing materials, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly conduct investigation and verification.  
Where the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level receives a report, complaint, or information alleging that investigators have collected evidence by illegal methods such as extorting confessions by torture, it may directly conduct investigation and verification, or may assign the matter to the People's Procuratorate at the lower level for investigation and verification. Where it is assigned to the lower-level People's Procuratorate for investigation and verification, the lower-level People's Procuratorate shall promptly report the investigation results to the higher-level People's Procuratorate.  
Where the People's Procuratorate decides to conduct investigation and verification, it shall promptly notify the public security organ.

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条  人民检察院经审查认定存在非法取证行为的，对该证据应当予以排除，其他证据不能证明犯罪嫌疑人实施犯罪行为的，应当不批准或者决定逮捕。已经移送起诉的，可以依法将案件退回监察机关补充调查或者退回公安机关补充侦查，或者作出不起诉决定。被排除的非法证据应当随案移送，并写明为依法排除的非法证据。  
对于侦查人员的非法取证行为，尚未构成犯罪的，应当依法向其所在机关提出纠正意见。对于需要补正或者作出合理解释的，应当提出明确要求。  
对于非法取证行为涉嫌犯罪需要追究刑事责任的，应当依法立案侦查。

**Article 73** Where, after review, the People's Procuratorate determines that there is illegal evidence collection, such evidence shall be excluded. If other evidence cannot prove that the criminal suspect committed the criminal act, the People's Procuratorate shall not approve or decide an arrest. If the case has already been referred for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate may, according to law, return the case to the supervisory organ for supplementary investigation or to the public security organ for supplementary investigation, or make a decision not to prosecute. The excluded illegal evidence shall be transferred along with the case, with a note indicating that it is illegal evidence excluded according to law.  
If the investigator's illegal evidence collection does not yet constitute a crime, the People's Procuratorate shall, according to law, issue a correction suggestion to the organ to which the investigator belongs. If corrections or a reasonable explanation are needed, the People's Procuratorate shall put forward clear requirements.  
If the illegal evidence collection is suspected of constituting a crime and criminal liability needs to be investigated, the People's Procuratorate shall, according to law, file a case for investigation.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条  人民检察院认为可能存在以刑讯逼供等非法方法收集证据情形的，可以书面要求监察机关或者公安机关对证据收集的合法性作出说明。说明应当加盖单位公章，并由调查人员或者侦查人员签名。

**Article 74** Where the People's Procuratorate believes that there may be circumstances of evidence collected by illegal methods such as extorting confessions by torture, it may, in writing, request the supervisory organ or public security organ to provide an explanation regarding the legality of the evidence collection. The explanation shall be affixed with the official seal of the organ and signed by the investigator or the person conducting the investigation.

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条  对于公安机关立案侦查的案件，存在下列情形之一的，人民检察院在审查逮捕、审查起诉和审判阶段，可以调取公安机关讯问犯罪嫌疑人的录音、录像，对证据收集的合法性以及犯罪嫌疑人、被告人供述的真实性进行审查：  
（一）认为讯问活动可能存在刑讯逼供等非法取证行为的；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者辩护人提出犯罪嫌疑人、被告人供述系非法取得，并提供相关线索或者材料的；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人提出讯问活动违反法定程序或者翻供，并提供相关线索或者材料的；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者辩护人提出讯问笔录内容不真实，并提供相关线索或者材料的；  
（五）案情重大、疑难、复杂的。  
人民检察院调取公安机关讯问犯罪嫌疑人的录音、录像，公安机关未提供，人民检察院经审查认为不能排除有刑讯逼供等非法取证行为的，相关供述不得作为批准逮捕、提起公诉的依据。  
人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，负责侦查的部门移送审查逮捕、移送起诉时，应当将讯问录音、录像连同案卷材料一并移送审查。

**Article 75** For cases filed for investigation by the public security organ, where any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Procuratorate may, during the review of arrest, review of prosecution, or trial stage, obtain audio and video recordings of the public security organ's interrogation of the criminal suspect to review the legality of the evidence collection and the truthfulness of the confessions of the criminal suspect or defendant:  
(1) It is believed that the interrogation may have involved illegal evidence collection such as extorting confessions by torture;  
(2) The criminal suspect, defendant, or defender claims that the confession of the criminal suspect or defendant was obtained illegally and provides relevant clues or materials;  
(3) The criminal suspect or defendant claims that the interrogation violated statutory procedures or retracts the confession, and provides relevant clues or materials;  
(4) The criminal suspect, defendant, or defender claims that the content of the interrogation record is untrue and provides relevant clues or materials;  
(5) The case is major, difficult, or complex.  
Where the People's Procuratorate obtains audio and video recordings of the public security organ's interrogation of the criminal suspect, but the public security organ does not provide them, and after review, the People's Procuratorate finds that it cannot exclude the possibility of illegal evidence collection such as extorting confessions by torture, the relevant confessions shall not be used as the basis for approving arrest or instituting a public prosecution.  
For cases directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate, when the department responsible for investigation refers the case for review of arrest or for prosecution, it shall transfer the audio and video recordings of the interrogation together with the case file materials for review.

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条  对于提起公诉的案件，被告人及其辩护人提出审前供述系非法取得，并提供相关线索或者材料的，人民检察院可以将讯问录音、录像连同案卷材料一并移送人民法院。

**Article 76** For cases where a public prosecution has been instituted, if the defendant or his defender claims that the pre-trial confession was obtained illegally and provides relevant clues or materials, the People's Procuratorate may transfer the audio and video recordings of the interrogation together with the case file materials to the People's Court.

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条  在法庭审理过程中，被告人或者辩护人对讯问活动合法性提出异议，公诉人可以要求被告人及其辩护人提供相关线索或者材料。必要时，公诉人可以提请法庭当庭播放相关时段的讯问录音、录像，对有关异议或者事实进行质证。  
需要播放的讯问录音、录像中涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私或者含有其他不宜公开内容的，公诉人应当建议在法庭组成人员、公诉人、侦查人员、被告人及其辩护人范围内播放。因涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私或者其他犯罪线索等内容，人民检察院对讯问录音、录像的相关内容进行技术处理的，公诉人应当向法庭作出说明。

**Article 77** During court proceedings, if the defendant or his defender raises an objection to the legality of the interrogation, the public prosecutor may request the defendant and his defender to provide relevant clues or materials. When necessary, the public prosecutor may request the court to play the audio and video recordings of the interrogation for the relevant period in court to examine the objection or facts through cross-examination.  
If the audio and video recordings of the interrogation to be played involve state secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, or other content unsuitable for public disclosure, the public prosecutor shall suggest that they be played within the scope of the court's composition, the public prosecutor, investigators, and the defendant and his defender. If, because of the involvement of state secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, or other criminal leads, the People's Procuratorate has technically processed the relevant content of the audio and video recordings of the interrogation, the public prosecutor shall explain this to the court.

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条  人民检察院认为第一审人民法院有关证据收集合法性的审查、调查结论导致第一审判决、裁定错误的，可以依照[刑事诉讼法第二百二十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-228)的规定向人民法院提出抗诉。

**Article 78** Where the People's Procuratorate considers that the conclusions of the first-instance People's Court regarding the review and investigation of the legality of evidence collection have led to an error in the first-instance judgment or ruling, it may, in accordance with the provisions of Article 228 of the Criminal Procedure Law, lodge a protest with the People's Court.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条  人民检察院在办理危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪、黑社会性质的组织犯罪、毒品犯罪等案件过程中，证人、鉴定人、被害人因在诉讼中作证，本人或者其近亲属人身安全面临危险，向人民检察院请求保护的，人民检察院应当受理并及时进行审查。对于确实存在人身安全危险的，应当立即采取必要的保护措施。人民检察院发现存在上述情形的，应当主动采取保护措施。  
人民检察院可以采取以下一项或者多项保护措施：  
（一）不公开真实姓名、住址和工作单位等个人信息；  
（二）建议法庭采取不暴露外貌、真实声音等出庭作证措施；  
（三）禁止特定的人员接触证人、鉴定人、被害人及其近亲属；  
（四）对人身和住宅采取专门性保护措施；  
（五）其他必要的保护措施。  
人民检察院依法决定不公开证人、鉴定人、被害人的真实姓名、住址和工作单位等个人信息的，可以在起诉书、询问笔录等法律文书、证据材料中使用化名。但是应当另行书面说明使用化名的情况并标明密级，单独成卷。  
人民检察院依法采取保护措施，可以要求有关单位和个人予以配合。  
对证人及其近亲属进行威胁、侮辱、殴打或者打击报复，构成犯罪或者应当给予治安管理处罚的，人民检察院应当移送公安机关处理；情节轻微的，予以批评教育、训诫。

**Article 79** When handling cases involving crimes of endangering national security, terrorist activities, organized crimes of a mafia nature, drug crimes, and the like, if a witness, expert evaluator, or victim requests protection from the People's Procuratorate because their personal safety or that of their close relatives is in danger due to testifying in the proceedings, the People's Procuratorate shall accept the request and promptly conduct a review. If there is indeed a danger to personal safety, it shall immediately take necessary protective measures. If the People's Procuratorate discovers the existence of the aforementioned circumstances, it shall proactively take protective measures.  
The People's Procuratorate may take one or more of the following protective measures:  
(1) Not disclosing personal information such as the true name, address, and work unit;  
(2) Suggesting that the court take measures when testifying in court to conceal appearance and true voice;  
(3) Prohibiting specific persons from contacting the witness, expert evaluator, victim, and their close relatives;  
(4) Taking specialized protective measures for personal safety and residence;  
(5) Other necessary protective measures.  
Where the People's Procuratorate decides according to law not to disclose personal information such as the true name, address, and work unit of the witness, expert evaluator, or victim, pseudonyms may be used in legal documents and evidence materials such as the indictment and interrogation records. However, a separate written explanation of the use of pseudonyms shall be provided, marked with the classification level, and kept in a separate file.  
When taking protective measures according to law, the People's Procuratorate may request the relevant entities and individuals to cooperate.  
If threats, insults, beatings, or retaliation against the witness or their close relatives constitute a crime or warrant public security punishment, the People's Procuratorate shall transfer the case to the public security organ for handling; if the circumstances are minor, criticism, education, or a reprimand shall be given.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条  证人在人民检察院侦查、审查逮捕、审查起诉期间因履行作证义务而支出的交通、住宿、就餐等费用，人民检察院应当给予补助。

**Article 80** Where a witness incurs expenses for transportation, accommodation, meals, and the like due to fulfilling the obligation to testify during the period of the People's Procuratorate's investigation, review of arrest, or review of prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall provide subsidies.

### 第六章 强制措施

#### 第一节 拘 传

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条  人民检察院根据案件情况，对犯罪嫌疑人可以拘传。

**Article 81** The People's Procuratorate may, based on the circumstances of the case, summon a criminal suspect for interrogation.

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条  拘传时，应当向被拘传的犯罪嫌疑人出示拘传证。对抗拒拘传的，可以使用戒具，强制到案。  
执行拘传的人员不得少于二人。

**Article 82** When summoning a criminal suspect for interrogation, the summoning warrant shall be presented to the summoned criminal suspect. If the criminal suspect resists the summoning, restraint implements may be used to compel his or her appearance.  
The number of personnel executing the summoning shall be no less than two.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条  拘传的时间从犯罪嫌疑人到案时开始计算。犯罪嫌疑人到案后，应当责令其在拘传证上填写到案时间，签名或者盖章，并捺指印，然后立即讯问。拘传结束后，应当责令犯罪嫌疑人在拘传证上填写拘传结束时间。犯罪嫌疑人拒绝填写的，应当在拘传证上注明。  
一次拘传持续的时间不得超过十二小时；案情特别重大、复杂，需要采取拘留、逮捕措施的，拘传持续的时间不得超过二十四小时。两次拘传间隔的时间一般不得少于十二小时，不得以连续拘传的方式变相拘禁犯罪嫌疑人。  
拘传犯罪嫌疑人，应当保证犯罪嫌疑人的饮食和必要的休息时间。

**Article 83** The duration of the summoning shall be calculated from the time when the criminal suspect appears. After the criminal suspect appears, he or she shall be ordered to fill in the time of appearance on the summoning warrant, sign or affix his or her seal, and affix his or her fingerprint, whereupon interrogation shall be conducted immediately. After the summoning concludes, the criminal suspect shall be ordered to fill in the time of conclusion of the summoning on the summoning warrant. If the criminal suspect refuses to fill in the information, a notation shall be made on the summoning warrant.  
The duration of a single summoning shall not exceed twelve hours; if the case is particularly grave and complex and measures of detention or arrest need to be taken, the duration of the summoning shall not exceed twenty-four hours. The interval between two summonings shall generally be no less than twelve hours, and continuous summoning shall not be used as a disguised form of custody of the criminal suspect.  
When summoning a criminal suspect for interrogation, the criminal suspect's meals and necessary rest time shall be ensured.

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条  人民检察院拘传犯罪嫌疑人，应当在犯罪嫌疑人所在市、县内的地点进行。  
犯罪嫌疑人工作单位与居住地不在同一市、县的，拘传应当在犯罪嫌疑人工作单位所在的市、县内进行；特殊情况下，也可以在犯罪嫌疑人居住地所在的市、县内进行。

**Article 84** The People's Procuratorate's summoning of a criminal suspect for interrogation shall be conducted at a place within the city or county where the criminal suspect is located.  
If the criminal suspect's work unit and residence are not in the same city or county, the summoning shall be conducted within the city or county where the criminal suspect's work unit is located; under special circumstances, it may also be conducted within the city or county where the criminal suspect's residence is located.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条  需要对被拘传的犯罪嫌疑人变更强制措施的，应当在拘传期限内办理变更手续。  
在拘传期间决定不采取其他强制措施的，拘传期限届满，应当结束拘传。

**Article 85** If it is necessary to change compulsory measures for the summoned criminal suspect, the change procedures shall be completed within the period of the summoning.  
If it is decided during the summoning not to take other compulsory measures, the summoning shall end when the period of the summoning expires.

#### 第二节 取保候审

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条  人民检察院对于具有下列情形之一的犯罪嫌疑人，可以取保候审：  
（一）可能判处管制、拘役或者独立适用附加刑的；  
（二）可能判处有期徒刑以上刑罚，采取取保候审不致发生社会危险性的；  
（三）患有严重疾病、生活不能自理，怀孕或者正在哺乳自己婴儿的妇女，采取取保候审不致发生社会危险性的；  
（四）羁押期限届满，案件尚未办结，需要采取取保候审的。

**Article 86** The People's Procuratorate may grant release on bail pending investigation or trial to a criminal suspect under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The criminal suspect may be sentenced to public surveillance, criminal detention, or a supplementary punishment independently applicable;  
(2) The criminal suspect may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment or a more severe punishment, and granting release on bail pending investigation or trial would not create any danger to society;  
(3) The criminal suspect suffers from a serious illness, cannot take care of himself or herself, or is a pregnant woman or a woman nursing her own infant, and granting release on bail pending investigation or trial would not create any danger to society;  
(4) The period of custody has expired, the case has not yet been concluded, and release on bail pending investigation or trial is necessary.

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条  人民检察院对于严重危害社会治安的犯罪嫌疑人，以及其他犯罪性质恶劣、情节严重的犯罪嫌疑人不得取保候审。

**Article 87** The People's Procuratorate shall not grant release on bail pending investigation or trial to a criminal suspect who has seriously endangered public security, or to a criminal suspect whose crime is heinous in nature and serious in circumstances.

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条  被羁押或者监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人向人民检察院申请取保候审，人民检察院应当在三日以内作出是否同意的答复。经审查符合[本规则第八十六条](#art-86)规定情形之一的，可以对被羁押或者监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人依法办理取保候审手续。经审查不符合取保候审条件的，应当告知申请人，并说明不同意取保候审的理由。

**Article 88** When a criminal suspect under custody or residential surveillance, or his or her legal representative, close relative, or defender, applies for release on bail pending investigation or trial with the People's Procuratorate, the People's Procuratorate shall reply within three days as to whether it agrees. If upon examination it is found that the circumstances fall under any of the circumstances provided for in Article 86 of these Rules, release on bail pending investigation or trial procedures may be lawfully handled for the criminal suspect under custody or residential surveillance. If upon examination it is found that the conditions for release on bail pending investigation or trial are not met, the applicant shall be informed, and the reasons for not agreeing to release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be provided.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条  人民检察院决定对犯罪嫌疑人取保候审，应当责令犯罪嫌疑人提出保证人或者交纳保证金。  
对同一犯罪嫌疑人决定取保候审，不得同时使用保证人保证和保证金保证方式。  
对符合取保候审条件，具有下列情形之一的犯罪嫌疑人，人民检察院决定取保候审时，可以责令其提供一至二名保证人：  
（一）无力交纳保证金的；  
（二）系未成年人或者已满七十五周岁的人；  
（三）其他不宜收取保证金的。

**Article 89** When the People's Procuratorate decides to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial to a criminal suspect, the criminal suspect shall be ordered to provide a guarantor or pay a deposit.  
For the same criminal suspect, the methods of guarantor guarantee and deposit guarantee shall not be used simultaneously.  
If a criminal suspect meets the conditions for release on bail pending investigation or trial and falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Procuratorate may, when deciding to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial, order him or her to provide one to two guarantors:  
(1) Unable to pay a deposit;  
(2) A minor or a person who has reached the age of seventy-five;  
(3) Other circumstances where it is inappropriate to collect a deposit.
