<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条  采取保证人保证方式的，保证人应当符合[刑事诉讼法第六十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-69)规定的条件，并经人民检察院审查同意。

**Article 90** If the method of guarantor guarantee is adopted, the guarantor shall meet the conditions provided for in Article 69 of the Criminal Procedure Law and shall be examined and approved by the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条  人民检察院应当告知保证人履行以下义务：  
（一）监督被保证人遵守[刑事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-71)的规定；  
（二）发现被保证人可能发生或者已经发生违反[刑事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-71)规定的行为的，及时向执行机关报告。  
保证人保证承担上述义务后，应当在取保候审保证书上签名或者盖章。

**Article 91** The People's Procuratorate shall inform the guarantor to perform the following obligations:  
(1) To supervise the guarantee to comply with the provisions of Article 71 of the Criminal Procedure Law;  
(2) To promptly report to the executing authority if it is discovered that the guarantee may have committed or has committed an act in violation of the provisions of Article 71 of the Criminal Procedure Law.  
After the guarantor guarantees to assume the above obligations, he or she shall sign or affix his or her seal on the release on bail pending investigation or trial guarantee document.

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条  采取保证金保证方式的，人民检察院可以根据犯罪嫌疑人的社会危险性，案件的性质、情节，可能判处刑罚的轻重，犯罪嫌疑人的经济状况等，责令犯罪嫌疑人交纳一千元以上的保证金。对于未成年犯罪嫌疑人，可以责令交纳五百元以上的保证金。

**Article 92** If the method of deposit guarantee is adopted, the People's Procuratorate may, based on the criminal suspect's danger to society, the nature and circumstances of the case, the severity of the possible punishment, the criminal suspect's financial condition, and other factors, order the criminal suspect to pay a deposit of not less than 1,000 yuan. For a juvenile criminal suspect, he or she may be ordered to pay a deposit of not less than 500 yuan.

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条  人民检察院决定对犯罪嫌疑人取保候审的，应当制作取保候审决定书，载明取保候审开始的时间、保证方式、被取保候审人应当履行的义务和应当遵守的规定。  
人民检察院作出取保候审决定时，可以根据犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌犯罪的性质、危害后果、社会影响，犯罪嫌疑人、被害人的具体情况等，有针对性地责令其遵守以下一项或者多项规定：  
（一）不得进入特定的场所；  
（二）不得与特定的人员会见或者通信；  
（三）不得从事特定的活动；  
（四）将护照等出入境证件、驾驶证件交执行机关保存。

**Article 93** When the People's Procuratorate decides to grant release on bail pending investigation or trial to a criminal suspect, it shall produce a written decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial, specifying the start time of the release on bail pending investigation or trial, the method of guarantee, and the obligations to be performed and the provisions to be complied with by the person released on bail pending investigation or trial.  
When making the decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial, the People's Procuratorate may, in light of the nature of the crime suspected, the harmful consequences, the social impact, and the specific circumstances of the criminal suspect, the victim, etc., specifically order the criminal suspect to comply with one or more of the following provisions:  
(1) Not to enter specific places;  
(2) Not to meet or correspond with specific persons;  
(3) Not to engage in specific activities;  
(4) To deliver passports, other entry-exit documents, and driving licenses to the executing authority for safekeeping.

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条  人民检察院应当向取保候审的犯罪嫌疑人宣读取保候审决定书，由犯罪嫌疑人签名或者盖章，并捺指印，责令犯罪嫌疑人遵守[刑事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-71)的规定，告知其违反规定应负的法律责任。以保证金方式保证的，应当同时告知犯罪嫌疑人一次性将保证金存入公安机关指定银行的专门账户。

**Article 94** The People's Procuratorate shall read out the written decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial to the criminal suspect released on bail pending investigation or trial, have the criminal suspect sign or affix his or her seal and affix his or her fingerprint, order the criminal suspect to comply with the provisions of Article 71 of the Criminal Procedure Law, and inform him or her of the legal liability to be borne for violating the provisions. If the method of deposit guarantee is adopted, the criminal suspect shall also be informed to deposit the full amount of the deposit at one time into the special account of the bank designated by the public security organ.

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条  向犯罪嫌疑人宣布取保候审决定后，人民检察院应当将执行取保候审通知书送达公安机关执行，并告知公安机关在执行期间拟批准犯罪嫌疑人离开所居住的市、县的，应当事先征得人民检察院同意。以保证人方式保证的，应当将取保候审保证书同时送交公安机关。  
人民检察院核实保证金已经交纳到公安机关指定银行的凭证后，应当将银行出具的凭证及其他有关材料与执行取保候审通知书一并送交公安机关。

**Article 95** After announcing the decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial to the criminal suspect, the People's Procuratorate shall serve the notice on execution of release on bail pending investigation or trial on the public security organ for execution, and shall inform the public security organ that if it intends to approve the criminal suspect's leaving the city or county where he or she resides during the execution period, it shall obtain the consent of the People's Procuratorate in advance. If the method of guarantor guarantee is adopted, the release on bail pending investigation or trial guarantee document shall be delivered to the public security organ at the same time.  
After the People's Procuratorate verifies the voucher that the deposit has been paid into the bank designated by the public security organ, it shall deliver the voucher issued by the bank and other relevant materials together with the notice on execution of release on bail pending investigation or trial to the public security organ.

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条  采取保证人保证方式的，如果保证人在取保候审期间不愿继续保证或者丧失保证条件的，人民检察院应当在收到保证人不愿继续保证的申请或者发现其丧失保证条件后三日以内，责令犯罪嫌疑人重新提出保证人或者交纳保证金，并将变更情况通知公安机关。

**Article 96** If the method of guarantor guarantee is adopted and the guarantor is unwilling to continue the guarantee or has lost the qualifications as a guarantor during the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial, the People's Procuratorate shall, within three days after receiving the application from the guarantor indicating unwillingness to continue the guarantee or discovering that he or she has lost the qualifications, order the criminal suspect to provide a new guarantor or pay a deposit, and shall notify the public security organ of the change.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条  采取保证金保证方式的，被取保候审人拒绝交纳保证金或者交纳保证金不足决定数额时，人民检察院应当作出变更取保候审措施、变更保证方式或者变更保证金数额的决定，并将变更情况通知公安机关。

**Article 97** If the method of deposit guarantee is adopted and the person released on bail pending investigation or trial refuses to pay the deposit or the deposit paid is less than the decided amount, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision to change the measure of release on bail pending investigation or trial, change the method of guarantee, or change the amount of the deposit, and shall notify the public security organ of the change.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条  公安机关在执行取保候审期间向人民检察院征询是否同意批准犯罪嫌疑人离开所居住的市、县时，人民检察院应当根据案件的具体情况及时作出决定，并通知公安机关。

**Article 98** When the public security organ, during the execution of release on bail pending investigation or trial, consults the People's Procuratorate as to whether it agrees to approve the criminal suspect's leaving the city or county where he or she resides, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly make a decision based on the specific circumstances of the case and shall notify the public security organ.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条  人民检察院发现保证人没有履行[刑事诉讼法第七十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-70)规定的义务，应当通知公安机关，要求公安机关对保证人作出罚款决定。构成犯罪的，依法追究保证人的刑事责任。

**Article 99** If the People's Procuratorate discovers that the guarantor has failed to perform the obligations provided for in Article 70 of the Criminal Procedure Law, it shall notify the public security organ and request the public security organ to make a decision to impose a fine on the guarantor. If a crime is constituted, the guarantor's criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条  人民检察院发现犯罪嫌疑人违反[刑事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-71)的规定，已交纳保证金的，应当书面通知公安机关没收部分或者全部保证金，并且根据案件的具体情况，责令犯罪嫌疑人具结悔过，重新交纳保证金、提出保证人，或者决定对其监视居住、予以逮捕。  
公安机关发现犯罪嫌疑人违反[刑事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-71)的规定，提出没收保证金或者变更强制措施意见的，人民检察院应当在收到意见后五日以内作出决定，并通知公安机关。  
重新交纳保证金的程序适用[本规则第九十二条](#art-92)的规定；提出保证人的程序适用[本规则第九十条](#art-90)、第九十一条的规定。对犯罪嫌疑人继续取保候审的，取保候审的时间应当累计计算。  
对犯罪嫌疑人决定监视居住的，应当办理监视居住手续。监视居住的期限应当自执行监视居住决定之日起计算并告知犯罪嫌疑人。

**Article 100** If the People's Procuratorate discovers that a criminal suspect has violated the provisions of Article 71 of the Criminal Procedure Law, and a deposit has been paid, it shall, in writing, notify the public security organ to confiscate part or all of the deposit, and, based on the specific circumstances of the case, order the criminal suspect to make a statement of repentance, pay a new deposit, provide a new guarantor, or decide to place him or her under residential surveillance or arrest him or her.  
If the public security organ discovers that a criminal suspect has violated the provisions of Article 71 of the Criminal Procedure Law and submits an opinion to confiscate the deposit or change the compulsory measures, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision within five days after receiving the opinion and notify the public security organ.  
The procedure for re-paying the deposit shall be governed by the provisions of Article 92 of these Rules; the procedure for providing a guarantor shall be governed by the provisions of Articles 90 and 91 of these Rules. If release on bail pending investigation or trial continues for the criminal suspect, the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be accumulated.  
If a decision is made to place the criminal suspect under residential surveillance, the procedures for residential surveillance shall be completed. The period of residential surveillance shall be calculated from the date of execution of the decision on residential surveillance and shall be notified to the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条  犯罪嫌疑人有下列违反取保候审规定的行为，人民检察院应当对犯罪嫌疑人予以逮捕：  
（一）故意实施新的犯罪；  
（二）企图自杀、逃跑；  
（三）实施毁灭、伪造证据，串供或者干扰证人作证，足以影响侦查、审查起诉工作正常进行；  
（四）对被害人、证人、鉴定人、举报人、控告人及其他人员实施打击报复。  
犯罪嫌疑人有下列违反取保候审规定的行为，人民检察院可以对犯罪嫌疑人予以逮捕：  
（一）未经批准，擅自离开所居住的市、县，造成严重后果，或者两次未经批准，擅自离开所居住的市、县；  
（二）经传讯不到案，造成严重后果，或者经两次传讯不到案；  
（三）住址、工作单位和联系方式发生变动，未在二十四小时以内向公安机关报告，造成严重后果；  
（四）违反规定进入特定场所、与特定人员会见或者通信、从事特定活动，严重妨碍诉讼程序正常进行。  
有前两款情形，需要对犯罪嫌疑人予以逮捕的，可以先行拘留；已交纳保证金的，同时书面通知公安机关没收保证金。

**Article 101** Where a criminal suspect commits any of the following acts in violation of the provisions on release on bail pending investigation or trial, the People's Procuratorate shall arrest the criminal suspect:  
(1) intentionally committing a new crime;  
(2) attempting suicide or escape;  
(3) committing acts of destroying or falsifying evidence, colluding with others or interfering with witness testimony, sufficient to affect the normal progress of investigation and examination of prosecution;  
(4) retaliating against victims, witnesses, expert evaluators, informants, accusers, or other persons.  
Where a criminal suspect commits any of the following acts in violation of the provisions on release on bail pending investigation or trial, the People's Procuratorate may arrest the criminal suspect:  
(1) leaving the city or county where he or she resides without approval, causing serious consequences, or twice leaving without approval;  
(2) failing to appear before the authorities when summoned, causing serious consequences, or twice failing to appear when summoned;  
(3) failing to report changes in address, work unit, or contact information to the public security organ within twenty-four hours, causing serious consequences;  
(4) entering specific places, meeting or communicating with specific persons, or engaging in specific activities in violation of regulations, seriously obstructing the normal conduct of the proceedings.  
In the circumstances set out in the preceding two paragraphs, if the criminal suspect needs to be arrested, he or she may be detained in advance; if a deposit has been paid, the public security organ shall be notified in writing to confiscate the deposit.

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条  人民检察院决定对犯罪嫌疑人取保候审，最长不得超过十二个月。

**Article 102** The period of release on bail pending investigation or trial decided by the People's Procuratorate shall not exceed twelve months at maximum.

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条  公安机关决定对犯罪嫌疑人取保候审，案件移送人民检察院审查起诉后，对于需要继续取保候审的，人民检察院应当依法重新作出取保候审决定，并对犯罪嫌疑人办理取保候审手续。取保候审的期限应当重新计算并告知犯罪嫌疑人。对继续采取保证金方式取保候审的，被取保候审人没有违反[刑事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-71)规定的，不变更保证金数额，不再重新收取保证金。

**Article 103** Where a public security organ decides to release a criminal suspect on bail pending investigation or trial, and after the case is transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination of prosecution, if continued release on bail pending investigation or trial is necessary, the People's Procuratorate shall make a new decision on release on bail pending investigation or trial according to law, and handle the procedures for release on bail pending investigation or trial for the criminal suspect. The period of release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be recalculated and notified to the criminal suspect. Where the method of release on bail pending investigation or trial by deposit is continued, if the person released on bail pending investigation or trial has not violated the provisions of Article 71 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the amount of the deposit shall not be changed, and the deposit shall not be collected again.

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条  在取保候审期间，不得中断对案件的侦查、审查起诉。

**Article 104** During the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial, the investigation and examination of prosecution of the case shall not be interrupted.

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条  取保候审期限届满或者发现不应当追究犯罪嫌疑人的刑事责任的，应当及时解除或者撤销取保候审。  
解除或者撤销取保候审的决定，应当及时通知执行机关，并将解除或者撤销取保候审的决定书送达犯罪嫌疑人；有保证人的，应当通知保证人解除保证义务。

**Article 105** Where the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial expires or it is discovered that the criminal suspect should not be investigated for criminal liability, the release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be terminated or revoked in a timely manner.  
The decision to terminate or revoke the release on bail pending investigation or trial shall be promptly notified to the executing authority, and the written decision on termination or revocation shall be served on the criminal suspect; if there is a surety, the surety shall be notified of the release from suretyship obligations.

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条  犯罪嫌疑人在取保候审期间没有违反[刑事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-71)的规定，或者发现不应当追究犯罪嫌疑人刑事责任的，变更、解除或者撤销取保候审时，应当告知犯罪嫌疑人可以凭变更、解除或者撤销取保候审的通知或者有关法律文书到银行领取退还的保证金。

**Article 106** Where a criminal suspect has not violated the provisions of Article 71 of the Criminal Procedure Law during the period of release on bail pending investigation or trial, or it is discovered that the criminal suspect should not be investigated for criminal liability, upon alteration, termination, or revocation of the release on bail pending investigation or trial, the criminal suspect shall be informed that he or she may, by presenting the notice on alteration, termination, or revocation of the release on bail pending investigation or trial or relevant legal documents, go to the bank to retrieve the refunded deposit.

#### 第三节 监视居住

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条  人民检察院对于符合逮捕条件，具有下列情形之一的犯罪嫌疑人，可以监视居住：  
（一）患有严重疾病、生活不能自理的；  
（二）怀孕或者正在哺乳自己婴儿的妇女；  
（三）系生活不能自理的人的唯一扶养人；  
（四）因为案件的特殊情况或者办理案件的需要，采取监视居住措施更为适宜的；  
（五）羁押期限届满，案件尚未办结，需要采取监视居住措施的。  
前款第三项中的扶养包括父母、祖父母、外祖父母对子女、孙子女、外孙子女的抚养和子女、孙子女、外孙子女对父母、祖父母、外祖父母的赡养以及配偶、兄弟姐妹之间的相互扶养。  
对符合取保候审条件，但犯罪嫌疑人不能提出保证人，也不交纳保证金的，可以监视居住。

**Article 107** The People's Procuratorate may place a criminal suspect under residential surveillance if the conditions for arrest are met and the criminal suspect falls under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) suffering from a serious illness and unable to take care of himself or herself;  
(2) being a pregnant woman or a woman nursing her own infant;  
(3) being the sole person supporting a person who cannot take care of himself or herself;  
(4) due to the special circumstances of the case or the needs of handling the case, residential surveillance is more appropriate;  
(5) the period of custody expires, the case has not been concluded, and residential surveillance measures are necessary.  
The term "support" in subparagraph (3) of the preceding paragraph includes the upbringing of children, grandchildren, and great-grandchildren by parents, paternal grandparents, maternal grandparents, and the maintenance of parents, paternal grandparents, maternal grandparents by children, grandchildren, and great-grandchildren, as well as mutual support between spouses and between siblings.  
Where the conditions for release on bail pending investigation or trial are met, but the criminal suspect cannot provide a surety or pay a deposit, residential surveillance may be imposed.

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条  人民检察院应当向被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人宣读监视居住决定书，由犯罪嫌疑人签名或者盖章，并捺指印，责令犯罪嫌疑人遵守[刑事诉讼法第七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-77)的规定，告知其违反规定应负的法律责任。  
指定居所监视居住的，不得要求被监视居住人支付费用。

**Article 108** The People's Procuratorate shall read the decision on residential surveillance to the criminal suspect under residential surveillance, have the criminal suspect sign or affix his or her seal and fingerprint, order the criminal suspect to comply with the provisions of Article 77 of the Criminal Procedure Law, and inform him or her of the legal liability for violation.  
For residential surveillance at a designated residence, the person under residential surveillance shall not be required to pay any fees.

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条  人民检察院核实犯罪嫌疑人住处或者为其指定居所后，应当制作监视居住执行通知书，将有关法律文书和案由、犯罪嫌疑人基本情况材料，送交监视居住地的公安机关执行，必要时人民检察院可以协助公安机关执行。  
人民检察院应当告知公安机关在执行期间拟批准犯罪嫌疑人离开执行监视居住的处所、会见他人或者通信的，应当事先征得人民检察院同意。

**Article 109** After verifying the criminal suspect's residence or designating a residence for him or her, the People's Procuratorate shall prepare a notice on enforcement of residential surveillance and submit relevant legal documents, the nature of the case, and basic information of the criminal suspect to the public security organ at the place of residential surveillance for enforcement. If necessary, the People's Procuratorate may assist the public security organ in enforcement.  
The People's Procuratorate shall inform the public security organ that if it intends to approve the criminal suspect's leaving the place of residential surveillance, meeting others, or communicating during enforcement, it shall obtain prior consent from the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条  人民检察院可以根据案件的具体情况，商请公安机关对被监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人采取电子监控、不定期检查等监视方法，对其遵守监视居住规定的情况进行监督。  
人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件对犯罪嫌疑人采取监视居住的，在侦查期间可以商请公安机关对其通信进行监控。

**Article 110** The People's Procuratorate may, based on the specific circumstances of the case, consult with the public security organ to adopt electronic surveillance, irregular inspections, and other surveillance methods for the criminal suspect under residential surveillance, and supervise his or her compliance with the provisions on residential surveillance.  
Where the People's Procuratorate handles a case directly accepted for investigation and places the criminal suspect under residential surveillance, it may consult with the public security organ to monitor his or her communications during the investigation period.

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条  犯罪嫌疑人有下列违反监视居住规定的行为，人民检察院应当对犯罪嫌疑人予以逮捕：  
（一）故意实施新的犯罪行为；  
（二）企图自杀、逃跑；  
（三）实施毁灭、伪造证据或者串供、干扰证人作证行为，足以影响侦查、审查起诉工作正常进行；  
（四）对被害人、证人、鉴定人、举报人、控告人及其他人员实施打击报复。  
犯罪嫌疑人有下列违反监视居住规定的行为，人民检察院可以对犯罪嫌疑人予以逮捕：  
（一）未经批准，擅自离开执行监视居住的处所，造成严重后果，或者两次未经批准，擅自离开执行监视居住的处所；  
（二）未经批准，擅自会见他人或者通信，造成严重后果，或者两次未经批准，擅自会见他人或者通信；  
（三）经传讯不到案，造成严重后果，或者经两次传讯不到案。  
有前两款情形，需要对犯罪嫌疑人予以逮捕的，可以先行拘留。

**Article 111** Where a criminal suspect commits any of the following acts in violation of the provisions on residential surveillance, the People's Procuratorate shall arrest the criminal suspect:  
(1) intentionally committing a new criminal act;  
(2) attempting suicide or escape;  
(3) committing acts of destroying or falsifying evidence, colluding with others, or interfering with witness testimony, sufficient to affect the normal progress of investigation and examination of prosecution;  
(4) retaliating against victims, witnesses, expert evaluators, informants, accusers, or other persons.  
Where a criminal suspect commits any of the following acts in violation of the provisions on residential surveillance, the People's Procuratorate may arrest the criminal suspect:  
(1) leaving the place of residential surveillance without approval, causing serious consequences, or twice leaving without approval;  
(2) meeting others or communicating without approval, causing serious consequences, or twice meeting or communicating without approval;  
(3) failing to appear before the authorities when summoned, causing serious consequences, or twice failing to appear when summoned.  
In the circumstances set out in the preceding two paragraphs, if the criminal suspect needs to be arrested, he or she may be detained in advance.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条  人民检察院决定对犯罪嫌疑人监视居住，最长不得超过六个月。

**Article 112** The period of residential surveillance decided by the People's Procuratorate shall not exceed six months at maximum.

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条  公安机关决定对犯罪嫌疑人监视居住，案件移送人民检察院审查起诉后，对于需要继续监视居住的，人民检察院应当依法重新作出监视居住决定，并对犯罪嫌疑人办理监视居住手续。监视居住的期限应当重新计算并告知犯罪嫌疑人。

**Article 113** Where a public security organ decides to place a criminal suspect under residential surveillance, and after the case is transferred to the People's Procuratorate for examination of prosecution, if continued residential surveillance is necessary, the People's Procuratorate shall make a new decision on residential surveillance according to law, and handle the procedures for residential surveillance for the criminal suspect. The period of residential surveillance shall be recalculated and notified to the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条  在监视居住期间，不得中断对案件的侦查、审查起诉。

**Article 114** During the period of residential surveillance, the investigation and examination of prosecution of the case shall not be interrupted.

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条  监视居住期限届满或者发现不应当追究犯罪嫌疑人刑事责任的，应当解除或者撤销监视居住。  
解除或者撤销监视居住的决定应当通知执行机关，并将解除或者撤销监视居住的决定书送达犯罪嫌疑人。

**Article 115** Where the period of residential surveillance expires or it is discovered that the criminal suspect should not be investigated for criminal liability, the residential surveillance shall be terminated or revoked in a timely manner.  
The decision to terminate or revoke the residential surveillance shall be notified to the executing authority, and the written decision on termination or revocation shall be served on the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条  监视居住应当在犯罪嫌疑人的住处执行。犯罪嫌疑人无固定住处的，可以在指定的居所执行。  
固定住处是指犯罪嫌疑人在办案机关所在地的市、县内工作、生活的合法居所。  
指定的居所应当符合下列条件：  
（一）具备正常的生活、休息条件；  
（二）便于监视、管理；  
（三）能够保证安全。  
采取指定居所监视居住，不得在看守所、拘留所、监狱等羁押、监管场所以及留置室、讯问室等专门的办案场所、办公区域执行。

**Article 116** Residential surveillance shall be executed at the criminal suspect's residence. If the criminal suspect has no fixed residence, it may be executed at a designated residence.  
"Fixed residence" means a lawful dwelling where the criminal suspect works and lives in the city or county where the case-handling authority is located.  
The designated residence shall meet the following conditions:  
(1) having normal living and rest conditions;  
(2) facilitating surveillance and management;  
(3) being able to ensure safety.  
Residential surveillance at a designated residence shall not be executed in custodial or supervisory venues such as detention centers, detention houses, prisons, or in special case-handling venues and office areas such as holding rooms and interrogation rooms.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条  在指定的居所执行监视居住，除无法通知的以外，人民检察院应当在执行监视居住后二十四小时以内，将指定居所监视居住的原因通知被监视居住人的家属。无法通知的，应当将原因写明附卷。无法通知的情形消除后，应当立即通知。  
无法通知包括下列情形：  
（一）被监视居住人无家属；  
（二）与其家属无法取得联系；  
（三）受自然灾害等不可抗力阻碍。

**Article 117** Where residential surveillance is executed at a designated residence, except where it is impossible to notify, the People's Procuratorate shall, within twenty-four hours after executing the residential surveillance, notify the family members of the person under residential surveillance of the reasons for the residential surveillance at a designated residence. If notification is impossible, the reasons shall be recorded in writing and attached to the case file. Once the impossibility of notification is eliminated, notification shall be given immediately.  
"Impossibility of notification" includes the following circumstances:  
(1) the person under residential surveillance has no family members;  
(2) it is impossible to contact his or her family members;  
(3) being hindered by force majeure such as natural disasters.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条  对于公安机关、人民法院决定指定居所监视居住的案件，由批准或者决定的公安机关、人民法院的同级人民检察院负责捕诉的部门对决定是否合法实行监督。  
人民检察院决定指定居所监视居住的案件，由负责控告申诉检察的部门对决定是否合法实行监督。

**Article 118** For cases where the public security organ or the People's Court decides on residential surveillance at a designated residence, the department responsible for prosecution and arrest of the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the approving or deciding public security organ or People's Court shall supervise the legality of the decision.  
For cases where the People's Procuratorate decides on residential surveillance at a designated residence, the department responsible for prosecution of complaints and appeals shall supervise the legality of the decision.

<a id="art-119"></a>第一百一十九条  被指定居所监视居住人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人认为指定居所监视居住决定存在违法情形，提出控告或者举报的，人民检察院应当受理。  
人民检察院可以要求有关机关提供指定居所监视居住决定书和相关案卷材料。经审查，发现存在下列违法情形之一的，应当及时通知其纠正：  
（一）不符合指定居所监视居住的适用条件的；  
（二）未按法定程序履行批准手续的；  
（三）在决定过程中有其他违反刑事诉讼法规定的行为的。

**Article 119** If a person under residential surveillance at a designated residence, or his or her legal representative, near relative, or defender, considers that the decision on residential surveillance at a designated residence is illegal and files a complaint or report, the People's Procuratorate shall accept it.  
The People's Procuratorate may request the relevant authority to provide the decision on residential surveillance at a designated residence and relevant case files. Upon review, if any of the following illegal circumstances are found, the authority shall be promptly notified to rectify:  
(1) not meeting the applicable conditions for residential surveillance at a designated residence;  
(2) failing to go through the approval procedures according to legal procedures;  
(3) other acts violating the provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law during the decision-making process.

<a id="art-120"></a>第一百二十条  对于公安机关、人民法院决定指定居所监视居住的案件，由人民检察院负责刑事执行检察的部门对指定居所监视居住的执行活动是否合法实行监督。发现存在下列违法情形之一的，应当及时提出纠正意见：  
（一）执行机关收到指定居所监视居住决定书、执行通知书等法律文书后不派员执行或者不及时派员执行的；  
（二）在执行指定居所监视居住后二十四小时以内没有通知被监视居住人的家属的；  
（三）在羁押场所、专门的办案场所执行监视居住的；  
（四）为被监视居住人通风报信、私自传递信件、物品的；  
（五）违反规定安排辩护人同被监视居住人会见、通信，或者违法限制被监视居住人与辩护人会见、通信的；  
（六）对被监视居住人刑讯逼供、体罚、虐待或者变相体罚、虐待的；  
（七）有其他侵犯被监视居住人合法权利行为或者其他违法行为的。  
被监视居住人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人认为执行机关或者执行人员存在上述违法情形，提出控告或者举报的，人民检察院应当受理。  
人民检察院决定指定居所监视居住的案件，由负责控告申诉检察的部门对指定居所监视居住的执行活动是否合法实行监督。

**Article 120** For cases where the public security organ or the People's Court decides on residential surveillance at a designated residence, the department responsible for criminal execution inspection of the People's Procuratorate shall supervise the legality of the execution activities of residential surveillance at a designated residence. If any of the following illegal circumstances are found, corrective opinions shall be promptly issued:  
(1) after receiving legal documents such as the decision on residential surveillance at a designated residence and the enforcement notice, the executing authority fails to dispatch personnel or does so untimely;  
(2) failing to notify the family members of the person under residential surveillance within twenty-four hours after executing residential surveillance at a designated residence;  
(3) executing residential surveillance in custodial venues or special case-handling venues;  
(4) tipping off the person under residential surveillance, privately transmitting letters or articles;  
(5) arranging meetings or communications between the defender and the person under residential surveillance in violation of regulations, or illegally restricting meetings or communications between the person under residential surveillance and the defender;  
(6) extorting confessions by torture, corporal punishment, abuse, or disguised corporal punishment or abuse against the person under residential surveillance;  
(7) other acts infringing on the lawful rights of the person under residential surveillance or other illegal acts.  
If the person under residential surveillance, or his or her legal representative, near relative, or defender, considers that the executing authority or personnel have committed the above illegal acts and files a complaint or report, the People's Procuratorate shall accept it.  
For cases where the People's Procuratorate decides on residential surveillance at a designated residence, the department responsible for prosecution of complaints and appeals shall supervise the legality of the execution activities of residential surveillance at a designated residence.

#### 第四节 拘 留

<a id="art-121"></a>第一百二十一条  人民检察院对于具有下列情形之一的犯罪嫌疑人，可以决定拘留：  
（一）犯罪后企图自杀、逃跑或者在逃的；  
（二）有毁灭、伪造证据或者串供可能的。

**Article 121** A People's Procuratorate may decide to detain a criminal suspect under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The suspect attempts suicide, escapes or is at large after committing a crime;  
(2) The suspect may destroy or falsify evidence or collude with others to make false statements.

<a id="art-122"></a>第一百二十二条  人民检察院作出拘留决定后，应当将有关法律文书和案由、犯罪嫌疑人基本情况的材料送交同级公安机关执行。必要时，人民检察院可以协助公安机关执行。  
拘留后，应当立即将被拘留人送看守所羁押，至迟不得超过二十四小时。

**Article 122** After a People's Procuratorate makes a decision to detain, it shall deliver the relevant legal documents and materials concerning the cause of action and the basic information of the criminal suspect to the public security organ at the same level for execution. If necessary, the People's Procuratorate may assist the public security organ in execution.  
After detention, the detained person shall be immediately sent to the detention center for custody, and the time shall not exceed twenty-four hours at the latest.

<a id="art-123"></a>第一百二十三条  对犯罪嫌疑人拘留后，除无法通知的以外，人民检察院应当在二十四小时以内，通知被拘留人的家属。  
无法通知的，应当将原因写明附卷。无法通知的情形消除后，应当立即通知其家属。

**Article 123** After a criminal suspect is detained, the People's Procuratorate shall, within twenty-four hours, notify the family members of the detained person, except where notification is impossible.  
Where notification is impossible, the reasons shall be recorded in writing and attached to the file. After the circumstances rendering notification impossible are eliminated, the family members shall be immediately notified.

<a id="art-124"></a>第一百二十四条  对被拘留的犯罪嫌疑人，应当在拘留后二十四小时以内进行讯问。

**Article 124** A detained criminal suspect shall be interrogated within twenty-four hours after detention.

<a id="art-125"></a>第一百二十五条  对被拘留的犯罪嫌疑人，发现不应当拘留的，应当立即释放；依法可以取保候审或者监视居住的，按照本规则的有关规定办理取保候审或者监视居住手续。  
对被拘留的犯罪嫌疑人，需要逮捕的，按照本规则的有关规定办理逮捕手续；决定不予逮捕的，应当及时变更强制措施。

**Article 125** Where a detained criminal suspect is found not to be subject to detention, he shall be immediately released; if he may be released on bail pending investigation or placed under residential surveillance according to law, the formalities for release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance shall be handled in accordance with the relevant provisions of these Rules.  
Where a detained criminal suspect needs to be arrested, the formalities for arrest shall be handled in accordance with the relevant provisions of these Rules; if a decision is made not to arrest, the compulsory measures shall be changed in a timely manner.

<a id="art-126"></a>第一百二十六条  人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，拘留犯罪嫌疑人的羁押期限为十四日，特殊情况下可以延长一日至三日。

**Article 126** For a case directly accepted and investigated by a People's Procuratorate, the period of custody for detaining a criminal suspect shall be fourteen days, and under special circumstances, it may be extended by one to three days.

<a id="art-127"></a>第一百二十七条  公民将正在实行犯罪或者在犯罪后即被发觉的、通缉在案的、越狱逃跑的、正在被追捕的犯罪嫌疑人或者犯罪人扭送到人民检察院的，人民检察院应当予以接受，并且根据具体情况决定是否采取相应的紧急措施。不属于自己管辖的，应当移送主管机关处理。

**Article 127** Where a citizen delivers to a People's Procuratorate a criminal suspect or offender who is committing a crime or is discovered immediately after committing a crime, or who is wanted, has escaped from prison, or is being pursued, the People's Procuratorate shall accept the delivery and decide whether to take corresponding emergency measures according to the specific circumstances. If the case does not fall within its jurisdiction, it shall be transferred to the competent authority for handling.

#### 第五节 逮 捕

<a id="art-128"></a>第一百二十八条  人民检察院对有证据证明有犯罪事实，可能判处徒刑以上刑罚的犯罪嫌疑人，采取取保候审尚不足以防止发生下列社会危险性的，应当批准或者决定逮捕：  
（一）可能实施新的犯罪的；  
（二）有危害国家安全、公共安全或者社会秩序的现实危险的；  
（三）可能毁灭、伪造证据，干扰证人作证或者串供的；  
（四）可能对被害人、举报人、控告人实施打击报复的；  
（五）企图自杀或者逃跑的。  
有证据证明有犯罪事实是指同时具备下列情形：  
（一）有证据证明发生了犯罪事实；  
（二）有证据证明该犯罪事实是犯罪嫌疑人实施的；  
（三）证明犯罪嫌疑人实施犯罪行为的证据已经查证属实。  
犯罪事实既可以是单一犯罪行为的事实，也可以是数个犯罪行为中任何一个犯罪行为的事实。

**Article 128** Where a People's Procuratorate considers that for a criminal suspect against whom there is evidence to substantiate the facts of a crime and who may be sentenced to a punishment of not less than fixed-term imprisonment, release on bail pending investigation is insufficient to prevent the following social dangers, it shall approve or decide to arrest:  
(1) The suspect may commit a new crime;  
(2) There is a real danger of endangering national security, public security, or social order;  
(3) The suspect may destroy or falsify evidence, interfere with witness testimony, or collude with others to make false statements;  
(4) The suspect may retaliate against the victim, informant, or accuser;  
(5) The suspect attempts suicide or escape.  
"Evidence to substantiate the facts of a crime" means the following circumstances are all present:  
(1) There is evidence to prove that a criminal fact has occurred;  
(2) There is evidence to prove that the criminal fact was committed by the criminal suspect;  
(3) The evidence proving that the criminal suspect committed the criminal act has been verified.  
The facts of a crime may be the facts of a single criminal act or the facts of any one of several criminal acts.

<a id="art-129"></a>第一百二十九条  犯罪嫌疑人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为“可能实施新的犯罪”：  
（一）案发前或者案发后正在策划、组织或者预备实施新的犯罪的；  
（二）扬言实施新的犯罪的；  
（三）多次作案、连续作案、流窜作案的；  
（四）一年内曾因故意实施同类违法行为受到行政处罚的；  
（五）以犯罪所得为主要生活来源的；  
（六）有吸毒、赌博等恶习的；  
（七）其他可能实施新的犯罪的情形。

**Article 129** Where a criminal suspect falls under any of the following circumstances, he may be found to be "likely to commit a new crime":  
(1) Before or after the incident, the suspect is planning, organizing, or preparing to commit a new crime;  
(2) The suspect openly declares that he will commit a new crime;  
(3) The suspect has committed multiple offenses, consecutive offenses, or transitory offenses;  
(4) Within one year, the suspect has been subjected to an administrative penalty for intentionally committing similar illegal acts;  
(5) The suspect relies on criminal proceeds as his main source of living;  
(6) The suspect has bad habits such as drug abuse or gambling;  
(7) Other circumstances where a new crime may be committed.

<a id="art-130"></a>第一百三十条  犯罪嫌疑人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为“有危害国家安全、公共安全或者社会秩序的现实危险”：  
（一）案发前或者案发后正在积极策划、组织或者预备实施危害国家安全、公共安全或者社会秩序的重大违法犯罪行为的；  
（二）曾因危害国家安全、公共安全或者社会秩序受到刑事处罚或者行政处罚的；  
（三）在危害国家安全、黑恶势力、恐怖活动、毒品犯罪中起组织、策划、指挥作用或者积极参加的；  
（四）其他有危害国家安全、公共安全或者社会秩序的现实危险的情形。

**Article 130** Where a criminal suspect falls under any of the following circumstances, he may be found to have "a real danger of endangering national security, public security, or social order":  
(1) Before or after the incident, the suspect is actively planning, organizing, or preparing to commit a major illegal or criminal act endangering national security, public security, or social order;  
(2) The suspect has previously received criminal punishment or administrative penalty for endangering national security, public security, or social order;  
(3) The suspect plays an organizing, planning, commanding role or actively participates in crimes endangering national security, organized crime of a triad nature, terrorist activities, or drug-related crimes;  
(4) Other circumstances where there is a real danger of endangering national security, public security, or social order.

<a id="art-131"></a>第一百三十一条  犯罪嫌疑人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为“可能毁灭、伪造证据，干扰证人作证或者串供”：  
（一）曾经或者企图毁灭、伪造、隐匿、转移证据的；  
（二）曾经或者企图威逼、恐吓、利诱、收买证人，干扰证人作证的；  
（三）有同案犯罪嫌疑人或者与其在事实上存在密切关联犯罪的犯罪嫌疑人在逃，重要证据尚未收集到位的；  
（四）其他可能毁灭、伪造证据，干扰证人作证或者串供的情形。

**Article 131** Where a criminal suspect falls under any of the following circumstances, he may be found to be "likely to destroy or falsify evidence, interfere with witness testimony, or collude with others to make false statements":  
(1) The suspect has previously attempted to or attempted to destroy, falsify, conceal, or transfer evidence;  
(2) The suspect has previously attempted to or attempted to intimidate, threaten, bribe, or buy off witnesses to interfere with their testimony;  
(3) There are co-suspects or suspects who are in fact closely related to the crime who are at large, and important evidence has not yet been collected;  
(4) Other circumstances where the suspect may destroy or falsify evidence, interfere with witness testimony, or collude with others to make false statements.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条  犯罪嫌疑人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为“可能对被害人、举报人、控告人实施打击报复”：  
（一）扬言或者准备、策划对被害人、举报人、控告人实施打击报复的；  
（二）曾经对被害人、举报人、控告人实施打击、要挟、迫害等行为的；  
（三）采取其他方式滋扰被害人、举报人、控告人的正常生活、工作的；  
（四）其他可能对被害人、举报人、控告人实施打击报复的情形。

**Article 132** Where a criminal suspect falls under any of the following circumstances, he may be found to be "likely to retaliate against the victim, informant, or accuser":  
(1) The suspect openly declares, prepares, or plans to retaliate against the victim, informant, or accuser;  
(2) The suspect has previously committed acts of retaliation, coercion, or persecution against the victim, informant, or accuser;  
(3) The suspect engages in other forms of harassment against the normal life or work of the victim, informant, or accuser;  
(4) Other circumstances where the suspect may retaliate against the victim, informant, or accuser.

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条  犯罪嫌疑人具有下列情形之一的，可以认定为“企图自杀或者逃跑”：  
（一）着手准备自杀、自残或者逃跑的；  
（二）曾经自杀、自残或者逃跑的；  
（三）有自杀、自残或者逃跑的意思表示的；  
（四）曾经以暴力、威胁手段抗拒抓捕的；  
（五）其他企图自杀或者逃跑的情形。

**Article 133** Where a criminal suspect falls under any of the following circumstances, he may be found to "attempt suicide or escape":  
(1) The suspect has started preparing for suicide, self-harm, or escape;  
(2) The suspect has previously committed suicide, self-harm, or escape;  
(3) The suspect has expressed intention to commit suicide, self-harm, or escape;  
(4) The suspect has previously resisted arrest by violent or threatening means;  
(5) Other circumstances where the suspect attempts suicide or escape.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条  人民检察院办理审查逮捕案件，应当全面把握逮捕条件，对有证据证明有犯罪事实、可能判处徒刑以上刑罚的犯罪嫌疑人，除具有[刑事诉讼法第八十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-81)第三款、第四款规定的情形外，应当严格审查是否具备社会危险性条件。

**Article 134** When handling a case of reviewing an arrest, a People's Procuratorate shall comprehensively grasp the conditions for arrest. For a criminal suspect against whom there is evidence to substantiate the facts of a crime and who may be sentenced to a punishment of not less than fixed-term imprisonment, except where the circumstances specified in paragraphs 3 and 4 of Article 81 of the Criminal Procedure Law apply, the People's Procuratorate shall strictly examine whether the condition of social danger is met.

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条  人民检察院审查认定犯罪嫌疑人是否具有社会危险性，应当以公安机关移送的社会危险性相关证据为依据，并结合案件具体情况综合认定。必要时，可以通过讯问犯罪嫌疑人、询问证人等诉讼参与人、听取辩护律师意见等方式，核实相关证据。  
依据在案证据不能认定犯罪嫌疑人符合逮捕社会危险性条件的，人民检察院可以要求公安机关补充相关证据，公安机关没有补充移送的，应当作出不批准逮捕的决定。

**Article 135** When a People's Procuratorate reviews and determines whether a criminal suspect presents a social danger, it shall base such determination on the evidence related to social danger submitted by the public security organ, and make a comprehensive determination in light of the specific circumstances of the case. If necessary, it may verify the relevant evidence by interrogating the criminal suspect, questioning witnesses and other participants in the proceedings, hearing the opinions of defense counsel, and other means.  
Where, based on the evidence on file, it cannot be determined that the criminal suspect meets the social danger conditions for arrest, the People's Procuratorate may request the public security organ to supplement the relevant evidence; if the public security organ fails to supplement and submit such evidence, the People's Procuratorate shall decide not to approve the arrest.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条  对有证据证明有犯罪事实，可能判处十年有期徒刑以上刑罚的犯罪嫌疑人，应当批准或者决定逮捕。  
对有证据证明有犯罪事实，可能判处徒刑以上刑罚，犯罪嫌疑人曾经故意犯罪或者不讲真实姓名、住址，身份不明的，应当批准或者决定逮捕。

**Article 136** For a criminal suspect against whom there is evidence to substantiate the facts of a crime and who may be sentenced to a punishment of fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, the arrest shall be approved or decided.  
For a criminal suspect against whom there is evidence to substantiate the facts of a crime and who may be sentenced to a punishment of not less than fixed-term imprisonment, where the suspect has previously intentionally committed a crime or refuses to disclose his true name and address, or his identity is unknown, the arrest shall be approved or decided.

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条  人民检察院经审查认为被取保候审、监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人违反取保候审、监视居住规定，依照[本规则第一百零一条](#art-101)、第一百一十一条的规定办理。  
对于被取保候审、监视居住的可能判处徒刑以下刑罚的犯罪嫌疑人，违反取保候审、监视居住规定，严重影响诉讼活动正常进行的，可以予以逮捕。

**Article 137** Where, after review, a People's Procuratorate finds that a criminal suspect released on bail pending investigation or placed under residential surveillance has violated the provisions on release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance, the matter shall be handled in accordance with Articles 101 and 111 of these Rules.  
For a criminal suspect released on bail pending investigation or placed under residential surveillance who may be sentenced to a punishment below fixed-term imprisonment, if he violates the provisions on release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance and seriously affects the normal progress of the proceedings, he may be arrested.

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条  对实施多个犯罪行为或者共同犯罪案件的犯罪嫌疑人，符合[本规则第一百二十八条](#art-128)的规定，具有下列情形之一的，应当批准或者决定逮捕：  
（一）有证据证明犯有数罪中的一罪的；  
（二）有证据证明实施多次犯罪中的一次犯罪的；  
（三）共同犯罪中，已有证据证明有犯罪事实的犯罪嫌疑人。

**Article 138** For a criminal suspect who has committed multiple criminal acts or is involved in a joint crime, who meets the conditions of Article 128 of these Rules and falls under any of the following circumstances, the arrest shall be approved or decided:  
(1) There is evidence to prove that the suspect has committed one of several crimes;  
(2) There is evidence to prove that the suspect has committed one of multiple offenses;  
(3) In a joint crime, there is already evidence to prove that the suspect committed the criminal fact.

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条  对具有下列情形之一的犯罪嫌疑人，人民检察院应当作出不批准逮捕或者不予逮捕的决定：  
（一）不符合本规则规定的逮捕条件的；  
（二）具有[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)规定的情形之一的。

**Article 139** Where a criminal suspect falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Procuratorate shall decide not to approve the arrest or not to arrest:  
(1) The conditions for arrest set forth in these Rules are not met;  
(2) The circumstances specified in Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law apply.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条  犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌的罪行较轻，且没有其他重大犯罪嫌疑，具有下列情形之一的，可以作出不批准逮捕或者不予逮捕的决定：  
（一）属于预备犯、中止犯，或者防卫过当、避险过当的；  
（二）主观恶性较小的初犯，共同犯罪中的从犯、胁从犯，犯罪后自首、有立功表现或者积极退赃、赔偿损失、确有悔罪表现的；  
（三）过失犯罪的犯罪嫌疑人，犯罪后有悔罪表现，有效控制损失或者积极赔偿损失的；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人与被害人双方根据刑事诉讼法的有关规定达成和解协议，经审查，认为和解系自愿、合法且已经履行或者提供担保的；  
（五）犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚的；  
（六）犯罪嫌疑人系已满十四周岁未满十八周岁的未成年人或者在校学生，本人有悔罪表现，其家庭、学校或者所在社区、居民委员会、村民委员会具备监护、帮教条件的；  
（七）犯罪嫌疑人系已满七十五周岁的人。

**Article 140** Where the crime suspected of being committed by a criminal suspect is relatively minor and there is no other major criminal suspicion, and the suspect falls under any of the following circumstances, a decision may be made not to approve the arrest or not to arrest:  
(1) The suspect is a preparator or a desister, or acts in excessive defense or excessive necessity;  
(2) The suspect is a first-time offender with relatively minor subjective malignancy, an accessory or coerced accomplice in a joint crime, and after the crime, voluntarily surrenders, performs meritorious acts, actively returns illegal gains, compensates for losses, or genuinely shows repentance;  
(3) The suspect is a negligent offender who shows repentance after the crime, effectively controls losses, or actively compensates for losses;  
(4) The suspect and the victim have reached a settlement agreement in accordance with the relevant provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law, and upon review, it is deemed that the settlement is voluntary, lawful, and has been performed or a guarantee has been provided;  
(5) The suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment;  
(6) The suspect is a minor who has reached the age of fourteen but under the age of eighteen, or is a student at school, and personally shows repentance, and his family, school, or the community, residents' committee, or villagers' committee have the conditions for guardianship and assistance;  
(7) The suspect has reached the age of seventy-five.

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条  对符合[刑事诉讼法第七十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-74)第一款规定的犯罪嫌疑人，人民检察院经审查认为不需要逮捕的，可以在作出不批准逮捕决定的同时，向公安机关提出采取监视居住措施的建议。

**Article 141** Where a criminal suspect meets the requirements provided in paragraph 1 of Article 74 of the Criminal Procedure Law, and the People's Procuratorate, upon review, deems arrest unnecessary, it may, at the same time as making a decision not to approve arrest, make a recommendation to the public security organ to take residential surveillance measures.

#### 第六节 监察机关移送案件的强制措施

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条  对于监察机关移送起诉的已采取留置措施的案件，人民检察院应当在受理案件后，及时对犯罪嫌疑人作出拘留决定，交公安机关执行。执行拘留后，留置措施自动解除。

**Article 142** For a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ where lien measures have already been taken, the People's Procuratorate shall, after accepting the case, promptly make a decision to detain the criminal suspect and deliver it to the public security organ for execution. After the detention is executed, the lien measures shall be automatically lifted.

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条  人民检察院应当在执行拘留后十日以内，作出是否逮捕、取保候审或者监视居住的决定。特殊情况下，决定的时间可以延长一日至四日。  
人民检察院决定采取强制措施的期间不计入审查起诉期限。

**Article 143** The People's Procuratorate shall, within ten days after the execution of detention, make a decision on whether to arrest, release on bail pending investigation or trial, or place under residential surveillance. Under special circumstances, the time for making the decision may be extended by one to four days.  
The period during which the People's Procuratorate decides to take compulsory measures shall not be counted in the time limit for examination and prosecution.

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条  除无法通知的以外，人民检察院应当在公安机关执行拘留、逮捕后二十四小时以内，通知犯罪嫌疑人的家属。

**Article 144** Unless it is impossible to give notice, the People's Procuratorate shall, within twenty-four hours after the public security organ executes detention or arrest, notify the family members of the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条  人民检察院应当自收到移送起诉的案卷材料之日起三日以内告知犯罪嫌疑人有权委托辩护人。对已经采取留置措施的，应当在执行拘留时告知。

**Article 145** The People's Procuratorate shall, within three days from the date of receiving the case file materials transferred for prosecution, inform the criminal suspect of the right to entrust a defender. For those who have already been subjected to lien measures, the notification shall be made at the time of execution of detention.

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条  对于监察机关移送起诉的未采取留置措施的案件，人民检察院受理后，在审查起诉过程中根据案件情况，可以依照本规则相关规定决定是否采取逮捕、取保候审或者监视居住措施。

**Article 146** For a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ without lien measures having been taken, the People's Procuratorate, after accepting the case and in the process of examination and prosecution, may, based on the circumstances of the case, decide in accordance with the relevant provisions of these Rules whether to take measures of arrest, release on bail pending investigation or trial, or residential surveillance.

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条  对于监察机关移送起诉案件的犯罪嫌疑人采取强制措施，本节未规定的，适用本规则相关规定。

**Article 147** Where compulsory measures are taken against a criminal suspect in a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ, if not provided for in this Section, the relevant provisions of these Rules shall apply.

#### 第七节 其他规定

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条  人民检察院对担任县级以上各级人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人决定采取拘传、取保候审、监视居住、拘留、逮捕强制措施的，应当报请该代表所属的人民代表大会主席团或者常务委员会许可。  
人民检察院对担任本级人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人决定采取强制措施的，应当报请本级人民代表大会主席团或者常务委员会许可。  
对担任上级人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人决定采取强制措施的，应当层报该代表所属的人民代表大会同级的人民检察院报请许可。  
对担任下级人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人决定采取强制措施的，可以直接报请该代表所属的人民代表大会主席团或者常务委员会许可，也可以委托该代表所属的人民代表大会同级的人民检察院报请许可。  
对担任两级以上的人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人决定采取强制措施的，分别依照本条第二、三、四款的规定报请许可。  
对担任办案单位所在省、市、县（区）以外的其他地区人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人决定采取强制措施的，应当委托该代表所属的人民代表大会同级的人民检察院报请许可；担任两级以上人民代表大会代表的，应当分别委托该代表所属的人民代表大会同级的人民检察院报请许可。  
对于公安机关提请人民检察院批准逮捕的案件，犯罪嫌疑人担任人民代表大会代表的，报请许可手续由公安机关负责办理。  
担任县级以上人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人，经报请该代表所属人民代表大会主席团或者常务委员会许可后被刑事拘留的，适用逮捕措施时不需要再次报请许可。

**Article 148** Where the People's Procuratorate decides to take compulsory measures of custodial summons, release on bail pending investigation or trial, residential surveillance, detention, or arrest against a criminal suspect who is a deputy to a people's congress at or above the county level, it shall report to and obtain permission from the presidium or the standing committee of the people's congress to which the deputy belongs.  
Where the People's Procuratorate decides to take compulsory measures against a criminal suspect who is a deputy to the people's congress at the same level, it shall report to and obtain permission from the presidium or the standing committee of the people's congress at that level.  
Where a decision is made to take compulsory measures against a criminal suspect who is a deputy to a people's congress at a higher level, the matter shall be reported level by level to the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the people's congress to which the deputy belongs to request permission.  
Where a decision is made to take compulsory measures against a criminal suspect who is a deputy to a people's congress at a lower level, permission may be directly requested from the presidium or the standing committee of the people's congress to which the deputy belongs, or the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the people's congress to which the deputy belongs may be entrusted to request permission.  
Where a decision is made to take compulsory measures against a criminal suspect who is a deputy to two or more levels of people's congresses, permission shall be requested respectively in accordance with paragraphs 2, 3, and 4 of this Article.  
Where a decision is made to take compulsory measures against a criminal suspect who is a deputy to a people's congress in a region other than the province, city, or county (district) where the case-handling unit is located, the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the people's congress to which the deputy belongs shall be entrusted to request permission; where the criminal suspect is a deputy to two or more levels of people's congresses, the People's Procuratorates at the same levels as the respective people's congresses to which the deputy belongs shall be separately entrusted to request permission.  
For a case where a public security organ requests the People's Procuratorate to approve an arrest, and the criminal suspect is a deputy to a people's congress, the procedures for requesting permission shall be handled by the public security organ.  
Where a criminal suspect who is a deputy to a people's congress at or above the county level is placed under criminal detention after permission has been requested from the presidium or the standing committee of the people's congress to which the deputy belongs, no further permission need be requested when applying arrest measures.

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条  担任县级以上人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人因现行犯被人民检察院拘留的，人民检察院应当立即向该代表所属的人民代表大会主席团或者常务委员会报告。报告的程序参照[本规则第一百四十八条](#art-148)报请许可的程序规定。  
对担任乡、民族乡、镇的人民代表大会代表的犯罪嫌疑人决定采取强制措施的，由县级人民检察院向乡、民族乡、镇的人民代表大会报告。

**Article 149** Where a criminal suspect who is a deputy to a people's congress at or above the county level is detained by the People's Procuratorate as a flagrant offender, the People's Procuratorate shall immediately report to the presidium or the standing committee of the people's congress to which the deputy belongs. The reporting procedure shall refer to the procedure for requesting permission provided in Article 148 of these Rules.  
Where a decision is made to take compulsory measures against a criminal suspect who is a deputy to a township, nationality township, or town people's congress, the county-level People's Procuratorate shall report to the township, nationality township, or town people's congress.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条  犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人认为人民检察院采取强制措施法定期限届满，要求解除、变更强制措施或者释放犯罪嫌疑人的，人民检察院应当在收到申请后三日以内作出决定。  
经审查，认为法定期限届满的，应当决定解除、变更强制措施或者释放犯罪嫌疑人，并通知公安机关执行；认为法定期限未满的，书面答复申请人。

**Article 150** Where a criminal suspect, his legal representative, near relative, or defender considers that the statutory time limit for the compulsory measures taken by the People's Procuratorate has expired and requests the release, modification, or discharge of the compulsory measures or the release of the criminal suspect, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision within three days after receiving the application.  
Upon review, if it is deemed that the statutory time limit has expired, a decision shall be made to discharge or modify the compulsory measures or to release the criminal suspect, and the public security organ shall be notified for execution; if it is deemed that the statutory time limit has not expired, the applicant shall be notified in writing of the reasons.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条  犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人向人民检察院提出变更强制措施申请的，人民检察院应当在收到申请后三日以内作出决定。  
经审查，同意变更强制措施的，应当在作出决定的同时通知公安机关执行；不同意变更强制措施的，应当书面告知申请人，并说明不同意的理由。  
犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人提出变更强制措施申请的，应当说明理由，有证据和其他材料的，应当附上相关材料。

**Article 151** Where a criminal suspect, his legal representative, near relative, or defender applies to the People's Procuratorate for modification of compulsory measures, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision within three days after receiving the application.  
Upon review, if the modification is approved, the public security organ shall be notified for execution at the same time as the decision is made; if the modification is not approved, the applicant shall be informed in writing of the reasons for disapproval.  
Where a criminal suspect, his legal representative, near relative, or defender applies for modification of compulsory measures, reasons shall be stated, and if there is evidence or other materials, the relevant materials shall be attached.

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条  人民检察院在侦查、审查起诉期间，对犯罪嫌疑人拘留、逮捕后发生依法延长侦查羁押期限、审查起诉期限，重新计算侦查羁押期限、审查起诉期限等期限改变的情形的，应当及时将变更后的期限书面通知看守所。

**Article 152** Where, during the period of investigation or examination and prosecution, after the detention or arrest of a criminal suspect, circumstances arise such as the lawful extension of the time limit for investigative custody or examination and prosecution, or the recalculation of such time limits, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly notify the detention center in writing of the changed time limit.

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条  人民检察院决定对涉嫌犯罪的机关事业单位工作人员取保候审、监视居住、拘留、逮捕的，应当在采取或者解除强制措施后五日以内告知其所在单位；决定撤销案件或者不起诉的，应当在作出决定后十日以内告知其所在单位。

**Article 153** Where the People's Procuratorate decides to release on bail pending investigation or trial, place under residential surveillance, detain, or arrest a staff member of an organ or public institution suspected of committing a crime, it shall inform the unit to which the person belongs within five days after taking or discharging the compulsory measures; where a decision is made to dismiss the case or not to prosecute, it shall inform the unit to which the person belongs within ten days after making the decision.

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条  取保候审变更为监视居住，或者取保候审、监视居住变更为拘留、逮捕的，在变更的同时原强制措施自动解除，不再办理解除法律手续。

**Article 154** Where release on bail pending investigation or trial is changed to residential surveillance, or release on bail pending investigation or trial or residential surveillance is changed to detention or arrest, the original compulsory measures shall be automatically discharged at the same time as the change, and the formalities for discharging need not be handled.

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条  人民检察院已经对犯罪嫌疑人取保候审、监视居住，案件起诉至人民法院后，人民法院决定取保候审、监视居住或者变更强制措施的，原强制措施自动解除，不再办理解除法律手续。

**Article 155** Where the People's Procuratorate has already released a criminal suspect on bail pending investigation or trial or placed him under residential surveillance, and after the case is prosecuted to the People's Court, the People's Court decides to release the suspect on bail pending investigation or trial, place him under residential surveillance, or modify the compulsory measures, the original compulsory measures shall be automatically discharged, and the formalities for discharging need not be handled.

### 第七章 案件受理

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条  下列案件，由人民检察院负责案件管理的部门统一受理：  
（一）公安机关提请批准逮捕、移送起诉、提请批准延长侦查羁押期限、要求复议、提请复核、申请复查、移送申请强制医疗、移送申请没收违法所得的案件；  
（二）监察机关移送起诉、提请没收违法所得、对不起诉决定提请复议的案件；  
（三）下级人民检察院提出或者提请抗诉、报请指定管辖、报请核准追诉、报请核准缺席审判或者提请死刑复核监督的案件；  
（四）人民法院通知出席第二审法庭或者再审法庭的案件；  
（五）其他依照规定由负责案件管理的部门受理的案件。

**Article 156** The following cases shall be uniformly accepted by the case management department of a People's Procuratorate:  
(1) Cases submitted by public security organs for approval of arrest, referral for prosecution, approval of extension of the period of criminal custody during investigation, request for reconsideration, request for review, application for reexamination, referral for application for compulsory medical treatment, and referral for application for confiscation of illegal gains;  
(2) Cases referred by supervisory organs for prosecution, request for confiscation of illegal gains, and request for reconsideration of a decision not to prosecute;  
(3) Cases where a lower-level People's Procuratorate raises or submits a protest, requests designation of jurisdiction, requests approval of prosecution, requests approval of trial in absentia, or requests supervision of review of death penalty;  
(4) Cases where a People's Court notifies attendance at a court of second instance or a retrial court;  
(5) Other cases that shall be accepted by the case management department according to the provisions.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条  人民检察院负责案件管理的部门受理案件时，应当接收案卷材料，并立即审查下列内容：  
（一）依据移送的法律文书载明的内容确定案件是否属于本院管辖；  
（二）案卷材料是否齐备、规范，符合有关规定的要求；  
（三）移送的款项或者物品与移送清单是否相符；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人是否在案以及采取强制措施的情况；  
（五）是否在规定的期限内移送案件。

**Article 157** When accepting a case, the case management department of a People's Procuratorate shall receive the case file materials and immediately examine the following:  
(1) Determine whether the case falls within the jurisdiction of this procuratorate based on the contents specified in the transferred legal documents;  
(2) Whether the case file materials are complete, standardized, and meet the requirements of relevant provisions;  
(3) Whether the transferred funds or articles match the transfer list;  
(4) Whether the criminal suspect is present and the status of compulsory measures taken;  
(5) Whether the case is transferred within the specified time limit.

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条  人民检察院负责案件管理的部门对接收的案卷材料审查后，认为具备受理条件的，应当及时进行登记，并立即将案卷材料和案件受理登记表移送办案部门办理。  
经审查，认为案卷材料不齐备的，应当及时要求移送案件的单位补送相关材料。对于案卷装订不符合要求的，应当要求移送案件的单位重新装订后移送。  
对于移送起诉的案件，犯罪嫌疑人在逃的，应当要求公安机关采取措施保证犯罪嫌疑人到案后再移送起诉。共同犯罪案件中部分犯罪嫌疑人在逃的，对在案犯罪嫌疑人的移送起诉应当受理。

**Article 158** After examining the received case file materials, the case management department of a People's Procuratorate shall, if it deems that the conditions for acceptance are met, promptly register the case and immediately transfer the case file materials and the case acceptance registration form to the handling department for processing.  
Upon examination, if it is deemed that the case file materials are incomplete, it shall promptly require the transferring unit to supplement the relevant materials. If the binding of the case files does not meet the requirements, it shall require the transferring unit to rebind the files before transfer.  
For cases referred for prosecution, if the criminal suspect is at large, it shall require the public security organ to take measures to ensure the criminal suspect appears before the referral for prosecution. If some criminal suspects in a joint crime are at large, the referral for prosecution of the criminal suspects who are present shall be accepted.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条  对公安机关送达的执行情况回执和人民法院送达的判决书、裁定书等法律文书，人民检察院负责案件管理的部门应当接收，即时登记。

**Article 159** The case management department of a People's Procuratorate shall receive the execution receipts served by public security organs and the judgments, rulings and other legal documents served by People's Courts, and register them immediately.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条  人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，移送审查逮捕、移送起诉的，按照[本规则第一百五十六条](#art-156)至第一百五十八条的规定办理。

**Article 160** For cases directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate, if they are transferred for review of arrest or for prosecution, they shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Articles 156 to 158 of these Rules.

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条  人民检察院负责控告申诉检察的部门统一接受报案、控告、举报、申诉和犯罪嫌疑人投案自首，并依法审查，在七日以内作出以下处理：  
（一）属于本院管辖且符合受理条件的，应当予以受理；  
（二）不属于本院管辖的报案、控告、举报、自首，应当移送主管机关处理。必须采取紧急措施的，应当先采取紧急措施，然后移送主管机关。不属于本院管辖的申诉，应当告知其向有管辖权的机关提出；  
（三）案件情况不明的，应当进行必要的调查核实，查明情况后依法作出处理。  
负责控告申诉检察的部门可以向下级人民检察院交办控告、申诉、举报案件，并依照有关规定进行督办。

**Article 161** The department responsible for procuratorial work on complaints and appeals of a People's Procuratorate shall uniformly accept reports of crimes, complaints, information, petitions and voluntary surrender of criminal suspects, examine them according to law, and make the following dispositions within seven days:  
(1) If the matter falls within the jurisdiction of this procuratorate and meets the conditions for acceptance, it shall be accepted;  
(2) If the report, complaint, information, or voluntary surrender does not fall within the jurisdiction of this procuratorate, it shall be transferred to the competent organ for handling. If emergency measures must be taken, it shall take emergency measures first and then transfer the matter to the competent organ. If the petition does not fall within the jurisdiction of this procuratorate, the petitioner shall be informed to submit it to the organ with jurisdiction;  
(3) If the case circumstances are unclear, necessary investigation and verification shall be conducted, and a disposition shall be made according to law after the circumstances are ascertained.  
The department responsible for procuratorial work on complaints and appeals may assign cases of complaints, appeals and information to lower-level People's Procuratorates and supervise their handling in accordance with relevant provisions.

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条  控告、申诉符合下列条件的，人民检察院应当受理：  
（一）属于人民检察院受理案件范围；  
（二）本院具有管辖权；  
（三）申诉人是原案的当事人或者其法定代理人、近亲属；  
（四）控告、申诉材料符合受理要求。  
控告人、申诉人委托律师代理控告、申诉，符合上述条件的，应当受理。  
控告、申诉材料不齐备的，应当告知控告人、申诉人补齐。受理时间从控告人、申诉人补齐相关材料之日起计算。

**Article 162** A People's Procuratorate shall accept a complaint or petition if the following conditions are met:  
(1) It falls within the scope of cases accepted by the People's Procuratorate;  
(2) This procuratorate has jurisdiction;  
(3) The petitioner is a party to the original case or his or her legal representative or near relative;  
(4) The complaint or petition materials meet the acceptance requirements.  
If the complainant or petitioner entrusts a lawyer to act as agent for the complaint or petition, and the above conditions are met, it shall be accepted.  
If the complaint or petition materials are incomplete, the complainant or petitioner shall be informed to supplement them. The acceptance time shall be calculated from the date when the complainant or petitioner supplements the relevant materials.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条  对于收到的群众来信，负责控告申诉检察的部门应当在七日以内进行程序性答复，办案部门应当在三个月以内将办理进展或者办理结果答复来信人。

**Article 163** For received letters from the public, the department responsible for procuratorial work on complaints and appeals shall make a procedural reply within seven days, and the handling department shall reply to the letter sender with the handling progress or result within three months.

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条  负责控告申诉检察的部门对受理的刑事申诉案件应当根据事实、法律进行审查，必要时可以进行调查核实。认为原案处理可能错误的，应当移送相关办案部门办理；认为原案处理没有错误的，应当书面答复申诉人。

**Article 164** The department responsible for procuratorial work on complaints and appeals shall examine accepted criminal petition cases based on facts and law, and may conduct investigation and verification when necessary. If it believes that the handling of the original case may be erroneous, it shall transfer the case to the relevant handling department for processing; if it believes that the handling of the original case is not erroneous, it shall reply to the petitioner in writing.

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条  办案部门应当在规定期限内办结控告、申诉案件，制作相关法律文书，送达报案人、控告人、申诉人、举报人、自首人，并做好释法说理工作。

**Article 165** The handling department shall complete the handling of complaint and petition cases within the prescribed time limit, produce relevant legal documents, serve them on the reporter, complainant, petitioner, informant, or person who voluntarily surrendered, and properly carry out the work of explaining the law and reasoning.

### 第八章 立 案

#### 第一节 立案审查

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条  人民检察院直接受理侦查案件的线索，由负责侦查的部门统一受理、登记和管理。负责控告申诉检察的部门接受的控告、举报，或者本院其他办案部门发现的案件线索，属于人民检察院直接受理侦查案件线索的，应当在七日以内移送负责侦查的部门。  
负责侦查的部门对案件线索进行审查后，认为属于本院管辖，需要进一步调查核实的，应当报检察长决定。

**Article 166** The leads of cases directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate shall be uniformly accepted, registered, and managed by the department responsible for investigation. Where the department responsible for prosecution of complaints and appeals accepts a complaint or report, or another case-handling department of the same procuratorate discovers a case lead that falls within the scope of cases directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate, it shall transfer the lead to the department responsible for investigation within seven days.  
After the department responsible for investigation reviews the case lead and considers that it falls under the jurisdiction of the procuratorate and requires further investigation and verification, it shall report to the chief procurator for a decision.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条  对于人民检察院直接受理侦查案件的线索，上级人民检察院在必要时，可以直接调查核实或者组织、指挥、参与下级人民检察院的调查核实，可以将下级人民检察院管辖的案件线索指定辖区内其他人民检察院调查核实，也可以将本院管辖的案件线索交由下级人民检察院调查核实；下级人民检察院认为案件线索重大、复杂，需要由上级人民检察院调查核实的，可以提请移送上级人民检察院调查核实。

**Article 167** For leads of cases directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate, the superior People's Procuratorate may, when necessary, directly investigate and verify, or organize, direct, or participate in the investigation and verification by the subordinate People's Procuratorate; may designate another People's Procuratorate within its jurisdiction to investigate and verify case leads under the jurisdiction of the subordinate People's Procuratorate; and may also transfer case leads under its own jurisdiction to the subordinate People's Procuratorate for investigation and verification. Where the subordinate People's Procuratorate considers a case lead to be significant or complex and requiring investigation and verification by the superior People's Procuratorate, it may request the transfer of the case lead to the superior People's Procuratorate for investigation and verification.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条  调查核实一般不得接触被调查对象。必须接触被调查对象的，应当经检察长批准。

**Article 168** Investigation and verification shall generally not involve contact with the subject under investigation. Where contact with the subject under investigation is necessary, approval from the chief procurator shall be obtained.

<a id="art-169"></a>第一百六十九条  进行调查核实，可以采取询问、查询、勘验、检查、鉴定、调取证据材料等不限制被调查对象人身、财产权利的措施。不得对被调查对象采取强制措施，不得查封、扣押、冻结被调查对象的财产，不得采取技术侦查措施。

**Article 169** In conducting investigation and verification, measures that do not restrict the personal or property rights of the subject under investigation, such as inquiry, search, inspection, examination, expert evaluation, and collection of evidence materials, may be taken. No compulsory measures shall be taken against the subject under investigation; no sealing, seizure, or freezing of the property of the subject under investigation shall be carried out; and no technical investigation measures shall be taken.

<a id="art-170"></a>第一百七十条  负责侦查的部门调查核实后，应当制作审查报告。  
调查核实终结后，相关材料应当立卷归档。立案进入侦查程序的，对于作为诉讼证据以外的其他材料应当归入侦查内卷。

**Article 170** After investigation and verification, the department responsible for investigation shall prepare a review report.  
After the conclusion of investigation and verification, the relevant materials shall be filed and archived. Where a case is placed on file and enters the investigation procedure, materials other than those used as litigation evidence shall be placed in the internal investigation file.

#### 第二节 立案决定

<a id="art-171"></a>第一百七十一条  人民检察院对于直接受理的案件，经审查认为有犯罪事实需要追究刑事责任的，应当制作立案报告书，经检察长批准后予以立案。  
符合立案条件，但犯罪嫌疑人尚未确定的，可以依据已查明的犯罪事实作出立案决定。  
对具有下列情形之一的，报请检察长决定不予立案：  
（一）具有[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)规定情形之一的；  
（二）认为没有犯罪事实的；  
（三）事实或者证据尚不符合立案条件的。

**Article 171** Where a People's Procuratorate, upon reviewing a case directly accepted for filing, finds that there is a criminal fact requiring investigation of criminal liability, it shall prepare a case-filing report and, upon approval by the Chief Prosecutor, file the case.  
If the filing conditions are met but the criminal suspect has not yet been determined, a decision to file the case may be made based on the established criminal facts.  
Where any of the following circumstances exists, the matter shall be submitted to the Chief Prosecutor for a decision not to file the case:  
(1) any circumstance specified in Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law exists;  
(2) it is deemed that there is no criminal fact; or  
(3) the facts or evidence do not yet meet the filing conditions.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条  对于其他机关或者本院其他办案部门移送的案件线索，决定不予立案的，负责侦查的部门应当制作不立案通知书，写明案由和案件来源、决定不立案的原因和法律依据，自作出不立案决定之日起十日以内送达移送案件线索的机关或者部门。

**Article 172** Where a decision is made not to file a case regarding a case clue transferred by another authority or another case-handling department of the same Procuratorate, the department responsible for investigation shall prepare a notice of non-filing, specifying the case subject and source, the reasons for the decision not to file, and the legal basis, and serve it on the authority or department that transferred the case clue within ten days from the date of the decision not to file.

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条  对于控告和实名举报，决定不予立案的，应当制作不立案通知书，写明案由和案件来源、决定不立案的原因和法律依据，由负责侦查的部门在十五日以内送达控告人、举报人，同时告知本院负责控告申诉检察的部门。  
控告人如果不服，可以在收到不立案通知书后十日以内向上一级人民检察院申请复议。不立案的复议，由上一级人民检察院负责侦查的部门审查办理。  
人民检察院认为被控告人、被举报人的行为未构成犯罪，决定不予立案，但需要追究其党纪、政纪、违法责任的，应当移送有管辖权的主管机关处理。

**Article 173** Where a decision is made not to file a case regarding a complaint or a real-name report, the department responsible for investigation shall prepare a notice of non-filing, specifying the case subject and source, the reasons for the decision not to file, and the legal basis, and serve it on the complainant or reporter within fifteen days, while simultaneously informing the department of the same Procuratorate responsible for complaint and petition prosecution.  
If the complainant refuses to accept the decision, it may apply for reconsideration to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level within ten days of receiving the notice of non-filing. The reconsideration of the non-filing shall be reviewed and handled by the department responsible for investigation of the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level.  
If the People's Procuratorate deems that the conduct of the accused person or reported person does not constitute a crime and decides not to file the case, but their Party discipline, administrative discipline, or legal liability needs to be investigated, it shall transfer the matter to the competent authority with jurisdiction for handling.

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条  错告对被控告人、被举报人造成不良影响的，人民检察院应当自作出不立案决定之日起一个月以内向其所在单位或者有关部门通报调查核实的结论，澄清事实。  
属于诬告陷害的，应当移送有关机关处理。

**Article 174** If a mistaken report has caused adverse effects on the accused person or reported person, the People's Procuratorate shall, within one month from the date of the decision not to file the case, notify their unit or relevant department of the conclusions of the investigation and verification, and clarify the facts.  
If it constitutes false accusation, the matter shall be transferred to the relevant authority for handling.

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条  人民检察院决定对人民代表大会代表立案，应当按照[本规则第一百四十八条](#art-148)、第一百四十九条规定的程序向该代表所属的人民代表大会主席团或者常务委员会进行通报。

**Article 175** Where a People's Procuratorate decides to file a case against a deputy to a People's Congress, it shall, in accordance with the procedures prescribed in Articles 148 and 149 of these Rules, notify the presidium or the standing committee of the People's Congress to which the deputy belongs.

### 第九章 侦 查

#### 第一节 一般规定

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，应当全面、客观地收集、调取犯罪嫌疑人有罪或者无罪、罪轻或者罪重的证据材料，并依法进行审查、核实。办案过程中必须重证据，重调查研究，不轻信口供。严禁刑讯逼供和以威胁、引诱、欺骗以及其他非法方法收集证据，不得强迫任何人证实自己有罪。

**Article 176** When handling cases directly accepted for investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall comprehensively and objectively collect and obtain evidence materials that are incriminating or exculpatory, or that show a lighter or heavier crime, and shall examine and verify them according to law. In the course of handling a case, the emphasis must be on evidence and investigation and research, and oral statements shall not be easily believed. The use of torture to extort confessions and the collection of evidence by threats, inducement, deception, or other illegal methods are strictly prohibited. No person shall be compelled to prove his or her own guilt.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，应当保障犯罪嫌疑人和其他诉讼参与人依法享有的辩护权和其他各项诉讼权利。

**Article 177** When handling cases directly accepted for investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall ensure that the criminal suspect and other participants in the proceedings enjoy the right to defense and other litigation rights in accordance with law.

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，应当严格依照刑事诉讼法规定的程序，严格遵守刑事案件办案期限的规定，依法提请批准逮捕、移送起诉、不起诉或者撤销案件。  
对犯罪嫌疑人采取强制措施，应当经检察长批准。

**Article 178** When handling cases directly accepted for investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall strictly follow the procedures prescribed in the Criminal Procedure Law, strictly observe the provisions on the time limits for handling criminal cases, and, according to law, submit requests for approval of arrest, transfer for prosecution, non-prosecution, or case dismissal.  
Compulsory measures against a criminal suspect shall be subject to the approval of the Chief Prosecutor.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，应当对侦查过程中知悉的国家秘密、商业秘密及个人隐私予以保密。

**Article 179** When handling cases directly accepted for investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall maintain confidentiality of State secrets, trade secrets, and personal privacy learned in the course of investigation.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条  办理案件的人民检察院需要派员到本辖区以外进行搜查，调取物证、书证等证据材料，或者查封、扣押财物和文件的，应当持相关法律文书和证明文件等与当地人民检察院联系，当地人民检察院应当予以协助。  
需要到本辖区以外调取证据材料的，必要时，可以向证据所在地的人民检察院发函调取证据。调取证据的函件应当注明具体的取证对象、地址和内容。证据所在地的人民检察院应当在收到函件后一个月以内将取证结果送达办理案件的人民检察院。  
被请求协助的人民检察院有异议的，可以与办理案件的人民检察院进行协商。必要时，报请共同的上级人民检察院决定。

**Article 180** If the People's Procuratorate handling a case needs to send personnel outside its jurisdiction to conduct a search, obtain physical evidence, documentary evidence, or other evidence materials, or to seal up or seize property and documents, it shall hold the relevant legal documents and certification documents and contact the local People's Procuratorate, and the local People's Procuratorate shall provide assistance.  
If it is necessary to obtain evidence materials outside its jurisdiction, when necessary, a letter may be sent to the People's Procuratorate in the location of the evidence to request the evidence. The letter requesting evidence shall specify the specific target, address, and content of the evidence to be obtained. The People's Procuratorate in the location of the evidence shall, within one month of receiving the letter, deliver the evidence results to the People's Procuratorate handling the case.  
If the requested assisting People's Procuratorate has objections, it may consult with the People's Procuratorate handling the case. When necessary, the matter shall be submitted to the common superior People's Procuratorate for a decision.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条  人民检察院对于直接受理案件的侦查，可以适用刑事诉讼法第二编第二章规定的各项侦查措施。  
刑事诉讼法规定进行侦查活动需要制作笔录的，应当制作笔录。必要时，可以对相关活动进行录音、录像。

**Article 181** The People's Procuratorate may apply the various investigative measures prescribed in Chapter II of Part II of the Criminal Procedure Law for the investigation of cases directly accepted for filing.  
Where the Criminal Procedure Law requires that records be made for investigative activities, such records shall be made. When necessary, audio or video recordings of the relevant activities may be made.

#### 第二节 讯问犯罪嫌疑人

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条  讯问犯罪嫌疑人，由检察人员负责进行。讯问时，检察人员或者检察人员和书记员不得少于二人。  
讯问同案的犯罪嫌疑人，应当个别进行。

**Article 182** Interrogation of a criminal suspect shall be conducted by a procuratorial officer. During interrogation, the number of procuratorial officers or procuratorial officers and clerks shall not be less than two.  
Interrogation of criminal suspects in the same case shall be conducted individually.

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条  对于不需要逮捕、拘留的犯罪嫌疑人，可以传唤到犯罪嫌疑人所在市、县内的指定地点或者到他的住处进行讯问。  
传唤犯罪嫌疑人，应当出示传唤证和工作证件，并责令犯罪嫌疑人在传唤证上签名或者盖章，并捺指印。  
犯罪嫌疑人到案后，应当由其在传唤证上填写到案时间。传唤结束时，应当由其在传唤证上填写传唤结束时间。拒绝填写的，应当在传唤证上注明。  
对在现场发现的犯罪嫌疑人，经出示工作证件，可以口头传唤，并将传唤的原因和依据告知被传唤人。在讯问笔录中应当注明犯罪嫌疑人到案时间、到案经过和传唤结束时间。  
[本规则第八十四条](#art-84)第二款的规定适用于传唤犯罪嫌疑人。

**Article 183** For a criminal suspect who does not need to be arrested or detained, the suspect may be summoned to a designated location within the city or county where the suspect is located, or to the suspect's residence for interrogation.  
When summoning a criminal suspect, the summoning warrant and work identification shall be presented, and the criminal suspect shall be ordered to sign or affix a seal and affix a fingerprint on the summoning warrant.  
After the criminal suspect appears, he or she shall fill in the time of appearance on the summoning warrant. At the conclusion of the summons, he or she shall fill in the time of conclusion of the summons on the summons warrant. If the suspect refuses to fill in, a notation shall be made on the summoning warrant.  
For a criminal suspect found at the scene, upon presentation of work identification, an oral summons may be made, and the reason and basis for the summons shall be informed to the summoned person.
