In the interrogation record, the time of appearance, the process of appearance, and the time of conclusion of the summons shall be noted.  
The provisions of paragraph 2 of Article 84 of these Rules apply to the summons of a criminal suspect.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条  传唤犯罪嫌疑人时，其家属在场的，应当当场将传唤的原因和处所口头告知其家属，并在讯问笔录中注明。其家属不在场的，应当及时将传唤的原因和处所通知被传唤人家属。无法通知的，应当在讯问笔录中注明。

**Article 184** When summoning a criminal suspect, if the suspect's family members are present, the reasons and location of the summons shall be orally informed to the family members on the spot, and a notation shall be made in the interrogation record. If the family members are not present, the reasons and location of the summons shall be promptly notified to the family members of the summoned person. If notification is impossible, a notation shall be made in the interrogation record.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条  传唤持续的时间不得超过十二小时。案情特别重大、复杂，需要采取拘留、逮捕措施的，传唤持续的时间不得超过二十四小时。两次传唤间隔的时间一般不得少于十二小时，不得以连续传唤的方式变相拘禁犯罪嫌疑人。  
传唤犯罪嫌疑人，应当保证犯罪嫌疑人的饮食和必要的休息时间。

**Article 185** The duration of a summons shall not exceed twelve hours. Where the case is particularly serious or complex and measures of detention or arrest are necessary, the duration of the summons shall not exceed twenty-four hours. The interval between two summonses shall generally not be less than twelve hours, and the criminal suspect shall not be detained in disguised form by way of continuous summons.  
When summoning a criminal suspect, the suspect's meals and necessary rest time shall be ensured.

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条  犯罪嫌疑人被送交看守所羁押后，检察人员对其进行讯问，应当填写提讯、提解证，在看守所讯问室进行。  
因辨认、鉴定、侦查实验或者追缴犯罪有关财物的需要，经检察长批准，可以提押犯罪嫌疑人出所，并应当由两名以上司法警察押解。不得以讯问为目的将犯罪嫌疑人提押出所进行讯问。

**Article 186** After a criminal suspect is delivered to a detention house for custody, procuratorial officers interrogating him or her shall fill in a certificate for bringing out for interrogation and extraction, and conduct the interrogation in the interrogation room of the detention house.  
For the need of identification, expert evaluation, investigative experiment, or recovery of property related to the crime, with the approval of the Chief Prosecutor, a criminal suspect may be taken out of the detention house, and shall be escorted by two or more judicial police. A criminal suspect shall not be taken out of the detention house for the purpose of interrogation.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条  讯问犯罪嫌疑人一般按照下列顺序进行：  
（一）核实犯罪嫌疑人的基本情况，包括姓名、出生年月日、户籍地、公民身份号码、民族、职业、文化程度、工作单位及职务、住所、家庭情况、社会经历、是否属于人大代表、政协委员等；  
（二）告知犯罪嫌疑人在侦查阶段的诉讼权利，有权自行辩护或者委托律师辩护，告知其如实供述自己罪行可以依法从宽处理和认罪认罚的法律规定；  
（三）讯问犯罪嫌疑人是否有犯罪行为，让他陈述有罪的事实或者无罪的辩解，应当允许其连贯陈述。  
犯罪嫌疑人对检察人员的提问，应当如实回答。但是对与本案无关的问题，有拒绝回答的权利。  
讯问犯罪嫌疑人时，应当告知犯罪嫌疑人将对讯问进行全程同步录音、录像。告知情况应当在录音、录像中予以反映，并记明笔录。  
讯问时，对犯罪嫌疑人提出的辩解要认真查核。严禁刑讯逼供和以威胁、引诱、欺骗以及其他非法的方法获取供述。

**Article 187** Interrogation of a criminal suspect shall generally proceed in the following order:  
(1) Verify the basic information of the criminal suspect, including name, date of birth, place of household registration, citizen identity number, ethnicity, occupation, education level, work unit and position, residence, family situation, social experience, and whether the suspect is a deputy to a People's Congress or a member of a Political Consultative Conference, etc.;  
(2) Inform the criminal suspect of the litigation rights during the investigation stage, including the right to self-defense or to entrust a lawyer for defense, and inform the suspect of the legal provisions that truthful confession of his or her crime may lead to lenient treatment according to law and the legal provisions on admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment;  
(3) Ask the criminal suspect whether he or she has committed a crime, allow the suspect to state the facts of guilt or plead innocence, and allow continuous statements.  
The criminal suspect shall answer the questions of the procuratorial officer truthfully. However, the suspect has the right to refuse to answer questions that are irrelevant to the case.  
When interrogating a criminal suspect, the suspect shall be informed that the entire interrogation will be subject to simultaneous audio and video recording. The notification shall be reflected in the recording and noted in the record.  
During interrogation, the suspect's defense shall be seriously examined and verified. The use of torture to extort confessions and obtaining confessions by threats, inducement, deception, or other illegal methods are strictly prohibited.

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条  讯问犯罪嫌疑人，应当制作讯问笔录。讯问笔录应当忠实于原话，字迹清楚，详细具体，并交犯罪嫌疑人核对。犯罪嫌疑人没有阅读能力的，应当向他宣读。如果记载有遗漏或者差错，应当补充或者改正。犯罪嫌疑人认为讯问笔录没有错误的，由其在笔录上逐页签名或者盖章，并捺指印，在末页写明“以上笔录我看过（向我宣读过），和我说的相符”，同时签名或者盖章，并捺指印，注明日期。如果犯罪嫌疑人拒绝签名、盖章、捺指印的，应当在笔录上注明。讯问的检察人员、书记员也应当在笔录上签名。

**Article 188** When interrogating a criminal suspect, an interrogation record shall be made. The interrogation record shall faithfully reproduce the original statements, be legible, detailed, and specific, and shall be handed to the criminal suspect for verification. If the criminal suspect lacks the ability to read, the record shall be read to him or her. If there are omissions or errors in the record, supplements or corrections shall be made. If the criminal suspect considers the interrogation record to be correct, the suspect shall sign or affix a seal and affix a fingerprint on each page, and write on the last page: 'I have read (or have had read to me) the above record and confirm that it corresponds with what I said,' and shall also sign or affix a seal and affix a fingerprint, and note the date. If the criminal suspect refuses to sign, affix a seal, or affix a fingerprint, a notation shall be made in the record. The interrogating procuratorial officer and clerk shall also sign the record.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条  犯罪嫌疑人请求自行书写供述的，检察人员应当准许。必要时，检察人员也可以要求犯罪嫌疑人亲笔书写供述。犯罪嫌疑人应当在亲笔供述的末页签名或者盖章，并捺指印，注明书写日期。检察人员收到后，应当在首页右上方写明“于某年某月某日收到”，并签名。

**Article 189** If a criminal suspect requests to write a confession in his or her own handwriting, the procuratorial officer shall allow it. When necessary, the procuratorial officer may also require the criminal suspect to write a confession in his or her own handwriting. The criminal suspect shall sign or affix a seal and affix a fingerprint on the last page of the handwritten confession, and note the date of writing. After receiving the confession, the procuratorial officer shall write on the upper right corner of the first page: 'Received on [year, month, day]' and sign.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，应当在每次讯问犯罪嫌疑人时，对讯问过程实行全程录音、录像，并在讯问笔录中注明。

**Article 190** When handling cases directly accepted for investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall, during each interrogation of a criminal suspect, conduct full audio and video recording of the interrogation process and note this in the interrogation record.

#### 第三节 询问证人、被害人

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条  人民检察院在侦查过程中，应当及时询问证人，并且告知证人履行作证的权利和义务。  
人民检察院应当保证一切与案件有关或者了解案情的公民有客观充分地提供证据的条件，并为他们保守秘密。除特殊情况外，人民检察院可以吸收他们协助调查。

**Article 191** In the process of investigation, a People's Procuratorate shall promptly question witnesses and inform them of their rights and obligations to testify.  
A People's Procuratorate shall ensure that all citizens relevant to or knowledgeable about the case have the conditions to objectively and fully provide evidence, and shall maintain confidentiality for them. Except in special circumstances, a People's Procuratorate may involve them to assist in the investigation.

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条  询问证人，应当由检察人员负责进行。询问时，检察人员或者检察人员和书记员不得少于二人。

**Article 192** Questioning of witnesses shall be conducted by procuratorial personnel. When questioning, there shall be no fewer than two procuratorial personnel, or one procuratorial personnel and one clerk.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条  询问证人，可以在现场进行，也可以到证人所在单位、住处或者证人提出的地点进行。必要时，也可以通知证人到人民检察院提供证言。到证人提出的地点进行询问的，应当在笔录中记明。  
询问证人应当个别进行。  
在现场询问证人，应当出示工作证件。到证人所在单位、住处或者证人提出的地点询问证人，应当出示人民检察院的证明文件。

**Article 193** Questioning of a witness may be conducted at the scene, at the witness's work unit, residence, or at a place proposed by the witness. When necessary, the witness may also be summoned to the People's Procuratorate to provide testimony. If the questioning is conducted at the place proposed by the witness, this shall be noted in the record.  
Witnesses shall be questioned individually.  
When questioning a witness at the scene, the examiner shall present their work identification. When questioning a witness at the witness's work unit, residence, or at a place proposed by the witness, the examiner shall present the certification documents of the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条  询问证人，应当问明证人的基本情况以及与当事人的关系，并且告知证人应当如实提供证据、证言和故意作伪证或者隐匿罪证应当承担的法律责任，但是不得向证人泄露案情，不得采用拘禁、暴力、威胁、引诱、欺骗以及其他非法方法获取证言。  
询问重大或者有社会影响的案件的重要证人，应当对询问过程实行全程录音、录像，并在询问笔录中注明。

**Article 194** When questioning a witness, the examiner shall ascertain the witness's basic information and relationship with the parties, and shall inform the witness of the legal liability for failing to truthfully provide evidence and testimony, and for intentionally giving false testimony or concealing criminal evidence. However, the examiner shall not disclose the case to the witness, and shall not obtain testimony through detention, violence, threats, inducement, deception, or other illegal methods.  
When questioning an important witness in a major case or a case with social impact, the entire questioning process shall be audio-recorded and video-recorded, and this shall be noted in the questioning record.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条  询问被害人，适用询问证人的规定。

**Article 195** The provisions on questioning witnesses shall apply to questioning victims.

#### 第四节 勘验、检查

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条  检察人员对于与犯罪有关的场所、物品、人身、尸体应当进行勘验或者检查。必要时，可以指派检察技术人员或者聘请其他具有专门知识的人，在检察人员的主持下进行勘验、检查。

**Article 196** Procuratorial personnel shall conduct inspection or examination of crime-related sites, objects, persons, and corpses. When necessary, they may assign procuratorial technical personnel or engage other persons with specialized knowledge to conduct the inspection or examination under the supervision of procuratorial personnel.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条  勘验时，人民检察院应当邀请两名与案件无关的见证人在场。  
勘查现场，应当拍摄现场照片。勘查的情况应当写明笔录并制作现场图，由参加勘查的人和见证人签名。勘查重大案件的现场，应当录像。

**Article 197** When conducting an inspection, a People's Procuratorate shall invite two witnesses unrelated to the case to be present.  
When inspecting the scene, photographs of the scene shall be taken. The circumstances of the inspection shall be recorded in a written record and a scene diagram shall be prepared, signed by the participants in the inspection and the witnesses. When inspecting the scene of a major case, video recording shall be conducted.

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条  人民检察院解剖死因不明的尸体，应当通知死者家属到场，并让其在解剖通知书上签名或者盖章。  
死者家属无正当理由拒不到场或者拒绝签名、盖章的，不影响解剖的进行，但是应当在解剖通知书上记明。对于身份不明的尸体，无法通知死者家属的，应当记明笔录。

**Article 198** When a People's Procuratorate performs an autopsy on a corpse of undetermined cause of death, it shall notify the family members of the deceased to be present and have them sign or affix their seals on the autopsy notice.  
If the family members of the deceased refuse to be present without justifiable reasons or refuse to sign or affix their seals, this shall not affect the conduct of the autopsy, but this shall be noted on the autopsy notice. For corpses of unidentified identity, where it is impossible to notify the family members of the deceased, this shall be recorded in the record.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条  为了确定被害人、犯罪嫌疑人的某些特征、伤害情况或者生理状态，人民检察院可以对其人身进行检查，可以提取指纹信息，采集血液、尿液等生物样本。  
必要时，可以指派、聘请法医或者医师进行人身检查。采集血液等生物样本应当由医师进行。  
犯罪嫌疑人如果拒绝检查，检察人员认为必要时可以强制检查。  
检查妇女的身体，应当由女工作人员或者医师进行。  
人身检查不得采用损害被检查人生命、健康或者贬低其名誉、人格的方法。在人身检查过程中知悉的被检查人的个人隐私，检察人员应当予以保密。

**Article 199** For the purpose of determining certain characteristics, injury conditions, or physiological states of a victim or criminal suspect, a People's Procuratorate may conduct a physical examination of them, may collect fingerprint information, and collect biological samples such as blood and urine.  
When necessary, forensic doctors or physicians may be assigned or engaged to conduct the physical examination. Collection of biological samples such as blood shall be performed by physicians.  
If a criminal suspect refuses the examination, the procuratorial personnel may conduct a forced examination when deemed necessary.  
When examining the body of a female, it shall be conducted by female staff or a physician.  
Physical examination shall not employ methods that harm the life or health of the person examined or degrade their reputation or dignity. Procuratorial personnel shall keep confidential any personal privacy of the person examined that becomes known during the physical examination.

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条  为了查明案情，必要时经检察长批准，可以进行侦查实验。  
侦查实验，禁止一切足以造成危险、侮辱人格或者有伤风化的行为。

**Article 200** In order to ascertain the facts of a case, an investigative experiment may be conducted when necessary with the approval of the Chief Procurator.  
During an investigative experiment, all acts that may cause danger, insult human dignity, or are contrary to public decency are prohibited.

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条  侦查实验，必要时可以聘请有关专业人员参加，也可以要求犯罪嫌疑人、被害人、证人参加。

**Article 201** When necessary, an investigative experiment may involve relevant professionals, and may also require the participation of the criminal suspect, victim, or witnesses.

#### 第五节 搜 查

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条  人民检察院有权要求有关单位和个人，交出能够证明犯罪嫌疑人有罪或者无罪以及犯罪情节轻重的证据。

**Article 202** A People's Procuratorate has the authority to require relevant entities and individuals to hand over evidence that can prove the guilt or innocence of a criminal suspect, as well as evidence of the seriousness or leniency of the criminal circumstances.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条  为了收集犯罪证据，查获犯罪人，经检察长批准，检察人员可以对犯罪嫌疑人以及可能隐藏罪犯或者犯罪证据的人的身体、物品、住处、工作地点和其他有关的地方进行搜查。

**Article 203** For the purpose of collecting criminal evidence and apprehending the offender, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, procuratorial personnel may conduct a search of the body, articles, residence, workplace, and other relevant places of the criminal suspect and of persons who may be hiding the criminal or criminal evidence.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条  搜查应当在检察人员的主持下进行，可以有司法警察参加。必要时，可以指派检察技术人员参加或者邀请当地公安机关、有关单位协助进行。  
执行搜查的人员不得少于二人。

**Article 204** A search shall be conducted under the direction of procuratorial personnel, and judicial police may participate. When necessary, procuratorial technical personnel may be assigned, or the local public security organ or relevant units may be invited to assist.  
The number of persons executing the search shall not be fewer than two.

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条  搜查时，应当向被搜查人或者他的家属出示搜查证。  
在执行逮捕、拘留的时候，遇有下列紧急情况之一，不另用搜查证也可以进行搜查：  
（一）可能随身携带凶器的；  
（二）可能隐藏爆炸、剧毒等危险物品的；  
（三）可能隐匿、毁弃、转移犯罪证据的；  
（四）可能隐匿其他犯罪嫌疑人的；  
（五）其他紧急情况。  
搜查结束后，搜查人员应当在二十四小时以内补办有关手续。

**Article 205** When conducting a search, a search warrant shall be presented to the person to be searched or their family members.  
When executing an arrest or detention, if any of the following emergency situations arise, a search may be conducted without a separate search warrant:  
(1) The person may be carrying a weapon;  
(2) The person may be hiding dangerous articles such as explosives or highly toxic substances;  
(3) The person may be concealing, destroying, or transferring criminal evidence;  
(4) The person may be hiding another criminal suspect;  
(5) Other emergency situations.  
After the search is completed, the search personnel shall complete the relevant formalities within twenty-four hours.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条  搜查时，应当有被搜查人或者其家属、邻居或者其他见证人在场，并且对被搜查人或者其家属说明阻碍搜查、妨碍公务应负的法律责任。  
搜查妇女的身体，应当由女工作人员进行。

**Article 206** When conducting a search, the person to be searched or their family members, neighbors, or other witnesses shall be present, and the legal liability for obstructing the search or interfering with official duties shall be explained to the person to be searched or their family members.  
When searching the body of a female, it shall be conducted by female staff.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条  搜查时，如果遇到阻碍，可以强制进行搜查。对以暴力、威胁方法阻碍搜查的，应当予以制止，或者由司法警察将其带离现场。阻碍搜查构成犯罪的，应当依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 207** If obstruction is encountered during a search, a forced search may be conducted. If the obstruction is carried out by means of violence or threats, it shall be stopped, or the obstructing person shall be removed from the scene by judicial police. If the obstruction of the search constitutes a crime, criminal liability shall be investigated according to law.

#### 第六节 调取、查封、扣押、查询、冻结

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条  检察人员可以凭人民检察院的证明文件，向有关单位和个人调取能够证明犯罪嫌疑人有罪或者无罪以及犯罪情节轻重的证据材料，并且可以根据需要拍照、录像、复印和复制。

**Article 208** Procuratorial personnel may, with the certification documents of the People's Procuratorate, retrieve evidentiary materials that can prove the guilt or innocence of a criminal suspect as well as the seriousness or leniency of the criminal circumstances from relevant entities and individuals, and may, as needed, take photographs, make video recordings, copies, and reproductions.

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条  调取物证应当调取原物。原物不便搬运、保存，或者依法应当返还被害人，或者因保密工作需要不能调取原物的，可以将原物封存，并拍照、录像。对原物拍照或者录像应当足以反映原物的外形、内容。  
调取书证、视听资料应当调取原件。取得原件确有困难或者因保密需要不能调取原件的，可以调取副本或者复制件。  
调取书证、视听资料的副本、复制件和物证的照片、录像的，应当书面记明不能调取原件、原物的原因，制作过程和原件、原物存放地点，并由制作人员和原书证、视听资料、物证持有人签名或者盖章。

**Article 209** When retrieving physical evidence, the original object shall be retrieved. If the original object is inconvenient to transport or preserve, or shall be returned to the victim according to law, or cannot be retrieved due to confidentiality requirements, the original object may be sealed and preserved, with photographs and video recordings taken. The photographs or video recordings of the original object shall sufficiently reflect its appearance and content.  
When retrieving documentary evidence or audio-visual materials, the original shall be retrieved. If it is truly difficult to obtain the original or the original cannot be retrieved due to confidentiality needs, a copy or reproduction may be retrieved.  
When retrieving copies or reproductions of documentary evidence or audio-visual materials, or photographs or video recordings of physical evidence, the reasons for being unable to retrieve the original document or object, the production process, and the location where the original document or object is stored shall be recorded in writing, and signed or sealed by the producer and the holder of the original documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, or physical evidence.

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条  在侦查活动中发现的可以证明犯罪嫌疑人有罪、无罪或者犯罪情节轻重的各种财物和文件，应当查封或者扣押；与案件无关的，不得查封或者扣押。查封或者扣押应当经检察长批准。  
不能立即查明是否与案件有关的可疑的财物和文件，也可以查封或者扣押，但应当及时审查。经查明确实与案件无关的，应当在三日以内解除查封或者予以退还。  
持有人拒绝交出应当查封、扣押的财物和文件的，可以强制查封、扣押。  
对于犯罪嫌疑人、被告人到案时随身携带的物品需要扣押的，可以依照前款规定办理。对于与案件无关的个人用品，应当逐件登记，并随案移交或者退还其家属。

**Article 210** Any property or documents discovered during investigative activities that may prove the guilt, innocence, or the seriousness or leniency of criminal circumstances of a criminal suspect shall be sealed or seized; property or documents unrelated to the case shall not be sealed or seized. Sealing or seizure shall be approved by the Chief Procurator.  
Suspicious property or documents that cannot be immediately determined to be related or unrelated to the case may also be sealed or seized, but shall be promptly reviewed. If it is determined upon examination that they are indeed unrelated to the case, the seal shall be removed or the items shall be returned within three days.  
If the holder refuses to hand over the property or documents that shall be sealed or seized, compulsory sealing or seizure may be conducted.  
For items carried by a criminal suspect or defendant at the time of their arrival in the case that need to be seized, the provisions of the preceding paragraph may be followed. For personal items unrelated to the case, they shall be registered item by item and transferred with the case or returned to the family members.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条  对犯罪嫌疑人使用违法所得与合法收入共同购置的不可分割的财产，可以先行查封、扣押、冻结。对无法分割退还的财产，应当在结案后予以拍卖、变卖，对不属于违法所得的部分予以退还。

**Article 211** Where a criminal suspect uses illegal gains and lawful income to jointly purchase indivisible property, such property may be sealed up, seized, or frozen in advance. For property that cannot be divided and returned, it shall be auctioned or sold after the case is concluded, and the portion that does not belong to illegal gains shall be returned.

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条  人民检察院根据侦查犯罪的需要，可以依照规定查询、冻结犯罪嫌疑人的存款、汇款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产，并可以要求有关单位和个人配合。  
查询、冻结前款规定的财产，应当制作查询、冻结财产通知书，通知银行或者其他金融机构、邮政部门执行。冻结财产的，应当经检察长批准。

**Article 212** A People's Procuratorate may, as needed for investigating crimes, inquire into and freeze the criminal suspect's deposits, remittances, bonds, stocks, fund shares, and other properties in accordance with the provisions, and may request relevant entities and individuals to cooperate.  
To inquire into or freeze the properties specified in the preceding paragraph, a notice of inquiry into or freezing of property shall be prepared and served on banks or other financial institutions or postal departments for enforcement. Where property is frozen, the approval of the chief procurator shall be obtained.

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条  犯罪嫌疑人的存款、汇款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产已冻结的，人民检察院不得重复冻结，可以轮候冻结。人民检察院应当要求有关银行或者其他金融机构、邮政部门在解除冻结或者作出处理前通知人民检察院。

**Article 213** Where the criminal suspect's deposits, remittances, bonds, stocks, fund shares, and other properties have already been frozen, the People's Procuratorate shall not freeze them again but may place a sequential freeze. The People's Procuratorate shall request the relevant banks or other financial institutions or postal departments to notify the People's Procuratorate before lifting the freeze or taking action.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条  扣押、冻结债券、股票、基金份额等财产，应当书面告知当事人或者其法定代理人、委托代理人有权申请出售。  
对于被扣押、冻结的债券、股票、基金份额等财产，在扣押、冻结期间权利人申请出售，经审查认为不损害国家利益、被害人利益，不影响诉讼正常进行的，以及扣押、冻结的汇票、本票、支票的有效期即将届满的，经检察长批准，可以在案件办结前依法出售或者变现，所得价款由人民检察院指定的银行账户保管，并及时告知当事人或者其近亲属。

**Article 214** When seizing or freezing bonds, stocks, fund shares, or other properties, the party or his legal representative or authorized agent shall be notified in writing that he has the right to apply for sale.  
Where, for the seized or frozen bonds, stocks, fund shares, or other properties, during the period of seizure or freezing, the right holder applies for sale, and upon examination, it is determined that the sale will not harm the interests of the State or the victim, nor affect the normal conduct of the proceedings, or where the validity period of the seized or frozen drafts, promissory notes, or checks is about to expire, with the approval of the chief procurator, the properties may be sold or liquidated according to law before the case is concluded. The proceeds shall be kept in a bank account designated by the People's Procuratorate, and the party or his close relative shall be promptly notified.

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条  对于冻结的存款、汇款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产，经查明确实与案件无关的，应当在三日以内解除冻结，并通知财产所有人。

**Article 215** Where the frozen deposits, remittances, bonds, stocks, fund shares, or other properties are verified to be irrelevant to the case, the freeze shall be lifted within three days, and the property owner shall be notified.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条  查询、冻结与案件有关的单位的存款、汇款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产的办法适用[本规则第二百一十二条](#art-212)至第二百一十五条的规定。

**Article 216** The provisions of Articles 212 through 215 of these Rules shall apply to the measures for inquiring into and freezing the deposits, remittances, bonds, stocks, fund shares, and other properties of entities involved in the case.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条  对于扣押的款项和物品，应当在三日以内将款项存入唯一合规账户，将物品送负责案件管理的部门保管。法律或者有关规定另有规定的除外。  
对于查封、扣押在人民检察院的物品、文件、邮件、电报，人民检察院应当妥善保管。经查明确实与案件无关的，应当在三日以内作出解除或者退还决定，并通知有关单位、当事人办理相关手续。

**Article 217** For the seized sums and articles, the sums shall be deposited into the sole compliant account within three days, and the articles shall be delivered to the department responsible for case management for custody, unless otherwise provided by law or relevant provisions.  
For articles, documents, mails, or telegrams sealed up or seized by the People's Procuratorate, the People's Procuratorate shall keep them in proper custody. Where they are verified to be irrelevant to the case, a decision to lift the seizure or return them shall be made within three days, and the relevant entities and parties shall be notified to go through the relevant procedures.

#### 第七节 鉴 定

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条  人民检察院为了查明案情，解决案件中某些专门性的问题，可以进行鉴定。  
鉴定由人民检察院有鉴定资格的人员进行。必要时，也可以聘请其他有鉴定资格的人员进行，但是应当征得鉴定人所在单位同意。

**Article 218** To ascertain the facts of a case and resolve certain specialized issues in the case, a People's Procuratorate may conduct an expert evaluation.  
The expert evaluation shall be conducted by personnel of the People's Procuratorate who are qualified for such evaluation. If necessary, other qualified personnel may also be engaged to conduct the evaluation, provided that the consent of the unit to which the evaluator belongs is obtained.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条  人民检察院应当为鉴定人提供必要条件，及时向鉴定人送交有关检材和对比样本等原始材料，介绍与鉴定有关的情况，并明确提出要求鉴定解决的问题，但是不得暗示或者强迫鉴定人作出某种鉴定意见。

**Article 219** The People's Procuratorate shall provide the evaluator with necessary conditions, timely submit relevant inspection materials and comparison samples and other original materials to the evaluator, introduce the circumstances related to the evaluation, and clearly state the issues required to be resolved through the evaluation, but shall not suggest or compel the evaluator to render a specific expert opinion.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条  对于鉴定意见，检察人员应当进行审查，必要时可以进行补充鉴定或者重新鉴定。重新鉴定的，应当另行指派或者聘请鉴定人。

**Article 220** The procuratorial personnel shall review the expert opinion, and if necessary, may conduct a supplementary evaluation or a new evaluation. Where a new evaluation is conducted, a different evaluator shall be designated or engaged.

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条  用作证据的鉴定意见，人民检察院办案部门应当告知犯罪嫌疑人、被害人；被害人死亡或者没有诉讼行为能力的，应当告知其法定代理人、近亲属或诉讼代理人。  
犯罪嫌疑人、被害人或被害人的法定代理人、近亲属、诉讼代理人提出申请，可以补充鉴定或者重新鉴定，鉴定费用由请求方承担。但原鉴定违反法定程序的，由人民检察院承担。  
犯罪嫌疑人的辩护人或者近亲属以犯罪嫌疑人有患精神病可能而申请对犯罪嫌疑人进行鉴定的，鉴定费用由申请方承担。

**Article 221** Where an expert opinion is used as evidence, the case-handling department of the People's Procuratorate shall inform the criminal suspect and the victim; if the victim is deceased or lacks capacity to engage in litigation, the victim's legal representative, close relative, or litigation agent shall be informed.  
If the criminal suspect, the victim, or the victim's legal representative, close relative, or litigation agent submits an application, a supplementary evaluation or a new evaluation may be conducted, and the evaluation fees shall be borne by the requesting party. However, if the original evaluation violated statutory procedures, the fees shall be borne by the People's Procuratorate.  
Where the criminal suspect's defender or close relative applies for an evaluation of the criminal suspect on the grounds that the criminal suspect may be mentally ill, the evaluation fees shall be borne by the applying party.

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条  对犯罪嫌疑人作精神病鉴定的期间不计入羁押期限和办案期限。

**Article 222** The period for conducting a psychiatric evaluation of a criminal suspect shall not be counted in the custody period or the case-handling period.

#### 第八节 辨 认

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条  为了查明案情，必要时，检察人员可以让被害人、证人和犯罪嫌疑人对与犯罪有关的物品、文件、尸体或场所进行辨认；也可以让被害人、证人对犯罪嫌疑人进行辨认，或者让犯罪嫌疑人对其他犯罪嫌疑人进行辨认。

**Article 223** To ascertain the facts of a case, if necessary, procuratorial personnel may have victims, witnesses, and criminal suspects identify objects, documents, corpses, or places related to the crime; they may also have victims or witnesses identify a criminal suspect, or have a criminal suspect identify other criminal suspects.

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条  辨认应当在检察人员的主持下进行，执行辨认的人员不得少于二人。在辨认前，应当向辨认人详细询问被辨认对象的具体特征，避免辨认人见到被辨认对象，并应当告知辨认人有意作虚假辨认应负的法律责任。

**Article 224** Identification shall be conducted under the auspices of procuratorial personnel, and the number of personnel carrying out the identification shall not be less than two. Before the identification, the identifier shall be questioned in detail about the specific characteristics of the subject to be identified, and the identifier shall be prevented from seeing the subject; the identifier shall also be informed of the legal liability he shall bear for intentionally making a false identification.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条  几名辨认人对同一被辨认对象进行辨认时，应当由每名辨认人单独进行。必要时，可以有见证人在场。

**Article 225** Where several identifiers identify the same subject, each identifier shall do so separately. If necessary, a witness may be present.

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条  辨认时，应当将辨认对象混杂在其他对象中。不得在辨认前向辨认人展示辨认对象及其影像资料，不得给辨认人任何暗示。  
辨认犯罪嫌疑人时，被辨认的人数不得少于七人，照片不得少于十张。  
辨认物品时，同类物品不得少于五件，照片不得少于五张。  
对犯罪嫌疑人的辨认，辨认人不愿公开进行时，可以在不暴露辨认人的情况下进行，并应当为其保守秘密。

**Article 226** When conducting an identification, the subject to be identified shall be mixed with other subjects. The identifier shall not be shown the subject or its image before the identification, and no hints shall be given to the identifier.  
When identifying a criminal suspect, the number of persons to be identified shall not be fewer than seven, and the number of photographs shall not be fewer than ten.  
When identifying an object, the number of similar objects shall not be fewer than five, and the number of photographs shall not be fewer than five.  
Where the identifier does not wish to conduct the identification of a criminal suspect publicly, the identification may be conducted without revealing the identity of the identifier, and the identifier's identity shall be kept confidential.

#### 第九节 技术侦查措施

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条  人民检察院在立案后，对于利用职权实施的严重侵犯公民人身权利的重大犯罪案件，经过严格的批准手续，可以采取技术侦查措施，交有关机关执行。

**Article 227** After filing a case, a People's Procuratorate may, for a serious criminal case involving grave infringement of citizens' personal rights committed by abusing functions or powers, take technical investigation measures subject to strict approval procedures, and assign the measures to the relevant organ for execution.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，需要追捕被通缉或者决定逮捕的在逃犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的，经过批准，可以采取追捕所必需的技术侦查措施，不受[本规则第二百二十七条](#art-227)规定的案件范围的限制。

**Article 228** Where, in handling a case directly accepted for investigation, a People's Procuratorate needs to pursue and arrest a wanted criminal suspect or defendant against whom an arrest decision is made, it may, upon approval, take technical investigation measures necessary for pursuit and arrest, without being limited by the scope of cases specified in Article 227 of these Rules.

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条  人民检察院采取技术侦查措施应当根据侦查犯罪的需要，确定采取技术侦查措施的种类和适用对象，按照有关规定报请批准。批准决定自签发之日起三个月以内有效。对于不需要继续采取技术侦查措施的，应当及时解除；对于复杂、疑难案件，期限届满仍有必要继续采取技术侦查措施的，应当在期限届满前十日以内制作呈请延长技术侦查措施期限报告书，写明延长的期限及理由，经过原批准机关批准，有效期可以延长，每次不得超过三个月。  
采取技术侦查措施收集的材料作为证据使用的，批准采取技术侦查措施的法律文书应当附卷，辩护律师可以依法查阅、摘抄、复制。

**Article 229** When taking technical investigation measures, a People's Procuratorate shall determine the types and applicable subjects of such measures based on the needs of criminal investigation, and shall report for approval in accordance with relevant provisions. The approval decision shall be valid for three months from the date of issuance. Where the technical investigation measures are no longer necessary, they shall be promptly lifted; for complex or difficult cases where it remains necessary to continue the technical investigation measures after the expiration of the period, a report requesting an extension of the period for technical investigation measures shall be prepared within ten days before the expiration of the period, specifying the extension period and the reasons. Upon approval by the original approving authority, the validity period may be extended, each time not exceeding three months.  
Where materials collected through technical investigation measures are used as evidence, the legal documents approving the taking of such measures shall be attached to the case file, and defense lawyers may, according to law, consult, excerpt, and copy them.

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条  采取技术侦查措施收集的物证、书证及其他证据材料，检察人员应当制作相应的说明材料，写明获取证据的时间、地点、数量、特征以及采取技术侦查措施的批准机关、种类等，并签名和盖章。  
对于使用技术侦查措施获取的证据材料，如果可能危及特定人员的人身安全、涉及国家秘密或者公开后可能暴露侦查秘密或者严重损害商业秘密、个人隐私的，应当采取不暴露有关人员身份、技术方法等保护措施。必要时，可以建议不在法庭上质证，由审判人员在庭外对证据进行核实。

**Article 230** For physical evidence, documentary evidence, and other evidentiary materials collected through technical investigation measures, the procuratorial personnel shall prepare corresponding explanatory materials, stating the time, place, quantity, characteristics of the evidence obtained, as well as the approving authority and type of the technical investigation measures, and shall sign and affix seals.  
Where the evidentiary materials obtained through technical investigation measures may endanger the personal safety of specific persons, involve State secrets, or, if disclosed, may expose investigation secrets or seriously damage trade secrets or personal privacy, protective measures such as not revealing the identities of relevant persons or technical methods shall be taken. If necessary, it may be proposed that the evidence not be examined in court, and the adjudicators shall verify the evidence outside court.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条  检察人员对采取技术侦查措施过程中知悉的国家秘密、商业秘密和个人隐私，应当保密；对采取技术侦查措施获取的与案件无关的材料，应当及时销毁，并对销毁情况制作记录。  
采取技术侦查措施获取的证据、线索及其他有关材料，只能用于对犯罪的侦查、起诉和审判，不得用于其他用途。

**Article 231** Procuratorial personnel shall keep confidential the state secrets, trade secrets, and personal privacy learned in the process of adopting technical investigation measures; they shall timely destroy the materials irrelevant to the case obtained by technical investigation measures and make a record of the destruction.  
Evidence, leads, and other relevant materials obtained by technical investigation measures may only be used for the investigation, prosecution, and adjudication of crimes, and shall not be used for other purposes.

#### 第十节 通 缉

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，应当逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人在逃，或者已被逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人脱逃的，经检察长批准，可以通缉。

**Article 232** If, when a People's Procuratorate handles a case directly accepted for investigation, the criminal suspect who shall be arrested is at large, or an arrested criminal suspect escapes, the People's Procuratorate may, with the approval of the chief procurator, issue a wanted order.

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条  各级人民检察院需要在本辖区内通缉犯罪嫌疑人的，可以直接决定通缉；需要在本辖区外通缉犯罪嫌疑人的，由有决定权的上级人民检察院决定。

**Article 233** If a People's Procuratorate at any level needs to issue a wanted order for a criminal suspect within its own jurisdiction, it may directly decide to issue the wanted order; if it needs to issue a wanted order for a criminal suspect outside its own jurisdiction, the decision shall be made by the superior People's Procuratorate with the authority to decide.

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条  人民检察院应当将通缉通知书和通缉对象的照片、身份、特征、案情简况送达公安机关，由公安机关发布通缉令，追捕归案。

**Article 234** A People's Procuratorate shall deliver the wanted notice and the photograph, identity, characteristics, and brief case details of the wanted person to the public security organ, and the public security organ shall issue a wanted order to pursue and arrest the wanted person.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条  为防止犯罪嫌疑人等涉案人员逃往境外，需要在边防口岸采取边控措施的，人民检察院应当按照有关规定制作边控对象通知书，商请公安机关办理边控手续。

**Article 235** To prevent criminal suspects or other persons involved in the case from fleeing abroad, if border control measures need to be adopted at border ports, the People's Procuratorate shall, in accordance with relevant provisions, prepare a border control subject notice and consult the public security organ to handle the border control formalities.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条  应当逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人潜逃出境的，可以按照有关规定层报最高人民检察院商请国际刑警组织中国国家中心局，请求有关方面协助，或者通过其他法律规定的途径进行追捕。

**Article 236** If a criminal suspect who shall be arrested flees abroad, the People's Procuratorate may, in accordance with relevant provisions, report layer by layer to the Supreme People's Procuratorate to consult the China National Central Bureau of INTERPOL to request assistance from relevant parties, or pursue the suspect through other channels provided by law.

#### 第十一节 侦查终结

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条  人民检察院经过侦查，认为犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分，依法应当追究刑事责任的，应当写出侦查终结报告，并且制作起诉意见书。  
犯罪嫌疑人自愿认罪的，应当记录在案，随案移送，并在起诉意见书中写明有关情况。  
对于犯罪情节轻微，依照刑法规定不需要判处刑罚或者免除刑罚的案件，应当写出侦查终结报告，并且制作不起诉意见书。  
侦查终结报告和起诉意见书或者不起诉意见书应当报请检察长批准。

**Article 237** If, after investigation, a People's Procuratorate finds that the criminal facts are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and criminal liability shall be investigated according to law, it shall produce an investigation conclusion report and prepare a prosecution opinion.  
If a criminal suspect voluntarily pleads guilty, it shall be recorded in the case file, transferred along with the case, and the relevant circumstances shall be stated in the prosecution opinion.  
For a case where the criminal circumstances are minor and, according to the Criminal Law, no punishment is needed or exemption from punishment is applicable, the People's Procuratorate shall produce an investigation conclusion report and prepare a non-prosecution opinion.  
The investigation conclusion report and the prosecution opinion or non-prosecution opinion shall be submitted to the chief procurator for approval.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条  负责侦查的部门应当将起诉意见书或者不起诉意见书，查封、扣押、冻结的犯罪嫌疑人的财物及其孳息、文件清单以及对查封、扣押、冻结的涉案财物的处理意见和其他案卷材料，一并移送本院负责捕诉的部门审查。国家或者集体财产遭受损失的，在提出提起公诉意见的同时，可以提出提起附带民事诉讼的意见。

**Article 238** The department responsible for investigation shall transfer the prosecution opinion or non-prosecution opinion, the list of the criminal suspect's property, its fruits, and documents that have been sealed, seized, or frozen, the opinions on handling the sealed, seized, or frozen property involved in the case, and other case materials, to the department of the same People's Procuratorate responsible for arrest and prosecution for review. If state or collective property suffers losses, the department may, while proposing an opinion on initiating a public prosecution, propose an opinion on initiating an incidental civil action.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条  在案件侦查过程中，犯罪嫌疑人委托辩护律师的，检察人员可以听取辩护律师的意见。  
辩护律师要求当面提出意见的，检察人员应当听取意见，并制作笔录附卷。辩护律师提出书面意见的，应当附卷。  
侦查终结前，犯罪嫌疑人提出无罪或者罪轻的辩解，辩护律师提出犯罪嫌疑人无罪或者依法不应当追究刑事责任意见的，人民检察院应当依法予以核实。  
案件侦查终结移送起诉时，人民检察院应当同时将案件移送情况告知犯罪嫌疑人及其辩护律师。

**Article 239** During the investigation of a case, if a criminal suspect entrusts a defense lawyer, the procuratorial personnel may hear the opinions of the defense lawyer.  
If the defense lawyer requests to present opinions in person, the procuratorial personnel shall hear the opinions and make a record to be attached to the case file. If the defense lawyer submits written opinions, they shall be attached to the case file.  
Before the conclusion of the investigation, if the criminal suspect gives a defense of innocence or lesser guilt, or if the defense lawyer submits an opinion that the criminal suspect is innocent or shall not be investigated for criminal liability according to law, the People's Procuratorate shall verify it according to law.  
When the case investigation is concluded and transferred for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall simultaneously inform the criminal suspect and his defense lawyer of the transfer.

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条  人民检察院侦查终结的案件，需要在异地起诉、审判的，应当在移送起诉前与人民法院协商指定管辖的相关事宜。

**Article 240** If a case for which the investigation has been concluded by a People's Procuratorate needs to be prosecuted or adjudicated in a different locality, the People's Procuratorate shall, before transferring the case for prosecution, consult the People's Court on matters related to designated jurisdiction.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条  上级人民检察院侦查终结的案件，依照刑事诉讼法的规定应当由下级人民检察院提起公诉或者不起诉的，应当将有关决定、侦查终结报告连同案卷材料交由下级人民检察院审查。  
下级人民检察院认为上级人民检察院的决定有错误的，可以向上级人民检察院报告。上级人民检察院维持原决定的，下级人民检察院应当执行。

**Article 241** If a case investigated and concluded by a superior People's Procuratorate shall, in accordance with the Criminal Procedure Law, be initiated for public prosecution or not prosecuted by a lower People's Procuratorate, the superior People's Procuratorate shall transfer the relevant decision, the investigation conclusion report, together with the case file materials, to the lower People's Procuratorate for review.  
If the lower People's Procuratorate considers that the decision of the superior People's Procuratorate is erroneous, it may report to the superior People's Procuratorate. If the superior People's Procuratorate upholds the original decision, the lower People's Procuratorate shall execute it.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条  人民检察院在侦查过程中或者侦查终结后，发现具有下列情形之一的，负责侦查的部门应当制作拟撤销案件意见书，报请检察长决定：  
（一）具有[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)规定情形之一的；  
（二）没有犯罪事实的，或者依照刑法规定不负刑事责任或者不是犯罪的；  
（三）虽有犯罪事实，但不是犯罪嫌疑人所为的。  
对于共同犯罪的案件，如有符合本条规定情形的犯罪嫌疑人，应当撤销对该犯罪嫌疑人的立案。

**Article 242** If, during or after the investigation, a People's Procuratorate discovers that any of the following circumstances exist, the department responsible for investigation shall prepare a draft opinion on dismissing the case and submit it to the chief procurator for decision:  
(1) Any of the circumstances provided in Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law exist;  
(2) There is no criminal fact, or according to the Criminal Law, the person does not bear criminal liability or the act does not constitute a crime;  
(3) Although a criminal fact exists, it was not committed by the criminal suspect.  
For a case of joint crime, if there is a criminal suspect who meets the circumstances provided in this Article, the filing of the case against that criminal suspect shall be dismissed.

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条  地方各级人民检察院决定撤销案件的，负责侦查的部门应当将撤销案件意见书连同本案全部案卷材料，在法定期限届满七日前报上一级人民检察院审查；重大、复杂案件在法定期限届满十日前报上一级人民检察院审查。  
对于共同犯罪案件，应当将处理同案犯罪嫌疑人的有关法律文书以及案件事实、证据材料复印件等，一并报送上一级人民检察院。  
上一级人民检察院负责侦查的部门应当对案件事实、证据和适用法律进行全面审查。必要时，可以讯问犯罪嫌疑人。  
上一级人民检察院负责侦查的部门审查后，应当提出是否同意撤销案件的意见，报请检察长决定。  
人民检察院决定撤销案件的，应当告知控告人、举报人，听取其意见并记明笔录。

**Article 243** If a local People's Procuratorate decides to dismiss a case, the department responsible for investigation shall submit the opinion on dismissing the case together with all case file materials to the next higher People's Procuratorate for review seven days before the expiry of the statutory time limit; for major or complex cases, it shall submit them ten days before the expiry of the statutory time limit.  
For a case of joint crime, the relevant legal documents concerning the handling of co-suspects and copies of case facts and evidence materials shall also be submitted to the next higher People's Procuratorate.  
The department responsible for investigation of the next higher People's Procuratorate shall conduct a comprehensive review of the case facts, evidence, and application of law. When necessary, it may interrogate the criminal suspect.  
After review, the department responsible for investigation of the next higher People's Procuratorate shall propose an opinion on whether to agree to dismiss the case and submit it to the chief procurator for decision.  
If the People's Procuratorate decides to dismiss the case, it shall inform the complainant and the whistleblower, hear their opinions, and record them in the record.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条  上一级人民检察院审查下级人民检察院报送的拟撤销案件，应当在收到案件后七日以内批复；重大、复杂案件，应当在收到案件后十日以内批复。情况紧急或者因其他特殊原因不能按时送达的，可以先行通知下级人民检察院执行。

**Article 244** The next higher People's Procuratorate reviewing a proposed dismissed case submitted by a lower People's Procuratorate shall reply within seven days after receiving the case; for major or complex cases, it shall reply within ten days after receiving the case. If the situation is urgent or the reply cannot be delivered on time due to other special reasons, it may first notify the lower People's Procuratorate to execute.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条  上一级人民检察院同意撤销案件的，下级人民检察院应当作出撤销案件决定，并制作撤销案件决定书。上一级人民检察院不同意撤销案件的，下级人民检察院应当执行上一级人民检察院的决定。  
报请上一级人民检察院审查期间，犯罪嫌疑人羁押期限届满的，应当依法释放犯罪嫌疑人或者变更强制措施。

**Article 245** If the next higher People's Procuratorate agrees to dismiss the case, the lower People's Procuratorate shall make a decision to dismiss the case and prepare a written decision on dismissing the case. If the next higher People's Procuratorate disagrees with dismissing the case, the lower People's Procuratorate shall execute the decision of the next higher People's Procuratorate.  
During the period when the case is submitted to the next higher People's Procuratorate for review, if the period of custody of the criminal suspect expires, the criminal suspect shall be released according to law or the compulsory measures shall be changed.

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条  撤销案件的决定，应当分别送达犯罪嫌疑人所在单位和犯罪嫌疑人。犯罪嫌疑人死亡的，应当送达犯罪嫌疑人原所在单位。如果犯罪嫌疑人在押，应当制作决定释放通知书，通知公安机关依法释放。

**Article 246** The decision to dismiss the case shall be served separately to the unit to which the criminal suspect belongs and to the criminal suspect. If the criminal suspect is deceased, it shall be served to the original unit of the criminal suspect. If the criminal suspect is in custody, a notice of decision on release shall be prepared to notify the public security organ to release the suspect according to law.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条  人民检察院作出撤销案件决定的，应当在三十日以内报经检察长批准，对犯罪嫌疑人的违法所得作出处理。情况特殊的，可以延长三十日。

**Article 247** After a People's Procuratorate makes a decision to dismiss a case, it shall, within 30 days, report to the chief procurator for approval and handle the criminal suspect's illegal gains. If the circumstances are special, the period may be extended by 30 days.

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条  人民检察院撤销案件时，对犯罪嫌疑人的违法所得及其他涉案财产应当区分不同情形，作出相应处理：  
（一）因犯罪嫌疑人死亡而撤销案件，依照刑法规定应当追缴其违法所得及其他涉案财产的，按照本规则第十二章第四节的规定办理。  
（二）因其他原因撤销案件，对于查封、扣押、冻结的犯罪嫌疑人违法所得及其他涉案财产需要没收的，应当提出检察意见，移送有关主管机关处理。  
（三）对于冻结的犯罪嫌疑人存款、汇款、债券、股票、基金份额等财产需要返还被害人的，可以通知金融机构、邮政部门返还被害人；对于查封、扣押的犯罪嫌疑人的违法所得及其他涉案财产需要返还被害人的，直接决定返还被害人。  
人民检察院申请人民法院裁定处理犯罪嫌疑人涉案财产的，应当向人民法院移送有关案卷材料。

**Article 248** When a People's Procuratorate dismisses a case, it shall distinguish different circumstances and make corresponding handling of the criminal suspect's illegal gains and other property involved in the case:  
(1) If the case is dismissed due to the death of the criminal suspect, and according to the Criminal Law, the illegal gains and other property involved in the case shall be recovered, it shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Section 4 of Chapter 12 of these Rules.  
(2) If the case is dismissed for other reasons, and the sealed, seized, or frozen illegal gains and other property involved in the case of the criminal suspect need to be confiscated, a procuratorial opinion shall be submitted and transferred to the relevant competent authority for handling.  
(3) If the frozen property such as the criminal suspect's deposits, remittances, bonds, stocks, fund shares, etc. needs to be returned to the victim, the financial institution or postal department may be notified to return it to the victim; if the sealed or seized illegal gains and other property involved in the case of the criminal suspect need to be returned to the victim, a direct decision shall be made to return them to the victim.  
If a People's Procuratorate applies to a People's Court for a ruling to handle the criminal suspect's property involved in the case, it shall transfer the relevant case file materials to the People's Court.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条  人民检察院撤销案件时，对查封、扣押、冻结的犯罪嫌疑人的涉案财物需要返还犯罪嫌疑人的，应当解除查封、扣押或者书面通知有关金融机构、邮政部门解除冻结，返还犯罪嫌疑人或者其合法继承人。

**Article 249** When a People's Procuratorate dismisses a case, if the sealed, seized, or frozen property involved in the case of the criminal suspect needs to be returned to the criminal suspect, it shall lift the seal or seizure, or notify in writing the relevant financial institution or postal department to lift the freeze, and return the property to the criminal suspect or his legal successor.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条  查封、扣押、冻结的财物，除依法应当返还被害人或者经查明确实与案件无关的以外，不得在诉讼程序终结之前处理。法律或者有关规定另有规定的除外。

**Article 250** Sealed, seized, or frozen property shall not be disposed of before the conclusion of the litigation proceedings, except where it shall be returned to the victim according to law or is confirmed to be genuinely unrelated to the case. This does not apply where otherwise provided by law or relevant provisions.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条  处理查封、扣押、冻结的涉案财物，应当由检察长决定。

**Article 251** : The handling of property involved in a case that is sealed, seized, or frozen shall be decided by the Chief Procurator.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条  人民检察院直接受理侦查的共同犯罪案件，如果同案犯罪嫌疑人在逃，但在案犯罪嫌疑人犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分的，对在案犯罪嫌疑人应当根据[本规则第二百三十七条](#art-237)的规定分别移送起诉或者移送不起诉。  
由于同案犯罪嫌疑人在逃，在案犯罪嫌疑人的犯罪事实无法查清的，对在案犯罪嫌疑人应当根据案件的不同情况分别报请延长侦查羁押期限、变更强制措施或者解除强制措施。

**Article 252** : For a joint crime case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate, if a co-suspect is at large, but the facts of the crime committed by the suspect in custody are clear and the evidence is reliable and sufficient, the suspect in custody shall be transferred for prosecution or for non-prosecution respectively in accordance with Article 237 of these Rules.  
If, due to the co-suspect being at large, the facts of the crime of the suspect in custody cannot be ascertained, the suspect in custody shall, depending on the circumstances of the case, have a report submitted for an extension of the period of custodial investigation, or for a change of compulsory measures or for the removal of compulsory measures.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条  人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，对犯罪嫌疑人没有采取取保候审、监视居住、拘留或者逮捕措施的，负责侦查的部门应当在立案后二年以内提出移送起诉、移送不起诉或者撤销案件的意见；对犯罪嫌疑人采取取保候审、监视居住、拘留或者逮捕措施的，负责侦查的部门应当在解除或者撤销强制措施后一年以内提出移送起诉、移送不起诉或者撤销案件的意见。

**Article 253** : For a case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate, if no compulsory measures such as release on bail pending investigation, residential surveillance, detention, or arrest have been taken against the criminal suspect, the department responsible for investigation shall, within two years after the case is placed on file, submit an opinion on transferring the case for prosecution, for non-prosecution, or for dismissal. If compulsory measures such as release on bail pending investigation, residential surveillance, detention, or arrest have been taken against the criminal suspect, the department responsible for investigation shall, within one year after the compulsory measures are removed or revoked, submit an opinion on transferring the case for prosecution, for non-prosecution, or for dismissal.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条  人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，撤销案件以后，又发现新的事实或者证据，认为有犯罪事实需要追究刑事责任的，可以重新立案侦查。

**Article 254** : For a case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate, if after the case is dismissed, new facts or evidence are discovered and it is deemed that there are criminal facts requiring the investigation of criminal liability, the case may be placed on file for investigation anew.

### 第十章 审查逮捕和审查起诉

#### 第一节 一般规定

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条  人民检察院办理审查逮捕、审查起诉案件，应当全面审查证明犯罪嫌疑人有罪或者无罪、罪轻或者罪重的证据。

**Article 255** : When handling cases of reviewing arrest and reviewing prosecution, a People's Procuratorate shall comprehensively review the evidence proving that the criminal suspect is guilty or innocent, or that the crime is minor or serious.

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条  经公安机关商请或者人民检察院认为确有必要时，可以派员适时介入重大、疑难、复杂案件的侦查活动，参加公安机关对于重大案件的讨论，对案件性质、收集证据、适用法律等提出意见，监督侦查活动是否合法。  
经监察机关商请，人民检察院可以派员介入监察机关办理的职务犯罪案件。

**Article 256** : At the request of a public security organ or when a People's Procuratorate deems it truly necessary, it may dispatch personnel to timely intervene in the investigation activities of major, difficult, and complex cases, participate in the discussion of major cases by the public security organ, offer opinions on the nature of the case, collection of evidence, application of law, etc., and supervise whether the investigation activities are lawful.  
At the request of a supervisory organ, a People's Procuratorate may dispatch personnel to intervene in duty-related crime cases handled by the supervisory organ.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条  对于批准逮捕后要求公安机关继续侦查、不批准逮捕后要求公安机关补充侦查或者审查起诉阶段退回公安机关补充侦查的案件，人民检察院应当分别制作继续侦查提纲或者补充侦查提纲，写明需要继续侦查或者补充侦查的事项、理由、侦查方向、需补充收集的证据及其证明作用等，送交公安机关。

**Article 257** : For cases where, after approval of arrest, the public security organ is required to continue investigation, or after disapproval of arrest, the public security organ is required to conduct supplementary investigation, or during the review of prosecution stage, the case is returned to the public security organ for supplementary investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall respectively prepare an outline for continued investigation or an outline for supplementary investigation, specifying the matters requiring continued investigation or supplementary investigation, the reasons, the direction of investigation, the evidence to be supplemented and its probative value, etc., and deliver it to the public security organ.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条  人民检察院讯问犯罪嫌疑人时，应当首先查明犯罪嫌疑人的基本情况，依法告知犯罪嫌疑人诉讼权利和义务，以及认罪认罚的法律规定，听取其供述和辩解。犯罪嫌疑人翻供的，应当讯问其原因。犯罪嫌疑人申请排除非法证据的，应当告知其提供相关线索或者材料。犯罪嫌疑人检举揭发他人犯罪的，应当予以记录，并依照有关规定移送有关机关、部门处理。  
讯问犯罪嫌疑人应当制作讯问笔录，并交犯罪嫌疑人核对或者向其宣读。经核对无误后逐页签名或者盖章，并捺指印后附卷。犯罪嫌疑人请求自行书写供述的，应当准许，但不得以自行书写的供述代替讯问笔录。  
犯罪嫌疑人被羁押的，讯问应当在看守所讯问室进行。

**Article 258** : When interrogating a criminal suspect, a People's Procuratorate shall first ascertain the basic information of the criminal suspect, inform the criminal suspect according to law of his litigation rights and obligations, as well as the legal provisions on pleading guilty and accepting punishment, and hear his statements and defenses. If the criminal suspect retracts his confession, the reasons therefor shall be inquired. If the criminal suspect applies for the exclusion of illegally obtained evidence, he shall be informed to provide relevant clues or materials. If the criminal suspect reports or exposes others' crimes, it shall be recorded and transferred to the relevant organs or departments for handling in accordance with relevant provisions.  
A record of interrogation shall be made when interrogating a criminal suspect, and the record shall be checked by the criminal suspect or read to him. After verification without error, the criminal suspect shall sign or seal each page, affix his fingerprint, and the record shall be attached to the case file. If the criminal suspect requests to write a statement on his own, such request shall be permitted; however, the self-written statement shall not replace the interrogation record.  
If the criminal suspect is in custody, the interrogation shall be conducted in the interrogation room of the detention center.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条  办理审查逮捕、审查起诉案件，可以询问证人、被害人、鉴定人等诉讼参与人，并制作笔录附卷。询问时，应当告知其诉讼权利和义务。  
询问证人、被害人的地点按照[刑事诉讼法第一百二十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-124)的规定执行。

**Article 259** : When handling cases of reviewing arrest and reviewing prosecution, witnesses, victims, expert evaluators, and other litigation participants may be questioned, and a record shall be made and attached to the case file. When questioning, they shall be informed of their litigation rights and obligations.  
The location for questioning witnesses and victims shall be carried out in accordance with the provisions of Article 124 of the Criminal Procedure Law.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条  讯问犯罪嫌疑人，询问被害人、证人、鉴定人，听取辩护人、被害人及其诉讼代理人的意见，应当由检察人员负责进行。检察人员或者检察人员和书记员不得少于二人。  
讯问犯罪嫌疑人，询问证人、鉴定人、被害人，应当个别进行。

**Article 260** : Interrogation of criminal suspects, questioning of victims, witnesses, and expert evaluators, and hearing of opinions from defenders, victims, and their legal representatives shall be conducted by procuratorial personnel. There shall be no fewer than two procuratorial personnel, or procuratorial personnel and court clerks.  
Interrogation of criminal suspects and questioning of witnesses, expert evaluators, and victims shall be conducted individually.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条  办理审查逮捕案件，犯罪嫌疑人已经委托辩护律师的，可以听取辩护律师的意见。辩护律师提出要求的，应当听取辩护律师的意见。对辩护律师的意见应当制作笔录，辩护律师提出的书面意见应当附卷。  
办理审查起诉案件，应当听取辩护人或者值班律师、被害人及其诉讼代理人的意见，并制作笔录。辩护人或者值班律师、被害人及其诉讼代理人提出书面意见的，应当附卷。  
对于辩护律师在审查逮捕、审查起诉阶段多次提出意见的，均应如实记录。  
辩护律师提出犯罪嫌疑人不构成犯罪、无社会危险性、不适宜羁押或者侦查活动有违法犯罪情形等书面意见的，检察人员应当审查，并在相关工作文书中说明是否采纳的情况和理由。

**Article 261** : When handling a case of reviewing arrest, if the criminal suspect has already retained a defense lawyer, the opinions of the defense lawyer may be heard. If the defense lawyer makes a request, the opinions of the defense lawyer shall be heard. A record shall be made of the opinions of the defense lawyer, and any written opinions submitted by the defense lawyer shall be attached to the case file.  
When handling a case of reviewing prosecution, the opinions of the defender or duty lawyer, the victim, and his legal representative shall be heard, and a record shall be made. If the defender, duty lawyer, victim, or his legal representative submits written opinions, they shall be attached to the case file.  
If the defense lawyer repeatedly submits opinions during the stage of reviewing arrest or reviewing prosecution, all instances shall be truthfully recorded.  
If the defense lawyer submits written opinions asserting that the criminal suspect does not constitute a crime, poses no social danger, is not suitable for custody, or that the investigation activities involve illegal or criminal circumstances, the procuratorial personnel shall review them and state in the relevant work documents whether the opinions have been adopted and the reasons therefor.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条  直接听取辩护人、被害人及其诉讼代理人的意见有困难的，可以通过电话、视频等方式听取意见并记录在案，或者通知辩护人、被害人及其诉讼代理人提出书面意见。无法通知或者在指定期限内未提出意见的，应当记录在案。

**Article 262** : If it is difficult to directly hear the opinions of the defender, the victim, and his legal representative, the opinions may be heard by telephone, video, or other means and recorded in the case file, or the defender, the victim, and his legal representative may be notified to submit written opinions. If it is impossible to notify them or if no opinions are submitted within the specified period, this shall be recorded in the case file.

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条  对于公安机关提请批准逮捕、移送起诉的案件，检察人员审查时发现存在[本规则第七十五条](#art-75)第一款规定情形的，可以调取公安机关讯问犯罪嫌疑人的录音、录像并审查相关的录音、录像。对于重大、疑难、复杂的案件，必要时可以审查全部录音、录像。  
对于监察机关移送起诉的案件，认为需要调取有关录音、录像的，可以商监察机关调取。  
对于人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，审查时发现负责侦查的部门未按照[本规则第七十五条](#art-75)第三款的规定移送录音、录像或者移送不全的，应当要求其补充移送。对取证合法性或者讯问笔录真实性等产生疑问的，应当有针对性地审查相关的录音、录像。对于重大、疑难、复杂的案件，可以审查全部录音、录像。

**Article 263** : For a case submitted by a public security organ for approval of arrest or for transfer for prosecution, if a procuratorial personnel during review discovers any circumstances as specified in Paragraph 1 of Article 75 of these Rules, he may obtain the audio and video recordings of the interrogation of the criminal suspect by the public security organ and review the relevant audio and video recordings. For major, difficult, and complex cases, when necessary, all audio and video recordings may be reviewed.  
For a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ, if it is deemed necessary to obtain the relevant audio and video recordings, the matter may be negotiated with the supervisory organ for their retrieval.  
For a case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate, if during review it is discovered that the department responsible for investigation failed to transfer audio and video recordings in accordance with Paragraph 3 of Article 75 of these Rules, or transferred incomplete recordings, the department shall be required to supplement the transfer. If doubts arise regarding the legality of evidence collection or the authenticity of interrogation records, the relevant audio and video recordings shall be reviewed in a targeted manner. For major, difficult, and complex cases, all audio and video recordings may be reviewed.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条  经审查讯问犯罪嫌疑人录音、录像，发现公安机关、本院负责侦查的部门讯问不规范，讯问过程存在违法行为，录音、录像内容与讯问笔录不一致等情形的，应当逐一列明并向公安机关、本院负责侦查的部门书面提出，要求其予以纠正、补正或者书面作出合理解释。发现讯问笔录与讯问犯罪嫌疑人录音、录像内容有重大实质性差异的，或者公安机关、本院负责侦查的部门不能补正或者作出合理解释的，该讯问笔录不能作为批准或者决定逮捕、提起公诉的依据。

**Article 264** : Upon reviewing the audio and video recordings of the interrogation of a criminal suspect, if it is discovered that the interrogation by the public security organ or the department responsible for investigation of this Procuratorate is irregular, that there are illegal acts during the interrogation, or that the content of the recordings is inconsistent with the interrogation record, such matters shall be listed one by one and raised in writing to the public security organ or the department responsible for investigation of this Procuratorate, requiring them to correct, supplement, or provide a reasonable explanation in writing. If it is discovered that there are material substantive differences between the interrogation record and the content of the audio and video recordings of the interrogation of the criminal suspect, or if the public security organ or the department responsible for investigation of this Procuratorate cannot supplement or provide a reasonable explanation, such interrogation record shall not serve as the basis for approving or deciding on arrest or initiating a public prosecution.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条  犯罪嫌疑人及其辩护人申请排除非法证据，并提供相关线索或者材料的，人民检察院应当调查核实。发现侦查人员以刑讯逼供等非法方法收集证据的，应当依法排除相关证据并提出纠正意见。  
审查逮捕期限届满前，经审查无法确定存在非法取证的行为，但也不能排除非法取证可能的，该证据不作为批准逮捕的依据。检察官应当根据在案的其他证据认定案件事实和决定是否逮捕，并在作出批准或者不批准逮捕的决定后，继续对可能存在的非法取证行为进行调查核实。经调查核实确认存在以刑讯逼供等非法方法收集证据情形的，应当向公安机关提出纠正意见。以非法方法收集的证据，不得作为提起公诉的依据。

**Article 265** : If a criminal suspect or his defender applies for the exclusion of illegally obtained evidence and provides relevant clues or materials, a People's Procuratorate shall investigate and verify. If it is discovered that the investigators collected evidence by illegal methods such as extorting confessions by torture, the relevant evidence shall be excluded according to law and a corrective opinion shall be submitted.  
If, before the expiration of the period for reviewing arrest, upon review it cannot be determined that illegal evidence collection exists, but the possibility of illegal evidence collection cannot be ruled out, such evidence shall not serve as the basis for approving arrest. The prosecutor shall determine the case facts and decide whether to approve arrest based on other evidence in the case, and after making a decision to approve or disapprove arrest, shall continue to investigate and verify the possible illegal evidence collection. If upon investigation and verification it is confirmed that evidence was collected by illegal methods such as extorting confessions by torture, a corrective opinion shall be submitted to the public security organ. Evidence collected by illegal methods shall not serve as the basis for initiating a public prosecution.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条  审查逮捕期间，犯罪嫌疑人申请排除非法证据，但未提交相关线索或者材料，人民检察院经全面审查案件事实、证据，未发现侦查人员存在以非法方法收集证据的情形，认为符合逮捕条件的，可以批准逮捕。  
审查起诉期间，犯罪嫌疑人及其辩护人又提出新的线索或者证据，或者人民检察院发现新的证据，经调查核实认为侦查人员存在以刑讯逼供等非法方法收集证据情形的，应当依法排除非法证据，不得作为提起公诉的依据。  
排除非法证据后，犯罪嫌疑人不再符合逮捕条件但案件需要继续审查起诉的，应当及时变更强制措施。案件不符合起诉条件的，应当作出不起诉决定。

**Article 266** : During the period of reviewing arrest, if the criminal suspect applies for the exclusion of illegally obtained evidence but does not submit relevant clues or materials, and the People's Procuratorate, after comprehensively reviewing the case facts and evidence, does not discover that the investigators collected evidence by illegal methods and deems that the conditions for arrest are met, it may approve the arrest.  
During the period of reviewing prosecution, if the criminal suspect or his defender submits new clues or evidence, or if the People's Procuratorate discovers new evidence, and upon investigation and verification it is determined that the investigators collected evidence by illegal methods such as extorting confessions by torture, the illegally obtained evidence shall be excluded according to law and shall not serve as the basis for initiating a public prosecution.  
After the exclusion of illegally obtained evidence, if the criminal suspect no longer meets the conditions for arrest but the case requires continued review for prosecution, the compulsory measures shall be changed in a timely manner. If the case does not meet the conditions for prosecution, a decision not to prosecute shall be made.

#### 第二节 认罪认罚从宽案件办理

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条  人民检察院办理犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚案件，应当保障犯罪嫌疑人获得有效法律帮助，确保其了解认罪认罚的性质和法律后果，自愿认罪认罚。  
人民检察院受理案件后，应当向犯罪嫌疑人了解其委托辩护人的情况。犯罪嫌疑人自愿认罪认罚、没有辩护人的，在审查逮捕阶段，人民检察院应当要求公安机关通知值班律师为其提供法律帮助；在审查起诉阶段，人民检察院应当通知值班律师为其提供法律帮助。符合通知辩护条件的，应当依法通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供辩护。

**Article 267** : When handling cases in which the criminal suspect pleads guilty and accepts punishment, a People's Procuratorate shall ensure that the criminal suspect obtains effective legal assistance, and ensure that he understands the nature and legal consequences of pleading guilty and accepting punishment, and that he pleads guilty and accepts punishment voluntarily.  
After accepting a case, a People's Procuratorate shall ascertain from the criminal suspect whether he has retained a defender. If the criminal suspect voluntarily pleads guilty and accepts punishment but has no defender, during the stage of reviewing arrest, the People's Procuratorate shall require the public security organ to notify a duty lawyer to provide him with legal assistance; during the stage of reviewing prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall notify a duty lawyer to provide him with legal assistance. If the conditions for notifying defense are met, the People's Procuratorate shall, according to law, notify a legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide him with defense.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条  人民检察院应当商法律援助机构设立法律援助工作站派驻值班律师或者及时安排值班律师，为犯罪嫌疑人提供法律咨询、程序选择建议、申请变更强制措施、对案件处理提出意见等法律帮助。  
人民检察院应当告知犯罪嫌疑人有权约见值班律师，并为其约见值班律师提供便利。

**Article 268** : A People's Procuratorate shall, through consultation with a legal aid institution, establish a legal aid workstation by stationing duty lawyers or arrange for duty lawyers in a timely manner to provide legal assistance to criminal suspects, such as legal consultation, suggestions on procedural options, applying for a change of compulsory measures, and offering opinions on case handling.  
A People's Procuratorate shall inform the criminal suspect of his right to meet with a duty lawyer and facilitate his meeting with the duty lawyer.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条  犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚的，人民检察院应当告知其享有的诉讼权利和认罪认罚的法律规定，听取犯罪嫌疑人、辩护人或者值班律师、被害人及其诉讼代理人对下列事项的意见，并记录在案：  
（一）涉嫌的犯罪事实、罪名及适用的法律规定；  
（二）从轻、减轻或者免除处罚等从宽处罚的建议；  
（三）认罪认罚后案件审理适用的程序；  
（四）其他需要听取意见的事项。  
依照前款规定听取值班律师意见的，应当提前为值班律师了解案件有关情况提供必要的便利。自人民检察院对案件审查起诉之日起，值班律师可以查阅案卷材料，了解案情。人民检察院应当为值班律师查阅案卷材料提供便利。  
人民检察院不采纳辩护人或者值班律师所提意见的，应当向其说明理由。

**Article 269** : If the criminal suspect pleads guilty and accepts punishment, a People's Procuratorate shall inform him of the litigation rights he enjoys and the legal provisions on pleading guilty and accepting punishment, and shall hear the opinions of the criminal suspect, the defender or duty lawyer, the victim, and his legal representative on the following matters, and record them in the case file:  
(1) The suspected criminal facts, charges, and applicable legal provisions;  
(2) Recommendations for lenient punishment such as lighter, mitigated, or exempted punishment;  
(3) The procedures applicable to the trial of the case after pleading guilty and accepting punishment;  
(4) Other matters on which opinions need to be heard.  
If the opinions of the duty lawyer are to be heard in accordance with the preceding paragraph, necessary convenience shall be provided in advance for the duty lawyer to understand the relevant circumstances of the case. From the date a People's Procuratorate begins to review the case for prosecution, the duty lawyer may consult the case file materials to understand the case. A People's Procuratorate shall facilitate the duty lawyer's access to the case file materials.  
If a People's Procuratorate does not adopt the opinions raised by the defender or duty lawyer, it shall explain the reasons therefor to them.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条  批准或者决定逮捕，应当将犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌犯罪的性质、情节，认罪认罚等情况，作为是否可能发生社会危险性的考虑因素。  
已经逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚的，人民检察院应当及时对羁押必要性进行审查。经审查，认为没有继续羁押必要的，应当予以释放或者变更强制措施。

**Article 270** : When approving or deciding on arrest, the nature and circumstances of the crime suspected to have been committed by the criminal suspect, his pleading guilty and accepting punishment, and other circumstances shall be taken into consideration as factors in assessing whether there is a likelihood of endangering society.  
If a criminal suspect who has already been arrested pleads guilty and accepts punishment, a People's Procuratorate shall promptly review the necessity of custody. Upon review, if it is deemed that continued custody is unnecessary, the criminal suspect shall be released or compulsory measures shall be changed.

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条  审查起诉阶段，对于在侦查阶段认罪认罚的案件，人民检察院应当重点审查以下内容：  
（一）犯罪嫌疑人是否自愿认罪认罚，有无因受到暴力、威胁、引诱而违背意愿认罪认罚；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚时的认知能力和精神状态是否正常；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人是否理解认罪认罚的性质和可能导致的法律后果；  
（四）公安机关是否告知犯罪嫌疑人享有的诉讼权利，如实供述自己罪行可以从宽处理和认罪认罚的法律规定，并听取意见；  
（五）起诉意见书中是否写明犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚情况；  
（六）犯罪嫌疑人是否真诚悔罪，是否向被害人赔礼道歉。  
经审查，犯罪嫌疑人违背意愿认罪认罚的，人民检察院可以重新开展认罪认罚工作。存在刑讯逼供等非法取证行为的，依照法律规定处理。

**Article 271** During the stage of examination and prosecution, for a case in which the criminal suspect has admitted guilt and accepted punishment during the investigation stage, the People's Procuratorate shall focus on examining the following:  
(1) Whether the criminal suspect has voluntarily admitted guilt and accepted punishment, and whether there has been any violation of his or her will to admit guilt and accept punishment due to violence, threats, or inducements;  
(2) Whether the criminal suspect's cognitive ability and mental state were normal at the time of admitting guilt and accepting punishment;  
(3) Whether the criminal suspect understands the nature of admitting guilt and accepting punishment and the possible legal consequences;  
(4) Whether the public security organ has informed the criminal suspect of the litigation rights he or she enjoys, the legal provisions that truthful confession of one's own crimes may lead to lenient treatment and that admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment are legal, and has heard opinions;  
(5) Whether the opinion on prosecution specifies the criminal suspect's admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment;  
(6) Whether the criminal suspect sincerely repents and whether he or she has apologized to the victim.  
Upon examination, if the criminal suspect has admitted guilt and accepted punishment against his or her will, the People's Procuratorate may re-conduct the work of admitting guilt and accepting punishment. If there are illegal evidence collection acts such as extorting confessions by torture, the matter shall be handled in accordance with law.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条  犯罪嫌疑人自愿认罪认罚，同意量刑建议和程序适用的，应当在辩护人或者值班律师在场的情况下签署认罪认罚具结书。具结书应当包括犯罪嫌疑人如实供述罪行、同意量刑建议和程序适用等内容，由犯罪嫌疑人及其辩护人、值班律师签名。  
犯罪嫌疑人具有下列情形之一的，不需要签署认罪认罚具结书：  
（一）犯罪嫌疑人是盲、聋、哑人，或者是尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人的；  
（二）未成年犯罪嫌疑人的法定代理人、辩护人对未成年人认罪认罚有异议的；  
（三）其他不需要签署认罪认罚具结书的情形。  
有前款情形，犯罪嫌疑人未签署认罪认罚具结书的，不影响认罪认罚从宽制度的适用。

**Article 272** Where a criminal suspect voluntarily admits guilt and accepts punishment and agrees to the sentencing recommendation and the application of the procedure, he or she shall sign a written statement of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment in the presence of a defender or a duty lawyer. The written statement shall include the criminal suspect's truthful confession of the offense, agreement to the sentencing recommendation and the application of the procedure, and shall be signed by the criminal suspect and his or her defender or the duty lawyer.  
A criminal suspect need not sign a written statement of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) The criminal suspect is blind, deaf, or mute, or is a person with a mental illness who has not completely lost the ability to recognize or control his or her conduct;  
(2) The legal representative or defender of a minor criminal suspect objects to the minor's admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment;  
(3) Other circumstances in which it is unnecessary to sign a written statement of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment.  
In the circumstances of the preceding paragraph, the criminal suspect's failure to sign a written statement of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment shall not affect the application of the system of leniency for admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条  犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚，人民检察院经审查，认为符合速裁程序适用条件的，应当在十日以内作出是否提起公诉的决定，对可能判处的有期徒刑超过一年的，可以延长至十五日；认为不符合速裁程序适用条件的，应当在[本规则第三百五十一条](#art-351)规定的期限以内作出是否提起公诉的决定。  
对于公安机关建议适用速裁程序办理的案件，人民检察院负责案件管理的部门应当在受理案件的当日将案件移送负责捕诉的部门。

**Article 273** Where a criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment, and the People's Procuratorate, upon examination, finds that the conditions for applying the expedited procedure are met, it shall decide whether to initiate public prosecution within ten days; if the likely sentence is fixed-term imprisonment of more than one year, it may extend the period to fifteen days. If it finds that the conditions for applying the expedited procedure are not met, it shall decide whether to initiate public prosecution within the time limit prescribed in Article 351 of these Rules.  
For a case in which the public security organ recommends the application of the expedited procedure, the case management department of the People's Procuratorate shall transfer the case to the department responsible for arrest and prosecution on the day the case is accepted.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条  认罪认罚案件，人民检察院向人民法院提起公诉的，应当提出量刑建议，在起诉书中写明被告人认罪认罚情况，并移送认罪认罚具结书等材料。量刑建议可以另行制作文书，也可以在起诉书中写明。

**Article 274** In a case involving admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, where the People's Procuratorate initiates public prosecution with the People's Court, it shall make a sentencing recommendation, specify in the indictment the defendant's admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, and transfer the written statement of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment and other materials. The sentencing recommendation may be made in a separate document or stated in the indictment.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条  犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚的，人民检察院应当就主刑、附加刑、是否适用缓刑等提出量刑建议。量刑建议一般应当为确定刑。对新类型、不常见犯罪案件，量刑情节复杂的重罪案件等，也可以提出幅度刑量刑建议。

**Article 275** Where a criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment, the People's Procuratorate shall make a sentencing recommendation regarding the principal punishment, supplementary punishment, and whether probation shall be applied. The sentencing recommendation shall generally be a definite sentence. For new types of crimes, uncommon criminal cases, and serious crimes with complex sentencing circumstances, a range-based sentencing recommendation may also be made.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条  办理认罪认罚案件，人民检察院应当将犯罪嫌疑人是否与被害方达成和解或者调解协议，或者赔偿被害方损失，取得被害方谅解，或者自愿承担公益损害修复、赔偿责任，作为提出量刑建议的重要考虑因素。  
犯罪嫌疑人自愿认罪并且愿意积极赔偿损失，但由于被害方赔偿请求明显不合理，未能达成和解或者调解协议的，一般不影响对犯罪嫌疑人从宽处理。  
对于符合当事人和解程序适用条件的公诉案件，犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚的，人民检察院应当积极促使当事人自愿达成和解。和解协议书和被害方出具的谅解意见应当随案移送。被害方符合司法救助条件的，人民检察院应当积极协调办理。

**Article 276** In handling a case involving admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, the People's Procuratorate shall regard as an important factor in making a sentencing recommendation whether the criminal suspect has reached a settlement or mediation agreement with the victim, or has compensated the victim for losses and obtained the victim's understanding, or has voluntarily assumed responsibility for public interest damage remediation and compensation.  
If the criminal suspect voluntarily admits guilt and is willing to actively compensate for losses, but fails to reach a settlement or mediation agreement due to the obviously unreasonable compensation claim of the victim, it shall not generally affect the lenient treatment of the criminal suspect.  
For a public prosecution case that meets the conditions for applying the party settlement procedure, if the criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment, the People's Procuratorate shall actively urge the parties to voluntarily reach a settlement. The settlement agreement and the written understanding opinion issued by the victim shall be transferred with the case. If the victim meets the conditions for judicial assistance, the People's Procuratorate shall actively coordinate the processing.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条  犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚，人民检察院拟提出适用缓刑或者判处管制的量刑建议，可以委托犯罪嫌疑人居住地的社区矫正机构进行调查评估，也可以自行调查评估。

**Article 277** Where a criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment, and the People's Procuratorate intends to make a sentencing recommendation of probation or public surveillance, it may entrust the community correction institution at the place of the criminal suspect's residence to conduct an investigation and evaluation, or may conduct the investigation and evaluation on its own.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条  犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚，人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-177)第二款作出不起诉决定后，犯罪嫌疑人反悔的，人民检察院应当进行审查，并区分下列情形依法作出处理：  
（一）发现犯罪嫌疑人没有犯罪事实，或者符合[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)规定的情形之一的，应当撤销原不起诉决定，依照[刑事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-177)第一款的规定重新作出不起诉决定；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人犯罪情节轻微，依照刑法不需要判处刑罚或者免除刑罚的，可以维持原不起诉决定；  
（三）排除认罪认罚因素后，符合起诉条件的，应当根据案件具体情况撤销原不起诉决定，依法提起公诉。

**Article 278** Where, after the People's Procuratorate makes a decision not to prosecute in accordance with paragraph 2 of Article 177 of the Criminal Procedure Law on the ground that the criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment, the criminal suspect withdraws his or her admission, the People's Procuratorate shall conduct a review and handle the matter according to law by distinguishing the following circumstances:  
(1) If it is found that the criminal suspect has no criminal facts or falls under one of the circumstances provided for in Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law, it shall revoke the original decision not to prosecute and make a new decision not to prosecute in accordance with paragraph 1 of Article 177 of the Criminal Procedure Law;  
(2) If the criminal suspect's crime is minor and no punishment is needed or criminal punishment may be exempted according to the Criminal Law, the original decision not to prosecute may be maintained;  
(3) If, after excluding the factor of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, the conditions for prosecution are met, it shall revoke the original decision not to prosecute according to the specific circumstances of the case and initiate public prosecution according to law.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条  犯罪嫌疑人自愿如实供述涉嫌犯罪的事实，有重大立功或者案件涉及国家重大利益的，经最高人民检察院核准，公安机关可以撤销案件，人民检察院可以作出不起诉决定，也可以对涉嫌数罪中的一项或者多项不起诉。  
前款规定的不起诉，应当由检察长决定。决定不起诉的，人民检察院应当及时对查封、扣押、冻结的财物及其孳息作出处理。

**Article 279** Where a criminal suspect voluntarily and truthfully confesses the facts of the suspected crime, has rendered major meritorious service, or the case involves a major national interest, with the approval of the Supreme People's Procuratorate, the public security organ may withdraw the case, and the People's Procuratorate may make a decision not to prosecute, or may decide not to prosecute one or more of the several crimes suspected.  
A decision not to prosecute as provided for in the preceding paragraph shall be made by the chief procurator. Where a decision not to prosecute is made, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly handle the sealed up, seized, or frozen property and its fruits.

#### 第三节 审查批准逮捕
