<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条  人民检察院办理审查逮捕案件，可以讯问犯罪嫌疑人；具有下列情形之一的，应当讯问犯罪嫌疑人：  
（一）对是否符合逮捕条件有疑问的；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人要求向检察人员当面陈述的；  
（三）侦查活动可能有重大违法行为的；  
（四）案情重大、疑难、复杂的；  
（五）犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚的；  
（六）犯罪嫌疑人系未成年人的；  
（七）犯罪嫌疑人是盲、聋、哑人或者是尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人的。  
讯问未被拘留的犯罪嫌疑人，讯问前应当听取公安机关的意见。  
办理审查逮捕案件，对被拘留的犯罪嫌疑人不予讯问的，应当送达听取犯罪嫌疑人意见书，由犯罪嫌疑人填写后及时收回审查并附卷。经审查认为应当讯问犯罪嫌疑人的，应当及时讯问。

**Article 280** When handling a case of examination for approval of arrest, the People's Procuratorate may interrogate the criminal suspect; under any of the following circumstances, it shall interrogate the criminal suspect:  
(1) There is doubt as to whether the conditions for arrest are met;  
(2) The criminal suspect requests to make a statement in person to the procuratorial personnel;  
(3) The investigation activities may involve major illegal acts;  
(4) The case is major, difficult, or complex;  
(5) The criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment;  
(6) The criminal suspect is a minor;  
(7) The criminal suspect is blind, deaf, or mute, or is a person with a mental illness who has not completely lost the ability to recognize or control his or her conduct.  
If the criminal suspect not in custody is to be interrogated, the opinions of the public security organ shall be heard before interrogation.  
When handling a case of examination for approval of arrest, if a criminal suspect in custody is not interrogated, a form for hearing the criminal suspect's opinion shall be served, and after being filled out by the criminal suspect, it shall be promptly retrieved, examined, and attached to the case file. If, upon examination, it is considered necessary to interrogate the criminal suspect, the interrogation shall be carried out promptly.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条  对有重大影响的案件，可以采取当面听取侦查人员、犯罪嫌疑人及其辩护人等意见的方式进行公开审查。

**Article 281** For a case with significant impact, an open review may be conducted by hearing opinions in person from the investigators, the criminal suspect, and his or her defender, among others.

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条  对公安机关提请批准逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人，已经被拘留的，人民检察院应当在收到提请批准逮捕书后七日以内作出是否批准逮捕的决定；未被拘留的，应当在收到提请批准逮捕书后十五日以内作出是否批准逮捕的决定，重大、复杂案件，不得超过二十日。

**Article 282** For a criminal suspect for whom the public security organ requests approval of arrest, if the suspect has been detained, the People's Procuratorate shall decide whether to approve the arrest within seven days after receiving the request for approval of arrest; if not detained, it shall decide whether to approve the arrest within fifteen days after receiving the request, and for major or complex cases, the time limit shall not exceed twenty days.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条  上级公安机关指定犯罪地或者犯罪嫌疑人居住地以外的下级公安机关立案侦查的案件，需要逮捕犯罪嫌疑人的，由侦查该案件的公安机关提请同级人民检察院审查批准逮捕。人民检察院应当依法作出批准或者不批准逮捕的决定。

**Article 283** Where a higher-level public security organ designates a lower-level public security organ other than that of the place where the crime was committed or the place of the criminal suspect's residence to file and investigate a case, and the arrest of the criminal suspect is necessary, the public security organ investigating the case shall request the People's Procuratorate at the same level to examine and approve the arrest. The People's Procuratorate shall make a decision to approve or disapprove the arrest according to law.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条  对公安机关提请批准逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人，人民检察院经审查认为符合[本规则第一百二十八条](#art-128)、第一百三十六条、第一百三十八条规定情形，应当作出批准逮捕的决定，连同案卷材料送达公安机关执行，并可以制作继续侦查提纲，送交公安机关。

**Article 284** Where, upon examination, the People's Procuratorate finds that a criminal suspect for whom the public security organ requests approval of arrest meets the circumstances provided for in Articles 128, 136, and 138 of these Rules, it shall make a decision to approve the arrest, and deliver the decision together with the case file materials to the public security organ for execution, and may prepare an outline for continued investigation and submit it to the public security organ.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条  对公安机关提请批准逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人，具有[本规则第一百三十九条](#art-139)至第一百四十一条规定情形，人民检察院作出不批准逮捕决定的，应当说明理由，连同案卷材料送达公安机关执行。需要补充侦查的，应当制作补充侦查提纲，送交公安机关。  
人民检察院办理审查逮捕案件，不另行侦查，不得直接提出采取取保候审措施的意见。  
对于因犯罪嫌疑人没有犯罪事实、具有[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)规定的情形之一或者证据不足，人民检察院拟作出不批准逮捕决定的，应当经检察长批准。

**Article 285** Where a criminal suspect for whom the public security organ requests approval of arrest falls under the circumstances provided for in Articles 139 to 141 of these Rules, and the People's Procuratorate decides not to approve the arrest, it shall state the reasons and deliver the decision together with the case file materials to the public security organ for execution. If supplementary investigation is necessary, it shall prepare an outline for supplementary investigation and submit it to the public security organ.  
When handling a case of examination for approval of arrest, the People's Procuratorate shall not conduct a separate investigation and shall not directly make an opinion on adopting the measure of release on bail pending investigation.  
If the People's Procuratorate intends to decide not to approve the arrest because the criminal suspect has no criminal facts, falls under one of the circumstances provided for in Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law, or the evidence is insufficient, it shall obtain approval from the chief procurator.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条  人民检察院应当将批准逮捕的决定交公安机关立即执行，并要求公安机关将执行回执及时送达作出批准决定的人民检察院。如果未能执行，也应当要求其将回执及时送达人民检察院，并写明未能执行的原因。对于人民检察院不批准逮捕的，应当要求公安机关在收到不批准逮捕决定书后，立即释放在押的犯罪嫌疑人或者变更强制措施，并将执行回执在收到不批准逮捕决定书后三日以内送达作出不批准逮捕决定的人民检察院。  
公安机关在收到不批准逮捕决定书后对在押的犯罪嫌疑人不立即释放或者变更强制措施的，人民检察院应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 286** The People's Procuratorate shall deliver the decision to approve the arrest to the public security organ for immediate execution, and require the public security organ to promptly serve the receipt of execution to the People's Procuratorate that made the approval decision. If execution is not possible, it shall also require the public security organ to promptly serve the receipt to the People's Procuratorate and specify the reasons for the failure to execute. Where the People's Procuratorate disapproves the arrest, it shall require the public security organ, upon receipt of the written decision disapproving the arrest, to immediately release the criminal suspect in custody or change the compulsory measures, and serve the receipt of execution to the People's Procuratorate that made the disapproval decision within three days after receiving the written decision disapproving the arrest.  
If the public security organ, after receiving the written decision disapproving the arrest, fails to immediately release the criminal suspect in custody or change the compulsory measures, the People's Procuratorate shall make a suggestion for correction.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条  对于没有犯罪事实或者犯罪嫌疑人具有[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)规定情形之一，人民检察院作出不批准逮捕决定的，应当同时告知公安机关撤销案件。  
对于有犯罪事实需要追究刑事责任，但不是被立案侦查的犯罪嫌疑人实施，或者共同犯罪案件中部分犯罪嫌疑人不负刑事责任，人民检察院作出不批准逮捕决定的，应当同时告知公安机关对有关犯罪嫌疑人终止侦查。  
公安机关在收到不批准逮捕决定书后超过十五日未要求复议、提请复核，也不撤销案件或者终止侦查的，人民检察院应当发出纠正违法通知书。公安机关仍不纠正的，报上一级人民检察院协商同级公安机关处理。

**Article 287** Where there is no criminal fact or the criminal suspect falls under one of the circumstances provided for in Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law, and the People's Procuratorate decides not to approve the arrest, it shall at the same time inform the public security organ to withdraw the case.  
Where there is a criminal fact requiring criminal liability, but it was not committed by the criminal suspect under investigation, or some criminal suspects in a joint crime bear no criminal liability, and the People's Procuratorate decides not to approve the arrest, it shall at the same time inform the public security organ to terminate the investigation of the relevant criminal suspect.  
If the public security organ, after receiving the written decision disapproving the arrest, fails to request review or submit for reconsideration within fifteen days, and also fails to withdraw the case or terminate the investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall issue a notice of correction of illegality. If the public security organ still fails to make corrections, the matter shall be reported to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level for consultation with the public security organ at the same level for handling.

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条  人民检察院办理公安机关提请批准逮捕的案件，发现遗漏应当逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人的，应当经检察长批准，要求公安机关提请批准逮捕。公安机关不提请批准逮捕或者说明的不提请批准逮捕的理由不成立的，人民检察院可以直接作出逮捕决定，送达公安机关执行。

**Article 288** When handling a case in which the public security organ requests approval of arrest, if the People's Procuratorate discovers a criminal suspect who should have been arrested but was missed, it shall, with the approval of the chief procurator, require the public security organ to request approval of arrest. If the public security organ fails to request approval of arrest or the reasons given for not requesting approval of arrest are untenable, the People's Procuratorate may directly make a decision to arrest and serve it on the public security organ for execution.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条  对已经作出的批准逮捕决定发现确有错误的，人民检察院应当撤销原批准逮捕决定，送达公安机关执行。  
对已经作出的不批准逮捕决定发现确有错误，需要批准逮捕的，人民检察院应当撤销原不批准逮捕决定，并重新作出批准逮捕决定，送达公安机关执行。  
对因撤销原批准逮捕决定而被释放的犯罪嫌疑人或者逮捕后公安机关变更为取保候审、监视居住的犯罪嫌疑人，又发现需要逮捕的，人民检察院应当重新办理逮捕手续。

**Article 289** Where a decision to approve arrest that has been made is found to be erroneous, the People's Procuratorate shall revoke the original decision to approve arrest and serve it on the public security organ for execution.  
Where a decision not to approve arrest that has been made is found to be erroneous and approval of arrest is necessary, the People's Procuratorate shall revoke the original decision not to approve arrest and make a new decision to approve arrest, and serve it on the public security organ for execution.  
Where a criminal suspect released due to the revocation of the original decision to approve arrest, or a criminal suspect whose compulsory measure has been changed to release on bail pending investigation or residential surveillance by the public security organ after arrest, is again found to require arrest, the People's Procuratorate shall re-handle the arrest formalities.

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条  对不批准逮捕的案件，公安机关要求复议的，人民检察院负责捕诉的部门应当另行指派检察官或者检察官办案组进行审查，并在收到要求复议意见书和案卷材料后七日以内，经检察长批准，作出是否变更的决定，通知公安机关。

**Article 290** Where the public security organ requests review of a case in which arrest was disapproved, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of the People's Procuratorate shall assign a different prosecutor or a prosecutorial team to conduct the review, and within seven days after receiving the written opinion requesting review and the case file materials, with the approval of the chief procurator, make a decision on whether to change the decision and notify the public security organ.

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条  对不批准逮捕的案件，公安机关提请上一级人民检察院复核的，上一级人民检察院应当在收到提请复核意见书和案卷材料后十五日以内，经检察长批准，作出是否变更的决定，通知下级人民检察院和公安机关执行。需要改变原决定的，应当通知作出不批准逮捕决定的人民检察院撤销原不批准逮捕决定，另行制作批准逮捕决定书。必要时，上级人民检察院也可以直接作出批准逮捕决定，通知下级人民检察院送达公安机关执行。  
对于经复议复核维持原不批准逮捕决定的，人民检察院向公安机关送达复议复核决定时应当说明理由。

**Article 291** . For cases where arrest is disapproved, if the public security organ petitions the next higher People's Procuratorate for review, the next higher People's Procuratorate shall, within 15 days from receipt of the petition for review opinion and case materials, make a decision on whether to change the decision upon the approval of the Chief Procurator, and notify the lower People's Procuratorate and the public security organ for enforcement. If the original decision needs to be changed, it shall notify the People's Procuratorate that made the decision to disapprove the arrest to revoke the original decision to disapprove the arrest and separately issue a decision to approve the arrest. When necessary, the higher People's Procuratorate may directly make a decision to approve the arrest and notify the lower People's Procuratorate to serve it on the public security organ for enforcement.  
For cases where the original decision to disapprove the arrest is maintained upon reconsideration and review, the People's Procuratorate shall state the reasons when serving the reconsideration and review decision on the public security organ.

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条  人民检察院作出不批准逮捕决定，并且通知公安机关补充侦查的案件，公安机关在补充侦查后又要求复议的，人民检察院应当告知公安机关重新提请批准逮捕。公安机关坚持要求复议的，人民检察院不予受理。  
对于公安机关补充侦查后应当提请批准逮捕而不提请批准逮捕的，按照[本规则第二百八十八条](#art-288)的规定办理。

**Article 292** . For a case where the People's Procuratorate has made a decision to disapprove arrest and has notified the public security organ to conduct supplementary investigation, if the public security organ requests reconsideration after completing the supplementary investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall inform the public security organ to re-petition for approval of arrest. If the public security organ insists on requesting reconsideration, the People's Procuratorate shall not accept it.  
If the public security organ should petition for approval of arrest after supplementary investigation but fails to do so, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Article 288 of these Rules.

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条  对公安机关提请批准逮捕的案件，负责捕诉的部门应当将批准、变更、撤销逮捕措施的情况书面通知本院负责刑事执行检察的部门。

**Article 293** . For cases where the public security organ petitions for approval of arrest, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall notify in writing the department responsible for criminal enforcement inspection of this People's Procuratorate of the circumstances of the approval, change, or revocation of the arrest measure.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条  外国人、无国籍人涉嫌危害国家安全犯罪的案件或者涉及国与国之间政治、外交关系的案件以及在适用法律上确有疑难的案件，需要逮捕犯罪嫌疑人的，按照刑事诉讼法关于管辖的规定，分别由基层人民检察院或者设区的市级人民检察院审查并提出意见，层报最高人民检察院审查。最高人民检察院认为需要逮捕的，经征求外交部的意见后，作出批准逮捕的批复；认为不需要逮捕的，作出不批准逮捕的批复。基层人民检察院或者设区的市级人民检察院根据最高人民检察院的批复，依法作出批准或者不批准逮捕的决定。层报过程中，上级人民检察院认为不需要逮捕的，应当作出不批准逮捕的批复。报送的人民检察院根据批复依法作出不批准逮捕的决定。  
基层人民检察院或者设区的市级人民检察院认为不需要逮捕的，可以直接依法作出不批准逮捕的决定。  
外国人、无国籍人涉嫌本条第一款规定以外的其他犯罪案件，决定批准逮捕的人民检察院应当在作出批准逮捕决定后四十八小时以内报上一级人民检察院备案，同时向同级人民政府外事部门通报。上一级人民检察院经审查发现批准逮捕决定错误的，应当依法及时纠正。

**Article 294** . For cases where a foreigner or stateless person is suspected of crimes endangering national security, or cases involving political or diplomatic relations between states, or cases where there is genuine doubt in the application of law, and an arrest of the criminal suspect is needed, the Primary People's Procuratorate or the People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts shall, in accordance with the provisions on jurisdiction of the Criminal Procedure Law, review the case and provide opinions, and report level by level to the Supreme People's Procuratorate for examination. If the Supreme People's Procuratorate deems arrest necessary, it shall, after soliciting the opinions of the Ministry of Foreign Affairs, issue a written reply approving the arrest; if it deems arrest unnecessary, it shall issue a written reply disapproving the arrest. The Primary People's Procuratorate or the People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts shall, based on the reply of the Supreme People's Procuratorate, make a decision to approve or disapprove the arrest according to law. During the process of level-by-level reporting, if the higher People's Procuratorate deems arrest unnecessary, it shall issue a written reply disapproving the arrest. The submitting People's Procuratorate shall make a decision to disapprove the arrest according to law based on the reply.  
If the Primary People's Procuratorate or the People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts deems arrest unnecessary, it may directly make a decision to disapprove the arrest according to law.  
For other criminal cases involving a foreigner or stateless person not covered by the first paragraph of this Article, the People's Procuratorate that decides to approve the arrest shall, within 48 hours after making the decision to approve the arrest, report to the next higher People's Procuratorate for record, and simultaneously notify the foreign affairs department of the people's government at the same level. If the next higher People's Procuratorate, upon examination, finds that the decision to approve the arrest is erroneous, it shall promptly correct it according to law.

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条  人民检察院办理审查逮捕的危害国家安全犯罪案件，应当报上一级人民检察院备案。  
上一级人民检察院经审查发现错误的，应当依法及时纠正。

**Article 295** . When a People's Procuratorate handles a case of a crime endangering national security under review for arrest, it shall report to the next higher People's Procuratorate for record.  
If the next higher People's Procuratorate, upon examination, finds errors, it shall promptly correct them according to law.

#### 第四节 审查决定逮捕

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，需要逮捕犯罪嫌疑人的，由负责侦查的部门制作逮捕犯罪嫌疑人意见书，连同案卷材料、讯问犯罪嫌疑人录音、录像一并移送本院负责捕诉的部门审查。犯罪嫌疑人已被拘留的，负责侦查的部门应当在拘留后七日以内将案件移送本院负责捕诉的部门审查。

**Article 296** . When a People's Procuratorate handles a case directly accepted for investigation and an arrest of the criminal suspect is needed, the department responsible for investigation shall prepare a written opinion on arresting the criminal suspect and transfer it together with the case materials and the audio and video recordings of the interrogation of the criminal suspect to the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of this People's Procuratorate for examination. If the criminal suspect has been detained, the department responsible for investigation shall transfer the case to the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of this People's Procuratorate within seven days after detention.

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条  对本院负责侦查的部门移送审查逮捕的案件，犯罪嫌疑人已被拘留的，负责捕诉的部门应当在收到逮捕犯罪嫌疑人意见书后七日以内，报请检察长决定是否逮捕，特殊情况下，决定逮捕的时间可以延长一日至三日；犯罪嫌疑人未被拘留的，负责捕诉的部门应当在收到逮捕犯罪嫌疑人意见书后十五日以内，报请检察长决定是否逮捕，重大、复杂案件，不得超过二十日。

**Article 297** . For a case transferred by the department responsible for investigation of this People's Procuratorate for examination of arrest, if the criminal suspect has been detained, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall, within seven days from receipt of the written opinion on arresting the criminal suspect, submit to the Chief Procurator a request to decide whether to arrest; under special circumstances, the time for deciding on the arrest may be extended by one to three days. If the criminal suspect has not been detained, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall, within 15 days from receipt of the written opinion on arresting the criminal suspect, submit to the Chief Procurator a request to decide whether to arrest; for major or complex cases, the time shall not exceed 20 days.

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条  对犯罪嫌疑人决定逮捕的，负责捕诉的部门应当将逮捕决定书连同案卷材料、讯问犯罪嫌疑人录音、录像移交负责侦查的部门，并可以对收集证据、适用法律提出意见。由负责侦查的部门通知公安机关执行，必要时可以协助执行。

**Article 298** . If a decision is made to arrest the criminal suspect, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall transfer the arrest decision document together with the case materials and the audio and video recordings of the interrogation of the criminal suspect to the department responsible for investigation, and may provide opinions on evidence collection and application of law. The department responsible for investigation shall notify the public security organ for enforcement, and may assist in enforcement when necessary.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条  对犯罪嫌疑人决定不予逮捕的，负责捕诉的部门应当将不予逮捕的决定连同案卷材料、讯问犯罪嫌疑人录音、录像移交负责侦查的部门，并说明理由。需要补充侦查的，应当制作补充侦查提纲。犯罪嫌疑人已被拘留的，负责侦查的部门应当通知公安机关立即释放。

**Article 299** . If a decision is made not to arrest the criminal suspect, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall transfer the decision not to arrest together with the case materials and the audio and video recordings of the interrogation of the criminal suspect to the department responsible for investigation, and shall state the reasons. If supplementary investigation is needed, it shall prepare an outline for supplementary investigation. If the criminal suspect has been detained, the department responsible for investigation shall notify the public security organ to immediately release him or her.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条  对应当逮捕而本院负责侦查的部门未移送审查逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人，负责捕诉的部门应当向负责侦查的部门提出移送审查逮捕犯罪嫌疑人的建议。建议不被采纳的，应当报请检察长决定。

**Article 300** . If there is a criminal suspect who should be arrested but the department responsible for investigation of this People's Procuratorate has not transferred the case for examination of arrest, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall make a suggestion to the department responsible for investigation to transfer the criminal suspect for examination of arrest. If the suggestion is not accepted, it shall report to the Chief Procurator for a decision.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条  逮捕犯罪嫌疑人后，应当立即送看守所羁押。除无法通知的以外，负责侦查的部门应当把逮捕的原因和羁押的处所，在二十四小时以内通知其家属。对于无法通知的，在无法通知的情形消除后，应当立即通知其家属。

**Article 301** . After arresting the criminal suspect, the suspect shall immediately be sent to a detention house for custody. Except where notification is impossible, the department responsible for investigation shall, within 24 hours, notify the family members of the reasons for arrest and the place of custody. For cases where notification is impossible, once the impossibility is removed, the family members shall be notified immediately.

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条  对被逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人，应当在逮捕后二十四小时以内进行讯问。  
发现不应当逮捕的，应当经检察长批准，撤销逮捕决定或者变更为其他强制措施，并通知公安机关执行，同时通知负责捕诉的部门。  
对按照前款规定被释放或者变更强制措施的犯罪嫌疑人，又发现需要逮捕的，应当重新移送审查逮捕。

**Article 302** . The arrested criminal suspect shall be interrogated within 24 hours after the arrest.  
If it is discovered that the arrest should not have been made, the arrest decision shall be revoked or changed to another compulsory measure upon the approval of the Chief Procurator, and the public security organ shall be notified to enforce it, with simultaneous notification to the department responsible for arrest and prosecution.  
If, in respect of a criminal suspect who has been released or whose compulsory measures have been changed in accordance with the preceding paragraph, it is later discovered that arrest is necessary, the case shall be re-transferred for examination of arrest.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条  已经作出不予逮捕的决定，又发现需要逮捕犯罪嫌疑人的，应当重新办理逮捕手续。

**Article 303** . If a decision not to arrest has been made, and later it is discovered that arrest of the criminal suspect is necessary, arrest procedures shall be handled anew.

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条  犯罪嫌疑人在异地羁押的，负责侦查的部门应当将决定、变更、撤销逮捕措施的情况书面通知羁押地人民检察院负责刑事执行检察的部门。

**Article 304** . If the criminal suspect is in custody in a different location, the department responsible for investigation shall notify in writing the department responsible for criminal enforcement inspection of the People's Procuratorate at the place of custody of the decision, change, or revocation of the arrest measure.

#### 第五节 延长侦查羁押期限和重新计算侦查羁押期限

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条  人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，对犯罪嫌疑人逮捕后的侦查羁押期限不得超过二个月。案情复杂、期限届满不能终结的案件，可以经上一级人民检察院批准延长一个月。

**Article 305** . For a case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate, the period of investigation custody after the arrest of the criminal suspect shall not exceed two months. For a case where the circumstances are complex and the case cannot be concluded upon expiration of the period, the period may be extended by one month with the approval of the next higher People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条  设区的市级人民检察院和基层人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，符合[刑事诉讼法第一百五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-158)规定，在[本规则第三百零五条](#art-305)规定的期限届满前不能侦查终结的，经省级人民检察院批准，可以延长二个月。  
省级人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，有前款情形的，可以直接决定延长二个月。

**Article 306** . For a case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts or a Primary People's Procuratorate, if the conditions provided in Article 158 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met and the investigation cannot be concluded before the expiration of the period provided in Article 305 of these Rules, the period may be extended by two months with the approval of the provincial People's Procuratorate.  
For a case directly accepted for investigation by a provincial People's Procuratorate, if the circumstances in the preceding paragraph exist, it may directly decide to extend the period by two months.

<a id="art-307"></a>第三百零七条  设区的市级人民检察院和基层人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，对犯罪嫌疑人可能判处十年有期徒刑以上刑罚，依照[本规则第三百零六条](#art-306)的规定依法延长羁押期限届满，仍不能侦查终结的，经省级人民检察院批准，可以再延长二个月。  
省级人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，有前款情形的，可以直接决定再延长二个月。

**Article 307** . For a case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate of a city divided into districts or a Primary People's Procuratorate, if the criminal suspect may be sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment of not less than ten years, and the extension of the custody period has expired in accordance with Article 306 of these Rules, but the investigation still cannot be concluded, the period may be further extended by two months with the approval of the provincial People's Procuratorate.  
For a case directly accepted for investigation by a provincial People's Procuratorate, if the circumstances in the preceding paragraph exist, it may directly decide to further extend the period by two months.

<a id="art-308"></a>第三百零八条  最高人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，依照刑事诉讼法的规定需要延长侦查羁押期限的，直接决定延长侦查羁押期限。

**Article 308** . For a case directly accepted for investigation by the Supreme People's Procuratorate, if an extension of the period of investigation custody is needed in accordance with the provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law, the Supreme People's Procuratorate shall directly decide to extend the period of investigation custody.

<a id="art-309"></a>第三百零九条  公安机关需要延长侦查羁押期限的，人民检察院应当要求其在侦查羁押期限届满七日前提请批准延长侦查羁押期限。  
人民检察院办理直接受理侦查的案件，负责侦查的部门认为需要延长侦查羁押期限的，应当按照前款规定向本院负责捕诉的部门移送延长侦查羁押期限意见书及有关材料。  
对于超过法定羁押期限提请延长侦查羁押期限的，不予受理。

**Article 309** . If the public security organ needs to extend the period of investigation custody, the People's Procuratorate shall require it to petition for approval of the extension of the period of investigation custody no later than seven days before the expiration of the period of investigation custody.  
For a case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate, if the department responsible for investigation deems it necessary to extend the period of investigation custody, it shall transfer a written opinion on extending the period of investigation custody and related materials to the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of this People's Procuratorate in accordance with the preceding paragraph.  
A petition for extension of the period of investigation custody made after the expiration of the statutory period of custody shall not be accepted.

<a id="art-310"></a>第三百一十条  人民检察院审查批准或者决定延长侦查羁押期限，由负责捕诉的部门办理。  
受理案件的人民检察院对延长侦查羁押期限的意见审查后，应当提出是否同意延长侦查羁押期限的意见，将公安机关延长侦查羁押期限的意见和本院的审查意见层报有决定权的人民检察院审查决定。

**Article 310** . The examination and approval or decision to extend the period of investigation custody by the People's Procuratorate shall be handled by the department responsible for arrest and prosecution.  
After the People's Procuratorate accepting the case examines the opinions on extending the period of investigation custody, it shall provide its own opinion on whether to agree to the extension, and report level by level the opinion of the public security organ on extension and the examination opinion of this People's Procuratorate to the People's Procuratorate with the power to decide for examination and decision.

<a id="art-311"></a>第三百一十一条  对于同时具备下列条件的案件，人民检察院应当作出批准延长侦查羁押期限一个月的决定：  
（一）符合[刑事诉讼法第一百五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-156)的规定；  
（二）符合逮捕条件；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人有继续羁押的必要。

**Article 311** For a case that concurrently meets the following conditions, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision to approve the extension of the period of custodial investigation by one month:  
(1) It meets the provisions of Article 156 of the Criminal Procedure Law;  
(2) It meets the conditions for arrest;  
(3) There is a need for continued custody of the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-312"></a>第三百一十二条  犯罪嫌疑人虽然符合逮捕条件，但经审查，公安机关在对犯罪嫌疑人执行逮捕后二个月以内未有效开展侦查工作或者侦查取证工作没有实质进展的，人民检察院可以作出不批准延长侦查羁押期限的决定。  
犯罪嫌疑人不符合逮捕条件，需要撤销下级人民检察院逮捕决定的，上级人民检察院在作出不批准延长侦查羁押期限决定的同时，应当作出撤销逮捕的决定，或者通知下级人民检察院撤销逮捕决定。

**Article 312** Where the criminal suspect meets the conditions for arrest, but upon examination, the public security organ has not effectively carried out investigation work or has made no substantial progress in investigation and evidence collection within two months after executing the arrest on the criminal suspect, the People's Procuratorate may make a decision not to approve the extension of the period of custodial investigation.  
If the criminal suspect does not meet the conditions for arrest and it is necessary to revoke the arrest decision of the lower people's procuratorate, the higher people's procuratorate, when making the decision not to approve the extension of the period of custodial investigation, shall also make a decision to revoke the arrest, or notify the lower people's procuratorate to revoke the arrest decision.

<a id="art-313"></a>第三百一十三条  有决定权的人民检察院作出批准延长侦查羁押期限或者不批准延长侦查羁押期限的决定后，应当将决定书交由最初受理案件的人民检察院送达公安机关。  
最初受理案件的人民检察院负责捕诉的部门收到批准延长侦查羁押期限决定书或者不批准延长侦查羁押期限决定书，应当书面告知本院负责刑事执行检察的部门。

**Article 313** After the People's Procuratorate with decision-making power makes a decision to approve or not to approve the extension of the period of custodial investigation, it shall deliver the decision document to the People's Procuratorate that initially accepted the case for service upon the public security organ.  
Upon receiving the decision document approving or not approving the extension of the period of custodial investigation, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of the People's Procuratorate that initially accepted the case shall inform in writing the department responsible for criminal execution supervision of that procuratorate.

<a id="art-314"></a>第三百一十四条  因为特殊原因，在较长时间内不宜交付审判的特别重大复杂的案件，由最高人民检察院报请全国人民代表大会常务委员会批准延期审理。

**Article 314** For particularly major and complex cases that, due to special reasons, are not suitable to be brought to trial within a relatively long period, the Supreme People's Procuratorate shall submit a request to the Standing Committee of the National People's Congress for approval to postpone the trial.

<a id="art-315"></a>第三百一十五条  人民检察院在侦查期间发现犯罪嫌疑人另有重要罪行的，自发现之日起依照[本规则第三百零五条](#art-305)的规定重新计算侦查羁押期限。  
另有重要罪行是指与逮捕时的罪行不同种的重大犯罪或者同种的影响罪名认定、量刑档次的重大犯罪。

**Article 315** If the People's Procuratorate discovers during the investigation that the criminal suspect has committed another major crime, the period of custodial investigation shall be recalculated from the date of discovery in accordance with Article 305 of these Rules.  
"Another major crime" means a major crime of a different type from the crime for which the arrest was made, or a major crime of the same type that affects the determination of the charge or the sentencing level.

<a id="art-316"></a>第三百一十六条  人民检察院重新计算侦查羁押期限，应当由负责侦查的部门提出重新计算侦查羁押期限的意见，移送本院负责捕诉的部门审查。负责捕诉的部门审查后应当提出是否同意重新计算侦查羁押期限的意见，报检察长决定。

**Article 316** To recalculate the period of custodial investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall have the department responsible for investigation submit an opinion on recalculating the period of custodial investigation to the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of that procuratorate for review. After review, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall submit an opinion on whether to approve the recalculation of the period of custodial investigation to the Chief Prosecutor for a decision.

<a id="art-317"></a>第三百一十七条  对公安机关重新计算侦查羁押期限的备案，由负责捕诉的部门审查。负责捕诉的部门认为公安机关重新计算侦查羁押期限不当的，应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 317** The filing of the public security organ's recalculation of the period of custodial investigation shall be reviewed by the department responsible for arrest and prosecution. If the department responsible for arrest and prosecution considers that the public security organ's recalculation of the period of custodial investigation is improper, it shall raise a corrective opinion.

<a id="art-318"></a>第三百一十八条  人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，不能在法定侦查羁押期限内侦查终结的，应当依法释放犯罪嫌疑人或者变更强制措施。

**Article 318** For cases directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate, if the investigation cannot be concluded within the statutory period of custodial investigation, the criminal suspect shall be released or compulsory measures shall be changed according to law.

<a id="art-319"></a>第三百一十九条  负责捕诉的部门审查延长侦查羁押期限、审查重新计算侦查羁押期限，可以讯问犯罪嫌疑人，听取辩护律师和侦查人员的意见，调取案卷及相关材料等。

**Article 319** When reviewing the extension of the period of custodial investigation or reviewing the recalculation of the period of custodial investigation, the department responsible for arrest and prosecution may interrogate the criminal suspect, hear opinions from the defense lawyer and the investigator, and obtain case files and relevant materials, among other actions.

#### 第六节 核准追诉

<a id="art-320"></a>第三百二十条  法定最高刑为无期徒刑、死刑的犯罪，已过二十年追诉期限的，不再追诉。如果认为必须追诉的，须报请最高人民检察院核准。

**Article 320** For crimes for which the statutory maximum punishment is life imprisonment or the death penalty, if the twenty-year limitation period for prosecution has expired, no prosecution shall be instituted. If it is deemed necessary to prosecute, approval must be obtained from the Supreme People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-321"></a>第三百二十一条  须报请最高人民检察院核准追诉的案件，公安机关在核准之前可以依法对犯罪嫌疑人采取强制措施。  
公安机关报请核准追诉并提请逮捕犯罪嫌疑人，人民检察院经审查认为必须追诉而且符合法定逮捕条件的，可以依法批准逮捕，同时要求公安机关在报请核准追诉期间不得停止对案件的侦查。  
未经最高人民检察院核准，不得对案件提起公诉。

**Article 321** For a case that requires a report to the Supreme People's Procuratorate for approval of prosecution, the public security organ may, before approval, take compulsory measures against the criminal suspect according to law.  
If the public security organ reports for approval of prosecution and requests the arrest of the criminal suspect, and after review, the People's Procuratorate considers that prosecution is necessary and the statutory conditions for arrest are met, it may approve the arrest according to law, while requiring that the public security organ shall not cease the investigation of the case during the period of reporting for approval of prosecution.  
Without approval by the Supreme People's Procuratorate, no public prosecution shall be instituted for the case.

<a id="art-322"></a>第三百二十二条  报请核准追诉的案件应当同时符合下列条件：  
（一）有证据证明存在犯罪事实，且犯罪事实是犯罪嫌疑人实施的；  
（二）涉嫌犯罪的行为应当适用的法定量刑幅度的最高刑为无期徒刑或者死刑；  
（三）涉嫌犯罪的性质、情节和后果特别严重，虽然已过二十年追诉期限，但社会危害性和影响依然存在，不追诉会严重影响社会稳定或者产生其他严重后果，而必须追诉的；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人能够及时到案接受追诉。

**Article 322** A case reported for approval of prosecution shall concurrently meet the following conditions:  
(1) There is evidence proving the existence of criminal facts and that the criminal facts were committed by the criminal suspect;  
(2) The maximum statutory punishment for the sentencing range applicable to the suspected criminal conduct is life imprisonment or the death penalty;  
(3) The nature, circumstances, and consequences of the suspected crime are particularly serious. Although the twenty-year limitation period for prosecution has expired, the social harmfulness and impact still exist, and failure to prosecute would seriously affect social stability or cause other serious consequences, making prosecution necessary;  
(4) The criminal suspect is able to appear for prosecution in a timely manner.

<a id="art-323"></a>第三百二十三条  公安机关报请核准追诉的案件，由同级人民检察院受理并层报最高人民检察院审查决定。

**Article 323** A case reported by a public security organ for approval of prosecution shall be accepted by the People's Procuratorate at the same level and reported level by level to the Supreme People's Procuratorate for review and decision.

<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条  地方各级人民检察院对公安机关报请核准追诉的案件，应当及时进行审查并开展必要的调查。经检察委员会审议提出是否同意核准追诉的意见，制作报请核准追诉案件报告书，连同案卷材料一并层报最高人民检察院。

**Article 324** Local people's procuratorates at all levels shall promptly review cases reported by public security organs for approval of prosecution and conduct necessary investigations. After deliberation by the Procuratorial Committee, an opinion on whether to approve the prosecution shall be proposed, and a report on the case for approval of prosecution shall be prepared and reported level by level to the Supreme People's Procuratorate together with the case file materials.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条  最高人民检察院收到省级人民检察院报送的报请核准追诉案件报告书及案卷材料后，应当及时审查，必要时指派检察人员到案发地了解案件有关情况。经检察长批准，作出是否核准追诉的决定，并制作核准追诉决定书或者不予核准追诉决定书，逐级下达至最初受理案件的人民检察院，由其送达报请核准追诉的公安机关。

**Article 325** After receiving the report on the case for approval of prosecution and the case file materials submitted by a provincial people's procuratorate, the Supreme People's Procuratorate shall promptly review them and, if necessary, assign procuratorial personnel to the place where the crime occurred to understand the relevant circumstances of the case. With the approval of the Chief Prosecutor, a decision on whether to approve the prosecution shall be made, and a decision document approving the prosecution or a decision document disapproving the prosecution shall be prepared and transmitted level by level to the People's Procuratorate that initially accepted the case, which shall serve it upon the public security organ that reported for approval of prosecution.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条  对已经采取强制措施的案件，强制措施期限届满不能作出是否核准追诉决定的，应当对犯罪嫌疑人变更强制措施或者延长侦查羁押期限。

**Article 326** For a case where compulsory measures have already been taken, if a decision on whether to approve prosecution cannot be made before the expiration of the compulsory measures period, compulsory measures against the criminal suspect shall be changed or the period of custodial investigation shall be extended.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条  最高人民检察院决定核准追诉的案件，最初受理案件的人民检察院应当监督公安机关的侦查工作。  
最高人民检察院决定不予核准追诉，公安机关未及时撤销案件的，同级人民检察院应当提出纠正意见。犯罪嫌疑人在押的，应当立即释放。

**Article 327** For a case where the Supreme People's Procuratorate decides to approve prosecution, the People's Procuratorate that initially accepted the case shall supervise the investigation work of the public security organ.  
If the Supreme People's Procuratorate decides not to approve prosecution and the public security organ fails to withdraw the case in a timely manner, the People's Procuratorate at the same level shall raise a corrective opinion. If the criminal suspect is in custody, he or she shall be released immediately.

#### 第七节 审查起诉

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条  各级人民检察院提起公诉，应当与人民法院审判管辖相适应。负责捕诉的部门收到移送起诉的案件后，经审查认为不属于本院管辖的，应当在发现之日起五日以内经由负责案件管理的部门移送有管辖权的人民检察院。  
属于上级人民法院管辖的第一审案件，应当报送上级人民检察院，同时通知移送起诉的公安机关；属于同级其他人民法院管辖的第一审案件，应当移送有管辖权的人民检察院或者报送共同的上级人民检察院指定管辖，同时通知移送起诉的公安机关。  
上级人民检察院受理同级公安机关移送起诉的案件，认为属于下级人民法院管辖的，可以交下级人民检察院审查，由下级人民检察院向同级人民法院提起公诉，同时通知移送起诉的公安机关。  
一人犯数罪、共同犯罪和其他需要并案审理的案件，只要其中一人或者一罪属于上级人民检察院管辖的，全案由上级人民检察院审查起诉。  
公安机关移送起诉的案件，需要依照刑事诉讼法的规定指定审判管辖的，人民检察院应当在公安机关移送起诉前协商同级人民法院办理指定管辖有关事宜。

**Article 328** The institution of public prosecution by people's procuratorates at all levels shall be consistent with the trial jurisdiction of the people's courts. After receiving a case transferred for prosecution, if upon review the department responsible for arrest and prosecution considers that the case is not within its jurisdiction, it shall, within five days from the date of discovery, transfer the case through the department responsible for case management to the People's Procuratorate with jurisdiction.  
If the case is a first-instance case within the jurisdiction of a higher people's court, it shall be submitted to the higher people's procuratorate, and the public security organ that transferred the case for prosecution shall be notified at the same time; if the case is a first-instance case within the jurisdiction of another people's court at the same level, it shall be transferred to the People's Procuratorate with jurisdiction or submitted to the common higher people's procuratorate for designation of jurisdiction, and the public security organ that transferred the case for prosecution shall be notified at the same time.  
If a higher people's procuratorate accepts a case transferred for prosecution by a public security organ at the same level and considers that it is within the jurisdiction of a lower people's court, it may refer the case to a lower people's procuratorate for review, and the lower people's procuratorate shall institute public prosecution in the people's court at the same level, while notifying the public security organ that transferred the case for prosecution.  
For cases where one person commits multiple crimes, joint crimes, or other cases requiring consolidated trial, if any one person or any one crime falls within the jurisdiction of a higher people's procuratorate, the entire case shall be reviewed and prosecuted by the higher people's procuratorate.  
For a case transferred for prosecution by a public security organ that requires the designation of trial jurisdiction in accordance with the provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Procuratorate shall, before the public security organ transfers the case for prosecution, consult with the people's court at the same level on matters related to the designation of jurisdiction.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条  监察机关移送起诉的案件，需要依照刑事诉讼法的规定指定审判管辖的，人民检察院应当在监察机关移送起诉二十日前协商同级人民法院办理指定管辖有关事宜。

**Article 329** If a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ requires the designation of trial jurisdiction in accordance with the provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Procuratorate shall, twenty days before the supervisory organ transfers the case for prosecution, consult with the people's court at the same level on matters related to the designation of jurisdiction.

<a id="art-330"></a>第三百三十条  人民检察院审查移送起诉的案件，应当查明：  
（一）犯罪嫌疑人身份状况是否清楚，包括姓名、性别、国籍、出生年月日、职业和单位等；单位犯罪的，单位的相关情况是否清楚；  
（二）犯罪事实、情节是否清楚；实施犯罪的时间、地点、手段、危害后果是否明确；  
（三）认定犯罪性质和罪名的意见是否正确；有无法定的从重、从轻、减轻或者免除处罚情节及酌定从重、从轻情节；共同犯罪案件的犯罪嫌疑人在犯罪活动中的责任认定是否恰当；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人是否认罪认罚；  
（五）证明犯罪事实的证据材料是否随案移送；证明相关财产系违法所得的证据材料是否随案移送；不宜移送的证据的清单、复制件、照片或者其他证明文件是否随案移送；  
（六）证据是否确实、充分，是否依法收集，有无应当排除非法证据的情形；  
（七）采取侦查措施包括技术侦查措施的法律手续和诉讼文书是否完备；  
（八）有无遗漏罪行和其他应当追究刑事责任的人；  
（九）是否属于不应当追究刑事责任的；  
（十）有无附带民事诉讼；对于国家财产、集体财产遭受损失的，是否需要由人民检察院提起附带民事诉讼；对于破坏生态环境和资源保护，食品药品安全领域侵害众多消费者合法权益，侵害英雄烈士的姓名、肖像、名誉、荣誉等损害社会公共利益的行为，是否需要由人民检察院提起附带民事公益诉讼；  
（十一）采取的强制措施是否适当，对于已经逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人，有无继续羁押的必要；  
（十二）侦查活动是否合法；  
（十三）涉案财物是否查封、扣押、冻结并妥善保管，清单是否齐备；对被害人合法财产的返还和对违禁品或者不宜长期保存的物品的处理是否妥当，移送的证明文件是否完备。

**Article 330** When reviewing a case transferred for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall ascertain:  
(1) Whether the identity status of the criminal suspect is clear, including name, gender, nationality, date of birth, occupation, and work unit; if it is a unit crime, whether the relevant information of the unit is clear;  
(2) Whether the criminal facts and circumstances are clear; whether the time, place, means, and harmful consequences of the crime are definite;  
(3) Whether the opinion on the nature of the crime and the charge is correct; whether there are statutory circumstances for heavier, lighter, mitigated, or exempted punishment and discretionary circumstances for heavier or lighter punishment; whether the determination of responsibility of the criminal suspect in the criminal activities in a joint crime case is appropriate;  
(4) Whether the criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment;  
(5) Whether the evidentiary materials proving the criminal facts are transferred with the case; whether the evidentiary materials proving that the relevant property is illegal gains are transferred with the case; whether the list, copies, photographs, or other certification documents of evidence that are not suitable for transfer are transferred with the case;  
(6) Whether the evidence is reliable and sufficient, whether it was collected according to law, and whether there are circumstances where illegal evidence should be excluded;  
(7) Whether the legal formalities and litigation documents for the investigation measures, including technical investigation measures, are complete;  
(8) Whether there are any omitted crimes or other persons who should be investigated for criminal liability;  
(9) Whether the case is one where criminal liability should not be investigated;  
(10) Whether there is an incidental civil action; if state property or collective property has suffered losses, whether it is necessary for the People's Procuratorate to initiate an incidental civil action; for acts that damage the ecological environment and resource protection, infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of many consumers in the fields of food and drug safety, or harm the name, portrait, reputation, honor, etc., of heroes and martyrs, thereby harming social public interests, whether it is necessary for the People's Procuratorate to initiate an incidental civil public interest action;  
(11) Whether the compulsory measures taken are appropriate; for a criminal suspect who has already been arrested, whether there is a need for continued custody;  
(12) Whether the investigation activities are lawful;  
(13) Whether the property involved in the case has been sealed, seized, frozen, and properly preserved, and whether the list is complete; whether the return of the lawful property of the victim and the disposition of prohibited goods or items not suitable for long-term preservation are appropriate, and whether the transferred certification documents are complete.

<a id="art-331"></a>第三百三十一条  人民检察院办理审查起诉案件应当讯问犯罪嫌疑人。

**Article 331** When handling a case for examination before prosecution, a People's Procuratorate shall interrogate the criminal suspect.

<a id="art-332"></a>第三百三十二条  人民检察院认为需要对案件中某些专门性问题进行鉴定而监察机关或者公安机关没有鉴定的，应当要求监察机关或者公安机关进行鉴定。必要时，也可以由人民检察院进行鉴定，或者由人民检察院聘请有鉴定资格的人进行鉴定。  
人民检察院自行进行鉴定的，可以商请监察机关或者公安机关派员参加，必要时可以聘请有鉴定资格或者有专门知识的人参加。

**Article 332** Where a People's Procuratorate deems it necessary to conduct an expert evaluation on certain specialized issues in a case but a supervisory organ or a public security organ has not done so, it shall request the supervisory organ or the public security organ to conduct such evaluation. When necessary, the People's Procuratorate may also conduct the evaluation itself, or appoint a person with the qualifications for expert evaluation to conduct it.  
Where a People's Procuratorate conducts the expert evaluation on its own, it may consult with the supervisory organ or the public security organ to send personnel to participate, and when necessary, may appoint a person with the qualifications for expert evaluation or with specialized knowledge to participate.

<a id="art-333"></a>第三百三十三条  在审查起诉中，发现犯罪嫌疑人可能患有精神病的，人民检察院应当依照本规则的有关规定对犯罪嫌疑人进行鉴定。  
犯罪嫌疑人的辩护人或者近亲属以犯罪嫌疑人可能患有精神病而申请对犯罪嫌疑人进行鉴定的，人民检察院也可以依照本规则的有关规定对犯罪嫌疑人进行鉴定。鉴定费用由申请方承担。

**Article 333** Where during examination before prosecution it is discovered that a criminal suspect may be suffering from a mental illness, the People's Procuratorate shall conduct an expert evaluation of the criminal suspect in accordance with the relevant provisions of these Rules.  
Where the defender or a close relative of the criminal suspect applies for an expert evaluation of the criminal suspect on the ground that the criminal suspect may be suffering from a mental illness, the People's Procuratorate may also conduct an expert evaluation of the criminal suspect in accordance with the relevant provisions of these Rules. The costs of the expert evaluation shall be borne by the applying party.

<a id="art-334"></a>第三百三十四条  人民检察院对鉴定意见有疑问的，可以询问鉴定人或者有专门知识的人并制作笔录附卷，也可以指派有鉴定资格的检察技术人员或者聘请其他有鉴定资格的人进行补充鉴定或者重新鉴定。  
人民检察院对鉴定意见等技术性证据材料需要进行专门审查的，按照有关规定交检察技术人员或者其他有专门知识的人进行审查并出具审查意见。

**Article 334** Where a People's Procuratorate has doubts about an expert opinion, it may question the expert or a person with specialized knowledge and make a record of such questioning to be attached to the case file, and may also assign a procuratorial technician qualified for expert evaluation or appoint another person with the qualifications for expert evaluation to conduct supplementary evaluation or re-evaluation.  
Where a People's Procuratorate needs to conduct specialized review of technical evidentiary materials such as an expert opinion, it shall, in accordance with relevant provisions, refer the matter to a procuratorial technician or another person with specialized knowledge for review and for the issuance of a review opinion.

<a id="art-335"></a>第三百三十五条  人民检察院审查案件时，对监察机关或者公安机关的勘验、检查，认为需要复验、复查的，应当要求其复验、复查，人民检察院可以派员参加；也可以自行复验、复查，商请监察机关或者公安机关派员参加，必要时也可以指派检察技术人员或者聘请其他有专门知识的人参加。

**Article 335** When reviewing a case, where a People's Procuratorate deems it necessary to re-inspect or re-examine the inspection or examination conducted by a supervisory organ or a public security organ, it shall request the organ to conduct such re-inspection or re-examination, and the People's Procuratorate may send personnel to participate; it may also conduct re-inspection or re-examination on its own, and consult with the supervisory organ or the public security organ to send personnel to participate, and when necessary, may assign procuratorial technicians or appoint other persons with specialized knowledge to participate.

<a id="art-336"></a>第三百三十六条  人民检察院对物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据及勘验、检查、辨认、侦查实验等笔录存在疑问的，可以要求调查人员或者侦查人员提供获取、制作的有关情况，必要时也可以询问提供相关证据材料的人员和见证人并制作笔录附卷，对物证、书证、视听资料、电子数据进行鉴定。

**Article 336** Where a People's Procuratorate has doubts about physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, electronic data, or records of inspection, examination, identification, or investigation experiments, it may request the investigator or investigator to provide relevant information on the acquisition and production, and when necessary, may also question the persons who provided the relevant evidentiary materials and the witnesses, make a record to be attached to the case file, and conduct expert evaluations on the physical evidence, documentary evidence, audio-visual materials, and electronic data.

<a id="art-337"></a>第三百三十七条  人民检察院在审查起诉阶段认为需要逮捕犯罪嫌疑人的，应当经检察长决定。

**Article 337** Where a People's Procuratorate, during the stage of examination before prosecution, deems it necessary to arrest a criminal suspect, the decision shall be made by the chief procurator.

<a id="art-338"></a>第三百三十八条  对于人民检察院正在审查起诉的案件，被逮捕的犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人认为羁押期限届满，向人民检察院提出释放犯罪嫌疑人或者变更强制措施要求的，人民检察院应当在三日以内审查决定。经审查，认为法定期限届满的，应当决定释放或者依法变更强制措施，并通知公安机关执行；认为法定期限未满的，书面答复申请人。

**Article 338** For a case being examined before prosecution by a People's Procuratorate, where the arrested criminal suspect, his or her legal representative, close relative, or defender considers that the custody period has expired and requests the People's Procuratorate to release the criminal suspect or change the compulsory measures, the People's Procuratorate shall examine and decide within three days. Upon examination, if it deems that the statutory period has expired, it shall decide to release or change the compulsory measures according to law, and notify the public security organ for enforcement; if it deems that the statutory period has not expired, it shall reply in writing to the applicant.

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条  人民检察院对案件进行审查后，应当依法作出起诉或者不起诉以及是否提起附带民事诉讼、附带民事公益诉讼的决定。

**Article 339** After examining a case, a People's Procuratorate shall, according to law, make a decision on whether to initiate a prosecution or not to prosecute, and whether to institute an incidental civil action or an incidental civil public interest action.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条  人民检察院对监察机关或者公安机关移送的案件进行审查后，在人民法院作出生效判决之前，认为需要补充提供证据材料的，可以书面要求监察机关或者公安机关提供。

**Article 340** After examining a case transferred by a supervisory organ or a public security organ, a People's Procuratorate, before the People's Court renders a legally effective judgment, if it deems it necessary to supplement evidentiary materials, may request in writing the supervisory organ or the public security organ to provide them.

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条  人民检察院在审查起诉中发现有应当排除的非法证据，应当依法排除，同时可以要求监察机关或者公安机关另行指派调查人员或者侦查人员重新取证。必要时，人民检察院也可以自行调查取证。

**Article 341** Where a People's Procuratorate discovers during examination before prosecution that there is illegally obtained evidence which shall be excluded, it shall exclude it according to law, and may at the same time request the supervisory organ or the public security organ to designate another investigator or investigator to collect evidence anew. When necessary, the People's Procuratorate may also investigate and collect evidence on its own.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条  人民检察院认为犯罪事实不清、证据不足或者存在遗漏罪行、遗漏同案犯罪嫌疑人等情形需要补充侦查的，应当制作补充侦查提纲，连同案卷材料一并退回公安机关补充侦查。人民检察院也可以自行侦查，必要时可以要求公安机关提供协助。

**Article 342** Where a People's Procuratorate deems that the criminal facts are unclear, the evidence is insufficient, or there are circumstances such as missing crimes or missing co-defendants requiring supplementary investigation, it shall prepare an outline of supplementary investigation and, together with the case file materials, remand the case to the public security organ for supplementary investigation. The People's Procuratorate may also conduct the investigation on its own, and when necessary, may request the public security organ to provide assistance.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条  人民检察院对于监察机关移送起诉的案件，认为需要补充调查的，应当退回监察机关补充调查。必要时，可以自行补充侦查。  
需要退回补充调查的案件，人民检察院应当出具补充调查决定书、补充调查提纲，写明补充调查的事项、理由、调查方向、需补充收集的证据及其证明作用等，连同案卷材料一并送交监察机关。  
人民检察院决定退回补充调查的案件，犯罪嫌疑人已被采取强制措施的，应当将退回补充调查情况书面通知强制措施执行机关。监察机关需要讯问的，人民检察院应当予以配合。

**Article 343** Where a People's Procuratorate deems that a case transferred by a supervisory organ for prosecution requires supplementary investigation, it shall remand the case to the supervisory organ for supplementary investigation. When necessary, it may conduct supplementary investigation on its own.  
For a case that needs to be remanded for supplementary investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall issue a decision on supplementary investigation and an outline of supplementary investigation, specifying the matters, reasons, direction of the investigation, evidence to be supplemented and its probative value, etc., and deliver them together with the case file materials to the supervisory organ.  
Where a People's Procuratorate decides to remand a case for supplementary investigation and the criminal suspect has been subjected to compulsory measures, it shall notify the enforcement organ of the compulsory measures in writing of the circumstances of the remand for supplementary investigation. If the supervisory organ needs to interrogate, the People's Procuratorate shall cooperate.

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条  对于监察机关移送起诉的案件，具有下列情形之一的，人民检察院可以自行补充侦查：  
（一）证人证言、犯罪嫌疑人供述和辩解、被害人陈述的内容主要情节一致，个别情节不一致的；  
（二）物证、书证等证据材料需要补充鉴定的；  
（三）其他由人民检察院查证更为便利、更有效率、更有利于查清案件事实的情形。  
自行补充侦查完毕后，应当将相关证据材料入卷，同时抄送监察机关。人民检察院自行补充侦查的，可以商请监察机关提供协助。

**Article 344** For a case transferred by a supervisory organ for prosecution, under any of the following circumstances, a People's Procuratorate may conduct supplementary investigation on its own:  
(1) The main facts in the witness testimony, the criminal suspect's confession and defense, and the victim's statement are consistent, but individual details are inconsistent;  
(2) Physical evidence, documentary evidence, and other evidentiary materials need supplementary expert evaluation;  
(3) Other circumstances where it is more convenient, more efficient, or more conducive to ascertaining the facts of the case for the People's Procuratorate to investigate.  
After the supplementary investigation on its own is completed, the relevant evidentiary materials shall be placed in the case file, and a copy shall be sent to the supervisory organ. Where a People's Procuratorate conducts supplementary investigation on its own, it may consult with the supervisory organ to provide assistance.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条  人民检察院负责捕诉的部门对本院负责侦查的部门移送起诉的案件进行审查后，认为犯罪事实不清、证据不足或者存在遗漏罪行、遗漏同案犯罪嫌疑人等情形需要补充侦查的，应当制作补充侦查提纲，连同案卷材料一并退回负责侦查的部门补充侦查。必要时，也可以自行侦查，可以要求负责侦查的部门予以协助。

**Article 345** After the department of a People's Procuratorate responsible for prosecution and arrest examines a case transferred for prosecution by the department of the same Procuratorate responsible for investigation, if it deems that the criminal facts are unclear, the evidence is insufficient, or there are circumstances such as missing crimes or missing co-defendants requiring supplementary investigation, it shall prepare an outline of supplementary investigation and, together with the case file materials, remand the case to the department responsible for investigation for supplementary investigation. When necessary, it may also conduct investigation on its own, and may request the department responsible for investigation to provide assistance.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条  退回监察机关补充调查、退回公安机关补充侦查的案件，均应当在一个月以内补充调查、补充侦查完毕。  
补充调查、补充侦查以二次为限。  
补充调查、补充侦查完毕移送起诉后，人民检察院重新计算审查起诉期限。  
人民检察院负责捕诉的部门退回本院负责侦查的部门补充侦查的期限、次数按照本条第一款至第三款的规定执行。

**Article 346** Cases remanded to a supervisory organ for supplementary investigation or to a public security organ for supplementary investigation shall be completed within one month.  
Supplementary investigation or supplementary investigation shall be limited to two times.  
After the supplementary investigation or supplementary investigation is completed and the case is transferred for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall recalculate the period for examination before prosecution.  
The period and number of times for the department of a People's Procuratorate responsible for prosecution and arrest to remand a case to the department of the same Procuratorate responsible for investigation for supplementary investigation shall be executed in accordance with paragraphs 1 through 3 of this Article.

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条  补充侦查期限届满，公安机关未将案件重新移送起诉的，人民检察院应当要求公安机关说明理由。  
人民检察院发现公安机关违反法律规定撤销案件的，应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 347** Where the period for supplementary investigation expires and the public security organ has not re-transferred the case for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall request the public security organ to state the reasons.  
Where a People's Procuratorate discovers that a public security organ has withdrawn a case in violation of legal provisions, it shall raise a correction opinion.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条  人民检察院在审查起诉中决定自行侦查的，应当在审查起诉期限内侦查完毕。

**Article 348** Where a People's Procuratorate decides to conduct its own investigation during examination before prosecution, it shall complete the investigation within the period for examination before prosecution.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条  人民检察院对已经退回监察机关二次补充调查或者退回公安机关二次补充侦查的案件，在审查起诉中又发现新的犯罪事实，应当将线索移送监察机关或者公安机关。对已经查清的犯罪事实，应当依法提起公诉。

**Article 349** Where a People's Procuratorate, during examination before prosecution of a case that has already been remanded to a supervisory organ for a second supplementary investigation or to a public security organ for a second supplementary investigation, discovers new criminal facts, it shall transfer the leads to the supervisory organ or the public security organ. For the criminal facts that have been ascertained, it shall initiate a public prosecution according to law.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条  对于在审查起诉期间改变管辖的案件，改变后的人民检察院对于符合[刑事诉讼法第一百七十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-175)第二款规定的案件，可以经原受理案件的人民检察院协助，直接退回原侦查案件的公安机关补充侦查，也可以自行侦查。改变管辖前后退回补充侦查的次数总共不得超过二次。

**Article 350** For a case where jurisdiction is changed during the period of examination before prosecution, the People's Procuratorate after the change, with respect to a case that meets the requirements of paragraph 2 of Article 175 of the Criminal Procedure Law, may, with the assistance of the People's Procuratorate that originally accepted the case, directly remand the case to the public security organ that originally investigated it for supplementary investigation, or may conduct the investigation on its own. The total number of times the case is remanded for supplementary investigation before and after the change of jurisdiction shall not exceed two.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条  人民检察院对于移送起诉的案件，应当在一个月以内作出决定；重大、复杂的案件，一个月以内不能作出决定的，可以延长十五日。  
人民检察院审查起诉的案件，改变管辖的，从改变后的人民检察院收到案件之日起计算审查起诉期限。

**Article 351** : The People's Procuratorate shall make a decision within one month for a case transferred for prosecution; for a major or complex case, if a decision cannot be made within one month, the period may be extended by fifteen days.  
For a case under review for prosecution by a People's Procuratorate, if the jurisdiction is changed, the time limit for review for prosecution shall be calculated from the date when the People's Procuratorate after the change receives the case.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条  追缴的财物中，属于被害人的合法财产，不需要在法庭出示的，应当及时返还被害人，并由被害人在发还款物清单上签名或者盖章，注明返还的理由，并将清单、照片附卷。

**Article 352** : Among the recovered property, property that is lawfully owned by the victim and does not need to be produced in court shall be returned promptly to the victim, and the victim shall sign or affix his seal on the list of returned property, specify the reason for the return, and the list and photographs shall be attached to the case file.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条  追缴的财物中，属于违禁品或者不宜长期保存的物品，应当依照国家有关规定处理，并将清单、照片、处理结果附卷。

**Article 353** : Among the recovered property, items that are contraband or not suitable for long-term preservation shall be disposed of in accordance with the relevant provisions of the state, and the list, photographs, and disposal results shall be attached to the case file.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条  人民检察院在审查起诉阶段，可以适用本规则规定的侦查措施和程序。

**Article 354** : During the stage of review for prosecution, a People's Procuratorate may apply the investigation measures and procedures provided in these Rules.

#### 第八节 起 诉

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条  人民检察院认为犯罪嫌疑人的犯罪事实已经查清，证据确实、充分，依法应当追究刑事责任的，应当作出起诉决定。  
具有下列情形之一的，可以认为犯罪事实已经查清：  
（一）属于单一罪行的案件，查清的事实足以定罪量刑或者与定罪量刑有关的事实已经查清，不影响定罪量刑的事实无法查清的；  
（二）属于数个罪行的案件，部分罪行已经查清并符合起诉条件，其他罪行无法查清的；  
（三）无法查清作案工具、赃物去向，但有其他证据足以对被告人定罪量刑的；  
（四）证人证言、犯罪嫌疑人供述和辩解、被害人陈述的内容主要情节一致，个别情节不一致，但不影响定罪的。  
对于符合前款第二项情形的，应当以已经查清的罪行起诉。

**Article 355** : If a People's Procuratorate finds that the criminal facts of a criminal suspect have been established, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and criminal liability shall be investigated according to law, it shall make a decision to initiate a prosecution.  
Under any of the following circumstances, the criminal facts may be deemed to have been established:  
(1) In a case involving a single crime, the established facts are sufficient for conviction and sentencing, or the facts relevant to conviction and sentencing have been established, while facts that do not affect conviction and sentencing cannot be established;  
(2) In a case involving multiple crimes, some crimes have been established and meet the conditions for prosecution, while other crimes cannot be established;  
(3) The whereabouts of the tools used in the crime or the stolen goods cannot be established, but other evidence is sufficient to convict and sentence the defendant;  
(4) The main facts stated in witness testimony, the criminal suspect's confession and defense, and the victim's statement are consistent, while individual details are inconsistent, but such inconsistency does not affect the conviction.  
For cases that fall under item (2) of the preceding paragraph, the prosecution shall be initiated based on the crimes that have been established.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条  人民检察院在办理公安机关移送起诉的案件中，发现遗漏罪行或者有依法应当移送起诉的同案犯罪嫌疑人未移送起诉的，应当要求公安机关补充侦查或者补充移送起诉。对于犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分的，也可以直接提起公诉。

**Article 356** : In the process of handling a case transferred for prosecution by a public security organ, if a People's Procuratorate discovers that a crime has been omitted or a co-suspect who shall be transferred for prosecution according to law has not been transferred, it shall request the public security organ to conduct supplementary investigation or supplementary transfer for prosecution. If the criminal facts are clear and the evidence is reliable and sufficient, the People's Procuratorate may directly initiate a public prosecution.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条  人民检察院立案侦查时认为属于直接受理侦查的案件，在审查起诉阶段发现属于监察机关管辖的，应当及时商监察机关办理。属于公安机关管辖，案件事实清楚，证据确实、充分，符合起诉条件的，可以直接起诉；事实不清、证据不足的，应当及时移送有管辖权的机关办理。  
在审查起诉阶段，发现公安机关移送起诉的案件属于监察机关管辖，或者监察机关移送起诉的案件属于公安机关管辖，但案件事实清楚，证据确实、充分，符合起诉条件的，经征求监察机关、公安机关意见后，没有不同意见的，可以直接起诉；提出不同意见，或者事实不清、证据不足的，应当将案件退回移送案件的机关并说明理由，建议其移送有管辖权的机关办理。

**Article 357** : If a People's Procuratorate, when initiating an investigation, considers the case to be one that is directly accepted for investigation, but finds during the stage of review for prosecution that the case is within the jurisdiction of a supervisory organ, it shall promptly consult with the supervisory organ for handling. If the case is within the jurisdiction of a public security organ, the facts of the case are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and the conditions for prosecution are met, the People's Procuratorate may directly initiate a prosecution; if the facts are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, it shall promptly transfer the case to the organ with jurisdiction for handling.  
During the stage of review for prosecution, if it is found that a case transferred for prosecution by a public security organ is within the jurisdiction of a supervisory organ, or a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ is within the jurisdiction of a public security organ, but the facts of the case are clear, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and the conditions for prosecution are met, after soliciting opinions from the supervisory organ and the public security organ, if there is no objection, the People's Procuratorate may directly initiate a prosecution; if objections are raised, or the facts are unclear or the evidence is insufficient, the case shall be returned to the organ that transferred the case with an explanation of the reasons, and it shall be recommended that the case be transferred to the organ with jurisdiction for handling.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条  人民检察院决定起诉的，应当制作起诉书。  
起诉书的主要内容包括：  
（一）被告人的基本情况，包括姓名、性别、出生年月日、出生地和户籍地、公民身份号码、民族、文化程度、职业、工作单位及职务、住址，是否受过刑事处分及处分的种类和时间，采取强制措施的情况等；如果是单位犯罪，应当写明犯罪单位的名称和组织机构代码、所在地址、联系方式，法定代表人和诉讼代表人的姓名、职务、联系方式；如果还有应当负刑事责任的直接负责的主管人员或其他直接责任人员，应当按上述被告人基本情况的内容叙写；  
（二）案由和案件来源；  
（三）案件事实，包括犯罪的时间、地点、经过、手段、动机、目的、危害后果等与定罪量刑有关的事实要素。起诉书叙述的指控犯罪事实的必备要素应当明晰、准确。被告人被控有多项犯罪事实的，应当逐一列举，对于犯罪手段相同的同一犯罪可以概括叙写；  
（四）起诉的根据和理由，包括被告人触犯的刑法条款、犯罪的性质及认定的罪名、处罚条款、法定从轻、减轻或者从重处罚的情节，共同犯罪各被告人应负的罪责等；  
（五）被告人认罪认罚情况，包括认罪认罚的内容、具结书签署情况等。  
被告人真实姓名、住址无法查清的，可以按其绰号或者自报的姓名、住址制作起诉书，并在起诉书中注明。被告人自报的姓名可能造成损害他人名誉、败坏道德风俗等不良影响的，可以对被告人编号并按编号制作起诉书，附具被告人的照片，记明足以确定被告人面貌、体格、指纹以及其他反映被告人特征的事项。  
起诉书应当附有被告人现在处所，证人、鉴定人、需要出庭的有专门知识的人的名单，需要保护的被害人、证人、鉴定人的化名名单，查封、扣押、冻结的财物及孳息的清单，附带民事诉讼、附带民事公益诉讼情况以及其他需要附注的情况。  
证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人的名单应当列明姓名、性别、年龄、职业、住址、联系方式，并注明证人、鉴定人是否出庭。

**Article 358** : If a People's Procuratorate decides to initiate a prosecution, it shall prepare an indictment.  
The main contents of the indictment include:  
(1) The basic information of the defendant, including name, gender, date of birth, place of birth and registered domicile, citizen identification number, ethnicity, education level, occupation, work unit and position, address, whether any criminal punishment has been imposed and the type and time of such punishment, and the status of compulsory measures taken; if it is a unit crime, the name and organization code of the criminal unit, its address and contact information, and the names, positions, and contact information of the legal representative and litigation representative shall be stated; if there are directly responsible supervisors or other directly responsible personnel who shall bear criminal liability, they shall be described in accordance with the content of the basic information of the defendant above;  
(2) The cause of action and the source of the case;  
(3) The facts of the case, including the time, place, process, means, motive, purpose, harmful consequences, and other factual elements relevant to conviction and sentencing. The essential elements of the alleged criminal facts described in the indictment shall be clear and accurate. If the defendant is accused of multiple criminal facts, they shall be listed one by one; for the same crime with identical means, a summary description may be used;  
(4) The basis and reasons for the prosecution, including the provisions of the Criminal Law violated by the defendant, the nature of the crime and the identified charge, the punishment provisions, the statutory circumstances for lighter, mitigated, or heavier punishment, and the criminal responsibility of each defendant in a joint crime;  
(5) The defendant's admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, including the content of the admission and acceptance, and the signing of the recognizance letter.  
If the true name and address of the defendant cannot be ascertained, the indictment may be prepared based on his alias or the name and address he reports, and such fact shall be noted in the indictment. If the name reported by the defendant may cause adverse effects such as harming the reputation of others or corrupting moral customs, the defendant may be assigned a number and the indictment prepared based on that number, with a photograph of the defendant attached, and a record of matters sufficient to identify the defendant's appearance, physique, fingerprints, and other characteristics.  
The indictment shall be accompanied by the defendant's current location, a list of witnesses, expert evaluators, and persons with specialized knowledge who need to appear in court, a pseudonym list of victims, witnesses, and expert evaluators who need protection, a list of property and its fruits that have been sealed up, seized, or frozen, information on incidental civil actions and incidental civil public interest litigation, and other matters requiring annotation.  
The list of witnesses, expert evaluators, and persons with specialized knowledge shall include their names, genders, ages, occupations, addresses, and contact information, and shall indicate whether the witnesses and expert evaluators will appear in court.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条  人民检察院提起公诉的案件，应当向人民法院移送起诉书、案卷材料、证据和认罪认罚具结书等材料。  
起诉书应当一式八份，每增加一名被告人增加起诉书五份。  
关于被害人姓名、住址、联系方式、被告人被采取强制措施的种类、是否在案及羁押处所等问题，人民检察院应当在起诉书中列明，不再单独移送材料；对于涉及被害人隐私或者为保护证人、鉴定人、被害人人身安全，而不宜公开证人、鉴定人、被害人姓名、住址、工作单位和联系方式等个人信息的，可以在起诉书中使用化名。但是应当另行书面说明使用化名的情况并标明密级，单独成卷。

**Article 359** : For a case in which a People's Procuratorate initiates a public prosecution, it shall transfer to the People's Court the indictment, case files, evidence, and the recognizance letter of admission and acceptance of punishment, among other materials.  
The indictment shall be made in eight copies, and for each additional defendant, five additional copies of the indictment shall be made.  
With respect to issues such as the name, address, and contact information of the victim, the type of compulsory measures taken against the defendant, whether the defendant is in custody and the place of custody, the People's Procuratorate shall list them in the indictment and shall not transfer separate materials; if it is inappropriate to disclose the personal information such as the name, address, work unit, and contact information of witnesses, expert evaluators, or victims due to involvement of the victim's privacy or to protect the personal safety of witnesses, expert evaluators, or victims, pseudonyms may be used in the indictment. However, the use of pseudonyms shall be separately explained in writing, with the classification level indicated, and filed as a separate volume.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条  人民检察院对于犯罪嫌疑人、被告人或者证人等翻供、翻证的材料以及对犯罪嫌疑人、被告人有利的其他证据材料，应当移送人民法院。

**Article 360** : A People's Procuratorate shall transfer to the People's Court any materials regarding the retraction of confession or testimony by a criminal suspect, defendant, or witness, and any other evidence materials favorable to the criminal suspect or defendant.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条  人民法院向人民检察院提出书面意见要求补充移送材料，人民检察院认为有必要移送的，应当自收到通知之日起三日以内补送。

**Article 361** : If a People's Court submits a written opinion to a People's Procuratorate requesting supplementary transfer of materials, and the People's Procuratorate considers the transfer necessary, it shall make the supplementary transfer within three days from the date of receiving the notice.

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条  对提起公诉后，在人民法院宣告判决前补充收集的证据材料，人民检察院应当及时移送人民法院。

**Article 362** : For evidence materials collected supplementarily after the initiation of a public prosecution but before the People's Court pronounces judgment, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly transfer them to the People's Court.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条  在审查起诉期间，人民检察院可以根据辩护人的申请，向监察机关、公安机关调取在调查、侦查期间收集的证明犯罪嫌疑人、被告人无罪或者罪轻的证据材料。

**Article 363** : During the period of review for prosecution, a People's Procuratorate may, upon application by the defender, obtain from a supervisory organ or public security organ evidence materials collected during the investigation or inquiry that prove the innocence or lesser guilt of the criminal suspect or defendant.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条  人民检察院提起公诉的案件，可以向人民法院提出量刑建议。除有减轻处罚或者免除处罚情节外，量刑建议应当在法定量刑幅度内提出。建议判处有期徒刑、管制、拘役的，可以具有一定的幅度，也可以提出具体确定的建议。  
提出量刑建议的，可以制作量刑建议书，与起诉书一并移送人民法院。量刑建议书的主要内容应当包括被告人所犯罪行的法定刑、量刑情节、建议人民法院对被告人判处刑罚的种类、刑罚幅度、可以适用的刑罚执行方式以及提出量刑建议的依据和理由等。  
认罪认罚案件的量刑建议，按照本章第二节的规定办理。

**Article 364** : For a case in which a People's Procuratorate initiates a public prosecution, it may propose a sentencing recommendation to the People's Court. Except where there are circumstances for mitigated punishment or exemption from punishment, the sentencing recommendation shall be made within the statutory sentencing range. If a recommendation is made for fixed-term imprisonment, public surveillance, or criminal detention, it may be within a certain range, or a specific and definite recommendation may be made.  
If a sentencing recommendation is made, a sentencing recommendation letter may be prepared and transferred together with the indictment to the People's Court. The main contents of the sentencing recommendation letter shall include the statutory punishment for the crime committed by the defendant, the sentencing circumstances, the type and range of punishment recommended for the defendant, the possible execution method of the punishment, and the basis and reasons for the sentencing recommendation.  
Sentencing recommendations for cases involving admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of Section 2 of this Chapter.

#### 第九节 不起诉

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条  人民检察院对于监察机关或者公安机关移送起诉的案件，发现犯罪嫌疑人没有犯罪事实，或者符合[刑事诉讼法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-16)规定的情形之一的，经检察长批准，应当作出不起诉决定。  
对于犯罪事实并非犯罪嫌疑人所为，需要重新调查或者侦查的，应当在作出不起诉决定后书面说明理由，将案卷材料退回监察机关或者公安机关并建议重新调查或者侦查。

**Article 365** : If a People's Procuratorate discovers that a criminal suspect has no criminal facts, or falls under any of the circumstances provided in Article 16 of the Criminal Procedure Law, in a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ or a public security organ, it shall, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, make a decision not to prosecute.  
If the criminal facts were not committed by the criminal suspect and a new investigation or inquiry is necessary, after making the decision not to prosecute, the People's Procuratorate shall explain the reasons in writing, return the case file to the supervisory organ or the public security organ, and recommend a new investigation or inquiry.

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条  负责捕诉的部门对于本院负责侦查的部门移送起诉的案件，发现具有[本规则第三百六十五条](#art-365)第一款规定情形的，应当退回本院负责侦查的部门，建议撤销案件。

**Article 366** : If the department responsible for arrest and prosecution discovers that a case transferred for prosecution by the department responsible for investigation of the same People's Procuratorate falls under the circumstances specified in the first paragraph of Article 365 of these Rules, it shall return the case to the department responsible for investigation and recommend that the case be dismissed.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条  人民检察院对于二次退回补充调查或者补充侦查的案件，仍然认为证据不足，不符合起诉条件的，经检察长批准，依法作出不起诉决定。  
人民检察院对于经过一次退回补充调查或者补充侦查的案件，认为证据不足，不符合起诉条件，且没有再次退回补充调查或者补充侦查必要的，经检察长批准，可以作出不起诉决定。

**Article 367** : If a People's Procuratorate still considers the evidence insufficient and not meeting the conditions for prosecution in a case that has been returned twice for supplementary investigation or supplementary inquiry, it shall, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, make a decision not to prosecute according to law.  
If a People's Procuratorate considers the evidence insufficient and not meeting the conditions for prosecution in a case that has been returned once for supplementary investigation or supplementary inquiry, and there is no need for another return for supplementary investigation or supplementary inquiry, it may, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, make a decision not to prosecute.

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条  具有下列情形之一，不能确定犯罪嫌疑人构成犯罪和需要追究刑事责任的，属于证据不足，不符合起诉条件：  
（一）犯罪构成要件事实缺乏必要的证据予以证明的；  
（二）据以定罪的证据存在疑问，无法查证属实的；  
（三）据以定罪的证据之间、证据与案件事实之间的矛盾不能合理排除的；  
（四）根据证据得出的结论具有其他可能性，不能排除合理怀疑的；  
（五）根据证据认定案件事实不符合逻辑和经验法则，得出的结论明显不符合常理的。

**Article 368** : Under any of the following circumstances, if it cannot be determined that a criminal suspect has committed a crime and that criminal liability shall be investigated, the evidence is deemed insufficient and does not meet the conditions for prosecution:  
(1) The facts of the constitutive elements of the crime lack necessary evidence to prove them;  
(2) The evidence on which the conviction is based is questionable and cannot be verified as true;  
(3) Contradictions among the evidence on which the conviction is based, or between the evidence and the facts of the case, cannot be reasonably resolved;  
(4) The conclusion drawn from the evidence has other possibilities, and reasonable doubt cannot be eliminated;  
(5) The determination of the facts of the case based on the evidence is inconsistent with logic and the rules of experience, and the conclusion reached is obviously contrary to common sense.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条  人民检察院根据[刑事诉讼法第一百七十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-175)第四款规定决定不起诉的，在发现新的证据，符合起诉条件时，可以提起公诉。

**Article 369** : If a People's Procuratorate decides not to prosecute pursuant to the provisions of paragraph 4 of Article 175 of the Criminal Procedure Law, when new evidence is discovered and the conditions for prosecution are met, it may initiate a public prosecution.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条  人民检察院对于犯罪情节轻微，依照刑法规定不需要判处刑罚或者免除刑罚的，经检察长批准，可以作出不起诉决定。

**Article 370** : If a People's Procuratorate, for a minor criminal offense where punishment is not required or exemption from punishment is provided for under the Criminal Law, may, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, make a decision not to prosecute.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条  人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，以及监察机关移送起诉的案件，拟作不起诉决定的，应当报请上一级人民检察院批准。

**Article 371** For a case directly accepted for investigation by a People's Procuratorate, or a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ, if a decision not to prosecute is intended, the matter shall be submitted to the next higher People's Procuratorate for approval.

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条  人民检察院决定不起诉的，应当制作不起诉决定书。  
不起诉决定书的主要内容包括：  
（一）被不起诉人的基本情况，包括姓名、性别、出生年月日、出生地和户籍地、公民身份号码、民族、文化程度、职业、工作单位及职务、住址，是否受过刑事处分，采取强制措施的情况以及羁押处所等；如果是单位犯罪，应当写明犯罪单位的名称和组织机构代码、所在地址、联系方式，法定代表人和诉讼代表人的姓名、职务、联系方式；  
（二）案由和案件来源；  
（三）案件事实，包括否定或者指控被不起诉人构成犯罪的事实以及作为不起诉决定根据的事实；  
（四）不起诉的法律根据和理由，写明作出不起诉决定适用的法律条款；  
（五）查封、扣押、冻结的涉案财物的处理情况；  
（六）有关告知事项。

**Article 372** Where a People's Procuratorate decides not to prosecute, it shall make a written decision not to prosecute.  
The main contents of the written decision not to prosecute include:
