(1) The basic information of the person not prosecuted, including name, gender, date of birth, place of birth and registered domicile, citizen identity number, ethnicity, education level, occupation, work unit and position, address, whether previously subjected to criminal punishment, compulsory measures taken, and place of custody; if it is a unit crime, the name and organization code of the criminal unit, its address, contact information, and the name, position, and contact information of its legal representative and litigation representative shall be stated;  
(2) The cause of action and source of the case;  
(3) The facts of the case, including facts that negate or allege that the person not prosecuted constitutes a crime and the facts serving as the basis for the decision not to prosecute;  
(4) The legal basis and reasons for non-prosecution, stating the applicable legal provisions for the decision not to prosecute;  
(5) The handling of the property involved that has been sealed, seized, or frozen;  
(6) Relevant matters to be notified.

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条  人民检察院决定不起诉的案件，可以根据案件的不同情况，对被不起诉人予以训诫或者责令具结悔过、赔礼道歉、赔偿损失。  
对被不起诉人需要给予行政处罚、政务处分或者其他处分的，经检察长批准，人民检察院应当提出检察意见，连同不起诉决定书一并移送有关主管机关处理，并要求有关主管机关及时通报处理情况。

**Article 373** For a case in which a People's Procuratorate decides not to prosecute, it may, depending on the circumstances of the case, reprimand the person not prosecuted, or order him or her to make a written statement of repentance, apologize, or compensate for losses.  
If the person not prosecuted needs to be subjected to an administrative penalty, a disciplinary sanction in government affairs, or other sanction, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, the People's Procuratorate shall propose a procuratorial suggestion and transfer it together with the written decision not to prosecute to the relevant competent authority for handling, and shall request the relevant competent authority to promptly report the handling situation.

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条  人民检察院决定不起诉的案件，应当同时书面通知作出查封、扣押、冻结决定的机关或者执行查封、扣押、冻结决定的机关解除查封、扣押、冻结。

**Article 374** For a case in which a People's Procuratorate decides not to prosecute, it shall simultaneously notify in writing the authority that made the decision to seal up, seize, or freeze, or the authority that executed the decision to seal up, seize, or freeze, to lift the seal-up, seizure, or freeze.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条  人民检察院决定不起诉的案件，需要没收违法所得的，经检察长批准，应当提出检察意见，移送有关主管机关处理，并要求有关主管机关及时通报处理情况。具体程序可以参照[本规则第二百四十八条](#art-248)的规定办理。

**Article 375** For a case in which a People's Procuratorate decides not to prosecute, if illegal gains need to be confiscated, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, the People's Procuratorate shall propose a procuratorial suggestion and transfer it to the relevant competent authority for handling, and shall request the relevant competent authority to promptly report the handling situation. The specific procedures may be handled by reference to the provisions of Article 248 of these Rules.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条  不起诉的决定，由人民检察院公开宣布。公开宣布不起诉决定的活动应当记录在案。  
不起诉决定书自公开宣布之日起生效。  
被不起诉人在押的，应当立即释放；被采取其他强制措施的，应当通知执行机关解除。

**Article 376** The decision not to prosecute shall be publicly announced by the People's Procuratorate. The activity of publicly announcing the decision not to prosecute shall be recorded in the case file.  
The written decision not to prosecute shall take effect from the date of public announcement.  
If the person not prosecuted is in custody, he or she shall be released immediately; if other compulsory measures have been taken, the executing authority shall be notified to lift them.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条  不起诉决定书应当送达被害人或者其近亲属及其诉讼代理人、被不起诉人及其辩护人以及被不起诉人所在单位。送达时，应当告知被害人或者其近亲属及其诉讼代理人，如果对不起诉决定不服，可以自收到不起诉决定书后七日以内向上一级人民检察院申诉；也可以不经申诉，直接向人民法院起诉。依照[刑事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-177)第二款作出不起诉决定的，应当告知被不起诉人，如果对不起诉决定不服，可以自收到不起诉决定书后七日以内向人民检察院申诉。

**Article 377** The written decision not to prosecute shall be served on the victim or his or her close relatives and their litigation agent, the person not prosecuted and his or her defender, and the unit where the person not prosecuted works. Upon service, the victim or his or her close relatives and their litigation agent shall be informed that, if they disagree with the decision not to prosecute, they may, within seven days from receipt of the written decision not to prosecute, file a petition with the next higher People's Procuratorate; or they may directly file a lawsuit with a People's Court without filing a petition. If the decision not to prosecute is made in accordance with Article 177, paragraph 2 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the person not prosecuted shall be informed that, if he or she disagrees with the decision, he or she may, within seven days from receipt of the written decision not to prosecute, file a petition with the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条  对于监察机关或者公安机关移送起诉的案件，人民检察院决定不起诉的，应当将不起诉决定书送达监察机关或者公安机关。

**Article 378** For a case transferred for prosecution by a supervisory organ or a public security organ, if the People's Procuratorate decides not to prosecute, it shall serve the written decision not to prosecute on the supervisory organ or the public security organ.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条  监察机关认为不起诉的决定有错误，向上一级人民检察院提请复议的，上一级人民检察院应当在收到提请复议意见书后三十日以内，经检察长批准，作出复议决定，通知监察机关。  
公安机关认为不起诉决定有错误要求复议的，人民检察院负责捕诉的部门应当另行指派检察官或者检察官办案组进行审查，并在收到要求复议意见书后三十日以内，经检察长批准，作出复议决定，通知公安机关。

**Article 379** If a supervisory organ considers that the decision not to prosecute is erroneous and requests reconsideration from the next higher People's Procuratorate, the next higher People's Procuratorate shall, within thirty days from receipt of the written request for reconsideration, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, make a reconsideration decision and notify the supervisory organ.  
If a public security organ considers that the decision not to prosecute is erroneous and requests reconsideration, the department of the People's Procuratorate in charge of prosecution and arrest shall assign a different prosecutor or a team of prosecutors to conduct review, and within thirty days from receipt of the written request for reconsideration, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, make a reconsideration decision and notify the public security organ.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条  公安机关对不起诉决定提请复核的，上一级人民检察院应当在收到提请复核意见书后三十日以内，经检察长批准，作出复核决定，通知提请复核的公安机关和下级人民检察院。经复核认为下级人民检察院不起诉决定错误的，应当指令下级人民检察院纠正，或者撤销、变更下级人民检察院作出的不起诉决定。

**Article 380** If a public security organ requests review of a decision not to prosecute, the next higher People's Procuratorate shall, within thirty days from receipt of the written request for review, with the approval of the Chief Procurator, make a review decision and notify the public security organ that requested the review and the lower People's Procuratorate. If upon review it is considered that the decision not to prosecute made by the lower People's Procuratorate is erroneous, it shall instruct the lower People's Procuratorate to correct it, or revoke or modify the decision not to prosecute made by the lower People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条  被害人不服不起诉决定，在收到不起诉决定书后七日以内提出申诉的，由作出不起诉决定的人民检察院的上一级人民检察院负责捕诉的部门进行复查。  
被害人向作出不起诉决定的人民检察院提出申诉的，作出决定的人民检察院应当将申诉材料连同案卷一并报送上一级人民检察院。

**Article 381** If a victim disagrees with a decision not to prosecute and files a petition within seven days from receipt of the written decision not to prosecute, the department in charge of prosecution and arrest of the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level than the People's Procuratorate that made the decision not to prosecute shall conduct a re-examination.  
If the victim files a petition with the People's Procuratorate that made the decision not to prosecute, the People's Procuratorate that made the decision shall transmit the petition materials together with the case file to the next higher People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条  被害人不服不起诉决定，在收到不起诉决定书七日以后提出申诉的，由作出不起诉决定的人民检察院负责控告申诉检察的部门进行审查。经审查，认为不起诉决定正确的，出具审查结论直接答复申诉人，并做好释法说理工作；认为不起诉决定可能存在错误的，移送负责捕诉的部门进行复查。

**Article 382** If a victim disagrees with a decision not to prosecute and files a petition more than seven days after receipt of the written decision not to prosecute, the department of the People's Procuratorate that made the decision not to prosecute in charge of handling complaints and petitions shall conduct a review. Upon review, if it is considered that the decision not to prosecute is correct, a review conclusion shall be issued and directly replied to the petitioner, and efforts shall be made to explain the law and reasoning; if it is considered that the decision not to prosecute may be erroneous, the matter shall be transferred to the department in charge of prosecution and arrest for re-examination.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条  人民检察院应当将复查决定书送达被害人、被不起诉人和作出不起诉决定的人民检察院。  
上级人民检察院经复查作出起诉决定的，应当撤销下级人民检察院的不起诉决定，交由下级人民检察院提起公诉，并将复查决定抄送移送起诉的监察机关或者公安机关。

**Article 383** The People's Procuratorate shall serve the written re-examination decision on the victim, the person not prosecuted, and the People's Procuratorate that made the decision not to prosecute.  
If the higher People's Procuratorate makes a decision to prosecute upon re-examination, it shall revoke the decision not to prosecute of the lower People's Procuratorate, instruct the lower People's Procuratorate to initiate a public prosecution, and send a copy of the re-examination decision to the supervisory organ or public security organ that transferred the case for prosecution.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条  人民检察院收到人民法院受理被害人对被不起诉人起诉的通知后，应当终止复查，将作出不起诉决定所依据的有关案卷材料移送人民法院。

**Article 384** After a People's Procuratorate receives a notice from a People's Court that it has accepted a lawsuit filed by a victim against the person not prosecuted, it shall terminate the re-examination and transfer the relevant case file materials on which the decision not to prosecute was based to the People's Court.

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条  对于人民检察院依照[刑事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-177)第二款规定作出的不起诉决定，被不起诉人不服，在收到不起诉决定书后七日以内提出申诉的，应当由作出决定的人民检察院负责捕诉的部门进行复查；被不起诉人在收到不起诉决定书七日以后提出申诉的，由负责控告申诉检察的部门进行审查。经审查，认为不起诉决定正确的，出具审查结论直接答复申诉人，并做好释法说理工作；认为不起诉决定可能存在错误的，移送负责捕诉的部门复查。  
人民检察院应当将复查决定书送达被不起诉人、被害人。复查后，撤销不起诉决定，变更不起诉的事实或者法律依据的，应当同时将复查决定书抄送移送起诉的监察机关或者公安机关。

**Article 385** If a person not prosecuted disagrees with a decision not to prosecute made by a People's Procuratorate in accordance with Article 177, paragraph 2 of the Criminal Procedure Law, and files a petition within seven days from receipt of the written decision not to prosecute, the department of the People's Procuratorate that made the decision in charge of prosecution and arrest shall conduct a re-examination; if the person not prosecuted files a petition more than seven days after receipt of the written decision not to prosecute, the department in charge of handling complaints and petitions shall conduct a review. Upon review, if it is considered that the decision not to prosecute is correct, a review conclusion shall be issued and directly replied to the petitioner, and efforts shall be made to explain the law and reasoning; if it is considered that the decision not to prosecute may be erroneous, the matter shall be transferred to the department in charge of prosecution and arrest for re-examination.  
The People's Procuratorate shall serve the written re-examination decision on the person not prosecuted and the victim. If after re-examination, the decision not to prosecute is revoked or the facts or legal basis for non-prosecution are modified, a copy of the written re-examination decision shall also be sent to the supervisory organ or public security organ that transferred the case for prosecution.

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条  人民检察院复查不服不起诉决定的申诉，应当在立案后三个月以内报经检察长批准作出复查决定。案情复杂的，不得超过六个月。

**Article 386** A People's Procuratorate re-examining a petition against a decision not to prosecute shall, within three months from the date of filing the case, submit the matter to the Chief Procurator for approval to make a re-examination decision. If the case is complex, the period shall not exceed six months.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条  被害人、被不起诉人对不起诉决定不服提出申诉的，应当递交申诉书，写明申诉理由。没有书写能力的，也可以口头提出申诉。人民检察院应当根据其口头提出的申诉制作笔录。

**Article 387** If a victim or a person not prosecuted disagrees with a decision not to prosecute and files a petition, he or she shall submit a written petition stating the grounds for the petition. If he or she is unable to write, the petition may also be made orally. The People's Procuratorate shall make a record based on the oral petition.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条  人民检察院发现不起诉决定确有错误，符合起诉条件的，应当撤销不起诉决定，提起公诉。

**Article 388** If a People's Procuratorate finds that a decision not to prosecute is indeed erroneous and the conditions for prosecution are met, it shall revoke the decision not to prosecute and initiate a public prosecution.

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条  最高人民检察院对地方各级人民检察院的起诉、不起诉决定，上级人民检察院对下级人民检察院的起诉、不起诉决定，发现确有错误的，应当予以撤销或者指令下级人民检察院纠正。

**Article 389** If the Supreme People's Procuratorate finds that a decision to prosecute or not to prosecute made by a local People's Procuratorate at any level is indeed erroneous, or if a higher People's Procuratorate finds that such a decision made by a lower People's Procuratorate is indeed erroneous, it shall revoke the decision or instruct the lower People's Procuratorate to correct it.

### 第十一章 出席法庭

#### 第一节 出席第一审法庭

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条  提起公诉的案件，人民检察院应当派员以国家公诉人的身份出席第一审法庭，支持公诉。  
公诉人应当由检察官担任。检察官助理可以协助检察官出庭。根据需要可以配备书记员担任记录。

**Article 390** For a case in which a public prosecution is initiated, the People's Procuratorate shall send personnel to appear before the court of first instance in the capacity of a public prosecutor to support the prosecution.  
The public prosecutor shall be a prosecutor. A prosecutor's assistant may assist the prosecutor in appearing in court. If necessary, a court clerk may be assigned to take records.

<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条  对于提起公诉后人民法院改变管辖的案件，提起公诉的人民检察院参照[本规则第三百二十八条](#art-328)的规定将案件移送与审判管辖相对应的人民检察院。  
接受移送的人民检察院重新对案件进行审查的，根据[刑事诉讼法第一百七十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-172)第二款的规定自收到案件之日起计算审查起诉期限。

**Article 391** Where the People's Court changes jurisdiction after a public prosecution is initiated, the People's Procuratorate that initiated the prosecution shall, by reference to Article 328 of these Rules, transfer the case to the People's Procuratorate corresponding to the adjudicatory jurisdiction.  
If the accepting People's Procuratorate re-examines the case, the time limit for examination and prosecution shall be calculated from the date of receipt of the case in accordance with Article 172, paragraph 2 of the Criminal Procedure Law.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条  人民法院决定开庭审判的，公诉人应当做好以下准备工作：  
（一）进一步熟悉案情，掌握证据情况；  
（二）深入研究与本案有关的法律政策问题；  
（三）充实审判中可能涉及的专业知识；  
（四）拟定讯问被告人、询问证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人和宣读、出示、播放证据的计划并制定质证方案；  
（五）对可能出现证据合法性争议的，拟定证明证据合法性的提纲并准备相关材料；  
（六）拟定公诉意见，准备辩论提纲；  
（七）需要对出庭证人等的保护向人民法院提出建议或者配合工作的，做好相关准备。

**Article 392** Where the People's Court decides to open a court session for trial, the public prosecutor shall make the following preparations:  
(1) Further familiarize himself with the case facts and master the evidence situation;  
(2) Conduct in-depth research on legal and policy issues related to the case;  
(3) Enrich the professional knowledge that may be involved in the trial;  
(4) Formulate plans for interrogating the defendant, questioning witnesses, expert evaluators, and persons with specialized knowledge, and for reading, presenting, and playing evidence, and develop a cross-examination plan;  
(5) Where disputes over the legality of evidence may arise, formulate an outline for proving the legality of evidence and prepare relevant materials;  
(6) Formulate public prosecution opinions and prepare a debate outline;  
(7) Where it is necessary to make recommendations to the People's Court or cooperate in protecting witnesses appearing in court, make relevant preparations.

<a id="art-393"></a>第三百九十三条  人民检察院在开庭审理前收到人民法院或者被告人及其辩护人、被害人、证人等送交的反映证据系非法取得的书面材料的，应当进行审查。对于审查逮捕、审查起诉期间已经提出并经查证不存在非法取证行为的，应当通知人民法院、有关当事人和辩护人，并按照查证的情况做好庭审准备。对于新的材料或者线索，可以要求监察机关、公安机关对证据收集的合法性进行说明或者提供相关证明材料。

**Article 393** Where, before the opening of the court session, the People's Procuratorate receives written materials from the People's Court, the defendant or his defender, the victim, a witness, or others indicating that evidence has been obtained illegally, it shall conduct a review. If the matter was already raised during the period of examination for arrest or examination for prosecution and it has been verified that no illegal evidence collection existed, the People's Procuratorate shall notify the People's Court, the relevant parties, and the defender, and shall prepare for trial in accordance with the verified circumstances. For new materials or leads, it may request the supervisory organ or the public security organ to explain the legality of evidence collection or to provide relevant supporting materials.

<a id="art-394"></a>第三百九十四条  人民法院通知人民检察院派员参加庭前会议的，由出席法庭的公诉人参加。检察官助理可以协助。根据需要可以配备书记员担任记录。  
人民检察院认为有必要召开庭前会议的，可以建议人民法院召开庭前会议。

**Article 394** Where the People's Court notifies the People's Procuratorate to send personnel to participate in a pre-trial conference, the public prosecutor who will appear in court shall participate. A prosecutor's assistant may provide assistance. A clerk may be assigned to take notes as needed.  
If the People's Procuratorate considers it necessary to convene a pre-trial conference, it may recommend that the People's Court convene one.

<a id="art-395"></a>第三百九十五条  在庭前会议中，公诉人可以对案件管辖、回避、出庭证人、鉴定人、有专门知识的人的名单、辩护人提供的无罪证据、非法证据排除、不公开审理、延期审理、适用简易程序或者速裁程序、庭审方案等与审判相关的问题提出和交换意见，了解辩护人收集的证据等情况。  
对辩护人收集的证据有异议的，应当提出，并简要说明理由。  
公诉人通过参加庭前会议，了解案件事实、证据和法律适用的争议和不同意见，解决有关程序问题，为参加法庭审理做好准备。

**Article 395** At the pre-trial conference, the public prosecutor may present and exchange opinions on issues related to the trial, such as jurisdiction of the case, withdrawal, the list of witnesses, expert evaluators, and persons with specialized knowledge to appear in court, evidence of innocence provided by the defender, exclusion of illegal evidence, trial in camera, adjournment of trial, application of summary procedures or fast-track sentencing procedures, and trial plans, and may learn about the evidence collected by the defender and other matters.  
If the public prosecutor objects to evidence collected by the defender, he shall raise the objection and briefly state the reasons.  
By participating in the pre-trial conference, the public prosecutor shall understand the disputes and differing opinions on the facts, evidence, and application of law of the case, resolve relevant procedural issues, and prepare for participating in the court trial.

<a id="art-396"></a>第三百九十六条  当事人、辩护人、诉讼代理人在庭前会议中提出证据系非法取得，人民法院认为可能存在以非法方法收集证据情形的，人民检察院应当对证据收集的合法性进行说明。需要调查核实的，在开庭审理前进行。

**Article 396** Where a party, defender, or litigation agent raises at the pre-trial conference that evidence has been obtained illegally, and the People's Court considers that evidence may have been collected by illegal methods, the People's Procuratorate shall explain the legality of the evidence collection. If investigation and verification are necessary, such action shall be taken before the opening of the court trial.

<a id="art-397"></a>第三百九十七条  人民检察院向人民法院移送全部案卷材料后，在法庭审理过程中，公诉人需要出示、宣读、播放有关证据的，可以申请法庭出示、宣读、播放。  
人民检察院基于出庭准备和庭审举证工作的需要，可以取回有关案卷材料和证据。  
取回案卷材料和证据后，辩护律师要求查阅案卷材料的，应当允许辩护律师在人民检察院查阅、摘抄、复制案卷材料。

**Article 397** After the People's Procuratorate transfers all case file materials to the People's Court, during the court trial, if the public prosecutor needs to present, read, or play relevant evidence, he may apply to the court to do so.  
For the purposes of preparation for court appearance and presentation of evidence at trial, the People's Procuratorate may retrieve relevant case file materials and evidence.  
After retrieving case file materials and evidence, if the defense lawyer requests to consult the case file materials, the defense lawyer shall be permitted to consult, excerpt, and copy the case file materials at the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-398"></a>第三百九十八条  公诉人在法庭上应当依法进行下列活动：  
（一）宣读起诉书，代表国家指控犯罪，提请人民法院对被告人依法审判；  
（二）讯问被告人；  
（三）询问证人、被害人、鉴定人；  
（四）申请法庭出示物证，宣读书证、未到庭证人的证言笔录、鉴定人的鉴定意见、勘验、检查、辨认、侦查实验等笔录和其他作为证据的文书，播放作为证据的视听资料、电子数据等；  
（五）对证据采信、法律适用和案件情况发表意见，提出量刑建议及理由，针对被告人、辩护人的辩护意见进行答辩，全面阐述公诉意见；  
（六）维护诉讼参与人的合法权利；  
（七）对法庭审理案件有无违反法律规定诉讼程序的情况记明笔录；  
（八）依法从事其他诉讼活动。

**Article 398** The public prosecutor shall, in court, carry out the following activities according to law:  
(1) Read the indictment, accuse the crime on behalf of the state, and request the People's Court to try the defendant according to law;  
(2) Interrogate the defendant;  
(3) Question witnesses, victims, and expert evaluators;  
(4) Apply to the court to present physical evidence, read documentary evidence, written testimony of witnesses not appearing in court, expert evaluation opinions, records of inquests, inspections, identifications, investigative experiments, and other documents as evidence, and play audio-visual materials, electronic data, etc., as evidence;  
(5) Express opinions on the admissibility of evidence, application of law, and the circumstances of the case, propose sentencing recommendations and reasons, respond to the defense opinions of the defendant and defender, and comprehensively elaborate the public prosecution opinions;  
(6) Protect the lawful rights of participants in the proceedings;  
(7) Record in the minutes whether the court's trial of the case violates legal procedures;  
(8) Engage in other litigation activities according to law.

<a id="art-399"></a>第三百九十九条  在法庭审理中，公诉人应当客观、全面、公正地向法庭出示与定罪、量刑有关的证明被告人有罪、罪重或者罪轻的证据。  
按照审判长要求，或者经审判长同意，公诉人可以按照以下方式举证、质证：  
（一）对于可能影响定罪量刑的关键证据和控辩双方存在争议的证据，一般应当单独举证、质证；  
（二）对于不影响定罪量刑且控辩双方无异议的证据，可以仅就证据的名称及其证明的事项、内容作出说明；  
（三）对于证明方向一致、证明内容相近或者证据种类相同，存在内在逻辑关系的证据，可以归纳、分组示证、质证。  
公诉人出示证据时，可以借助多媒体设备等方式出示、播放或者演示证据内容。  
定罪证据与量刑证据需要分开的，应当分别出示。

**Article 399** In court trial, the public prosecutor shall objectively, comprehensively, and fairly present to the court evidence that proves the guilt, aggravated guilt, or mitigated guilt of the defendant, which is related to conviction and sentencing.  
At the request of the presiding judge, or with the consent of the presiding judge, the public prosecutor may present evidence and conduct cross-examination in the following manners:  
(1) For key evidence that may affect conviction and sentencing and evidence in dispute between the prosecution and the defense, it shall generally be presented and cross-examined separately;  
(2) For evidence that does not affect conviction and sentencing and to which neither the prosecution nor the defense objects, an explanation may be made only with respect to the name of the evidence and the facts or content it proves;  
(3) For evidence that has consistent probative direction, similar probative content, or is of the same type, and has inherent logical relationships, it may be summarized, presented in groups, and cross-examined.  
When presenting evidence, the public prosecutor may use multimedia equipment or other means to present, play, or demonstrate the content of evidence.  
Where evidence for conviction and evidence for sentencing need to be separated, they shall be presented separately.

<a id="art-400"></a>第四百条  公诉人讯问被告人，询问证人、被害人、鉴定人，出示物证，宣读书证、未出庭证人的证言笔录等应当围绕下列事实进行：  
（一）被告人的身份；  
（二）指控的犯罪事实是否存在，是否为被告人所实施；  
（三）实施犯罪行为的时间、地点、方法、手段、结果，被告人犯罪后的表现等；  
（四）犯罪集团或者其他共同犯罪案件中参与犯罪人员的各自地位和应负的责任；  
（五）被告人有无刑事责任能力，有无故意或者过失，行为的动机、目的；  
（六）有无依法不应当追究刑事责任的情况，有无法定的从重或者从轻、减轻以及免除处罚的情节；  
（七）犯罪对象、作案工具的主要特征，与犯罪有关的财物的来源、数量以及去向；  
（八）被告人全部或者部分否认起诉书指控的犯罪事实的，否认的根据和理由能否成立；  
（九）与定罪、量刑有关的其他事实。

**Article 400** When interrogating the defendant, questioning witnesses, victims, and expert evaluators, presenting physical evidence, reading documentary evidence, written testimony of witnesses not appearing in court, etc., the public prosecutor shall focus on the following facts:  
(1) The identity of the defendant;  
(2) Whether the alleged criminal facts exist and whether they were committed by the defendant;  
(3) The time, place, method, means, and result of the commission of the criminal act, and the conduct of the defendant after the crime;  
(4) The respective status and liability of persons participating in the crime in a criminal group or other joint crime cases;  
(5) Whether the defendant has the capacity for criminal liability, whether there is intent or negligence, and the motive and purpose of the act;  
(6) Whether there are circumstances under which criminal liability should not be investigated according to law, and whether there are legally prescribed circumstances for aggravated, mitigated, reduced, or exempted punishment;  
(7) The main characteristics of the object of crime and the tools used, and the source, quantity, and destination of property related to the crime;  
(8) If the defendant denies in whole or in part the criminal facts alleged in the indictment, whether the grounds and reasons for the denial are tenable;  
(9) Other facts related to conviction and sentencing.

<a id="art-401"></a>第四百零一条  在法庭审理中，下列事实不必提出证据进行证明：  
（一）为一般人共同知晓的常识性事实；  
（二）人民法院生效裁判所确认并且未依审判监督程序重新审理的事实；  
（三）法律、法规的内容以及适用等属于审判人员履行职务所应当知晓的事实；  
（四）在法庭审理中不存在异议的程序事实；  
（五）法律规定的推定事实；  
（六）自然规律或者定律。

**Article 401** In court trial, the following facts need not be proved by evidence:  
(1) Common knowledge facts generally known to the public;  
(2) Facts confirmed by a legally effective judgment of the People's Court and not retried under the trial supervision procedure;  
(3) Facts that the content and application of laws and regulations, etc., fall within the scope that trial personnel should know in performing their duties;  
(4) Procedural facts in court trial to which no objection exists;  
(5) Facts presumed by law;  
(6) Laws of nature or established principles.

<a id="art-402"></a>第四百零二条  讯问被告人、询问证人不得采取可能影响陈述或者证言客观真实的诱导性发问以及其他不当发问方式。  
辩护人向被告人或者证人进行诱导性发问以及其他不当发问可能影响陈述或者证言的客观真实的，公诉人可以要求审判长制止或者要求对该项陈述或者证言不予采纳。  
讯问共同犯罪案件的被告人、询问证人应当个别进行。  
被告人、证人、被害人对同一事实的陈述存在矛盾的，公诉人可以建议法庭传唤有关被告人、通知有关证人同时到庭对质，必要时可以建议法庭询问被害人。

**Article 402** When interrogating the defendant or questioning witnesses, leading questions or other improper questioning methods that may affect the objectivity and truthfulness of statements or testimony shall not be used.  
If the defender uses leading questions or other improper questioning methods that may affect the objectivity and truthfulness of statements or testimony when questioning the defendant or a witness, the public prosecutor may request the presiding judge to stop such questioning or request that such statements or testimony not be admitted.  
The interrogation of defendants in a joint crime case and the questioning of witnesses shall be conducted individually.  
If there are contradictions between the statements of the defendant, a witness, or the victim concerning the same fact, the public prosecutor may recommend that the court summon the relevant defendant and notify the relevant witness to appear simultaneously for confrontation, and if necessary, may recommend that the court question the victim.

<a id="art-403"></a>第四百零三条  被告人在庭审中的陈述与在侦查、审查起诉中的供述一致或者不一致的内容不影响定罪量刑的，可以不宣读被告人供述笔录。  
被告人在庭审中的陈述与在侦查、审查起诉中的供述不一致，足以影响定罪量刑的，可以宣读被告人供述笔录，并针对笔录中被告人的供述内容对被告人进行讯问，或者提出其他证据进行证明。

**Article 403** Where the content of the defendant's statement in court is consistent or inconsistent with his confession during investigation or examination for prosecution and does not affect conviction and sentencing, the record of the defendant's confession may not be read out.  
Where the defendant's statement in court is inconsistent with his confession during investigation or examination for prosecution to an extent sufficient to affect conviction and sentencing, the record of the defendant's confession may be read out, and the defendant may be interrogated regarding the content of his confession in the record, or other evidence may be presented to prove the facts.

<a id="art-404"></a>第四百零四条  公诉人对证人证言有异议，且该证人证言对案件定罪量刑有重大影响的，可以申请人民法院通知证人出庭作证。  
人民警察就其执行职务时目击的犯罪情况作为证人出庭作证，适用前款规定。  
公诉人对鉴定意见有异议的，可以申请人民法院通知鉴定人出庭作证。经人民法院通知，鉴定人拒不出庭作证的，公诉人可以建议法庭不予采纳该鉴定意见作为定案的根据，也可以申请法庭重新通知鉴定人出庭作证或者申请重新鉴定。  
必要时，公诉人可以申请法庭通知有专门知识的人出庭，就鉴定人作出的鉴定意见提出意见。  
当事人或者辩护人、诉讼代理人对证人证言、鉴定意见有异议的，公诉人认为必要时，可以申请人民法院通知证人、鉴定人出庭作证。

**Article 404** Where the public prosecutor objects to witness testimony, and such testimony has a significant impact on the conviction and sentencing of the case, he may apply to the People's Court to notify the witness to appear in court to testify.  
Where a people's police officer testifies as a witness regarding criminal circumstances witnessed while performing his duties, the preceding paragraph applies.  
Where the public prosecutor objects to an expert evaluation opinion, he may apply to the People's Court to notify the expert evaluator to appear in court to testify. If, after notification by the People's Court, the expert evaluator refuses to appear in court to testify, the public prosecutor may recommend that the court refuse to admit the expert evaluation opinion as the basis for the decision, or may apply to the court to re-notify the expert evaluator to appear in court to testify or to apply for a new expert evaluation.  
If necessary, the public prosecutor may apply to the court to notify a person with specialized knowledge to appear in court to give opinions on the expert evaluation opinion made by the expert evaluator.  
Where a party, defender, or litigation agent objects to witness testimony or an expert evaluation opinion, and the public prosecutor considers it necessary, he may apply to the People's Court to notify the witness or expert evaluator to appear in court to testify.

<a id="art-405"></a>第四百零五条  证人应当由人民法院通知并负责安排出庭作证。  
对于经人民法院通知而未到庭的证人或者出庭后拒绝作证的证人的证言笔录，公诉人应当当庭宣读。  
对于经人民法院通知而未到庭的证人的证言笔录存在疑问，确实需要证人出庭作证，且可以强制其到庭的，公诉人应当建议人民法院强制证人到庭作证和接受质证。

**Article 405** Witnesses shall be notified by the People's Court and arrangements shall be made for them to appear in court to testify.  
For a witness who, after notification by the People's Court, fails to appear in court, or who appears but refuses to testify, the public prosecutor shall read out the written testimony of such witness in court.  
If there are doubts about the written testimony of a witness who, after notification by the People's Court, fails to appear in court, and it is truly necessary for the witness to appear to testify and the witness can be compelled to appear, the public prosecutor shall recommend that the People's Court compel the witness to appear in court to testify and undergo cross-examination.

<a id="art-406"></a>第四百零六条  证人在法庭上提供证言，公诉人应当按照审判长确定的顺序向证人发问。可以要求证人就其所了解的与案件有关的事实进行陈述，也可以直接发问。  
证人不能连贯陈述的，公诉人可以直接发问。  
向证人发问，应当针对证言中有遗漏、矛盾、模糊不清和有争议的内容，并着重围绕与定罪量刑紧密相关的事实进行。  
发问采取一问一答形式，提问应当简洁、清楚。  
证人进行虚假陈述的，应当通过发问澄清事实，必要时可以宣读在侦查、审查起诉阶段制作的该证人的证言笔录或者出示、宣读其他证据。  
当事人和辩护人、诉讼代理人向证人发问后，公诉人可以根据证人回答的情况，经审判长许可，再次向证人发问。  
询问鉴定人、有专门知识的人参照上述规定进行。

**Article 406** When a witness provides testimony in court, the public prosecutor shall question the witness in the order determined by the presiding judge. The public prosecutor may request the witness to make a statement about the facts related to the case that he knows, or may directly ask questions.  
If the witness cannot give a coherent statement, the public prosecutor may directly ask questions.  
Questions to the witness shall be directed to omissions, contradictions, ambiguities, and disputed content in the testimony, and shall focus on facts closely related to conviction and sentencing.  
Questioning shall be conducted in a question-and-answer format, and the questions shall be concise and clear.  
If the witness gives false testimony, the public prosecutor shall clarify the facts through questioning, and if necessary, may read out the written testimony of the witness made during the investigation or examination for prosecution, or present or read out other evidence.  
After the parties, defender, or litigation agent have questioned the witness, the public prosecutor may, with the permission of the presiding judge, question the witness again based on the witness's answers.  
The questioning of expert evaluators and persons with specialized knowledge shall be conducted by reference to the above provisions.

<a id="art-407"></a>第四百零七条  必要时，公诉人可以建议法庭采取不暴露证人、鉴定人、被害人外貌、真实声音等出庭作证保护措施，或者建议法庭根据[刑事诉讼法第一百五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-154)的规定在庭外对证据进行核实。

**Article 407** If necessary, the public prosecutor may recommend that the court take protective measures for witnesses, expert evaluators, and victims appearing in court, such as not exposing their appearance, real voice, etc., or may recommend that the court verify evidence outside the court in accordance with Article 154 of the Criminal Procedure Law.

<a id="art-408"></a>第四百零八条  对于鉴定意见、勘验、检查、辨认、侦查实验等笔录和其他作为证据的文书以及经人民法院通知而未到庭的被害人的陈述笔录，公诉人应当当庭宣读。

**Article 408** The public prosecutor shall read out in court the expert evaluation opinions, records of inquests, inspections, identifications, investigative experiments, and other documents used as evidence, as well as the written statement of a victim who, after notification by the People's Court, fails to appear in court.

<a id="art-409"></a>第四百零九条  公诉人向法庭出示物证，一般应当出示原物，原物不易搬运、不易保存或者已返还被害人的，可以出示反映原物外形和特征的照片、录像、复制品，并向法庭说明情况及与原物的同一性。  
公诉人向法庭出示书证，一般应当出示原件。获取书证原件确有困难的，可以出示书证副本或者复制件，并向法庭说明情况及与原件的同一性。  
公诉人向法庭出示物证、书证，应当对该物证、书证所要证明的内容、获取情况作出说明，并向当事人、证人等问明物证的主要特征，让其辨认。对该物证、书证进行鉴定的，应当宣读鉴定意见。

**Article 409** When presenting physical evidence to the court, the public prosecutor shall generally present the original object. If the original object is difficult to transport, difficult to preserve, or has been returned to the victim, photographs, videos, or reproductions that reflect the appearance and characteristics of the original object may be presented, and an explanation shall be given to the court regarding the circumstances and the identity with the original object.  
When presenting documentary evidence to the court, the public prosecutor shall generally present the original document. If it is truly difficult to obtain the original document, a duplicate or copy of the document may be presented, and an explanation shall be given to the court regarding the circumstances and the identity with the original.  
When presenting physical evidence or documentary evidence to the court, the public prosecutor shall explain the content to be proved by such evidence and the circumstances under which it was obtained, and shall inquire of the parties, witnesses, etc., about the main characteristics of the physical evidence for their identification. If expert evaluation has been conducted on such physical or documentary evidence, the expert evaluation opinion shall be read out.

<a id="art-410"></a>第四百一十条  在法庭审理过程中，被告人及其辩护人提出被告人庭前供述系非法取得，审判人员认为需要进行法庭调查的，公诉人可以通过出示讯问笔录、提讯登记、体检记录、采取强制措施或者侦查措施的法律文书、侦查终结前对讯问合法性进行核查的材料等证据材料，有针对性地播放讯问录音、录像，提请法庭通知调查人员、侦查人员或者其他人员出庭说明情况等方式，对证据收集的合法性加以证明。  
审判人员认为可能存在[刑事诉讼法第五十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-56)规定的以非法方法收集其他证据的情形，需要进行法庭调查的，公诉人可以参照前款规定对证据收集的合法性进行证明。  
公诉人不能当庭证明证据收集的合法性，需要调查核实的，可以建议法庭休庭或者延期审理。  
在法庭审理期间，人民检察院可以要求监察机关或者公安机关对证据收集的合法性进行说明或者提供相关证明材料。必要时，可以自行调查核实。

**Article 410** During the court trial, if the defendant or his defender alleges that the defendant's pre-trial confession was obtained illegally, and the trial personnel consider that a court investigation is necessary, the public prosecutor may prove the legality of evidence collection by presenting evidence materials such as interrogation records, registration of bringing in for interrogation, physical examination records, legal documents on compulsory measures or investigative measures, materials verifying the legality of interrogation before the conclusion of investigation, playing targeted audio or video recordings of interrogations, or requesting the court to notify investigators, detectives, or other persons to appear in court to explain the circumstances.  
If the trial personnel consider that there may be circumstances of collecting other evidence by illegal methods as provided in Article 56 of the Criminal Procedure Law and a court investigation is necessary, the public prosecutor may prove the legality of evidence collection by reference to the preceding paragraph.  
If the public prosecutor cannot prove the legality of evidence collection in court and investigation and verification are necessary, he may recommend that the court adjourn or postpone the trial.  
During the court trial, the People's Procuratorate may request the supervisory organ or the public security organ to explain the legality of evidence collection or provide relevant supporting materials. If necessary, it may conduct its own investigation and verification.

<a id="art-411"></a>第四百一十一条  公诉人对证据收集的合法性进行证明后，法庭仍有疑问的，可以建议法庭休庭，由人民法院对相关证据进行调查核实。人民法院调查核实证据，通知人民检察院派员到场的，人民检察院可以派员到场。

**Article 411** After the public prosecutor has proved the legality of evidence collection, if the court still has doubts, the public prosecutor may suggest that the court adjourn and that the People's Court investigate and verify the relevant evidence. Where the People's Court investigates and verifies the evidence and notifies the People's Procuratorate to send personnel to be present, the People's Procuratorate may send personnel to be present.

<a id="art-412"></a>第四百一十二条  在法庭审理过程中，对证据合法性以外的其他程序事实存在争议的，公诉人应当出示、宣读有关诉讼文书、侦查或者审查起诉活动笔录。

**Article 412** During court adjudication, where there is a dispute over procedural facts other than the legality of evidence, the public prosecutor shall present and read out the relevant procedural documents, and the records of investigation or examination and prosecution activities.

<a id="art-413"></a>第四百一十三条  对于搜查、查封、扣押、冻结、勘验、检查、辨认、侦查实验等活动中形成的笔录存在争议，需要调查人员、侦查人员以及上述活动的见证人出庭陈述有关情况的，公诉人可以建议合议庭通知其出庭。

**Article 413** Where there is a dispute over the records formed in activities such as search, sealing, seizure, freezing, inspection, examination, identification, and investigative experiment, and it is necessary for investigators, case handlers, and witnesses of the above activities to appear in court to testify on the relevant circumstances, the public prosecutor may suggest that the collegial panel notify them to appear in court.

<a id="art-414"></a>第四百一十四条  在法庭审理过程中，合议庭对证据有疑问或者人民法院根据辩护人、被告人的申请，向人民检察院调取在侦查、审查起诉中收集的有关被告人无罪或者罪轻的证据材料的，人民检察院应当自收到人民法院要求调取证据材料决定书后三日以内移交。没有上述材料的，应当向人民法院说明情况。

**Article 414** During court adjudication, where the collegial panel has doubts about the evidence or the People's Court, upon application by the defender or the defendant, requests the People's Procuratorate to produce evidentiary materials collected during investigation and examination and prosecution that relate to the defendant's innocence or lesser guilt, the People's Procuratorate shall transfer such materials within three days after receiving the decision of the People's Court requesting the production of evidence materials. If no such materials exist, the People's Procuratorate shall explain the circumstances to the People's Court.

<a id="art-415"></a>第四百一十五条  在法庭审理过程中，合议庭对证据有疑问并在休庭后进行勘验、检查、查封、扣押、鉴定和查询、冻结的，人民检察院应当依法进行监督，发现上述活动有违法情况的，应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 415** During court adjudication, where the collegial panel has doubts about the evidence and, after adjournment, conducts inspection, examination, sealing, seizure, expert evaluation, inquiry, and freezing, the People's Procuratorate shall supervise in accordance with law, and if it discovers any illegal activity in the above actions, it shall provide corrective opinions.

<a id="art-416"></a>第四百一十六条  人民法院根据申请收集、调取的证据或者在合议庭休庭后自行调查取得的证据，应当经过庭审出示、质证才能决定是否作为判决的依据。未经庭审出示、质证直接采纳为判决依据的，人民检察院应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 416** Evidence collected or produced by the People's Court upon application or obtained through the collegial panel's own investigation after adjournment shall be presented and cross-examined in court before a decision is made on whether to use it as the basis for judgment. Where such evidence is directly adopted as the basis for judgment without presentation and cross-examination in court, the People's Procuratorate shall provide corrective opinions.

<a id="art-417"></a>第四百一十七条  在法庭审理过程中，经审判长许可，公诉人可以逐一对正在调查的证据和案件情况发表意见，并同被告人、辩护人进行辩论。证据调查结束时，公诉人应当发表总结性意见。  
在法庭辩论中，公诉人与被害人、诉讼代理人意见不一致的，公诉人应当认真听取被害人、诉讼代理人的意见，阐明自己的意见和理由。

**Article 417** During court adjudication, with the permission of the presiding judge, the public prosecutor may, one by one, express opinions on the evidence under investigation and the circumstances of the case, and engage in debate with the defendant and his defender. When the investigation of evidence concludes, the public prosecutor shall deliver a summary opinion.  
During court debate, where the public prosecutor holds a different opinion from the victim or the litigation agent, the public prosecutor shall carefully listen to the opinions of the victim and the litigation agent, and state his own opinions and reasons.

<a id="art-418"></a>第四百一十八条  人民检察院向人民法院提出量刑建议的，公诉人应当在发表公诉意见时提出。  
对认罪认罚案件，人民法院经审理认为人民检察院的量刑建议明显不当向人民检察院提出的，或者被告人、辩护人对量刑建议提出异议的，人民检察院可以调整量刑建议。

**Article 418** Where the People's Procuratorate submits a sentencing recommendation to the People's Court, the public prosecutor shall make such recommendation when delivering the prosecution opinion.  
In cases where the defendant confesses guilt and accepts punishment, if the People's Court after trial believes that the People's Procuratorate's sentencing recommendation is manifestly improper and raises the issue with the People's Procuratorate, or if the defendant or his defender objects to the sentencing recommendation, the People's Procuratorate may adjust the sentencing recommendation.

<a id="art-419"></a>第四百一十九条  适用普通程序审理的认罪认罚案件，公诉人可以建议适当简化法庭调查、辩论程序。

**Article 419** In cases where the defendant confesses guilt and accepts punishment and which are tried under ordinary procedure, the public prosecutor may suggest appropriate simplification of the court investigation and debate procedures.

<a id="art-420"></a>第四百二十条  在法庭审判过程中，遇有下列情形之一的，公诉人可以建议法庭延期审理：  
（一）发现事实不清、证据不足，或者遗漏罪行、遗漏同案犯罪嫌疑人，需要补充侦查或者补充提供证据的；  
（二）被告人揭发他人犯罪行为或者提供重要线索，需要补充侦查进行查证的；  
（三）发现遗漏罪行或者遗漏同案犯罪嫌疑人，虽不需要补充侦查和补充提供证据，但需要补充、追加起诉的；  
（四）申请人民法院通知证人、鉴定人出庭作证或者有专门知识的人出庭提出意见的；  
（五）需要调取新的证据，重新鉴定或者勘验的；  
（六）公诉人出示、宣读开庭前移送人民法院的证据以外的证据，或者补充、追加、变更起诉，需要给予被告人、辩护人必要时间进行辩护准备的；  
（七）被告人、辩护人向法庭出示公诉人不掌握的与定罪量刑有关的证据，需要调查核实的；  
（八）公诉人对证据收集的合法性进行证明，需要调查核实的。  
在人民法院开庭审理前发现具有前款情形之一的，人民检察院可以建议人民法院延期审理。

**Article 420** During court adjudication, in any of the following circumstances, the public prosecutor may suggest that the court adjourn the hearing:  
(1) Where facts are unclear or evidence is insufficient, or there is an omission of crimes or of co-suspects, and supplementary investigation or supplementary provision of evidence is necessary;  
(2) Where the defendant reveals another person's criminal act or provides important leads, requiring supplementary investigation for verification;  
(3) Where an omission of crimes or co-suspects is discovered, and although supplementary investigation or supplementary provision of evidence is not necessary, supplementary or additional prosecution is needed;  
(4) Where a request is made for the People's Court to notify witnesses, expert evaluators to testify in court, or experts to appear in court to provide opinions;  
(5) Where it is necessary to obtain new evidence, conduct a new expert evaluation or inspection;  
(6) Where the public prosecutor presents or reads out evidence not among the evidence transferred to the People's Court before trial, or supplements, adds to, or modifies the prosecution, requiring that the defendant and his defender be given necessary time to prepare their defense;  
(7) Where the defendant or his defender presents to the court evidence related to conviction and sentencing that is not in the possession of the public prosecutor and requires investigation and verification;  
(8) Where the public prosecutor proves the legality of evidence collection and requires investigation and verification.  
If any of the circumstances in the preceding paragraph is discovered before the People's Court opens the trial, the People's Procuratorate may suggest that the People's Court adjourn the hearing.

<a id="art-421"></a>第四百二十一条  法庭宣布延期审理后，人民检察院应当在补充侦查期限内提请人民法院恢复法庭审理或者撤回起诉。  
公诉人在法庭审理过程中建议延期审理的次数不得超过两次，每次不得超过一个月。

**Article 421** After the court announces the adjournment, the People's Procuratorate shall, within the time limit for supplementary investigation, request the People's Court to resume the court hearing or withdraw the prosecution.  
During court adjudication, the public prosecutor may suggest an adjournment no more than twice, each time no longer than one month.

<a id="art-422"></a>第四百二十二条  在审判过程中，对于需要补充提供法庭审判所必需的证据或者补充侦查的，人民检察院应当自行收集证据和进行侦查，必要时可以要求监察机关或者公安机关提供协助；也可以书面要求监察机关或者公安机关补充提供证据。  
人民检察院补充侦查，适用本规则第六章、第九章、第十章的规定。  
补充侦查不得超过一个月。

**Article 422** During trial, where it is necessary to supplementally provide evidence necessary for trial or to conduct supplementary investigation, the People's Procuratorate shall collect evidence and conduct investigation on its own; if necessary, it may request assistance from the supervisory organ or the public security organ; it may also request in writing that the supervisory organ or the public security organ supplementally provide evidence.  
For supplementary investigation by the People's Procuratorate, the provisions of Chapters Six, Nine, and Ten of these Rules shall apply.  
Supplementary investigation shall not exceed one month.

<a id="art-423"></a>第四百二十三条  人民法院宣告判决前，人民检察院发现被告人的真实身份或者犯罪事实与起诉书中叙述的身份或者指控犯罪事实不符的，或者事实、证据没有变化，但罪名、适用法律与起诉书不一致的，可以变更起诉。发现遗漏同案犯罪嫌疑人或者罪行的，应当要求公安机关补充移送起诉或者补充侦查；对于犯罪事实清楚，证据确实、充分的，可以直接追加、补充起诉。

**Article 423** Before the People's Court pronounces judgment, if the People's Procuratorate discovers that the defendant's true identity or the facts of the crime are inconsistent with the identity or the alleged criminal facts stated in the indictment, or if the facts and evidence have not changed but the charge or applicable law is inconsistent with the indictment, the People's Procuratorate may modify the indictment. If it discovers an omission of a co-suspect or a crime, it shall request the public security organ to supplementally transfer the prosecution or conduct supplementary investigation; if the facts of the crime are clear and the evidence is reliable and sufficient, it may directly add or supplement the prosecution.

<a id="art-424"></a>第四百二十四条  人民法院宣告判决前，人民检察院发现具有下列情形之一的，经检察长批准，可以撤回起诉：  
（一）不存在犯罪事实的；  
（二）犯罪事实并非被告人所为的；  
（三）情节显著轻微、危害不大，不认为是犯罪的；  
（四）证据不足或证据发生变化，不符合起诉条件的；  
（五）被告人因未达到刑事责任年龄，不负刑事责任的；  
（六）法律、司法解释发生变化导致不应当追究被告人刑事责任的；  
（七）其他不应当追究被告人刑事责任的。  
对于撤回起诉的案件，人民检察院应当在撤回起诉后三十日以内作出不起诉决定。需要重新调查或者侦查的，应当在作出不起诉决定后将案卷材料退回监察机关或者公安机关，建议监察机关或者公安机关重新调查或者侦查，并书面说明理由。  
对于撤回起诉的案件，没有新的事实或者新的证据，人民检察院不得再行起诉。  
新的事实是指原起诉书中未指控的犯罪事实。该犯罪事实触犯的罪名既可以是原指控罪名的同一罪名，也可以是其他罪名。  
新的证据是指撤回起诉后收集、调取的足以证明原指控犯罪事实的证据。

**Article 424** Before the People's Court pronounces judgment, if the People's Procuratorate discovers any of the following circumstances, with the approval of the chief procurator, it may withdraw the prosecution:  
(1) There is no criminal fact;  
(2) The criminal fact was not committed by the defendant;  
(3) The circumstances are obviously minor, the harm done is not significant, and it is not considered a crime;  
(4) The evidence is insufficient or has changed, and the conditions for prosecution are not met;  
(5) The defendant does not bear criminal liability because he has not reached the age of criminal liability;  
(6) Changes in laws or judicial interpretations result in the defendant no longer being subject to criminal liability;  
(7) Other circumstances where the defendant should not be subject to criminal liability.  
For a case where the prosecution is withdrawn, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision not to prosecute within thirty days after the withdrawal. If reinvestigation or supplementary investigation is necessary, after making the decision not to prosecute, the case file materials shall be returned to the supervisory organ or the public security organ, and the People's Procuratorate shall suggest that the supervisory organ or the public security organ reinvestigate or conduct supplementary investigation, and state the reasons in writing.  
For a case where the prosecution is withdrawn, the People's Procuratorate shall not again initiate prosecution unless there are new facts or new evidence.  
New facts refer to criminal facts not alleged in the original indictment. The crime implicated by such criminal facts may be the same as the originally charged crime or a different crime.  
New evidence refers to evidence collected or produced after the withdrawal that is sufficient to prove the originally alleged criminal facts.

<a id="art-425"></a>第四百二十五条  在法庭审理过程中，人民法院建议人民检察院补充侦查、补充起诉、追加起诉或者变更起诉的，人民检察院应当审查有关理由，并作出是否补充侦查、补充起诉、追加起诉或者变更起诉的决定。人民检察院不同意的，可以要求人民法院就起诉指控的犯罪事实依法作出裁判。

**Article 425** During court adjudication, where the People's Court suggests that the People's Procuratorate conduct supplementary investigation, supplement the prosecution, add to the prosecution, or modify the indictment, the People's Procuratorate shall examine the relevant reasons and make a decision on whether to conduct supplementary investigation, supplement the prosecution, add to the prosecution, or modify the indictment. If the People's Procuratorate disagrees, it may request that the People's Court render a judgment on the criminal facts charged in the indictment in accordance with law.

<a id="art-426"></a>第四百二十六条  变更、追加、补充或者撤回起诉应当以书面方式在判决宣告前向人民法院提出。

**Article 426** Modification, addition, supplementation, or withdrawal of a prosecution shall be submitted to the People's Court in writing before judgment is pronounced.

<a id="art-427"></a>第四百二十七条  出庭的书记员应当制作出庭笔录，详细记载庭审的时间、地点、参加人员、公诉人出庭执行任务情况和法庭调查、法庭辩论的主要内容以及法庭判决结果，由公诉人和书记员签名。

**Article 427** The court clerk present at the hearing shall make a hearing record, detailing the time and place of the hearing, the participants, the execution of duties by the public prosecutor, and the main contents of the court investigation and debate, as well as the judgment result of the court, signed by the public prosecutor and the court clerk.

<a id="art-428"></a>第四百二十八条  人民检察院应当当庭向人民法院移交取回的案卷材料和证据。在审判长宣布休庭后，公诉人应当与审判人员办理交接手续。无法当庭移交的，应当在休庭后三日以内移交。

**Article 428** The People's Procuratorate shall, in court, transfer to the People's Court the case file materials and evidence that have been retrieved. After the presiding judge announces the adjournment, the public prosecutor shall handle the handover procedures with the judicial personnel. If the materials cannot be transferred in court, they shall be transferred within three days after adjournment.

<a id="art-429"></a>第四百二十九条  人民检察院对查封、扣押、冻结的被告人财物及其孳息，应当根据不同情况作以下处理：  
（一）对作为证据使用的实物，应当依法随案移送；对不宜移送的，应当将其清单、照片或者其他证明文件随案移送。  
（二）冻结在金融机构、邮政部门的违法所得及其他涉案财产，应当向人民法院随案移送该金融机构、邮政部门出具的证明文件。待人民法院作出生效判决、裁定后，由人民法院通知该金融机构上缴国库。  
（三）查封、扣押的涉案财物，对依法不移送的，应当随案移送清单、照片或者其他证明文件。待人民法院作出生效判决、裁定后，由人民检察院根据人民法院的通知上缴国库，并向人民法院送交执行回单。  
（四）对于被扣押、冻结的债券、股票、基金份额等财产，在扣押、冻结期间权利人申请出售的，参照[本规则第二百一十四条](#art-214)的规定办理。

**Article 429** The People's Procuratorate shall handle the defendant's property and its fruits that have been sealed, seized, or frozen according to different circumstances as follows:  
(1) For physical evidence that is used as evidence, it shall be transferred with the case file in accordance with law; for items that are not suitable for transfer, their inventory, photographs, or other documentary evidence shall be transferred with the case file.  
(2) For illegal gains and other property involved in the case that have been frozen in financial institutions or postal departments, the documentary evidence issued by such financial institutions or postal departments shall be transferred with the case file to the People's Court. After the People's Court renders an effective judgment or ruling, it shall notify the financial institution to turn over the property to the State Treasury.  
(3) For sealed or seized property involved in the case that is not transferred in accordance with law, an inventory, photographs, or other documentary evidence shall be transferred with the case file. After the People's Court renders an effective judgment or ruling, the People's Procuratorate shall, based on the notification from the People's Court, turn over the property to the State Treasury and submit a receipt of execution to the People's Court.  
(4) For bonds, stocks, fund shares, and other property that have been seized or frozen, if during the period of seizure or freezing the right holder applies for sale, it shall be handled with reference to the provisions of Article 214 of these Rules.

#### 第二节 简易程序

<a id="art-430"></a>第四百三十条  人民检察院对于基层人民法院管辖的案件，符合下列条件的，可以建议人民法院适用简易程序审理：  
（一）案件事实清楚、证据充分的；  
（二）被告人承认自己所犯罪行，对指控的犯罪事实没有异议的；  
（三）被告人对适用简易程序没有异议的。

**Article 430** For cases under the jurisdiction of a Primary People's Court, if the following conditions are met, the People's Procuratorate may suggest that the People's Court apply summary procedure:  
(1) The facts of the case are clear and the evidence is sufficient;  
(2) The defendant admits the crime he committed and does not object to the alleged criminal facts;  
(3) The defendant does not object to the application of summary procedure.

<a id="art-431"></a>第四百三十一条  具有下列情形之一的，人民检察院不得建议人民法院适用简易程序：  
（一）被告人是盲、聋、哑人，或者是尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人的；  
（二）有重大社会影响的；  
（三）共同犯罪案件中部分被告人不认罪或者对适用简易程序有异议的；  
（四）比较复杂的共同犯罪案件；  
（五）辩护人作无罪辩护或者对主要犯罪事实有异议的；  
（六）其他不宜适用简易程序的。  
人民法院决定适用简易程序审理的案件，人民检察院认为具有[刑事诉讼法第二百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-215)规定情形之一的，应当向人民法院提出纠正意见；具有其他不宜适用简易程序情形的，人民检察院可以建议人民法院不适用简易程序。

**Article 431** In any of the following circumstances, the People's Procuratorate shall not recommend that the People's Court apply the summary procedure:  
(1) The defendant is blind, deaf or mute, or is a mental patient who has not completely lost the ability to recognize or control his own conduct;  
(2) The case has a significant social impact;  
(3) In a joint crime case, some defendants do not plead guilty or object to the application of the summary procedure;  
(4) The joint crime case is relatively complex;  
(5) The defender makes a defense of innocence or objects to the principal criminal facts;  
(6) Other circumstances where the summary procedure is not appropriate.  
If the People's Court decides to try a case by applying the summary procedure, and the People's Procuratorate deems that the case falls under any of the circumstances provided for in Article 215 of the Criminal Procedure Law, it shall submit corrective opinions to the People's Court; if there are other circumstances where the summary procedure is not appropriate, the People's Procuratorate may recommend that the People's Court not apply the summary procedure.

<a id="art-432"></a>第四百三十二条  基层人民检察院审查案件，认为案件事实清楚、证据充分的，应当在讯问犯罪嫌疑人时，了解其是否承认自己所犯罪行，对指控的犯罪事实有无异议，告知其适用简易程序的法律规定，确认其是否同意适用简易程序。

**Article 432** When a primary People's Procuratorate reviews a case and deems that the facts are clear and the evidence is sufficient, it shall, when interrogating the criminal suspect, ascertain whether he confesses to the crime he committed and whether he has any objection to the charged criminal facts, inform him of the legal provisions on the application of the summary procedure, and confirm whether he agrees to the application of the summary procedure.

<a id="art-433"></a>第四百三十三条  适用简易程序审理的公诉案件，人民检察院应当派员出席法庭。

**Article 433** In a public prosecution case tried under the summary procedure, the People's Procuratorate shall send an officer to appear before the court.

<a id="art-434"></a>第四百三十四条  公诉人出席简易程序法庭时，应当主要围绕量刑以及其他有争议的问题进行法庭调查和法庭辩论。在确认被告人庭前收到起诉书并对起诉书指控的犯罪事实没有异议后，可以简化宣读起诉书，根据案件情况决定是否讯问被告人，询问证人、鉴定人和出示证据。  
根据案件情况，公诉人可以建议法庭简化法庭调查和法庭辩论程序。

**Article 434** When appearing before a court applying the summary procedure, the public prosecutor shall primarily conduct court investigation and court debate focusing on sentencing and other disputed issues. After confirming that the defendant has received the indictment before trial and has no objection to the criminal facts charged in the indictment, the public prosecutor may simplify the reading of the indictment, decide whether to interrogate the defendant, question witnesses and forensic experts, and present evidence based on the circumstances of the case.  
Based on the circumstances of the case, the public prosecutor may recommend that the court simplify the procedures for court investigation and court debate.

<a id="art-435"></a>第四百三十五条  适用简易程序审理的公诉案件，公诉人发现不宜适用简易程序审理的，应当建议法庭按照第一审普通程序重新审理。

**Article 435** In a public prosecution case tried under the summary procedure, if the public prosecutor discovers that it is not appropriate to apply the summary procedure, he shall recommend that the court conduct a retrial under the ordinary procedure of first instance.

<a id="art-436"></a>第四百三十六条  转为普通程序审理的案件，公诉人需要为出席法庭进行准备的，可以建议人民法院延期审理。

**Article 436** In a case that is converted to be tried under the ordinary procedure, if the public prosecutor needs to prepare for appearance before the court, he may recommend that the People's Court adjourn the trial.

#### 第三节 速裁程序

<a id="art-437"></a>第四百三十七条  人民检察院对基层人民法院管辖的案件，符合下列条件的，在提起公诉时，可以建议人民法院适用速裁程序审理：  
（一）可能判处三年有期徒刑以下刑罚；  
（二）案件事实清楚，证据确实、充分；  
（三）被告人认罪认罚、同意适用速裁程序。

**Article 437** Where a case under the jurisdiction of a Primary People's Court meets the following conditions, the People's Procuratorate may, when initiating a public prosecution, recommend that the People's Court try the case by applying the fast-track sentencing procedure:  
(1) The defendant may be sentenced to a punishment of fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years;  
(2) The facts of the case are clear, and the evidence is reliable and sufficient;  
(3) The defendant pleads guilty and accepts punishment, and agrees to the application of the fast-track sentencing procedure.

<a id="art-438"></a>第四百三十八条  具有下列情形之一的，人民检察院不得建议人民法院适用速裁程序：  
（一）被告人是盲、聋、哑人，或者是尚未完全丧失辨认或者控制自己行为能力的精神病人的；  
（二）被告人是未成年人的；  
（三）案件有重大社会影响的；  
（四）共同犯罪案件中部分被告人对指控的犯罪事实、罪名、量刑建议或者适用速裁程序有异议的；  
（五）被告人与被害人或者其法定代理人没有就附带民事诉讼赔偿等事项达成调解或者和解协议的；  
（六）其他不宜适用速裁程序审理的。

**Article 438** In any of the following circumstances, the People's Procuratorate shall not recommend that the People's Court apply the fast-track sentencing procedure:  
(1) The defendant is blind, deaf or mute, or is a mental patient who has not completely lost the ability to recognize or control his own conduct;  
(2) The defendant is a minor;  
(3) The case has a significant social impact;  
(4) In a joint crime case, some defendants object to the charged criminal facts, the charges, the sentencing recommendation, or the application of the fast-track sentencing procedure;  
(5) The defendant and the victim or his statutory agent have not reached a mediation or settlement agreement on matters such as compensation in the incidental civil action;  
(6) Other circumstances where the fast-track sentencing procedure is not appropriate to be applied.

<a id="art-439"></a>第四百三十九条  公安机关、犯罪嫌疑人及其辩护人建议适用速裁程序，人民检察院经审查认为符合条件的，可以建议人民法院适用速裁程序审理。  
公安机关、辩护人未建议适用速裁程序，人民检察院经审查认为符合速裁程序适用条件，且犯罪嫌疑人同意适用的，可以建议人民法院适用速裁程序审理。

**Article 439** If a public security organ, a criminal suspect, or his defender recommends the application of the fast-track sentencing procedure, and the People's Procuratorate, upon review, considers that the conditions are met, it may recommend that the People's Court try the case by applying the fast-track sentencing procedure.  
If neither the public security organ nor the defender recommends the application of the fast-track sentencing procedure, but the People's Procuratorate, upon review, considers that the conditions for applying the fast-track sentencing procedure are met and the criminal suspect agrees to its application, it may recommend that the People's Court try the case by applying the fast-track sentencing procedure.

<a id="art-440"></a>第四百四十条  人民检察院建议人民法院适用速裁程序的案件，起诉书内容可以适当简化，重点写明指控的事实和适用的法律。

**Article 440** In a case where the People's Procuratorate recommends that the People's Court apply the fast-track sentencing procedure, the content of the indictment may be appropriately simplified, focusing on the alleged facts and the applicable law.

<a id="art-441"></a>第四百四十一条  人民法院适用速裁程序审理的案件，人民检察院应当派员出席法庭。

**Article 441** In a case tried by the People's Court under the fast-track sentencing procedure, the People's Procuratorate shall send an officer to appear before the court.

<a id="art-442"></a>第四百四十二条  公诉人出席速裁程序法庭时，可以简要宣读起诉书指控的犯罪事实、证据、适用法律及量刑建议，一般不再讯问被告人。

**Article 442** When appearing before a court applying the fast-track sentencing procedure, the public prosecutor may briefly read out the criminal facts, evidence, applicable law, and sentencing recommendation charged in the indictment, and generally shall not interrogate the defendant again.

<a id="art-443"></a>第四百四十三条  适用速裁程序审理的案件，人民检察院发现有不宜适用速裁程序审理情形的，应当建议人民法院转为普通程序或者简易程序重新审理。

**Article 443** In a case tried under the fast-track sentencing procedure, if the People's Procuratorate discovers a circumstance where the fast-track sentencing procedure is not appropriate, it shall recommend that the People's Court convert the case to the ordinary procedure or the summary procedure for retrial.

<a id="art-444"></a>第四百四十四条  转为普通程序审理的案件，公诉人需要为出席法庭进行准备的，可以建议人民法院延期审理。

**Article 444** In a case that is converted to be tried under the ordinary procedure, if the public prosecutor needs to prepare for appearance before the court, he may recommend that the People's Court adjourn the trial.

#### 第四节 出席第二审法庭

<a id="art-445"></a>第四百四十五条  对提出抗诉的案件或者公诉案件中人民法院决定开庭审理的上诉案件，同级人民检察院应当指派检察官出席第二审法庭。检察官助理可以协助检察官出庭。根据需要可以配备书记员担任记录。

**Article 445** For a case in which a protest has been filed or an appeal case in a public prosecution case in which the People's Court decides to open a court session, the People's Procuratorate at the same level shall assign a prosecutor to appear before the court of second instance. A prosecutor's assistant may assist the prosecutor in appearing before the court. A court clerk may be assigned to take records as needed.

<a id="art-446"></a>第四百四十六条  检察官出席第二审法庭的任务是：  
（一）支持抗诉或者听取上诉意见，对原审人民法院作出的错误判决或者裁定提出纠正意见；  
（二）维护原审人民法院正确的判决或者裁定，建议法庭维持原判；  
（三）维护诉讼参与人的合法权利；  
（四）对法庭审理案件有无违反法律规定诉讼程序的情况记明笔录；  
（五）依法从事其他诉讼活动。

**Article 446** The tasks of a prosecutor appearing before the court of second instance are:  
(1) To support the protest or to hear the opinions on appeal, and to submit corrective opinions on the erroneous judgment or ruling made by the original trial People's Court;  
(2) To uphold the correct judgment or ruling of the original trial People's Court, and to recommend that the court affirm the original judgment;  
(3) To protect the lawful rights of participants in the proceedings;  
(4) To record in the transcript whether the court's trial violates the litigation procedures provided by law;  
(5) To engage in other litigation activities according to law.

<a id="art-447"></a>第四百四十七条  对抗诉和上诉案件，第二审人民法院的同级人民检察院可以调取下级人民检察院与案件有关的材料。  
人民检察院在接到第二审人民法院决定开庭、查阅案卷通知后，可以查阅或者调阅案卷材料。查阅或者调阅案卷材料应当在接到人民法院的通知之日起一个月以内完成。在一个月以内无法完成的，可以商请人民法院延期审理。

**Article 447** For cases of protest and appeal, the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the People's Court of second instance may obtain the materials related to the case from the lower People's Procuratorate.  
After receiving the notice from the People's Court of second instance on the decision to open a court session and to consult the case file, the People's Procuratorate may consult or obtain the case file materials. The consultation or obtaining of case file materials shall be completed within one month from the date of receiving the notice from the People's Court. If it cannot be completed within one month, the People's Procuratorate may consult with the People's Court to adjourn the trial.

<a id="art-448"></a>第四百四十八条  检察人员应当客观全面地审查原审案卷材料，不受上诉或者抗诉范围的限制。应当审查原审判决认定案件事实、适用法律是否正确，证据是否确实、充分，量刑是否适当，审判活动是否合法，并应当审查下级人民检察院的抗诉书或者上诉人的上诉状，了解抗诉或者上诉的理由是否正确、充分，重点审查有争议的案件事实、证据和法律适用问题，有针对性地做好庭审准备工作。

**Article 448** The procuratorial personnel shall objectively and comprehensively review the case file materials of the original trial, without being limited by the scope of the appeal or protest. They shall review whether the facts found and the law applied in the original trial judgment are correct, whether the evidence is reliable and sufficient, whether the sentencing is appropriate, whether the trial activities are lawful, and shall review the protest letter of the lower People's Procuratorate or the appeal petition of the appellant to ascertain whether the reasons for the protest or appeal are correct and sufficient, focusing on the disputed facts, evidence, and legal application issues, and make targeted preparations for the court trial.

<a id="art-449"></a>第四百四十九条  检察人员在审查第一审案卷材料时，应当复核主要证据，可以讯问原审被告人。必要时，可以补充收集证据、重新鉴定或者补充鉴定。需要原侦查案件的公安机关补充收集证据的，可以要求其补充收集。  
被告人、辩护人提出被告人自首、立功等可能影响定罪量刑的材料和线索的，可以移交公安机关调查核实，也可以自行调查核实。发现遗漏罪行或者同案犯罪嫌疑人的，应当建议公安机关侦查。  
对于下列原审被告人，应当进行讯问：  
（一）提出上诉的；  
（二）人民检察院提出抗诉的；  
（三）被判处无期徒刑以上刑罚的。

**Article 449** When reviewing the case file materials of the first instance, the procuratorial personnel shall verify the main evidence and may interrogate the original trial defendant. When necessary, they may supplement the collection of evidence, conduct a new expert evaluation, or supplement an expert evaluation. If the public security organ that originally investigated the case needs to supplement the collection of evidence, they may request such supplementation.  
If the defendant or the defender submits materials or clues concerning the defendant's voluntary surrender, meritorious service, or other matters that may affect conviction and sentencing, such materials and clues may be handed over to the public security organ for investigation and verification, or they may be investigated and verified by the procuratorial personnel themselves. If omitted crimes or joint crime suspects are discovered, the procuratorial personnel shall recommend that the public security organ investigate.  
The following original trial defendants shall be interrogated:  
(1) Those who file an appeal;  
(2) Those against whom the People's Procuratorate files a protest;  
(3) Those who have been sentenced to a punishment of life imprisonment or above.

<a id="art-450"></a>第四百五十条  人民检察院办理死刑上诉、抗诉案件，应当进行下列工作：  
（一）讯问原审被告人，听取原审被告人的上诉理由或者辩解；  
（二）听取辩护人的意见；  
（三）复核主要证据，必要时询问证人；  
（四）必要时补充收集证据；  
（五）对鉴定意见有疑问的，可以重新鉴定或者补充鉴定；  
（六）根据案件情况，可以听取被害人的意见。

**Article 450** When handling a death penalty appeal or protest case, the People's Procuratorate shall carry out the following work:  
(1) Interrogate the original trial defendant and hear the reasons for his appeal or his defense;  
(2) Hear the defender's opinions;  
(3) Verify the main evidence and, when necessary, question witnesses;  
(4) Supplement the collection of evidence when necessary;  
(5) If there is doubt about the expert opinion, a new expert evaluation may be conducted or an expert evaluation may be supplemented;  
(6) Depending on the circumstances of the case, the victim's opinions may be heard.

<a id="art-451"></a>第四百五十一条  出席第二审法庭前，检察人员应当制作讯问原审被告人、询问被害人、证人、鉴定人和出示、宣读、播放证据计划，拟写答辩提纲，并制作出庭意见。

**Article 451** Before appearing in the court of second instance, the prosecutors shall prepare a plan for interrogating the original defendant, questioning the victim, witnesses, and expert evaluators, and for presenting, reading out, and playing evidence; draft an outline of defense; and prepare opinions for appearing in court.

<a id="art-452"></a>第四百五十二条  在法庭审理中，检察官应当针对原审判决或者裁定认定事实或适用法律、量刑等方面的问题，围绕抗诉或者上诉理由以及辩护人的辩护意见，讯问原审被告人，询问被害人、证人、鉴定人，出示和宣读证据，并提出意见和进行辩论。

**Article 452** During court adjudication, the prosecutor shall, with respect to issues such as the facts found or the application of law or sentencing in the original judgment or ruling, focus on the grounds for protest or appeal and the defense opinions of the defender, interrogate the original defendant, question the victim, witnesses, and expert evaluators, present and read out evidence, and put forward opinions and engage in debate.

<a id="art-453"></a>第四百五十三条  需要出示、宣读、播放第一审期间已移交人民法院的证据的，出庭的检察官可以申请法庭出示、宣读、播放。  
在第二审法庭宣布休庭后需要移交证据材料的，参照[本规则第四百二十八条](#art-428)的规定办理。

**Article 453** Where it is necessary to present, read out, or play evidence that has already been transferred to the People's Court during the first instance, the prosecutor appearing in court may apply to the court for presenting, reading out, or playing it.  
Where it is necessary to transfer evidentiary materials after the court of second instance announces an adjournment, the matter shall be handled by reference to the provisions of Article 428 of these Rules.

#### 第五节 出席再审法庭

<a id="art-454"></a>第四百五十四条  人民法院开庭审理再审案件，同级人民检察院应当派员出席法庭。

**Article 454** Where the People's Court holds a hearing for a retrial case, the People's Procuratorate at the same level shall send personnel to appear in court.

<a id="art-455"></a>第四百五十五条  人民检察院对于人民法院按照审判监督程序重新审判的案件，应当对原判决、裁定认定的事实、证据、适用法律进行全面审查，重点审查有争议的案件事实、证据和法律适用问题。

**Article 455** For a case retried by the People's Court under the trial supervision procedure, the People's Procuratorate shall conduct a comprehensive review of the facts, evidence, and application of law in the original judgment or ruling, with focus on the disputed facts, evidence, and application of law issues in the case.

<a id="art-456"></a>第四百五十六条  人民检察院派员出席再审法庭，如果再审案件按照第一审程序审理，参照本章第一节有关规定执行；如果再审案件按照第二审程序审理，参照本章第四节有关规定执行。

**Article 456** Where the People's Procuratorate sends personnel to appear in a retrial court, if the retrial case is tried under the procedure of first instance, the relevant provisions of Section 1 of this Chapter shall be followed; if the retrial case is tried under the procedure of second instance, the relevant provisions of Section 4 of this Chapter shall be followed.

### 第十二章 特别程序

#### 第一节 未成年人刑事案件诉讼程序

<a id="art-457"></a>第四百五十七条  人民检察院办理未成年人刑事案件，应当贯彻“教育、感化、挽救”方针和“教育为主、惩罚为辅”的原则，坚持优先保护、特殊保护、双向保护，以帮助教育和预防重新犯罪为目的。  
人民检察院可以借助社会力量开展帮助教育未成年人的工作。

**Article 457** When handling criminal cases involving minors, the People's Procuratorate shall implement the policy of "education, persuasion, and rescue" and the principle of "education as the main approach, punishment as supplementary," adhere to priority protection, special protection, and two-way protection, with the aim of assisting education and preventing re-offending.  
The People's Procuratorate may leverage social forces to carry out work of assisting and educating minors.

<a id="art-458"></a>第四百五十八条  人民检察院应当指定熟悉未成年人身心特点的检察人员办理未成年人刑事案件。

**Article 458** The People's Procuratorate shall designate prosecutors who are familiar with the physical and psychological characteristics of minors to handle criminal cases involving minors.

<a id="art-459"></a>第四百五十九条  人民检察院办理未成年人与成年人共同犯罪案件，一般应当对未成年人与成年人分案办理、分别起诉。不宜分案处理的，应当对未成年人采取隐私保护、快速办理等特殊保护措施。

**Article 459** When handling cases where minors and adults jointly commit crimes, the People's Procuratorate shall generally handle the cases separately and initiate separate prosecutions for minors and adults. Where separate handling is not appropriate, special protective measures such as privacy protection and expedited handling shall be adopted for the minors.

<a id="art-460"></a>第四百六十条  人民检察院受理案件后，应当向未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人了解其委托辩护人的情况，并告知其有权委托辩护人。  
未成年犯罪嫌疑人没有委托辩护人的，人民检察院应当书面通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供辩护。  
对于公安机关未通知法律援助机构指派律师为未成年犯罪嫌疑人提供辩护的，人民检察院应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 460** After accepting a case, the People's Procuratorate shall inquire of the minor criminal suspect and his or her legal representative about the status of retaining a defender, and inform him or her of the right to retain a defender.  
Where the minor criminal suspect has not retained a defender, the People's Procuratorate shall notify in writing the legal aid agency to assign a lawyer to provide defense for him or her.  
Where a public security organ fails to notify the legal aid agency to assign a lawyer to provide defense for a minor criminal suspect, the People's Procuratorate shall put forward corrective opinions.

<a id="art-461"></a>第四百六十一条  人民检察院根据情况可以对未成年犯罪嫌疑人的成长经历、犯罪原因、监护教育等情况进行调查，并制作社会调查报告，作为办案和教育的参考。  
人民检察院开展社会调查，可以委托有关组织和机构进行。开展社会调查应当尊重和保护未成年人隐私，不得向不知情人员泄露未成年犯罪嫌疑人的涉案信息。  
人民检察院应当对公安机关移送的社会调查报告进行审查。必要时，可以进行补充调查。  
人民检察院制作的社会调查报告应当随案移送人民法院。

**Article 461** The People's Procuratorate may, based on the circumstances, conduct an investigation into the growth experience, causes of crime, guardianship and education, etc., of the minor criminal suspect, and prepare a social investigation report, to serve as a reference for case handling and education.  
When conducting a social investigation, the People's Procuratorate may entrust relevant organizations and institutions to carry it out. The social investigation shall respect and protect the privacy of the minor, and shall not disclose information related to the minor criminal suspect's involvement in the case to persons who are not informed.  
The People's Procuratorate shall review the social investigation report transferred by the public security organ. If necessary, it may conduct a supplementary investigation.  
The social investigation report prepared by the People's Procuratorate shall be transferred together with the case to the People's Court.

<a id="art-462"></a>第四百六十二条  人民检察院对未成年犯罪嫌疑人审查逮捕，应当根据未成年犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌犯罪的性质、情节、主观恶性、有无监护与社会帮教条件、认罪认罚等情况，综合衡量其社会危险性，严格限制适用逮捕措施。

**Article 462** When reviewing arrest of a minor criminal suspect, the People's Procuratorate shall, based on the nature, circumstances, subjective malignancy, availability of guardianship and community assistance and education conditions, admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, and other circumstances of the suspected crime, comprehensively assess his or her social dangerousness, and strictly restrict the application of arrest measures.

<a id="art-463"></a>第四百六十三条  对于罪行较轻，具备有效监护条件或者社会帮教措施，没有社会危险性或者社会危险性较小的未成年犯罪嫌疑人，应当不批准逮捕。  
对于罪行比较严重，但主观恶性不大，有悔罪表现，具备有效监护条件或者社会帮教措施，具有下列情形之一，不逮捕不致发生社会危险性的未成年犯罪嫌疑人，可以不批准逮捕：  
（一）初次犯罪、过失犯罪的；  
（二）犯罪预备、中止、未遂的；  
（三）防卫过当、避险过当的；  
（四）有自首或者立功表现的；  
（五）犯罪后认罪认罚，或者积极退赃，尽力减少和赔偿损失，被害人谅解的；  
（六）不属于共同犯罪的主犯或者集团犯罪中的首要分子的；  
（七）属于已满十四周岁不满十六周岁的未成年人或者系在校学生的；  
（八）其他可以不批准逮捕的情形。  
对于没有固定住所、无法提供保证人的未成年犯罪嫌疑人适用取保候审的，可以指定合适的成年人作为保证人。

**Article 463** Where a minor criminal suspect has committed a relatively minor crime, has effective guardianship conditions or community assistance and education measures, and has no social dangerousness or has relatively low social dangerousness, the People's Procuratorate shall disapprove the arrest.  
Where a minor criminal suspect has committed a relatively serious crime, but his or her subjective malignancy is not high, shows signs of repentance, has effective guardianship conditions or community assistance and education measures, and falls under any of the following circumstances, and non-arrest would not create social dangerousness, the People's Procuratorate may disapprove the arrest:  
(1) first offense or negligent offense;  
(2) preparation for a crime, discontinuation of a crime, or attempted crime;  
(3) excessive defense or excessive necessity;  
(4) voluntary surrender or meritorious performance;  
(5) after committing the crime, admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, or active return of illegal gains, doing the utmost to reduce and compensate for losses, and the victim has provided understanding;  
(6) not being a principal offender in a joint crime or a ringleader in a gang crime;  
(7) being a minor who has reached the age of 14 but not the age of 16, or being a student;  
(8) other circumstances where arrest may be disapproved.  
Where release on bail pending investigation or trial is applied to a minor criminal suspect who does not have a fixed domicile and cannot provide a guarantor, a suitable adult may be designated as the guarantor.

<a id="art-464"></a>第四百六十四条  审查逮捕未成年犯罪嫌疑人，应当重点查清其是否已满十四、十六、十八周岁。  
对犯罪嫌疑人实际年龄难以判断，影响对该犯罪嫌疑人是否应当负刑事责任认定的，应当不批准逮捕。需要补充侦查的，同时通知公安机关。

**Article 464** When reviewing arrest of a minor criminal suspect, the People's Procuratorate shall focus on ascertaining whether he or she has reached the age of 14, 16, or 18.  
Where it is difficult to determine the actual age of the criminal suspect, which affects the determination of whether he or she shall bear criminal liability, arrest shall be disapproved. If supplementary investigation is needed, the public security organ shall be notified at the same time.

<a id="art-465"></a>第四百六十五条  在审查逮捕、审查起诉中，人民检察院应当讯问未成年犯罪嫌疑人，听取辩护人的意见，并制作笔录附卷。辩护人提出书面意见的，应当附卷。对于辩护人提出犯罪嫌疑人无罪、罪轻或者减轻、免除刑事责任、不适宜羁押或者侦查活动有违法情形等意见的，检察人员应当进行审查，并在相关工作文书中叙明辩护人提出的意见，说明是否采纳的情况和理由。  
讯问未成年犯罪嫌疑人，应当通知其法定代理人到场，告知法定代理人依法享有的诉讼权利和应当履行的义务。到场的法定代理人可以代为行使未成年犯罪嫌疑人的诉讼权利，代为行使权利时不得损害未成年犯罪嫌疑人的合法权益。  
无法通知、法定代理人不能到场或者法定代理人是共犯的，也可以通知未成年犯罪嫌疑人的其他成年亲属，所在学校、单位或者居住地的村民委员会、居民委员会、未成年人保护组织的代表到场，并将有关情况记录在案。未成年犯罪嫌疑人明确拒绝法定代理人以外的合适成年人到场，且有正当理由的，人民检察院可以准许，但应当在征求其意见后通知其他合适成年人到场。  
到场的法定代理人或者其他人员认为检察人员在讯问中侵犯未成年犯罪嫌疑人合法权益提出意见的，人民检察院应当记录在案。对合理意见，应当接受并纠正。讯问笔录应当交由到场的法定代理人或者其他人员阅读或者向其宣读，并由其在笔录上签名或者盖章，并捺指印。  
讯问女性未成年犯罪嫌疑人，应当有女性检察人员参加。  
询问未成年被害人、证人，适用本条第二款至第五款的规定。询问应当以一次为原则，避免反复询问。

**Article 465** During the review of arrest and review of prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall interrogate the minor criminal suspect, hear the opinions of the defender, and prepare a record to be attached to the case file. If the defender submits written opinions, they shall be attached to the case file. With respect to opinions raised by the defender that the criminal suspect is innocent, has a minor offense, or has grounds for mitigating or exemption from criminal liability, is unsuitable for custody, or that investigative activities are illegal, the prosecutor shall review them and, in the relevant work documents, state the opinions raised by the defender and explain whether they are adopted and the reasons.  
When interrogating a minor criminal suspect, the People's Procuratorate shall notify his or her legal representative to be present, and inform the legal representative of the procedural rights enjoyed and obligations to be performed according to law. The legal representative present may exercise the procedural rights of the minor criminal suspect on his or her behalf, but shall not damage the lawful rights and interests of the minor criminal suspect when exercising rights on his or her behalf.  
If the legal representative cannot be notified, cannot be present, or is a co-offender, other adult relatives of the minor criminal suspect, representatives from the school, work unit, villagers' committee, residents' committee of the place of residence, or minor protection organization may also be notified to be present, and the relevant circumstances shall be recorded. If the minor criminal suspect expressly refuses the presence of a suitable adult other than the legal representative and has legitimate reasons, the People's Procuratorate may permit it, but shall notify another suitable adult to be present after soliciting the minor's opinions.  
Where the legal representative or other person present raises an opinion that the prosecutor has infringed upon the lawful rights and interests of the minor criminal suspect during interrogation, the People's Procuratorate shall record it. Reasonable opinions shall be accepted and corrected.
