The interrogation record shall be given to the legal representative or other person present for reading or read out to them, and they shall sign or affix their seal and place their fingerprint on the record.  
When interrogating a female minor criminal suspect, a female prosecutor shall participate.  
When questioning a minor victim or witness, the provisions of paragraphs 2 to 5 of this Article shall apply. Questioning shall be conducted once in principle to avoid repeated questioning.

<a id="art-466"></a>第四百六十六条  讯问未成年犯罪嫌疑人应当保护其人格尊严。  
讯问未成年犯罪嫌疑人一般不得使用戒具。对于确有人身危险性必须使用戒具的，在现实危险消除后应当立即停止使用。

**Article 466** When interrogating a minor criminal suspect, his or her personal dignity shall be protected.  
Generally, restraints shall not be used when interrogating a minor criminal suspect. Where restraints must be used due to actual personal dangerousness, they shall be immediately discontinued after the actual danger is eliminated.

<a id="art-467"></a>第四百六十七条  未成年犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚的，人民检察院应当告知本人及其法定代理人享有的诉讼权利和认罪认罚的法律规定，并依照[刑事诉讼法第一百七十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-173)的规定，听取、记录未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、辩护人、被害人及其诉讼代理人的意见。

**Article 467** Where a minor criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment, the People's Procuratorate shall inform him or her and his or her legal representative of the procedural rights enjoyed and the legal provisions on admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, and, in accordance with the provisions of Article 173 of the Criminal Procedure Law, hear and record the opinions of the minor criminal suspect, his or her legal representative, defender, victim, and his or her litigation agent.

<a id="art-468"></a>第四百六十八条  未成年犯罪嫌疑人认罪认罚的，应当在法定代理人、辩护人在场的情况下签署认罪认罚具结书。法定代理人、辩护人对认罪认罚有异议的，不需要签署具结书。  
因未成年犯罪嫌疑人的法定代理人、辩护人对其认罪认罚有异议而不签署具结书的，人民检察院应当对未成年人认罪认罚情况，法定代理人、辩护人的异议情况如实记录。提起公诉的，应当将该材料与其他案卷材料一并移送人民法院。  
未成年犯罪嫌疑人的法定代理人、辩护人对认罪认罚有异议而不签署具结书的，不影响从宽处理。  
法定代理人无法到场的，合适成年人可以代为行使到场权、知情权、异议权等。法定代理人未到场的原因以及听取合适成年人意见等情况应当记录在案。

**Article 468** Where a minor criminal suspect admits guilt and accepts punishment, he or she shall sign a written statement of admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment in the presence of the legal representative and the defender. If the legal representative or the defender raises objections to the admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, they are not required to sign the statement.  
Where the legal representative or the defender of the minor criminal suspect does not sign the written statement due to objections to the admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment, the People's Procuratorate shall truthfully record the circumstances of the minor's admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment and the objections of the legal representative and the defender. If a public prosecution is initiated, such materials shall be transferred together with other case file materials to the People's Court.  
The fact that the legal representative or the defender of the minor criminal suspect does not sign the written statement due to objections to the admission of guilt and acceptance of punishment shall not affect the lenient treatment.  
If the legal representative cannot be present, a suitable adult may exercise the rights to be present, to be informed, and to raise objections on his or her behalf. The reason for the absence of the legal representative and the circumstances of hearing the opinions of the suitable adult shall be recorded.

<a id="art-469"></a>第四百六十九条  对于符合[刑事诉讼法第二百八十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-282)第一款规定条件的未成年人刑事案件，人民检察院可以作出附条件不起诉的决定。  
人民检察院在作出附条件不起诉的决定以前，应当听取公安机关、被害人、未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、辩护人的意见，并制作笔录附卷。

**Article 469** For a minor criminal case that meets the conditions set forth in the first paragraph of Article 282 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Procuratorate may make a decision on conditional non-prosecution.  
Before making a decision on conditional non-prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall hear the opinions of the public security organ, the victim, the minor criminal suspect and his or her legal representative, and the defender, and prepare a record to be attached to the case file.

<a id="art-470"></a>第四百七十条  未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人对拟作出附条件不起诉决定提出异议的，人民检察院应当提起公诉。但是，未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人提出无罪辩解，人民检察院经审查认为无罪辩解理由成立的，应当按照[本规则第三百六十五条](#art-365)的规定作出不起诉决定。  
未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人对案件作附条件不起诉处理没有异议，仅对所附条件及考验期有异议的，人民检察院可以依法采纳其合理的意见，对考察的内容、方式、时间等进行调整；其意见不利于对未成年犯罪嫌疑人帮教，人民检察院不采纳的，应当进行释法说理。  
人民检察院作出起诉决定前，未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人撤回异议的，人民检察院可以依法作出附条件不起诉决定。

**Article 470** Where a minor criminal suspect and his or her legal representative raise objections to the proposed decision on conditional non-prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall initiate a public prosecution. However, if the minor criminal suspect and his or her legal representative present a defense of innocence, and upon review the People's Procuratorate considers the grounds for the defense of innocence to be valid, it shall make a decision on non-prosecution in accordance with the provisions of Article 365 of these Rules.  
Where the minor criminal suspect and his or her legal representative have no objection to the handling of the case by conditional non-prosecution but only have objections to the attached conditions and the probation period, the People's Procuratorate may adopt their reasonable opinions according to law and adjust the content, methods, time, etc. of the supervision; if their opinions are not conducive to the assistance and education of the minor criminal suspect and the People's Procuratorate does not adopt them, it shall provide legal reasoning.  
If, before the People's Procuratorate makes a decision to initiate a public prosecution, the minor criminal suspect and his or her legal representative withdraw their objections, the People's Procuratorate may make a decision on conditional non-prosecution according to law.

<a id="art-471"></a>第四百七十一条  人民检察院作出附条件不起诉的决定后，应当制作附条件不起诉决定书，并在三日以内送达公安机关、被害人或者其近亲属及其诉讼代理人、未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人、辩护人。  
人民检察院应当当面向未成年犯罪嫌疑人及其法定代理人宣布附条件不起诉决定，告知考验期限、在考验期内应当遵守的规定以及违反规定应负的法律责任，并制作笔录附卷。

**Article 471** After making a decision on conditional non-prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall produce a written decision on conditional non-prosecution and serve it on the public security organ, the victim or his or her near relatives and their litigation agent, the juvenile criminal suspect and his or her legal representative and defender within three days.  
The People's Procuratorate shall announce the decision on conditional non-prosecution to the juvenile criminal suspect and his or her legal representative in person, inform them of the probation period, the rules that shall be observed during the probation period, and the legal liability for violating such rules, and make a written record to be placed in the file.

<a id="art-472"></a>第四百七十二条  对附条件不起诉的决定，公安机关要求复议、提请复核或者被害人提出申诉的，具体程序参照[本规则第三百七十九条](#art-379)至第三百八十三条的规定。被害人不服附条件不起诉决定的，应当告知其不适用[刑事诉讼法第一百八十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-180)关于被害人可以向人民法院起诉的规定，并做好释法说理工作。  
前款规定的复议、复核、申诉由相应人民检察院负责未成年人检察的部门进行审查。

**Article 472** Where the public security organ requests reconsideration or petitions for review of a decision on conditional non-prosecution, or the victim lodges a complaint, the specific procedures shall be handled by reference to Articles 379 through 383 of these Rules. If the victim does not accept the decision on conditional non-prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall inform the victim that Article 180 of the Criminal Procedure Law, which provides that the victim may bring a lawsuit to the People's Court, is not applicable, and shall properly explain the law and reasoning.  
The reconsideration, review, and complaint specified in the preceding paragraph shall be reviewed by the juvenile prosecution department of the corresponding People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-473"></a>第四百七十三条  人民检察院作出附条件不起诉决定的，应当确定考验期。考验期为六个月以上一年以下，从人民检察院作出附条件不起诉的决定之日起计算。

**Article 473** Where the People's Procuratorate makes a decision on conditional non-prosecution, it shall determine a probation period. The probation period shall be not less than six months and not more than one year, calculated from the date on which the People's Procuratorate makes the decision on conditional non-prosecution.

<a id="art-474"></a>第四百七十四条  在附条件不起诉的考验期内，由人民检察院对被附条件不起诉的未成年犯罪嫌疑人进行监督考察。人民检察院应当要求未成年犯罪嫌疑人的监护人对未成年犯罪嫌疑人加强管教，配合人民检察院做好监督考察工作。  
人民检察院可以会同未成年犯罪嫌疑人的监护人、所在学校、单位、居住地的村民委员会、居民委员会、未成年人保护组织等的有关人员，定期对未成年犯罪嫌疑人进行考察、教育，实施跟踪帮教。

**Article 474** During the probation period for conditional non-prosecution, the People's Procuratorate shall supervise and review the juvenile criminal suspect who is subject to conditional non-prosecution. The People's Procuratorate shall require the guardian of the juvenile criminal suspect to strengthen discipline and education of the juvenile criminal suspect and cooperate with the People's Procuratorate in carrying out supervision and review.  
The People's Procuratorate may, together with the guardian of the juvenile criminal suspect, the relevant personnel from the school, work unit, villagers' committee, residents' committee, or juvenile protection organization of the place where the juvenile criminal suspect resides, periodically conduct review and education of the juvenile criminal suspect and implement follow-up assistance and correction.

<a id="art-475"></a>第四百七十五条  人民检察院对于被附条件不起诉的未成年犯罪嫌疑人，应当监督考察其是否遵守下列规定：  
（一）遵守法律法规，服从监督；  
（二）按照规定报告自己的活动情况；  
（三）离开所居住的市、县或者迁居，应当报经批准；  
（四）按照要求接受矫治和教育。

**Article 475** The People's Procuratorate shall supervise and review whether the juvenile criminal suspect who is subject to conditional non-prosecution complies with the following provisions:  
(1) Comply with laws and regulations and submit to supervision;  
(2) Report his or her activities in accordance with the provisions;  
(3) Obtain approval before leaving the city or county where he or she resides or changing residence;  
(4) Receive correction and education as required.

<a id="art-476"></a>第四百七十六条  人民检察院可以要求被附条件不起诉的未成年犯罪嫌疑人接受下列矫治和教育：  
（一）完成戒瘾治疗、心理辅导或者其他适当的处遇措施；  
（二）向社区或者公益团体提供公益劳动；  
（三）不得进入特定场所，与特定的人员会见或者通信，从事特定的活动；  
（四）向被害人赔偿损失、赔礼道歉等；  
（五）接受相关教育；  
（六）遵守其他保护被害人安全以及预防再犯的禁止性规定。

**Article 476** The People's Procuratorate may require the juvenile criminal suspect who is subject to conditional non-prosecution to receive the following correction and education:  
(1) Complete addiction treatment, psychological counseling, or other appropriate treatment measures;  
(2) Provide community service or volunteer work for the community or public welfare organizations;  
(3) Not enter specific places, meet or communicate with specific persons, or engage in specific activities;  
(4) Compensate the victim for losses, apologize, etc.;  
(5) Receive relevant education;  
(6) Comply with other prohibitive provisions to protect the safety of the victim and prevent recidivism.

<a id="art-477"></a>第四百七十七条  考验期届满，检察人员应当制作附条件不起诉考察意见书，提出起诉或者不起诉的意见，报请检察长决定。  
考验期满作出不起诉的决定以前，应当听取被害人意见。

**Article 477** Upon expiry of the probation period, the prosecutor shall produce a written opinion on the review of conditional non-prosecution, put forward an opinion on prosecution or non-prosecution, and submit it to the Chief Prosecutor for a decision.  
Before making a decision on non-prosecution upon expiry of the probation period, the opinions of the victim shall be heard.

<a id="art-478"></a>第四百七十八条  考验期满作出不起诉决定，被害人提出申诉的，依照[本规则第四百七十二条](#art-472)规定办理。

**Article 478** Where a decision on non-prosecution is made upon expiry of the probation period and the victim lodges a complaint, the matter shall be handled in accordance with Article 472 of these Rules.

<a id="art-479"></a>第四百七十九条  被附条件不起诉的未成年犯罪嫌疑人，在考验期内具有下列情形之一的，人民检察院应当撤销附条件不起诉的决定，提起公诉：  
（一）实施新的犯罪的；  
（二）发现决定附条件不起诉以前还有其他犯罪需要追诉的；  
（三）违反治安管理规定，造成严重后果，或者多次违反治安管理规定的；  
（四）违反有关附条件不起诉的监督管理规定，造成严重后果，或者多次违反有关附条件不起诉的监督管理规定的。

**Article 479** Where the juvenile criminal suspect who is subject to conditional non-prosecution falls under any of the following circumstances during the probation period, the People's Procuratorate shall revoke the decision on conditional non-prosecution and initiate a public prosecution:  
(1) Commits a new crime;  
(2) It is discovered that there are other crimes that need to be prosecuted before the decision on conditional non-prosecution;  
(3) Violates public security administration provisions, causing serious consequences, or repeatedly violates public security administration provisions;  
(4) Violates the supervision and management provisions on conditional non-prosecution, causing serious consequences, or repeatedly violates the supervision and management provisions on conditional non-prosecution.

<a id="art-480"></a>第四百八十条  被附条件不起诉的未成年犯罪嫌疑人，在考验期内没有[本规则第四百七十九条](#art-479)规定的情形，考验期满的，人民检察院应当作出不起诉的决定。

**Article 480** Where the juvenile criminal suspect who is subject to conditional non-prosecution does not have any of the circumstances specified in Article 479 of these Rules during the probation period, and the probation period expires, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision on non-prosecution.

<a id="art-481"></a>第四百八十一条  人民检察院办理未成年人刑事案件过程中，应当对涉案未成年人的资料予以保密，不得公开或者传播涉案未成年人的姓名、住所、照片、图像及可能推断出该未成年人的其他资料。

**Article 481** When handling juvenile criminal cases, the People's Procuratorate shall keep confidential the information of the juvenile involved, and shall not disclose or disseminate the juvenile's name, domicile, photographs, images, or any other information that may identify the juvenile.

<a id="art-482"></a>第四百八十二条  犯罪的时候不满十八周岁，被判处五年有期徒刑以下刑罚的，人民检察院应当在收到人民法院生效判决、裁定后，对犯罪记录予以封存。  
生效判决、裁定由第二审人民法院作出的，同级人民检察院依照前款规定封存犯罪记录时，应当通知下级人民检察院对相关犯罪记录予以封存。

**Article 482** Where a person was under the age of 18 at the time of committing a crime and is sentenced to a punishment of not more than five years of fixed-term imprisonment, the People's Procuratorate shall seal the criminal record upon receipt of the effective judgment or ruling of the People's Court.  
Where the effective judgment or ruling is made by the second-instance People's Court, the People's Procuratorate at the same level, when sealing the criminal record in accordance with the preceding paragraph, shall notify the People's Procuratorate at the lower level to seal the relevant criminal record.

<a id="art-483"></a>第四百八十三条  人民检察院应当将拟封存的未成年人犯罪记录、案卷等相关材料装订成册，加密保存，不予公开，并建立专门的未成年人犯罪档案库，执行严格的保管制度。

**Article 483** The People's Procuratorate shall bind into volumes the juvenile criminal records, case files, and other relevant materials to be sealed, encrypt and preserve them, and keep them from public access, and shall establish a specialized juvenile criminal record archive and implement a strict storage system.

<a id="art-484"></a>第四百八十四条  除司法机关为办案需要或者有关单位根据国家规定进行查询的以外，人民检察院不得向任何单位和个人提供封存的犯罪记录，并不得提供未成年人有犯罪记录的证明。  
司法机关或者有关单位需要查询犯罪记录的，应当向封存犯罪记录的人民检察院提出书面申请。人民检察院应当在七日以内作出是否许可的决定。

**Article 484** Except where judicial authorities need to query for case handling purposes or relevant units need to query in accordance with state regulations, the People's Procuratorate shall not provide any sealed criminal records to any entity or individual, and shall not provide any certificate that a juvenile has a criminal record.  
Where judicial authorities or relevant units need to query criminal records, they shall submit a written application to the People's Procuratorate that sealed the criminal record. The People's Procuratorate shall make a decision on whether to permit the query within seven days.

<a id="art-485"></a>第四百八十五条  未成年人犯罪记录封存后，没有法定事由、未经法定程序不得解封。  
对被封存犯罪记录的未成年人，符合下列条件之一的，应当对其犯罪记录解除封存：  
（一）实施新的犯罪，且新罪与封存记录之罪数罪并罚后被决定执行五年有期徒刑以上刑罚的；  
（二）发现漏罪，且漏罪与封存记录之罪数罪并罚后被决定执行五年有期徒刑以上刑罚的。

**Article 485** After the juvenile criminal record is sealed, it shall not be unsealed without legal grounds and through legal procedures.  
For a juvenile whose criminal record has been sealed, the criminal record shall be unsealed if one of the following conditions is met:  
(1) The juvenile commits a new crime, and after combined punishment for the new crime and the crime subject to the sealed record, the punishment imposed is a fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years;  
(2) It is discovered that there is an undiscovered crime, and after combined punishment for the undiscovered crime and the crime subject to the sealed record, the punishment imposed is a fixed-term imprisonment of not less than five years.

<a id="art-486"></a>第四百八十六条  人民检察院对未成年犯罪嫌疑人作出不起诉决定后，应当对相关记录予以封存。除司法机关为办案需要进行查询外，不得向任何单位和个人提供。封存的具体程序参照[本规则第四百八十三条](#art-483)至第四百八十五条的规定。

**Article 486** After the People's Procuratorate makes a decision on non-prosecution for a juvenile criminal suspect, it shall seal the relevant records. Except where judicial authorities need to query for case handling purposes, they shall not be provided to any entity or individual. The specific procedures for sealing shall be by reference to Articles 483 through 485 of these Rules.

<a id="art-487"></a>第四百八十七条  被封存犯罪记录的未成年人或者其法定代理人申请出具无犯罪记录证明的，人民检察院应当出具。需要协调公安机关、人民法院为其出具无犯罪记录证明的，人民检察院应当予以协助。

**Article 487** Where a juvenile whose criminal record has been sealed or his or her legal representative applies for the issuance of a certificate of no criminal record, the People's Procuratorate shall issue it. If it is necessary to coordinate with the public security organ or the People's Court to issue a certificate of no criminal record for the juvenile, the People's Procuratorate shall provide assistance.

<a id="art-488"></a>第四百八十八条  负责未成年人检察的部门应当依法对看守所、未成年犯管教所监管未成年人的活动实行监督，配合做好对未成年人的教育。发现没有对未成年犯罪嫌疑人、被告人与成年犯罪嫌疑人、被告人分别关押、管理或者违反规定对未成年犯留所执行刑罚的，应当依法提出纠正意见。  
负责未成年人检察的部门发现社区矫正机构违反未成年人社区矫正相关规定的，应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 488** The juvenile prosecution department shall, according to law, supervise the activities of detention centers and juvenile correctional facilities in supervising juveniles, and cooperate in providing education for juveniles. If it is discovered that juvenile criminal suspects or defendants are not separately detained or managed from adult criminal suspects or defendants, or that juvenile offenders are retained in detention facilities for execution of punishment in violation of regulations, a corrective opinion shall be submitted according to law.  
If the juvenile prosecution department discovers that a community correction institution violates relevant provisions on community correction for juveniles, it shall submit a corrective opinion according to law.

<a id="art-489"></a>第四百八十九条  本节所称未成年人刑事案件，是指犯罪嫌疑人实施涉嫌犯罪行为时已满十四周岁、未满十八周岁的刑事案件。  
本节第四百六十条、第四百六十五条、第四百六十六条、第四百六十七条、第四百六十八条所称的未成年犯罪嫌疑人，是指在诉讼过程中未满十八周岁的人。犯罪嫌疑人实施涉嫌犯罪行为时未满十八周岁，在诉讼过程中已满十八周岁的，人民检察院可以根据案件的具体情况适用上述规定。

**Article 489** For the purposes of this Section, 'juvenile criminal case' means a criminal case in which the criminal suspect has reached the age of 14 but has not reached the age of 18 at the time of committing the suspected criminal act.  
The term 'juvenile criminal suspect' as used in Articles 460, 465, 466, 467, and 468 of this Section means a person who has not reached the age of 18 during the proceedings. If the criminal suspect has not reached the age of 18 at the time of committing the suspected criminal act but has reached the age of 18 during the proceedings, the People's Procuratorate may apply the above provisions based on the specific circumstances of the case.

<a id="art-490"></a>第四百九十条  人民检察院办理侵害未成年人犯罪案件，应当采取适合未成年被害人身心特点的方法，充分保护未成年被害人的合法权益。

**Article 490** When handling criminal cases involving crimes against juveniles, the People's Procuratorate shall adopt methods suitable for the physical and mental characteristics of juvenile victims, and fully protect the lawful rights and interests of juvenile victims.

<a id="art-491"></a>第四百九十一条  办理未成年人刑事案件，除本节已有规定的以外，按照刑事诉讼法和其他有关规定进行。

**Article 491** : In handling criminal cases involving minors, unless otherwise provided in this Section, the provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law and other relevant regulations shall be followed.

#### 第二节 当事人和解的公诉案件诉讼程序

<a id="art-492"></a>第四百九十二条  下列公诉案件，双方当事人可以和解：  
（一）因民间纠纷引起，涉嫌 刑法分则第四章、第五章规定的犯罪案件，可能判处三年有期徒刑以下刑罚的；  
（二）除渎职犯罪以外的可能判处七年有期徒刑以下刑罚的过失犯罪案件。  
当事人和解的公诉案件应当同时符合下列条件：  
（一）犯罪嫌疑人真诚悔罪，向被害人赔偿损失、赔礼道歉等；  
（二）被害人明确表示对犯罪嫌疑人予以谅解；  
（三）双方当事人自愿和解，符合有关法律规定；  
（四）属于侵害特定被害人的故意犯罪或者有直接被害人的过失犯罪；  
（五）案件事实清楚，证据确实、充分。  
犯罪嫌疑人在五年以内曾经故意犯罪的，不适用本节规定的程序。  
犯罪嫌疑人在犯[刑事诉讼法第二百八十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-288)第一款规定的犯罪前五年内曾经故意犯罪，无论该故意犯罪是否已经追究，均应当认定为前款规定的五年以内曾经故意犯罪。

**Article 492** : The following public prosecution cases may be subject to settlement between the parties:  
(1) cases arising from civil disputes, involving crimes stipulated in Chapters IV and V of the Specific Provisions of the Criminal Law, which may be punishable by a sentence of fixed-term imprisonment of not more than three years; and  
(2) negligent crime cases, other than crimes of dereliction of duty, which may be punishable by a sentence of fixed-term imprisonment of not more than seven years.  
A public prosecution case subject to party settlement shall simultaneously meet the following conditions:  
(1) the criminal suspect sincerely repents, and provides compensation for losses, makes an apology, etc. to the victim;  
(2) the victim expressly indicates that he or she has forgiven the criminal suspect;  
(3) both parties voluntarily seek settlement, which conforms to the relevant legal provisions;  
(4) it is an intentional crime against a specific victim or a negligent crime with a direct victim; and  
(5) the facts of the case are clear and the evidence is reliable and sufficient.  
If the criminal suspect has previously committed an intentional crime within five years, the procedures provided in this Section shall not apply.  
If the criminal suspect has committed an intentional crime within five years prior to the commission of the crime specified in Article 288, paragraph 1 of the Criminal Procedure Law, regardless of whether that intentional crime has been investigated, it shall be deemed as having committed an intentional crime within the preceding five years as mentioned in the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-493"></a>第四百九十三条  被害人死亡的，其法定代理人、近亲属可以与犯罪嫌疑人和解。  
被害人系无行为能力或者限制行为能力人的，其法定代理人可以代为和解。

**Article 493** : Where the victim has died, his or her legal representative or close relative may enter into a settlement with the criminal suspect.  
Where the victim is a person without or with limited capacity for conduct, his or her legal representative may enter into a settlement on his or her behalf.

<a id="art-494"></a>第四百九十四条  犯罪嫌疑人系限制行为能力人的，其法定代理人可以代为和解。  
犯罪嫌疑人在押的，经犯罪嫌疑人同意，其法定代理人、近亲属可以代为和解。

**Article 494** : Where the criminal suspect is a person with limited capacity for conduct, his or her legal representative may enter into a settlement on his or her behalf.  
Where the criminal suspect is in custody, with the consent of the criminal suspect, his or her legal representative or close relative may enter into a settlement on his or her behalf.

<a id="art-495"></a>第四百九十五条  双方当事人可以就赔偿损失、赔礼道歉等民事责任事项进行和解，并且可以就被害人及其法定代理人或者近亲属是否要求或者同意公安机关、人民检察院、人民法院对犯罪嫌疑人依法从宽处理进行协商，但不得对案件的事实认定、证据采信、法律适用和定罪量刑等依法属于公安机关、人民检察院、人民法院职权范围的事宜进行协商。

**Article 495** : The parties may enter into a settlement on civil liability matters such as compensation for losses and apology, and may negotiate on whether the victim, his or her legal representative or close relative requests or agrees that the public security organ, the People's Procuratorate, or the People's Court shall handle the criminal suspect leniently according to law. However, they shall not negotiate on matters such as the determination of facts, the admission of evidence, the application of law, and the conviction and sentencing, which, according to law, fall within the scope of authority of the public security organ, the People's Procuratorate, or the People's Court.

<a id="art-496"></a>第四百九十六条  双方当事人可以自行达成和解，也可以经人民调解委员会、村民委员会、居民委员会、当事人所在单位或者同事、亲友等组织或者个人调解后达成和解。  
人民检察院对于[本规则第四百九十二条](#art-492)规定的公诉案件，可以建议当事人进行和解，并告知相应的权利义务，必要时可以提供法律咨询。

**Article 496** : The parties may reach a settlement by themselves, or may reach a settlement after mediation by a people's mediation committee, a villagers' committee, a residents' committee, the unit where the parties work, or colleagues, relatives, friends or other organizations or individuals.  
For the public prosecution cases specified in Article 492 of these Rules, the People's Procuratorate may recommend the parties to settle and inform them of the corresponding rights and obligations, and may provide legal consultation when necessary.

<a id="art-497"></a>第四百九十七条  人民检察院应当对和解的自愿性、合法性进行审查，重点审查以下内容：  
（一）双方当事人是否自愿和解；  
（二）犯罪嫌疑人是否真诚悔罪，是否向被害人赔礼道歉，赔偿数额与其所造成的损害和赔偿能力是否相适应；  
（三）被害人及其法定代理人或者近亲属是否明确表示对犯罪嫌疑人予以谅解；  
（四）是否符合法律规定；  
（五）是否损害国家、集体和社会公共利益或者他人的合法权益；  
（六）是否符合社会公德。  
审查时，应当听取双方当事人和其他有关人员对和解的意见，告知刑事案件可能从宽处理的法律后果和双方的权利义务，并制作笔录附卷。

**Article 497** : The People's Procuratorate shall examine the voluntariness and legality of the settlement, focusing on the following contents:  
(1) whether the parties voluntarily entered into the settlement;  
(2) whether the criminal suspect sincerely repents, has made an apology to the victim, and whether the amount of compensation is commensurate with the harm caused and the ability to compensate;  
(3) whether the victim, his or her legal representative or close relative expressly indicates that he or she has forgiven the criminal suspect;  
(4) whether it complies with the legal provisions;  
(5) whether it damages the national, collective or social public interests or the lawful rights and interests of others; and  
(6) whether it conforms to social morality.  
When conducting the examination, opinions on the settlement shall be heard from the parties and other relevant persons, the legal consequences of possible lenient handling in criminal cases and the rights and obligations of both parties shall be informed, and records shall be made and attached to the case file.

<a id="art-498"></a>第四百九十八条  经审查认为双方自愿和解，内容合法，且符合[本规则第四百九十二条](#art-492)规定的范围和条件的，人民检察院应当主持制作和解协议书。  
和解协议书的主要内容包括：  
（一）双方当事人的基本情况；  
（二）案件的主要事实；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人真诚悔罪，承认自己所犯罪行，对指控的犯罪没有异议，向被害人赔偿损失、赔礼道歉等。赔偿损失的，应当写明赔偿的数额、履行的方式、期限等；  
（四）被害人及其法定代理人或者近亲属对犯罪嫌疑人予以谅解，并要求或者同意公安机关、人民检察院、人民法院对犯罪嫌疑人依法从宽处理。  
和解协议书应当由双方当事人签字，可以写明和解协议书系在人民检察院主持下制作。检察人员不在当事人和解协议书上签字，也不加盖人民检察院印章。  
和解协议书一式三份，双方当事人各持一份，另一份交人民检察院附卷备查。

**Article 498** : If, after examination, it is believed that both parties voluntarily settle, the content is lawful, and it meets the scope and conditions specified in Article 492 of these Rules, the People's Procuratorate shall preside over the preparation of a settlement agreement.  
The main contents of the settlement agreement include:  
(1) basic information of the parties;  
(2) the main facts of the case;  
(3) the criminal suspect sincerely repents, admits the crime he or she committed, has no objection to the charged crime, and provides compensation for losses, makes an apology, etc. to the victim. For compensation for losses, the amount of compensation, the method and time limit for performance, etc. shall be specified;  
(4) the victim, his or her legal representative or close relative has forgiven the criminal suspect and requests or agrees that the public security organ, the People's Procuratorate, or the People's Court shall handle the criminal suspect leniently according to law.  
The settlement agreement shall be signed by the parties, and it may be stated that the settlement agreement was prepared under the auspices of the People's Procuratorate. The procuratorial personnel shall not sign the settlement agreement, nor shall the seal of the People's Procuratorate be affixed.  
The settlement agreement shall be made in triplicate, with one copy held by each party and the third copy submitted to the People's Procuratorate for attachment to the case file for reference.

<a id="art-499"></a>第四百九十九条  和解协议书约定的赔偿损失内容，应当在双方签署协议后立即履行，至迟在人民检察院作出从宽处理决定前履行。确实难以一次性履行的，在提供有效担保并且被害人同意的情况下，也可以分期履行。

**Article 499** : The compensation for losses as stipulated in the settlement agreement shall be performed immediately after the parties sign the agreement, and at the latest before the People's Procuratorate makes a decision on lenient handling. If it is truly difficult to perform in a lump sum, performance by installments may also be made provided that valid security is provided and the victim agrees.

<a id="art-500"></a>第五百条  双方当事人在侦查阶段达成和解协议，公安机关向人民检察院提出从宽处理建议的，人民检察院在审查逮捕和审查起诉时应当充分考虑公安机关的建议。

**Article 500** : Where the parties reach a settlement agreement during the investigation stage, and the public security organ submits a recommendation for lenient handling to the People's Procuratorate, the People's Procuratorate shall give full consideration to the recommendation of the public security organ when reviewing the arrest and the prosecution.

<a id="art-501"></a>第五百零一条  人民检察院对于公安机关提请批准逮捕的案件，双方当事人达成和解协议的，可以作为有无社会危险性或者社会危险性大小的因素予以考虑。经审查认为不需要逮捕的，可以作出不批准逮捕的决定；在审查起诉阶段可以依法变更强制措施。

**Article 501** : Where the parties have reached a settlement agreement regarding a case submitted by the public security organ for approval of arrest, the People's Procuratorate may consider this as a factor in assessing whether there is social danger or the degree of social danger. If, after examination, it is determined that arrest is not necessary, a decision not to approve arrest may be made; during the review for prosecution stage, compulsory measures may be changed according to law.

<a id="art-502"></a>第五百零二条  人民检察院对于公安机关移送起诉的案件，双方当事人达成和解协议的，可以作为是否需要判处刑罚或者免除刑罚的因素予以考虑。符合法律规定的不起诉条件的，可以决定不起诉。  
对于依法应当提起公诉的，人民检察院可以向人民法院提出从宽处罚的量刑建议。

**Article 502** : Where the parties have reached a settlement agreement regarding a case transferred by the public security organ for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate may consider this as a factor in assessing whether a punishment is necessary or whether punishment may be exempted. If the conditions for non-prosecution as stipulated by law are met, a decision on non-prosecution may be made.  
For cases that shall be prosecuted according to law, the People's Procuratorate may submit to the People's Court a sentencing recommendation for a lenient punishment.

<a id="art-503"></a>第五百零三条  人民检察院拟对当事人达成和解的公诉案件作出不起诉决定的，应当听取双方当事人对和解的意见，并且查明犯罪嫌疑人是否已经切实履行和解协议、不能即时履行的是否已经提供有效担保，将其作为是否决定不起诉的因素予以考虑。  
当事人在不起诉决定作出之前反悔的，可以另行达成和解。不能另行达成和解的，人民检察院应当依法作出起诉或者不起诉决定。  
当事人在不起诉决定作出之后反悔的，人民检察院不撤销原决定，但有证据证明和解违反自愿、合法原则的除外。

**Article 503** : Where the People's Procuratorate intends to make a decision for non-prosecution on a public prosecution case in which the parties have reached a settlement, it shall hear the opinions of the parties regarding the settlement, and ascertain whether the criminal suspect has actually performed the settlement agreement, and if it cannot be performed immediately, whether valid security has been provided, and consider this as a factor in deciding whether to make a non-prosecution decision.  
If a party repudiates before the non-prosecution decision is made, a new settlement may be reached. If a new settlement cannot be reached, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision on prosecution or non-prosecution according to law.  
If a party repudiates after the non-prosecution decision is made, the People's Procuratorate shall not revoke the original decision, unless there is evidence proving that the settlement violated the principles of voluntariness and legality.

<a id="art-504"></a>第五百零四条  犯罪嫌疑人或者其亲友等以暴力、威胁、欺骗或者其他非法方法强迫、引诱被害人和解，或者在协议履行完毕之后威胁、报复被害人的，应当认定和解协议无效。已经作出不批准逮捕或者不起诉决定的，人民检察院根据案件情况可以撤销原决定，对犯罪嫌疑人批准逮捕或者提起公诉。

**Article 504** : If the criminal suspect or his or her relatives or friends, etc., use violence, threats, deception or other illegal methods to force or induce the victim to settle, or threaten or retaliate against the victim after the performance of the settlement agreement, the settlement agreement shall be deemed invalid. If a decision not to approve arrest or not to prosecute has already been made, the People's Procuratorate may, depending on the circumstances of the case, revoke the original decision and approve the arrest of the criminal suspect or initiate a public prosecution.

#### 第三节 缺席审判程序

<a id="art-505"></a>第五百零五条  对于监察机关移送起诉的贪污贿赂犯罪案件，犯罪嫌疑人、被告人在境外，人民检察院认为犯罪事实已经查清，证据确实、充分，依法应当追究刑事责任的，可以向人民法院提起公诉。  
对于公安机关移送起诉的需要及时进行审判的严重危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪案件，犯罪嫌疑人、被告人在境外，人民检察院认为犯罪事实已经查清，证据确实、充分，依法应当追究刑事责任的，经最高人民检察院核准，可以向人民法院提起公诉。  
前两款规定的案件，由有管辖权的中级人民法院的同级人民检察院提起公诉。  
人民检察院提起公诉的，应当向人民法院提交被告人已出境的证据。

**Article 505** : For cases of embezzlement or bribery transferred by the supervisory organ for prosecution, where the criminal suspect or defendant is outside the territory of China, if the People's Procuratorate believes that the facts of the crime have been established, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and criminal liability shall be pursued according to law, it may initiate a public prosecution to the People's Court.  
For cases of crimes seriously endangering national security or terrorist activities transferred by the public security organ for prosecution that require timely adjudication, where the criminal suspect or defendant is outside the territory of China, if the People's Procuratorate believes that the facts of the crime have been established, the evidence is reliable and sufficient, and criminal liability shall be pursued according to law, it may, upon approval by the Supreme People's Procuratorate, initiate a public prosecution to the People's Court.  
The cases specified in the preceding two paragraphs shall be initiated by the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the Intermediate People's Court with jurisdiction.  
Where the People's Procuratorate initiates a public prosecution, it shall submit to the People's Court evidence that the defendant has left the country.

<a id="art-506"></a>第五百零六条  人民检察院对公安机关移送起诉的需要报请最高人民检察院核准的案件，经检察委员会讨论提出提起公诉意见的，应当层报最高人民检察院核准。报送材料包括起诉意见书、案件审查报告、报请核准的报告及案件证据材料。

**Article 506** : Where a People's Procuratorate, for a case transferred by the public security organ for prosecution that requires submission to the Supreme People's Procuratorate for approval, after discussion by the Procuratorial Committee, proposes an opinion to initiate a public prosecution, it shall report to the Supreme People's Procuratorate level by level for approval. The materials submitted shall include the opinion on prosecution, the case review report, the report requesting approval, and the evidence materials of the case.

<a id="art-507"></a>第五百零七条  最高人民检察院收到下级人民检察院报请核准提起公诉的案卷材料后，应当及时指派检察官对案卷材料进行审查，提出核准或者不予核准的意见，报检察长决定。

**Article 507** : After receiving the case file materials submitted by a lower-level People's Procuratorate for approval to initiate a public prosecution, the Supreme People's Procuratorate shall promptly assign a procurator to review the case file materials and submit an opinion of approval or disapproval, which shall be reported to the Chief Procurator for decision.

<a id="art-508"></a>第五百零八条  报请核准的人民检察院收到最高人民检察院核准决定书后，应当提起公诉，起诉书中应当载明经最高人民检察院核准的内容。

**Article 508** : After receiving the approval decision from the Supreme People's Procuratorate, the People's Procuratorate that submitted the request for approval shall initiate a public prosecution, and the indictment shall state the content approved by the Supreme People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-509"></a>第五百零九条  审查起诉期间，犯罪嫌疑人自动投案或者被抓获的，人民检察院应当重新审查。  
对严重危害国家安全犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪案件报请核准期间，犯罪嫌疑人自动投案或者被抓获的，报请核准的人民检察院应当及时撤回报请，重新审查案件。

**Article 509** : During the period of review for prosecution, if the criminal suspect voluntarily surrenders or is captured, the People's Procuratorate shall re-review the case.  
During the period of requesting approval for cases involving crimes seriously endangering national security or terrorist activities, if the criminal suspect voluntarily surrenders or is captured, the People's Procuratorate that submitted the request for approval shall promptly withdraw the request and re-review the case.

<a id="art-510"></a>第五百一十条  提起公诉后被告人到案，人民法院拟重新审理的，人民检察院应当商人民法院将案件撤回并重新审查。

**Article 510** : If, after the initiation of a public prosecution, the defendant appears in court and the People's Court intends to re-try the case, the People's Procuratorate shall consult with the People's Court to withdraw the case and re-review it.

<a id="art-511"></a>第五百一十一条  因被告人患有严重疾病无法出庭，中止审理超过六个月，被告人仍无法出庭，被告人及其法定代理人、近亲属申请或者同意恢复审理的，人民检察院可以建议人民法院适用缺席审判程序审理。

**Article 511** Where a defendant is unable to appear in court due to serious illness, the trial is suspended for more than six months, and the defendant remains unable to appear in court, if the defendant and his or her legal representative or close relative apply for or consent to the resumption of the trial, the People's Procuratorate may recommend that the People's Court apply the trial by default procedure.

#### 第四节 犯罪嫌疑人、被告人逃匿、死亡案件违法所得的没收程序

<a id="art-512"></a>第五百一十二条  对于贪污贿赂犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪等重大犯罪案件，犯罪嫌疑人、被告人逃匿，在通缉一年后不能到案，依照刑法规定应当追缴其违法所得及其他涉案财产的，人民检察院可以向人民法院提出没收违法所得的申请。  
对于犯罪嫌疑人、被告人死亡，依照刑法规定应当追缴其违法所得及其他涉案财产的，人民检察院也可以向人民法院提出没收违法所得的申请。

**Article 512** For cases of major crimes such as embezzlement, bribery, or terrorist activities, if a criminal suspect or defendant absconds and cannot be brought to justice after being wanted for one year, and the illegal gains and other property involved in the case shall be confiscated according to the provisions of the Criminal Law, the People's Procuratorate may file an application with the People's Court for confiscation of illegal gains.  
If a criminal suspect or defendant dies, and the illegal gains and other property involved in the case shall be confiscated according to the provisions of the Criminal Law, the People's Procuratorate may also file an application with the People's Court for confiscation of illegal gains.

<a id="art-513"></a>第五百一十三条  犯罪嫌疑人、被告人为逃避侦查和刑事追究潜逃、隐匿，或者在刑事诉讼过程中脱逃的，应当认定为“逃匿”。  
犯罪嫌疑人、被告人因意外事故下落不明满二年，或者因意外事故下落不明，经有关机关证明其不可能生存的，按照前款规定处理。

**Article 513** Where a criminal suspect or defendant flees or hides to evade investigation and criminal prosecution, or escapes during criminal proceedings, it shall be determined as "absconding".  
Where a criminal suspect or defendant has been missing for two years due to an accident, or is missing due to an accident and is proven by the relevant authorities to be unlikely to survive, the matter shall be handled in accordance with the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-514"></a>第五百一十四条  公安机关发布通缉令或者公安部通过国际刑警组织发布红色国际通报，应当认定为“通缉”。

**Article 514** A wanted notice issued by a public security organ or a Red Notice issued by the Ministry of Public Security through Interpol shall be determined as "being wanted".

<a id="art-515"></a>第五百一十五条  犯罪嫌疑人、被告人通过实施犯罪直接或者间接产生、获得的任何财产，应当认定为“违法所得”。  
违法所得已经部分或者全部转变、转化为其他财产的，转变、转化后的财产应当视为前款规定的“违法所得”。  
来自违法所得转变、转化后的财产收益，或者来自已经与违法所得相混合财产中违法所得相应部分的收益，也应当视为第一款规定的违法所得。

**Article 515** Any property directly or indirectly generated or obtained by a criminal suspect or defendant through the commission of a crime shall be determined as "illegal gains".  
Where illegal gains have been partially or entirely transformed or converted into other property, the transformed or converted property shall be regarded as the "illegal gains" as provided in the preceding paragraph.  
Income derived from property transformed or converted from illegal gains, or income derived from the portion of property corresponding to illegal gains in property already mixed with illegal gains, shall also be regarded as illegal gains as provided in the first paragraph.

<a id="art-516"></a>第五百一十六条  犯罪嫌疑人、被告人非法持有的违禁品、供犯罪所用的本人财物，应当认定为“其他涉案财产”。

**Article 516** Contraband illegally held by a criminal suspect or defendant, and the property of the person used for the commission of a crime, shall be determined as "other property involved in the case".

<a id="art-517"></a>第五百一十七条  [刑事诉讼法第二百九十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-299)第三款规定的“利害关系人”包括犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的近亲属和其他对申请没收的财产主张权利的自然人和单位。  
[刑事诉讼法第二百九十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-299)第二款、第三百条第二款规定的“其他利害关系人”是指前款规定的“其他对申请没收的财产主张权利的自然人和单位”。

**Article 517** The term "interested party" as provided in paragraph 3 of Article 299 of the Criminal Procedure Law includes the close relatives of the criminal suspect or defendant, and other natural persons and entities that claim rights to the property subject to the application for confiscation.  
The term "other interested parties" as provided in paragraph 2 of Article 299 and paragraph 2 of Article 300 of the Criminal Procedure Law refers to the "other natural persons and entities that claim rights to the property subject to the application for confiscation" as provided in the preceding paragraph.

<a id="art-518"></a>第五百一十八条  人民检察院审查监察机关或者公安机关移送的没收违法所得意见书，向人民法院提出没收违法所得的申请以及对违法所得没收程序中调查活动、审判活动的监督，由负责捕诉的部门办理。

**Article 518** The People's Procuratorate's review of the opinion on confiscation of illegal gains transferred by a supervisory organ or public security organ, filing an application with the People's Court for confiscation of illegal gains, and the supervision of investigation activities and trial activities in the procedure for confiscation of illegal gains shall be handled by the department responsible for arrest and prosecution.

<a id="art-519"></a>第五百一十九条  没收违法所得的申请，应当由有管辖权的中级人民法院的同级人民检察院提出。

**Article 519** An application for confiscation of illegal gains shall be filed by the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the intermediate People's Court with jurisdiction.

<a id="art-520"></a>第五百二十条  人民检察院向人民法院提出没收违法所得的申请，应当制作没收违法所得申请书。没收违法所得申请书应当载明以下内容：  
（一）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的基本情况，包括姓名、性别、出生年月日、出生地、户籍地、公民身份号码、民族、文化程度、职业、工作单位及职务、住址等；  
（二）案由及案件来源；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的犯罪事实及相关证据材料；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人、被告人逃匿、被通缉或者死亡的情况；  
（五）申请没收的财产种类、数量、价值、所在地以及查封、扣押、冻结财产清单和相关法律手续；  
（六）申请没收的财产属于违法所得及其他涉案财产的相关事实及证据材料；  
（七）提出没收违法所得申请的理由和法律依据；  
（八）有无近亲属和其他利害关系人以及利害关系人的姓名、身份、住址、联系方式；  
（九）其他应当写明的内容。  
上述材料需要翻译件的，人民检察院应当随没收违法所得申请书一并移送人民法院。

**Article 520** Where the People's Procuratorate files an application with the People's Court for confiscation of illegal gains, it shall prepare a written application for confiscation of illegal gains. The written application for confiscation of illegal gains shall specify the following:  
(1) The basic information of the criminal suspect or defendant, including name, sex, date of birth, place of birth, place of household registration, citizen identity number, ethnicity, education level, occupation, work unit and position, address, etc.;  
(2) The cause of action and source of the case;  
(3) The facts of the crime committed by the criminal suspect or defendant and relevant evidence materials;  
(4) The circumstances of the criminal suspect or defendant absconding, being wanted, or dying;  
(5) The type, quantity, value, and location of the property for which confiscation is applied, as well as the list of sealed, seized, or frozen property and relevant legal formalities;  
(6) The relevant facts and evidence materials that the property for which confiscation is applied constitutes illegal gains or other property involved in the case;  
(7) The reasons and legal basis for filing the application for confiscation of illegal gains;  
(8) Whether there are close relatives or other interested parties, and the names, identities, addresses, and contact information of the interested parties;  
(9) Other content that shall be specified.  
If the above materials require translation, the People's Procuratorate shall transfer them to the People's Court together with the written application for confiscation of illegal gains.

<a id="art-521"></a>第五百二十一条  监察机关或者公安机关向人民检察院移送没收违法所得意见书，应当由有管辖权的人民检察院的同级监察机关或者公安机关移送。

**Article 521** Where a supervisory organ or public security organ transfers an opinion on confiscation of illegal gains to the People's Procuratorate, such transfer shall be made by the supervisory organ or public security organ at the same level as the People's Procuratorate with jurisdiction.

<a id="art-522"></a>第五百二十二条  人民检察院审查监察机关或者公安机关移送的没收违法所得意见书，应当审查下列内容：  
（一）是否属于本院管辖；  
（二）是否符合[刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)第一款规定的条件；  
（三）犯罪嫌疑人基本情况，包括姓名、性别、国籍、出生年月日、职业和单位等；  
（四）犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌犯罪的事实和相关证据材料；  
（五）犯罪嫌疑人逃匿、下落不明、被通缉或者死亡的情况，通缉令或者死亡证明是否随案移送；  
（六）违法所得及其他涉案财产的种类、数量、所在地以及查封、扣押、冻结的情况，查封、扣押、冻结的财产清单和相关法律手续是否随案移送；  
（七）违法所得及其他涉案财产的相关事实和证据材料；  
（八）有无近亲属和其他利害关系人以及利害关系人的姓名、身份、住址、联系方式。  
对于与犯罪事实、违法所得及其他涉案财产相关的证据材料，不宜移送的，应当审查证据的清单、复制件、照片或者其他证明文件是否随案移送。

**Article 522** When reviewing the opinion on confiscation of illegal gains transferred by a supervisory organ or public security organ, the People's Procuratorate shall review the following:  
(1) Whether it falls under the jurisdiction of this People's Procuratorate;  
(2) Whether the conditions provided in paragraph 1 of Article 298 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met;  
(3) The basic information of the criminal suspect, including name, sex, nationality, date of birth, occupation, and work unit;  
(4) The facts of the crime suspected by the criminal suspect and relevant evidence materials;  
(5) The circumstances of the criminal suspect absconding, missing, being wanted, or dying, and whether the wanted notice or death certificate is transferred with the case;  
(6) The type, quantity, and location of illegal gains and other property involved in the case, and the circumstances of sealing, seizure, and freezing, and whether the list of sealed, seized, or frozen property and relevant legal formalities are transferred with the case;  
(7) The relevant facts and evidence materials concerning the illegal gains and other property involved in the case;  
(8) Whether there are close relatives and other interested parties, and the names, identities, addresses, and contact information of the interested parties.  
If it is inappropriate to transfer the evidence materials related to the facts of the crime, illegal gains, and other property involved in the case, it shall be reviewed whether a list of evidence, copies, photographs, or other certifying documents are transferred with the case.

<a id="art-523"></a>第五百二十三条  人民检察院应当在接到监察机关或者公安机关移送的没收违法所得意见书后三十日以内作出是否提出没收违法所得申请的决定。三十日以内不能作出决定的，可以延长十五日。  
对于监察机关或者公安机关移送的没收违法所得案件，经审查认为不符合[刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)第一款规定条件的，应当作出不提出没收违法所得申请的决定，并向监察机关或者公安机关书面说明理由；认为需要补充证据的，应当书面要求监察机关或者公安机关补充证据，必要时也可以自行调查。  
监察机关或者公安机关补充证据的时间不计入人民检察院办案期限。

**Article 523** The People's Procuratorate shall make a decision on whether to file an application for confiscation of illegal gains within 30 days after receiving the opinion on confiscation of illegal gains transferred by the supervisory organ or public security organ. If a decision cannot be made within 30 days, the period may be extended by 15 days.  
For a case of confiscation of illegal gains transferred by a supervisory organ or public security organ, if upon review it is found that the conditions provided in paragraph 1 of Article 298 of the Criminal Procedure Law are not met, a decision not to file an application for confiscation of illegal gains shall be made, and the reasons shall be explained in writing to the supervisory organ or public security organ; if it is deemed necessary to supplement evidence, the supervisory organ or public security organ shall be requested in writing to supplement evidence, and if necessary, the People's Procuratorate may also conduct its own investigation.  
The time for the supervisory organ or public security organ to supplement evidence shall not be counted in the time limit for case handling by the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-524"></a>第五百二十四条  人民检察院发现公安机关应当启动违法所得没收程序而不启动的，可以要求公安机关在七日以内书面说明不启动的理由。  
经审查，认为公安机关不启动理由不能成立的，应当通知公安机关启动程序。

**Article 524** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that a public security organ should initiate the procedure for confiscation of illegal gains but fails to do so, it may require the public security organ to provide a written explanation of the reasons for not initiating the procedure within seven days.  
If upon review it is found that the reasons for not initiating the procedure provided by the public security organ are not valid, the public security organ shall be notified to initiate the procedure.

<a id="art-525"></a>第五百二十五条  人民检察院发现公安机关在违法所得没收程序的调查活动中有违法情形的，应当向公安机关提出纠正意见。

**Article 525** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers illegal circumstances in the investigation activities of the public security organ during the procedure for confiscation of illegal gains, it shall issue a correction opinion to the public security organ.

<a id="art-526"></a>第五百二十六条  在审查监察机关或者公安机关移送的没收违法所得意见书的过程中，在逃的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人自动投案或者被抓获的，人民检察院应当终止审查，并将案卷退回监察机关或者公安机关处理。

**Article 526** If during the review of the opinion on confiscation of illegal gains transferred by a supervisory organ or public security organ, the criminal suspect or defendant who is at large voluntarily surrenders or is apprehended, the People's Procuratorate shall terminate the review and return the case files to the supervisory organ or public security organ for handling.

<a id="art-527"></a>第五百二十七条  人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，犯罪嫌疑人死亡而撤销案件，符合[刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)第一款规定条件的，负责侦查的部门应当启动违法所得没收程序进行调查。  
负责侦查的部门进行调查应当查明犯罪嫌疑人涉嫌的犯罪事实，犯罪嫌疑人死亡的情况，以及犯罪嫌疑人的违法所得及其他涉案财产的情况，并可以对违法所得及其他涉案财产依法进行查封、扣押、查询、冻结。  
负责侦查的部门认为符合[刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)第一款规定条件的，应当写出没收违法所得意见书，连同案卷材料一并移送有管辖权的人民检察院负责侦查的部门，并由有管辖权的人民检察院负责侦查的部门移送本院负责捕诉的部门。  
负责捕诉的部门对没收违法所得意见书进行审查，作出是否提出没收违法所得申请的决定，具体程序按照[本规则第五百二十二条](#art-522)、第五百二十三条的规定办理。

**Article 527** For a case directly accepted for investigation by the People's Procuratorate, if the case is dismissed due to the death of the criminal suspect and the conditions provided in paragraph 1 of Article 298 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met, the department responsible for investigation shall initiate the procedure for confiscation of illegal gains to conduct an investigation.  
When conducting the investigation, the department responsible for investigation shall ascertain the facts of the crime suspected by the criminal suspect, the circumstances of the death of the criminal suspect, and the situation of the illegal gains and other property involved in the case of the criminal suspect, and may legally seal, seize, inquire about, or freeze the illegal gains and other property involved in the case.  
If the department responsible for investigation deems that the conditions provided in paragraph 1 of Article 298 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met, it shall prepare an opinion on confiscation of illegal gains and transfer it together with the case materials to the department responsible for investigation of the People's Procuratorate with jurisdiction, and the department responsible for investigation of the People's Procuratorate with jurisdiction shall transfer it to the department responsible for arrest and prosecution of the same People's Procuratorate.  
The department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall review the opinion on confiscation of illegal gains and make a decision on whether to file an application for confiscation of illegal gains, and the specific procedures shall be handled in accordance with Articles 522 and 523 of these Rules.

<a id="art-528"></a>第五百二十八条  在人民检察院审查起诉过程中，犯罪嫌疑人死亡，或者贪污贿赂犯罪、恐怖活动犯罪等重大犯罪案件的犯罪嫌疑人逃匿，在通缉一年后不能到案，依照刑法规定应当追缴其违法所得及其他涉案财产的，人民检察院可以直接提出没收违法所得的申请。  
在人民法院审理案件过程中，被告人死亡而裁定终止审理，或者被告人脱逃而裁定中止审理，人民检察院可以依法另行向人民法院提出没收违法所得的申请。

**Article 528** Where, during the People's Procuratorate's review for prosecution, the criminal suspect dies, or for cases of major crimes such as embezzlement, bribery, or terrorist activities, the criminal suspect absconds and cannot be brought to justice after being wanted for one year, and the illegal gains and other property involved in the case shall be confiscated according to the provisions of the Criminal Law, the People's Procuratorate may directly file an application for confiscation of illegal gains.  
Where, during the trial by the People's Court, the defendant dies and the trial is terminated by ruling, or the defendant escapes and the trial is suspended by ruling, the People's Procuratorate may separately file an application with the People's Court for confiscation of illegal gains according to law.

<a id="art-529"></a>第五百二十九条  人民法院对没收违法所得的申请进行审理，人民检察院应当承担举证责任。  
人民法院对没收违法所得的申请开庭审理的，人民检察院应当派员出席法庭。

**Article 529** Where the People's Court tries the application for confiscation of illegal gains, the People's Procuratorate shall bear the burden of adducing evidence.  
Where the People's Court holds a court session to try the application for confiscation of illegal gains, the People's Procuratorate shall send personnel to appear before the court.

<a id="art-530"></a>第五百三十条  出席法庭的检察官应当宣读没收违法所得申请书，并在法庭调查阶段就申请没收的财产属于违法所得及其他涉案财产等相关事实出示、宣读证据。

**Article 530** The prosecutor appearing before the court shall read out the written application for confiscation of illegal gains, and during the court investigation stage, shall present and read out evidence regarding relevant facts such as that the property for which confiscation is applied constitutes illegal gains or other property involved in the case.

<a id="art-531"></a>第五百三十一条  人民检察院发现人民法院或者审判人员审理没收违法所得案件违反法律规定的诉讼程序，应当向人民法院提出纠正意见。  
人民检察院认为同级人民法院按照违法所得没收程序所作的第一审裁定确有错误的，应当在五日以内向上一级人民法院提出抗诉。  
最高人民检察院、省级人民检察院认为下级人民法院按照违法所得没收程序所作的已经发生法律效力的裁定确有错误的，应当按照审判监督程序向同级人民法院提出抗诉。

**Article 531** Where a People's Procuratorate discovers that a People's Court or a judicial officer has violated the legally prescribed procedure in adjudicating a case of confiscation of illegal gains, it shall submit a corrective opinion to the People's Court.  
Where a People's Procuratorate considers that a first-instance ruling made by the People's Court at the same level under the procedure for confiscation of illegal gains is indeed erroneous, it shall lodge a protest with the People's Court at the next higher level within five days.  
Where the Supreme People's Procuratorate or a provincial People's Procuratorate considers that a legally effective ruling made by a lower People's Court under the procedure for confiscation of illegal gains is indeed erroneous, it shall lodge a protest with the People's Court at the same level under the trial supervision procedure.

<a id="art-532"></a>第五百三十二条  在审理案件过程中，在逃的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人自动投案或者被抓获，人民法院按照[刑事诉讼法第三百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-301)第一款的规定终止审理的，人民检察院应当将案卷退回监察机关或者公安机关处理。

**Article 532** Where, during the adjudication of a case, a criminal suspect or defendant who is a fugitive voluntarily surrenders or is arrested, and the People's Court terminates the adjudication in accordance with the provisions of Article 301(1) of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Procuratorate shall return the case files to the supervisory organ or the public security organ for handling.

<a id="art-533"></a>第五百三十三条  对于[刑事诉讼法第二百九十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-298)第一款规定以外需要没收违法所得的，按照有关规定执行。

**Article 533** For cases where the confiscation of illegal gains is required other than those provided in Article 298(1) of the Criminal Procedure Law, the relevant provisions shall be followed.

#### 第五节 依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的强制医疗程序

<a id="art-534"></a>第五百三十四条  对于实施暴力行为，危害公共安全或者严重危害公民人身安全，已经达到犯罪程度，经法定程序鉴定依法不负刑事责任的精神病人，有继续危害社会可能的，人民检察院应当向人民法院提出强制医疗的申请。  
提出强制医疗的申请以及对强制医疗决定的监督，由负责捕诉的部门办理。

**Article 534** Where a mental patient who has committed violent acts, endangered public security or seriously endangered the personal safety of citizens, such that the conduct has reached the level of a crime, and who is determined through legally prescribed procedures to be exempt from criminal liability according to law, and who may continue to endanger society, the People's Procuratorate shall apply to the People's Court for compulsory medical treatment.  
The application for compulsory medical treatment and the supervision of the decision on compulsory medical treatment shall be handled by the department responsible for arrest and prosecution.

<a id="art-535"></a>第五百三十五条  强制医疗的申请由被申请人实施暴力行为所在地的基层人民检察院提出；由被申请人居住地的人民检察院提出更为适宜的，可以由被申请人居住地的基层人民检察院提出。

**Article 535** The application for compulsory medical treatment shall be submitted by the Primary People's Procuratorate of the place where the respondent committed the violent acts; if it is more appropriate to submit it by the People's Procuratorate of the respondent's place of residence, it may be submitted by the Primary People's Procuratorate of the respondent's place of residence.

<a id="art-536"></a>第五百三十六条  人民检察院向人民法院提出强制医疗的申请，应当制作强制医疗申请书。强制医疗申请书的主要内容包括：  
（一）涉案精神病人的基本情况，包括姓名、性别、出生年月日、出生地、户籍地、公民身份号码、民族、文化程度、职业、工作单位及职务、住址，采取临时保护性约束措施的情况及处所等；  
（二）涉案精神病人的法定代理人的基本情况，包括姓名、住址、联系方式等；  
（三）案由及案件来源；  
（四）涉案精神病人实施危害公共安全或者严重危害公民人身安全的暴力行为的事实，包括实施暴力行为的时间、地点、手段、后果等及相关证据情况；  
（五）涉案精神病人不负刑事责任的依据，包括有关鉴定意见和其他证据材料；  
（六）涉案精神病人继续危害社会的可能；  
（七）提出强制医疗申请的理由和法律依据。

**Article 536** Where a People's Procuratorate submits an application for compulsory medical treatment to the People's Court, it shall prepare a written application for compulsory medical treatment. The main contents of the written application for compulsory medical treatment shall include:  
(1) The basic information of the mental patient involved in the case, including name, sex, date of birth, place of birth, registered permanent residence, citizen identity number, ethnicity, education level, occupation, work unit and position, address, the circumstances and location of temporary protective restraint measures, etc.;  
(2) The basic information of the legal representative of the mental patient involved in the case, including name, address, contact information, etc.;  
(3) The cause of action and the source of the case;  
(4) The facts of the violent acts committed by the mental patient involved in the case that endangered public security or seriously endangered the personal safety of citizens, including the time, place, means, consequences, etc. of the violent acts and relevant evidence;  
(5) The basis for the mental patient involved in the case being exempt from criminal liability, including relevant expert opinions and other evidentiary materials;  
(6) The possibility that the mental patient involved in the case may continue to endanger society;  
(7) The reasons and legal basis for applying for compulsory medical treatment.

<a id="art-537"></a>第五百三十七条  人民检察院审查公安机关移送的强制医疗意见书，应当查明：  
（一）是否属于本院管辖；  
（二）涉案精神病人身份状况是否清楚，包括姓名、性别、国籍、出生年月日、职业和单位等；  
（三）涉案精神病人实施危害公共安全或者严重危害公民人身安全的暴力行为的事实；  
（四）公安机关对涉案精神病人进行鉴定的程序是否合法，涉案精神病人是否依法不负刑事责任；  
（五）涉案精神病人是否有继续危害社会的可能；  
（六）证据材料是否随案移送，不宜移送的证据的清单、复制件、照片或者其他证明文件是否随案移送；  
（七）证据是否确实、充分；  
（八）采取的临时保护性约束措施是否适当。

**Article 537** When reviewing the written opinion on compulsory medical treatment transferred by a public security organ, the People's Procuratorate shall ascertain:  
(1) Whether the case falls within its own jurisdiction;  
(2) Whether the identity status of the mental patient involved in the case is clear, including name, sex, nationality, date of birth, occupation, and work unit, etc.;  
(3) The facts of the violent acts committed by the mental patient involved in the case that endangered public security or seriously endangered the personal safety of citizens;  
(4) Whether the procedures for the public security organ's evaluation of the mental patient involved in the case are lawful, and whether the mental patient involved in the case is exempt from criminal liability according to law;  
(5) Whether the mental patient involved in the case may continue to endanger society;  
(6) Whether the evidentiary materials are transferred together with the case, and whether the list, copies, photographs or other supporting documents of evidence not suitable for transfer are transferred together with the case;  
(7) Whether the evidence is truthful and sufficient;  
(8) Whether the temporary protective restraint measures taken are appropriate.

<a id="art-538"></a>第五百三十八条  人民检察院办理公安机关移送的强制医疗案件，可以采取以下方式开展调查，调查情况应当记录并附卷：  
（一）会见涉案精神病人，听取涉案精神病人的法定代理人、诉讼代理人意见；  
（二）询问办案人员、鉴定人；  
（三）向被害人及其法定代理人、近亲属了解情况；  
（四）向涉案精神病人的主治医生、近亲属、邻居、其他知情人员或者基层组织等了解情况；  
（五）就有关专门性技术问题委托具有法定资质的鉴定机构、鉴定人进行鉴定。

**Article 538** When handling a compulsory medical treatment case transferred by a public security organ, the People's Procuratorate may conduct investigations by the following methods, and the investigation circumstances shall be recorded and attached to the case file:  
(1) Meeting the mental patient involved in the case and hearing the opinions of the legal representative and litigation agent of the mental patient involved in the case;  
(2) Questioning the case-handling personnel and expert evaluators;  
(3) Inquiring about the circumstances from the victim and the victim's legal representative and close relatives;  
(4) Inquiring about the circumstances from the attending physician, close relatives, neighbors, other knowledgeable persons, or grassroots organizations of the mental patient involved in the case;  
(5) Commissioning an evaluation institution or expert evaluator with legally prescribed qualifications to conduct an evaluation on relevant specialized technical issues.

<a id="art-539"></a>第五百三十九条  人民检察院应当在接到公安机关移送的强制医疗意见书后三十日以内作出是否提出强制医疗申请的决定。  
对于公安机关移送的强制医疗案件，经审查认为不符合[刑事诉讼法第三百零二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-302)规定条件的，应当作出不提出强制医疗申请的决定，并向公安机关书面说明理由。认为需要补充证据的，应当书面要求公安机关补充证据，必要时也可以自行调查。  
公安机关补充证据的时间不计入人民检察院办案期限。

**Article 539** The People's Procuratorate shall, within thirty days after receiving the written opinion on compulsory medical treatment transferred by the public security organ, make a decision on whether to submit an application for compulsory medical treatment.  
For a compulsory medical treatment case transferred by a public security organ, if upon review it is considered that the conditions provided in Article 302 of the Criminal Procedure Law are not met, a decision shall be made not to submit an application for compulsory medical treatment, and the reasons shall be explained to the public security organ in writing. If it is considered that supplementary evidence is needed, the public security organ shall be requested in writing to supplement the evidence, and if necessary, the procuratorate may also conduct its own investigation.  
The time for the public security organ to supplement evidence shall not be counted in the time limit for the People's Procuratorate to handle the case.

<a id="art-540"></a>第五百四十条  人民检察院发现公安机关应当启动强制医疗程序而不启动的，可以要求公安机关在七日以内书面说明不启动的理由。  
经审查，认为公安机关不启动理由不能成立的，应当通知公安机关启动强制医疗程序。  
公安机关收到启动强制医疗程序通知书后，未按要求启动强制医疗程序的，人民检察院应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 540** Where a People's Procuratorate discovers that a public security organ should initiate the compulsory medical treatment procedure but fails to do so, it may require the public security organ to provide a written explanation of the reasons for not initiating it within seven days.  
After review, if it is considered that the reasons for not initiating given by the public security organ are untenable, the public security organ shall be notified to initiate the compulsory medical treatment procedure.  
Where the public security organ, after receiving the notice to initiate the compulsory medical treatment procedure, fails to initiate it as required, the People's Procuratorate shall submit a corrective opinion.

<a id="art-541"></a>第五百四十一条  人民检察院对公安机关移送的强制医疗案件，发现公安机关对涉案精神病人进行鉴定违反法律规定，具有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）鉴定机构不具备法定资质的；  
（二）鉴定人不具备法定资质或者违反回避规定的；  
（三）鉴定程序违反法律或者有关规定，鉴定的过程和方法违反相关专业规范要求的；  
（四）鉴定文书不符合法定形式要件的；  
（五）鉴定意见没有依法及时告知相关人员的；  
（六）鉴定人故意作虚假鉴定的；  
（七）其他违反法律规定的情形。  
人民检察院对精神病鉴定程序进行监督，可以要求公安机关补充鉴定或者重新鉴定。必要时，可以询问鉴定人并制作笔录，或者委托具有法定资质的鉴定机构进行补充鉴定或者重新鉴定。

**Article 541** Where a People's Procuratorate, in a compulsory medical treatment case transferred by a public security organ, discovers that the public security organ's evaluation of the mental patient involved in the case violates legal provisions and falls under any of the following circumstances, it shall submit a corrective opinion according to law:  
(1) The evaluation institution does not possess the legally prescribed qualifications;  
(2) The evaluator does not possess the legally prescribed qualifications or violates the recusal provisions;  
(3) The evaluation procedure violates the law or relevant provisions, and the process and methods of evaluation violate relevant professional standards;  
(4) The evaluation document does not meet the legally prescribed formal requirements;  
(5) The evaluation opinion was not timely informed to the relevant persons according to law;  
(6) The evaluator intentionally makes a false evaluation;  
(7) Other circumstances that violate legal provisions.  
When supervising the psychiatric evaluation procedure, the People's Procuratorate may request the public security organ to supplement or conduct a new evaluation. If necessary, it may question the evaluator and make a record, or commission an evaluation institution with legally prescribed qualifications to conduct a supplementary or new evaluation.

<a id="art-542"></a>第五百四十二条  人民检察院发现公安机关对涉案精神病人不应当采取临时保护性约束措施而采取的，应当提出纠正意见。  
认为公安机关应当采取临时保护性约束措施而未采取的，应当建议公安机关采取临时保护性约束措施。

**Article 542** Where a People's Procuratorate discovers that a public security organ has taken temporary protective restraint measures against a mental patient involved in the case when such measures should not have been taken, it shall submit a corrective opinion.  
If it considers that the public security organ should have taken temporary protective restraint measures but failed to do so, it shall suggest that the public security organ take temporary protective restraint measures.

<a id="art-543"></a>第五百四十三条  在审查起诉中，犯罪嫌疑人经鉴定系依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的，人民检察院应当作出不起诉决定。认为符合[刑事诉讼法第三百零二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-302)规定条件的，应当向人民法院提出强制医疗的申请。

**Article 543** During the review of prosecution, if a criminal suspect is determined through evaluation to be a mental patient exempt from criminal liability according to law, the People's Procuratorate shall make a decision not to prosecute. If it considers that the conditions provided in Article 302 of the Criminal Procedure Law are met, it shall submit an application for compulsory medical treatment to the People's Court.

<a id="art-544"></a>第五百四十四条  人民法院对强制医疗案件开庭审理的，人民检察院应当派员出席法庭。

**Article 544** Where a People's Court hears a compulsory medical treatment case in open court, the People's Procuratorate shall send personnel to appear in court.

<a id="art-545"></a>第五百四十五条  人民检察院发现人民法院强制医疗案件审理活动具有下列情形之一的，应当提出纠正意见：  
（一）未通知被申请人或者被告人的法定代理人到场的；  
（二）被申请人或者被告人没有委托诉讼代理人，未通知法律援助机构指派律师为其提供法律帮助的；  
（三）未组成合议庭或者合议庭组成人员不合法的；  
（四）未经被申请人、被告人的法定代理人请求直接作出不开庭审理决定的；  
（五）未会见被申请人的；  
（六）被申请人、被告人要求出庭且具备出庭条件，未准许其出庭的；  
（七）违反法定审理期限的；  
（八）收到人民检察院对强制医疗决定不当的书面纠正意见后，未另行组成合议庭审理或者未在一个月以内作出复议决定的；  
（九）人民法院作出的强制医疗决定或者驳回强制医疗申请决定不当的；  
（十）其他违反法律规定的情形。

**Article 545** Where a People's Procuratorate discovers that the People's Court's adjudication activities in a compulsory medical treatment case fall under any of the following circumstances, it shall submit a corrective opinion:  
(1) Failure to notify the legal representative of the respondent or the defendant to be present;  
(2) Where the respondent or the defendant has not appointed a litigation agent, failure to notify a legal aid institution to assign a lawyer to provide legal assistance;  
(3) Failure to form a collegial panel or the composition of the collegial panel is unlawful;  
(4) Making a decision not to hold a court hearing without a request from the legal representative of the respondent or the defendant;  
(5) Failure to meet the respondent;  
(6) Where the respondent or the defendant requests to appear in court and meets the conditions for appearing in court, failure to permit their appearance;  
(7) Violation of the statutory adjudication time limit;  
(8) After receiving written corrective opinions from the People's Procuratorate regarding an improper compulsory medical treatment decision, failure to form a new collegial panel for adjudication or failure to make a reconsideration decision within one month;  
(9) The People's Court's decision on compulsory medical treatment or decision to reject the application for compulsory medical treatment is improper;  
(10) Other circumstances that violate legal provisions.

<a id="art-546"></a>第五百四十六条  出席法庭的检察官发现人民法院或者审判人员审理强制医疗案件违反法律规定的诉讼程序，应当记录在案，并在休庭后及时向检察长报告，由人民检察院在庭审后向人民法院提出纠正意见。

**Article 546** Where a prosecutor appearing in court discovers that the People's Court or a judicial officer has violated the legally prescribed procedure in adjudicating a compulsory medical treatment case, the prosecutor shall record it in the case file and promptly report it to the Chief Prosecutor after adjournment, and the People's Procuratorate shall submit a corrective opinion to the People's Court after the court session.

<a id="art-547"></a>第五百四十七条  人民检察院认为人民法院作出的强制医疗决定或者驳回强制医疗申请的决定，具有下列情形之一的，应当在收到决定书副本后二十日以内向人民法院提出纠正意见：  
（一）据以作出决定的事实不清或者确有错误的；  
（二）据以作出决定的证据不确实、不充分的；  
（三）据以作出决定的证据依法应当予以排除的；  
（四）据以作出决定的主要证据之间存在矛盾的；  
（五）有确实、充分的证据证明应当决定强制医疗而予以驳回的，或者不应当决定强制医疗而决定强制医疗的；  
（六）审理过程中严重违反法定诉讼程序，可能影响公正审理和决定的。

**Article 547** Where a People's Procuratorate considers that a People's Court's decision on compulsory medical treatment or decision to reject an application for compulsory medical treatment falls under any of the following circumstances, it shall submit a corrective opinion to the People's Court within twenty days after receiving the copy of the decision:  
(1) The facts on which the decision is based are unclear or indeed erroneous;  
(2) The evidence on which the decision is based is not truthful or insufficient;  
(3) The evidence on which the decision is based should be excluded according to law;  
(4) There are contradictions among the main evidence on which the decision is based;  
(5) There is truthful and sufficient evidence proving that a decision for compulsory medical treatment should have been made, but the application was rejected, or that a decision for compulsory medical treatment should not have been made, but such a decision was made;  
(6) There are serious violations of statutory adjudication procedure during the adjudication process that may affect the fair adjudication and decision.

<a id="art-548"></a>第五百四十八条  人民法院在审理案件过程中发现被告人符合强制医疗条件，适用强制医疗程序对案件进行审理的，人民检察院应当在庭审中发表意见。  
人民法院作出宣告被告人无罪或者不负刑事责任的判决和强制医疗决定的，人民检察院应当进行审查。对判决确有错误的，应当依法提出抗诉；对强制医疗决定不当或者未作出强制医疗的决定不当的，应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 548** Where, during the adjudication of a case, the People's Court discovers that the defendant meets the conditions for compulsory medical treatment and applies the compulsory medical treatment procedure to adjudicate the case, the People's Procuratorate shall deliver its opinions during the court session.  
Where the People's Court renders a judgment acquitting the defendant or exempting the defendant from criminal liability and a decision on compulsory medical treatment, the People's Procuratorate shall review the case. If the judgment is indeed erroneous, it shall lodge a protest according to law; if the decision on compulsory medical treatment is improper or the failure to make a decision on compulsory medical treatment is improper, it shall submit a corrective opinion.

<a id="art-549"></a>第五百四十九条  人民法院收到被决定强制医疗的人、被害人及其法定代理人、近亲属复议申请后，未组成合议庭审理，或者未在一个月以内作出复议决定，或者有其他违法行为的，人民检察院应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 549** Where, after receiving a reconsideration application from the person subject to compulsory medical treatment, the victim, or their legal representatives or close relatives, the People's Court fails to form a collegial panel for adjudication, or fails to make a reconsideration decision within one month, or commits other illegal acts, the People's Procuratorate shall submit a corrective opinion.

<a id="art-550"></a>第五百五十条  人民检察院对于人民法院批准解除强制医疗的决定实行监督，发现人民法院解除强制医疗的决定不当的，应当提出纠正意见。

**Article 550** The People's Procuratorate shall supervise the People's Court's decision to approve the discharge from compulsory medical treatment. If it discovers that the People's Court's decision to discharge from compulsory medical treatment is improper, it shall submit a corrective opinion.

### 第十三章 刑事诉讼法律监督

#### 第一节 一般规定

<a id="art-551"></a>第五百五十一条  人民检察院对刑事诉讼活动实行法律监督，发现违法情形的，依法提出抗诉、纠正意见或者检察建议。  
人民检察院对于涉嫌违法的事实，可以采取以下方式进行调查核实：  
（一）讯问、询问犯罪嫌疑人；  
（二）询问证人、被害人或者其他诉讼参与人；  
（三）询问办案人员；  
（四）询问在场人员或者其他可能知情的人员；  
（五）听取申诉人或者控告人的意见；  
（六）听取辩护人、值班律师意见；  
（七）调取、查询、复制相关登记表册、法律文书、体检记录及案卷材料等；  
（八）调取讯问笔录、询问笔录及相关录音、录像或其他视听资料；  
（九）进行伤情、病情检查或者鉴定；  
（十）其他调查核实方式。  
人民检察院在调查核实过程中不得限制被调查对象的人身、财产权利。

**Article 551** A People's Procuratorate shall exercise legal supervision over criminal proceedings, and where it discovers an illegal act, it shall, according to law, lodge a protest, issue a correction opinion, or make a procuratorial suggestion.  
Where a People's Procuratorate suspects illegal facts, it may conduct investigation and verification by the following means:  
(1) interrogating or questioning a criminal suspect;  
(2) questioning a witness, victim, or other participant in the proceedings;  
(3) questioning the case-handling personnel;  
(4) questioning persons present or other persons who may have knowledge of the facts;  
(5) hearing the opinions of a petitioner or complainant;  
(6) hearing the opinions of a defender or duty lawyer;  
(7) retrieving, inquiring into, or copying relevant registers, legal documents, medical examination records, case files and materials, etc.;  
(8) retrieving interrogation transcripts, questioning transcripts, and related audio or video recordings or other audio-visual materials;  
(9) conducting examination or evaluation of injuries or illnesses; and  
(10) other means of investigation and verification.  
During the process of investigation and verification, a People's Procuratorate shall not restrict the personal or property rights of the investigated subject.

<a id="art-552"></a>第五百五十二条  人民检察院发现刑事诉讼活动中的违法行为，对于情节较轻的，由检察人员以口头方式提出纠正意见；对于情节较重的，经检察长决定，发出纠正违法通知书。对于带有普遍性的违法情形，经检察长决定，向相关机关提出检察建议。构成犯罪的，移送有关机关、部门依法追究刑事责任。  
有申诉人、控告人的，调查核实和纠正违法情况应予告知。

**Article 552** Where a People's Procuratorate discovers an illegal act in criminal proceedings, if the circumstances are minor, the procurator shall orally give a correction opinion; if the circumstances are relatively serious, with the decision of the chief procurator, a notice of correction of the illegal act shall be issued. For illegal acts of a prevalent nature, with the decision of the chief procurator, a procuratorial suggestion shall be submitted to the relevant authority. If the act constitutes a crime, the matter shall be transferred to the relevant authority or department for investigation of criminal liability according to law.  
Where there is a petitioner or complainant, the investigation, verification, and correction of the illegal act shall be notified.

<a id="art-553"></a>第五百五十三条  人民检察院发出纠正违法通知书的，应当监督落实。被监督单位在纠正违法通知书规定的期限内没有回复纠正情况的，人民检察院应当督促回复。经督促被监督单位仍不回复或者没有正当理由不纠正的，人民检察院应当向上一级人民检察院报告。

**Article 553** Where a People's Procuratorate issues a notice of correction of an illegal act, it shall supervise its implementation. If the supervised entity fails to reply regarding the correction within the time limit specified in the notice of correction, the People's Procuratorate shall urge a reply. If, after such urging, the supervised entity still fails to reply or does not correct the act without justifiable reasons, the People's Procuratorate shall report to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level.

<a id="art-554"></a>第五百五十四条  被监督单位对纠正意见申请复查的，人民检察院应当在收到被监督单位的书面意见后七日以内进行复查，并将复查结果及时通知申请复查的单位。经过复查，认为纠正意见正确的，应当及时向上一级人民检察院报告；认为纠正意见错误的，应当及时予以撤销。  
上一级人民检察院经审查，认为下级人民检察院纠正意见正确的，应当及时通报被监督单位的上级机关或者主管机关，并建议其督促被监督单位予以纠正；认为下级人民检察院纠正意见错误的，应当书面通知下级人民检察院予以撤销，下级人民检察院应当执行，并及时向被监督单位说明情况。

**Article 554** Where a supervised entity applies for review of a correction opinion, the People's Procuratorate shall conduct a review within seven days after receiving the written opinion of the supervised entity, and shall promptly notify the entity that applied for review of the result of the review. If after review it considers the correction opinion to be correct, it shall promptly report to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level; if it considers the correction opinion to be erroneous, it shall promptly revoke it.  
Where the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level, after examination, considers the correction opinion of the lower-level People's Procuratorate to be correct, it shall promptly notify the superior authority or competent authority of the supervised entity and suggest that it urge the supervised entity to make corrections; if it considers the correction opinion of the lower-level People's Procuratorate to be erroneous, it shall notify the lower-level People's Procuratorate in writing to revoke it, and the lower-level People's Procuratorate shall implement the revocation and promptly explain the situation to the supervised entity.

<a id="art-555"></a>第五百五十五条  当事人和辩护人、诉讼代理人、利害关系人对于办案机关及其工作人员有[刑事诉讼法第一百一十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-117)规定的行为，向该机关申诉或者控告，对该机关作出的处理不服或者该机关未在规定时间内作出答复，而向人民检察院申诉的，办案机关的同级人民检察院应当受理。  
人民检察院直接受理侦查的案件，当事人和辩护人、诉讼代理人、利害关系人对办理案件的人民检察院的处理不服的，可以向上一级人民检察院申诉，上一级人民检察院应当受理。  
未向办案机关申诉或者控告，或者办案机关在规定时间内尚未作出处理决定，直接向人民检察院申诉的，人民检察院应当告知其向办案机关申诉或者控告。人民检察院在审查逮捕、审查起诉中发现有[刑事诉讼法第一百一十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-117)规定的违法情形的，可以直接监督纠正。  
当事人和辩护人、诉讼代理人、利害关系人对[刑事诉讼法第一百一十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-117)规定情形之外的违法行为提出申诉或者控告的，人民检察院应当受理，并及时审查，依法处理。

**Article 555** Where a party, defender, litigation agent, or interested person, due to the acts specified in Article 117 of the Criminal Procedure Law by a case-handling authority and its staff, petitions or complains to that authority, and is dissatisfied with the decision made by that authority or that authority fails to give a reply within the prescribed time limit, and then petitions to a People's Procuratorate, the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the case-handling authority shall accept the petition.  
For a case directly accepted and investigated by a People's Procuratorate, if a party, defender, litigation agent, or interested person is dissatisfied with the decision of the People's Procuratorate handling the case, the person may petition to the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level, and the People's Procuratorate at the next higher level shall accept the petition.  
Where a person directly petitions to a People's Procuratorate without first petitioning or complaining to the case-handling authority, or where the case-handling authority has not yet made a decision within the prescribed time limit, the People's Procuratorate shall inform the person to petition or complain to the case-handling authority. If a People's Procuratorate discovers illegal acts specified in Article 117 of the Criminal Procedure Law during the review of arrest or prosecution, it may directly supervise and correct them.  
Where a party, defender, litigation agent, or interested person petitions or complains about illegal acts other than those specified in Article 117 of the Criminal Procedure Law, the People's Procuratorate shall accept the petition or complaint, promptly review it, and handle it according to law.

<a id="art-556"></a>第五百五十六条  对人民检察院及其工作人员办理案件中违法行为的申诉、控告，由负责控告申诉检察的部门受理和审查办理。对其他司法机关处理决定不服向人民检察院提出的申诉，由负责控告申诉检察的部门受理后，移送相关办案部门审查办理。  
审查办理的部门应当在受理之日起十五日以内提出审查意见。人民检察院对[刑事诉讼法第一百一十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/刑事诉讼/中华人民共和国刑事诉讼法.md#art-117)的申诉，经审查认为需要其他司法机关说明理由的，应当要求有关机关说明理由，并在收到理由说明后十五日以内提出审查意见。  
人民检察院及其工作人员办理案件中存在的违法情形属实的，应当予以纠正；不存在违法行为的，书面答复申诉人、控告人。  
其他司法机关对申诉、控告的处理不正确的，人民检察院应当通知有关机关予以纠正；处理正确的，书面答复申诉人、控告人。

**Article 556** Petitions and complaints concerning illegal acts in the handling of cases by a People's Procuratorate and its staff shall be accepted and reviewed by the department responsible for prosecutorial work on petitions and complaints.
