If it discovers that the recommendation for commutation of punishment or parole is improper, or that the submission of the recommendation for commutation of punishment or parole violates statutory procedures, it shall, within ten days, submit a written procuratorial opinion to the People's Court that tries the commutation of punishment or parole case after reporting to and obtaining approval from the Chief Procurator, and may also submit a written corrective opinion to the executing authority. In complex or special circumstances, the period may be extended by ten days.

<a id="art-636"></a>第六百三十六条  人民检察院发现监狱等执行机关提请人民法院裁定减刑、假释的活动具有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）将不符合减刑、假释法定条件的罪犯，提请人民法院裁定减刑、假释的；  
（二）对依法应当减刑、假释的罪犯，不提请人民法院裁定减刑、假释的；  
（三）提请对罪犯减刑、假释违反法定程序，或者没有完备的合法手续的；  
（四）提请对罪犯减刑的减刑幅度、起始时间、间隔时间或者减刑后又假释的间隔时间不符合有关规定的；  
（五）被提请减刑、假释的罪犯被减刑后实际执行的刑期或者假释考验期不符合有关法律规定的；  
（六）其他违法情形。

**Article 636** If the People's Procuratorate discovers that the activities of a prison or other executing authority in submitting a recommendation to the People's Court for a ruling on commutation of punishment or parole fall under any of the following circumstances, it shall present corrective opinions according to law:  
(1) submitting a recommendation to the People's Court for a ruling on commutation of punishment or parole for a convict who does not meet the statutory conditions for commutation of punishment or parole;  
(2) failing to submit a recommendation to the People's Court for a ruling on commutation of punishment or parole for a convict who should have commutation of punishment or parole according to law;  
(3) submitting a recommendation for commutation of punishment or parole for a convict in violation of statutory procedures, or without complete legal formalities;  
(4) the extent of commutation, starting time, interval, or the interval between commutation and subsequent parole recommended for a convict do not conform to relevant provisions;  
(5) the actual sentence to be served by a convict after commutation, or the parole probation period, as recommended, do not conform to relevant legal provisions;  
(6) other illegal circumstances.

<a id="art-637"></a>第六百三十七条  人民法院开庭审理减刑、假释案件，人民检察院应当指派检察人员出席法庭，发表意见。

**Article 637** When the People's Court holds a hearing on a case of commutation of punishment or parole, the People's Procuratorate shall assign procuratorial personnel to attend the court and present opinions.

<a id="art-638"></a>第六百三十八条  人民检察院收到人民法院减刑、假释的裁定书副本后，应当及时审查下列内容：  
（一）被减刑、假释的罪犯是否符合法定条件，对罪犯减刑的减刑幅度、起始时间、间隔时间或者减刑后又假释的间隔时间、罪犯被减刑后实际执行的刑期或者假释考验期是否符合有关规定；  
（二）执行机关提请减刑、假释的程序是否合法；  
（三）人民法院审理、裁定减刑、假释的程序是否合法；  
（四）人民法院对罪犯裁定不予减刑、假释是否符合有关规定；  
（五）人民法院减刑、假释裁定书是否依法送达执行并向社会公布。

**Article 638** After receiving a copy of the ruling on commutation of punishment or parole from the People's Court, the People's Procuratorate shall promptly examine the following:  
(1) whether the convict who has been commuted or paroled meets the statutory conditions, and whether the extent of commutation, starting time, interval, or the interval between commutation and subsequent parole, the actual sentence to be served after commutation, or the parole probation period conform to relevant provisions;  
(2) whether the procedures for the executing authority's submission of the recommendation for commutation of punishment or parole are lawful;  
(3) whether the procedures for the People's Court's hearing and ruling on commutation of punishment or parole are lawful;  
(4) whether the People's Court's ruling not to grant commutation of punishment or parole to the convict conforms to relevant provisions;  
(5) whether the People's Court's ruling on commutation of punishment or parole has been served to the executing authority according to law and published to the public.

<a id="art-639"></a>第六百三十九条  人民检察院经审查认为人民法院减刑、假释的裁定不当，应当在收到裁定书副本后二十日以内，向作出减刑、假释裁定的人民法院提出纠正意见。

**Article 639** If, upon examination, the People's Procuratorate deems that the People's Court's ruling on commutation of punishment or parole is improper, it shall, within twenty days from the date of receiving the copy of the ruling, present corrective opinions to the People's Court that made the ruling on commutation of punishment or parole.

<a id="art-640"></a>第六百四十条  对人民法院减刑、假释裁定的纠正意见，由作出减刑、假释裁定的人民法院的同级人民检察院书面提出。  
下级人民检察院发现人民法院减刑、假释裁定不当的，应当向作出减刑、假释裁定的人民法院的同级人民检察院报告。

**Article 640** The corrective opinions on the People's Court's ruling on commutation of punishment or parole shall be submitted in writing by the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the People's Court that made the ruling on commutation of punishment or parole.  
If a lower People's Procuratorate discovers that the People's Court's ruling on commutation of punishment or parole is improper, it shall report to the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the People's Court that made the ruling on commutation of punishment or parole.

<a id="art-641"></a>第六百四十一条  人民检察院对人民法院减刑、假释的裁定提出纠正意见后，应当监督人民法院是否在收到纠正意见后一个月以内重新组成合议庭进行审理，并监督重新作出的裁定是否符合法律规定。对最终裁定不符合法律规定的，应当向同级人民法院提出纠正意见。

**Article 641** After presenting corrective opinions on the People's Court's ruling on commutation of punishment or parole, the People's Procuratorate shall supervise whether the People's Court re-forms a collegial panel for trial within one month after receiving the corrective opinions, and supervise whether the new ruling conforms to legal provisions. If the final ruling does not conform to legal provisions, it shall present corrective opinions to the People's Court at the same level.

#### 第四节 社区矫正监督

<a id="art-642"></a>第六百四十二条  人民检察院发现社区矫正决定机关、看守所、监狱、社区矫正机构在交付、接收社区矫正对象活动中违反有关规定的，应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 642** If the People's Procuratorate discovers that the community correction decision-making authority, detention center, prison, or community correction institution violates relevant provisions in the delivery and reception of community correction subjects, it shall present corrective opinions according to law.

<a id="art-643"></a>第六百四十三条  人民检察院发现社区矫正执法活动具有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）社区矫正对象报到后，社区矫正机构未履行法定告知义务，致使其未按照有关规定接受监督管理的；  
（二）违反法律规定批准社区矫正对象离开所居住的市、县，或者违反人民法院禁止令的内容批准社区矫正对象进入特定区域或者场所的；  
（三）没有依法监督管理而导致社区矫正对象脱管的；  
（四）社区矫正对象违反监督管理规定或者人民法院的禁止令，未依法予以警告、未提请公安机关给予治安管理处罚的；  
（五）对社区矫正对象有殴打、体罚、虐待、侮辱人格、强迫其参加超时间或者超体力社区服务等侵犯其合法权利行为的；  
（六）未依法办理解除、终止社区矫正的；  
（七）其他违法情形。

**Article 643** If the People's Procuratorate discovers that community correction enforcement activities have any of the following circumstances, it shall present corrective opinions according to law:  
(1) after the community correction subject reports, the community correction institution fails to perform the statutory notification obligation, causing the subject not to accept supervision and management in accordance with relevant provisions;  
(2) approving, in violation of legal provisions, the community correction subject to leave the city or county where he/she resides, or approving the subject to enter a specific area or place in violation of the content of the People's Court's restraining order;  
(3) failing to supervise and manage according to law, resulting in the community correction subject being out of supervision;  
(4) when the community correction subject violates supervision and management regulations or the People's Court's restraining order, failing to give a warning according to law, or failing to submit a recommendation to the public security organ for public security penalties;  
(5) committing acts that infringe upon the lawful rights of the community correction subject, such as beating, corporal punishment, maltreatment, humiliation of personality, or forcing him/her to participate in community service exceeding the time limit or physical capacity;  
(6) failing to handle the release or termination of community correction according to law;  
(7) other illegal circumstances.

<a id="art-644"></a>第六百四十四条  人民检察院发现对社区矫正对象的刑罚变更执行活动具有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）社区矫正机构未依法向人民法院、公安机关、监狱管理机关提出撤销缓刑、撤销假释建议或者对暂予监外执行的收监执行建议，或者未依法向人民法院提出减刑建议的；  
（二）人民法院、公安机关、监狱管理机关未依法作出裁定、决定，或者未依法送达的；  
（三）公安机关未依法将罪犯送交看守所、监狱，或者看守所、监狱未依法收监执行的；  
（四）公安机关未依法对在逃的罪犯实施追捕的；  
（五）其他违法情形。

**Article 644** If the People's Procuratorate discovers that the activities of changing the execution of criminal punishment for a community correction subject have any of the following circumstances, it shall present corrective opinions according to law:  
(1) the community correction institution fails to submit a recommendation to the People's Court, public security organ, or prison administration authority for revocation of probation, revocation of parole, or for committing a convict under temporary execution outside prison to prison, or fails to submit a recommendation for commutation of punishment to the People's Court according to law;  
(2) the People's Court, public security organ, or prison administration authority fails to make a ruling or decision according to law, or fails to serve it according to law;  
(3) the public security organ fails to deliver the convict to a detention center or prison according to law, or the detention center or prison fails to commit the convict to prison according to law;  
(4) the public security organ fails to pursue and arrest a fugitive convict according to law;  
(5) other illegal circumstances.

#### 第五节 刑事裁判涉财产部分执行监督

<a id="art-645"></a>第六百四十五条  人民检察院发现人民法院执行刑事裁判涉财产部分具有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）执行立案活动违法的；  
（二）延期缴纳、酌情减少或者免除罚金违法的；  
（三）中止执行或者终结执行违法的；  
（四）被执行人有履行能力，应当执行而不执行的；  
（五）损害被执行人、被害人、利害关系人或者案外人合法权益的；  
（六）刑事裁判全部或者部分被撤销后未依法返还或者赔偿的；  
（七）执行的财产未依法上缴国库的；  
（八）其他违法情形。  
人民检察院对人民法院执行刑事裁判涉财产部分进行监督，可以对公安机关查封、扣押、冻结涉案财物的情况，人民法院审判部门、立案部门、执行部门移送、立案、执行情况，被执行人的履行能力等情况向有关单位和个人进行调查核实。

**Article 645** If the People's Procuratorate discovers that the People's Court's enforcement of the property-related part of criminal judgments has any of the following circumstances, it shall present corrective opinions according to law:  
(1) the case-filing activity for enforcement is illegal;  
(2) the extension of time for payment, discretionary reduction or exemption of a fine is illegal;  
(3) the suspension of enforcement or termination of enforcement is illegal;  
(4) the person subject to enforcement has the capacity to perform but should perform without performing;  
(5) infringing upon the lawful rights and interests of the person subject to enforcement, victim, interested party, or person not involved in the case;  
(6) after the criminal judgment is wholly or partially revoked, failing to return or compensate according to law;  
(7) the property enforced is not turned over to the state treasury according to law;  
(8) other illegal circumstances.  
In supervising the People's Court's enforcement of the property-related part of criminal judgments, the People's Procuratorate may investigate and verify with the relevant entities or individuals regarding the public security organ's sealing, seizure, and freezing of case-related property; the transfer, case-filing, and enforcement activities of the People's Court's trial department, case-filing department, and enforcement department; and the performance capacity of the person subject to enforcement.

<a id="art-646"></a>第六百四十六条  人民检察院发现被执行人或者其他人员有隐匿、转移、变卖财产等妨碍执行情形的，可以建议人民法院及时查封、扣押、冻结。  
公安机关不依法向人民法院移送涉案财物、相关清单、照片和其他证明文件，或者对涉案财物的查封、扣押、冻结、返还、处置等活动存在违法情形的，人民检察院应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 646** If the People's Procuratorate discovers that the person subject to enforcement or other persons have acts hindering enforcement, such as concealment, transfer, or sale of property, it may recommend that the People's Court promptly seal, seize, or freeze.  
If the public security organ fails to transfer case-related property, relevant lists, photos, and other evidentiary documents to the People's Court according to law, or has illegal acts in sealing, seizing, freezing, returning, or disposing of case-related property, the People's Procuratorate shall present corrective opinions according to law.

#### 第六节 死刑执行监督

<a id="art-647"></a>第六百四十七条  被判处死刑立即执行的罪犯在被执行死刑时，人民检察院应当指派检察官临场监督。  
死刑执行临场监督由人民检察院负责刑事执行检察的部门承担。人民检察院派驻看守所、监狱的检察人员应当予以协助，负责捕诉的部门应当提供有关情况。  
执行死刑过程中，人民检察院临场监督人员根据需要可以进行拍照、录像。执行死刑后，人民检察院临场监督人员应当检查罪犯是否确已死亡，并填写死刑执行临场监督笔录，签名后入卷归档。

**Article 647** When a convict sentenced to death with immediate execution is to be executed, the People's Procuratorate shall assign a procurator to supervise the execution at the scene.  
The on-site supervision of execution of death sentences shall be carried out by the department of the People's Procuratorate responsible for criminal execution procuratorial work. The procuratorial personnel stationed by the People's Procuratorate in the detention center or prison shall provide assistance, and the department responsible for arrest and prosecution shall provide relevant information.  
During the execution of the death sentence, the on-site supervisor of the People's Procuratorate may take photographs or videos as needed. After the execution, the on-site supervisor shall check whether the convict is indeed dead, fill in the record of on-site supervision of death sentence execution, sign it, and file it into the case file.

<a id="art-648"></a>第六百四十八条  省级人民检察院负责案件管理的部门收到高级人民法院报请最高人民法院复核的死刑判决书、裁定书副本后，应当在三日以内将判决书、裁定书副本移送本院负责刑事执行检察的部门。  
判处死刑的案件一审是由中级人民法院审理的，省级人民检察院应当及时将死刑判决书、裁定书副本移送中级人民法院的同级人民检察院负责刑事执行检察的部门。  
人民检察院收到同级人民法院执行死刑临场监督通知后，应当查明同级人民法院是否收到最高人民法院核准死刑的裁定或者作出的死刑判决、裁定和执行死刑的命令。

**Article 648** After receiving a copy of the death sentence judgment or ruling submitted by the Higher People's Court to the Supreme People's Court for review, the case management department of the provincial People's Procuratorate shall, within three days, transfer the copy of the judgment or ruling to the department responsible for criminal execution procuratorial work of the same procuratorate.  
If the first instance of a death sentence case was tried by an Intermediate People's Court, the provincial People's Procuratorate shall promptly transfer the copy of the death sentence judgment or ruling to the department responsible for criminal execution procuratorial work of the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the Intermediate People's Court.  
After receiving the notice of on-site supervision of death sentence execution from the People's Court at the same level, the People's Procuratorate shall ascertain whether the People's Court at the same level has received the Supreme People's Court's ruling approving the death sentence or the death sentence judgment or ruling and the order for execution of the death sentence.

<a id="art-649"></a>第六百四十九条  执行死刑前，人民检察院发现具有下列情形之一的，应当建议人民法院立即停止执行，并层报最高人民检察院负责死刑复核监督的部门：  
（一）被执行人并非应当执行死刑的罪犯的；  
（二）罪犯犯罪时不满十八周岁，或者审判的时候已满七十五周岁，依法不应当适用死刑的；  
（三）罪犯正在怀孕的；  
（四）共同犯罪的其他犯罪嫌疑人到案，共同犯罪的其他罪犯被暂停或者停止执行死刑，可能影响罪犯量刑的；  
（五）罪犯可能有其他犯罪的；  
（六）罪犯揭发他人重大犯罪事实或者有其他重大立功表现，可能需要改判的；  
（七）判决、裁定可能有影响定罪量刑的其他错误的。  
在执行死刑活动中，发现人民法院有侵犯被执行死刑罪犯的人身权、财产权或者其近亲属、继承人合法权利等违法情形的，人民检察院应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 649** Before the execution of a death sentence, if the People's Procuratorate discovers any of the following circumstances, it shall recommend that the People's Court immediately suspend the execution and report level by level to the Supreme People's Procuratorate's department responsible for supervision of death sentence review:  
(1) the person subject to execution is not the convict who should be executed;  
(2) the convict was under the age of 18 at the time of the crime, or was 75 years of age or older at the time of trial, and should not be sentenced to death according to law;  
(3) the convict is pregnant;  
(4) another criminal suspect in the joint crime has been brought to justice, or another convict in the joint crime has been suspended or stopped from execution, which may affect the sentencing of the convict;  
(5) the convict may have committed other crimes;  
(6) the convict has disclosed a major criminal fact of another person or has other major meritorious service, which may require a changed sentence;  
(7) the judgment or ruling may have other errors affecting the determination of guilt or sentencing.  
During the execution of the death sentence, if the People's Procuratorate discovers that the People's Court has violated the personal or property rights of the convict being executed, or the lawful rights of his/her close relatives or heirs, it shall present corrective opinions according to law.

<a id="art-650"></a>第六百五十条  判处被告人死刑缓期二年执行的判决、裁定在执行过程中，人民检察院监督的内容主要包括：  
（一）死刑缓期执行期满，符合法律规定应当减为无期徒刑、有期徒刑条件的，监狱是否及时提出减刑建议提请人民法院裁定，人民法院是否依法裁定；  
（二）罪犯在缓期执行期间故意犯罪，监狱是否依法侦查和移送起诉；罪犯确系故意犯罪，情节恶劣，查证属实，应当执行死刑的，人民法院是否依法核准或者裁定执行死刑。  
被判处死刑缓期二年执行的罪犯在死刑缓期执行期间故意犯罪，执行机关向人民检察院移送起诉的，由罪犯服刑所在地设区的市级人民检察院审查决定是否提起公诉。  
人民检察院发现人民法院对被判处死刑缓期二年执行的罪犯减刑不当的，应当依照[本规则第六百三十九条](#art-639)、第六百四十条的规定，向人民法院提出纠正意见。罪犯在死刑缓期执行期间又故意犯罪，经人民检察院起诉后，人民法院仍然予以减刑的，人民检察院应当依照本规则相关规定，向人民法院提出抗诉。

**Article 650** During the execution of a judgment or ruling sentencing the defendant to death with a two-year reprieve, the content of supervision by the People's Procuratorate mainly includes:  
(1) upon expiration of the reprieve period, if the conditions for reduction to life imprisonment or fixed-term imprisonment according to law are met, whether the prison has promptly submitted a recommendation for commutation of punishment to the People's Court for a ruling, and whether the People's Court has ruled according to law;  
(2) if the convict commits an intentional crime during the reprieve period, whether the prison has investigated according to law and transferred the case for prosecution; if the convict is indeed guilty of an intentional crime, with grave circumstances, and the facts are verified, and the death penalty should be executed, whether the People's Court has approved or ruled on the execution of the death sentence according to law.  
If a convict sentenced to death with a two-year reprieve commits an intentional crime during the reprieve period and the executing authority transfers the case to the People's Procuratorate for prosecution, the People's Procuratorate at the level of a city divided into districts where the convict is serving the sentence shall examine and decide whether to initiate a public prosecution.  
If the People's Procuratorate discovers that the People's Court has improperly commuted the sentence of a convict sentenced to death with a two-year reprieve, it shall present corrective opinions to the People's Court in accordance with Articles 639 and 640 of these Rules. If the convict commits another intentional crime during the reprieve period, and after prosecution by the People's Procuratorate, the People's Court still commutes the sentence, the People's Procuratorate shall lodge a protest to the People's Court in accordance with the relevant provisions of these Rules.

#### 第七节 强制医疗执行监督

<a id="art-651"></a>第六百五十一条  人民检察院发现人民法院、公安机关、强制医疗机构在对依法不负刑事责任的精神病人的强制医疗的交付执行、医疗、解除等活动中违反有关规定的，应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 651** : Where a People's Procuratorate discovers that a People's Court, public security organ, or compulsory medical institution has violated relevant provisions in the delivery, execution, medical treatment, or release of a mentally ill person who is not criminally responsible in accordance with law, it shall propose a corrective opinion in accordance with law.

<a id="art-652"></a>第六百五十二条  人民检察院在强制医疗执行监督中发现被强制医疗的人不符合强制医疗条件或者需要依法追究刑事责任，人民法院作出的强制医疗决定可能错误的，应当在五日以内将有关材料转交作出强制医疗决定的人民法院的同级人民检察院。收到材料的人民检察院负责捕诉的部门应当在二十日以内进行审查，并将审查情况和处理意见反馈负责强制医疗执行监督的人民检察院。

**Article 652** Where the People's Procuratorate, during supervision over the enforcement of compulsory medical treatment, discovers that the person subjected to compulsory medical treatment does not meet the conditions for compulsory medical treatment or needs to be investigated for criminal liability according to law, and the compulsory medical treatment decision made by the People's Court may be erroneous, it shall, within five days, transfer the relevant materials to the People's Procuratorate at the same level as the People's Court that made the compulsory medical treatment decision. The department responsible for prosecution and arrest in the People's Procuratorate that receives the materials shall, within twenty days, conduct review and feed back the review status and handling opinions to the People's Procuratorate responsible for supervision over the enforcement of compulsory medical treatment.

<a id="art-653"></a>第六百五十三条  人民检察院发现公安机关在对涉案精神病人采取临时保护性约束措施时有违法情形的，应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 653** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that a public security organ has illegal circumstances when taking temporary protective restraint measures against a mentally ill person involved in a case, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law.

#### 第八节 监管执法监督

<a id="art-654"></a>第六百五十四条  人民检察院发现看守所收押活动和监狱收监活动中具有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）没有收押、收监文书、凭证，文书、凭证不齐全，或者被收押、收监人员与文书、凭证不符的；  
（二）依法应当收押、收监而不收押、收监，或者对依法不应当关押的人员收押、收监的；  
（三）未告知被收押、收监人员权利、义务的；  
（四）其他违法情形。

**Article 654** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers any of the following circumstances in the admission activities of a detention center or a prison, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law:  
(1) There is no admission document or certificate, the documents or certificates are incomplete, or the admitted person does not match the documents or certificates;  
(2) A person who should be admitted according to law is not admitted, or a person who should not be detained according to law is admitted;  
(3) The admitted person is not informed of his or her rights and obligations;  
(4) Other illegal circumstances.

<a id="art-655"></a>第六百五十五条  人民检察院发现监狱、看守所等执行机关在管理、教育改造罪犯等活动中有违法行为的，应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 655** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that an enforcement organ such as a prison or a detention center has illegal acts in activities such as management, education, and reform of criminals, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law.

<a id="art-656"></a>第六百五十六条  看守所对收押的犯罪嫌疑人进行身体检查时，人民检察院驻看守所检察人员可以在场。发现收押的犯罪嫌疑人有伤或者身体异常的，应当要求看守所进行拍照或者录像，由送押人员、犯罪嫌疑人说明原因，在体检记录中写明，并由送押人员、收押人员和犯罪嫌疑人签字确认。必要时，驻看守所检察人员可以自行拍照或者录像，并将相关情况记录在案。

**Article 656** When a detention center conducts a physical examination of a criminal suspect admitted, the procuratorial personnel of the People's Procuratorate stationed at the detention center may be present. If it is discovered that the admitted criminal suspect has injuries or abnormal physical conditions, the detention center shall be required to take photos or videos, and the personnel who sent the suspect and the criminal suspect shall explain the reasons, which shall be recorded in the medical examination records, and shall be signed for confirmation by the personnel who sent the suspect, the admission personnel, and the criminal suspect. When necessary, the procuratorial personnel stationed at the detention center may take photos or videos themselves and record the relevant circumstances in the case file.

<a id="art-657"></a>第六百五十七条  人民检察院发现看守所、监狱等监管场所有殴打、体罚、虐待、违法使用戒具、违法适用禁闭等侵害在押人员人身权利情形的，应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 657** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that a custodial facility such as a detention center or a prison has circumstances such as beating, corporal punishment, maltreatment, illegal use of restraint instruments, or illegal use of solitary confinement that infringe upon the personal rights of detained persons, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law.

<a id="art-658"></a>第六百五十八条  人民检察院发现看守所违反有关规定，有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）为在押人员通风报信，私自传递信件、物品，帮助伪造、毁灭、隐匿证据或者干扰证人作证、串供的；  
（二）违反规定同意侦查人员将犯罪嫌疑人提出看守所讯问的；  
（三）收到在押犯罪嫌疑人、被告人及其法定代理人、近亲属或者辩护人的变更强制措施申请或者其他申请、申诉、控告、举报，不及时转交、转告人民检察院或者有关办案机关的；  
（四）应当安排辩护律师依法会见在押的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人而没有安排的；  
（五）违法安排辩护律师或者其他人员会见在押的犯罪嫌疑人、被告人的；  
（六）辩护律师会见犯罪嫌疑人、被告人时予以监听的；  
（七）其他违法情形。

**Article 658** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that a detention center violates the relevant provisions and has any of the following circumstances, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law:  
(1) Disclosing information to detained persons, privately transmitting letters or articles, assisting in forging, destroying, or concealing evidence, or interfering with witness testimony or collusion of confessions;  
(2) Violating the provisions by agreeing to allow investigators to bring a criminal suspect out of the detention center for interrogation;  
(3) Upon receiving an application for modification of compulsory measures or other applications, petitions, accusations, or reports from a detained criminal suspect or defendant, his or her legal representative, near relative, or defender, failing to timely transfer or notify the People's Procuratorate or the relevant case-handling organ;  
(4) Failing to arrange for a defense lawyer to meet with a detained criminal suspect or defendant according to law when such arrangement should be made;  
(5) Illegally arranging for a defense lawyer or other person to meet with a detained criminal suspect or defendant;  
(6) Monitoring a defense lawyer's meeting with a criminal suspect or defendant;  
(7) Other illegal circumstances.

<a id="art-659"></a>第六百五十九条  人民检察院发现看守所代为执行刑罚的活动具有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）将被判处有期徒刑剩余刑期在三个月以上的罪犯留所服刑的；  
（二）将留所服刑罪犯与犯罪嫌疑人、被告人混押、混管、混教的；  
（三）其他违法情形。

**Article 659** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers any of the following circumstances in the activities of a detention center in executing punishments on behalf of a prison, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law:  
(1) Retaining a criminal sentenced to fixed-term imprisonment with a remaining term of more than three months in the detention center to serve the sentence;  
(2) Mixing the custody, management, or education of criminals serving sentences in the detention center with criminal suspects or defendants;  
(3) Other illegal circumstances.

<a id="art-660"></a>第六百六十条  人民检察院发现监狱没有按照规定对罪犯进行分押分管、监狱人民警察没有对罪犯实行直接管理等违反监管规定情形的，应当依法提出纠正意见。  
人民检察院发现监狱具有未按照规定安排罪犯与亲属或者监护人会见、对伤病罪犯未及时治疗以及未执行国家规定的罪犯生活标准等侵犯罪犯合法权益情形的，应当依法提出纠正意见。

**Article 660** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that a prison fails to separately detain and manage criminals according to regulations, or that prison police officers fail to exercise direct management over criminals, or other circumstances violating the supervision provisions, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law.  
Where the People's Procuratorate discovers that a prison has circumstances infringing upon the lawful rights and interests of criminals, such as failing to arrange for criminals to meet with their relatives or guardians according to regulations, failing to promptly treat sick or injured criminals, or failing to implement the national standards for the living conditions of criminals, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law.

<a id="art-661"></a>第六百六十一条  人民检察院发现看守所出所活动和监狱出监活动具有下列情形之一的，应当依法提出纠正意见：  
（一）没有出所、出监文书、凭证，文书、凭证不齐全，或者出所、出监人员与文书、凭证不符的；  
（二）应当释放而没有释放，不应当释放而释放，或者未依照规定送达释放通知书的；  
（三）对提押、押解、转押出所的在押人员，特许离监、临时离监、调监或者暂予监外执行的罪犯，未依照规定派员押送并办理交接手续的；  
（四）其他违法情形。

**Article 661** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers any of the following circumstances in the release activities of a detention center or a prison, it shall put forward corrective opinions according to law:  
(1) There is no release document or certificate, the documents or certificates are incomplete, or the released person does not match the documents or certificates;  
(2) A person who should be released is not released, a person who should not be released is released, or the release notice is not served according to the provisions;  
(3) For detained persons who are taken out, escorted, or transferred out of the detention center, or for criminals who are granted special leave from prison, temporary leave from prison, transfer to another prison, or temporary execution of sentence outside prison, no personnel are assigned to escort them and handle the handover procedures according to the provisions;  
(4) Other illegal circumstances.

#### 第九节 事故检察

<a id="art-662"></a>第六百六十二条  人民检察院发现看守所、监狱、强制医疗机构等场所具有下列情形之一的，应当开展事故检察：  
（一）被监管人、被强制医疗人非正常死亡、伤残、脱逃的；  
（二）被监管人破坏监管秩序，情节严重的；  
（三）突发公共卫生事件的；  
（四）其他重大事故。  
发生被监管人、被强制医疗人非正常死亡的，应当组织巡回检察。

**Article 662** Where the People's Procuratorate discovers any of the following circumstances in a facility such as a detention center, a prison, or a compulsory medical institution, it shall conduct an accident inspection:  
(1) A person under supervision or a person subjected to compulsory medical treatment dies abnormally, is injured or disabled, or escapes;  
(2) A person under supervision disrupts the supervision order, where the circumstances are serious;  
(3) A public health emergency occurs;  
(4) Other major accidents.  
If a person under supervision or a person subjected to compulsory medical treatment dies abnormally, a circuit inspection shall be organized.

<a id="art-663"></a>第六百六十三条  人民检察院应当对看守所、监狱、强制医疗机构等场所或者主管机关的事故调查结论进行审查。具有下列情形之一的，人民检察院应当调查核实：  
（一）被监管人、被强制医疗人及其法定代理人、近亲属对调查结论有异议的，人民检察院认为有必要调查的；  
（二）人民检察院对调查结论有异议的；  
（三）其他需要调查的。  
人民检察院应当将调查核实的结论书面通知监管场所或者主管机关和被监管人、被强制医疗人的近亲属。认为监管场所或者主管机关处理意见不当，或者监管执法存在问题的，应当提出纠正意见或者检察建议；认为可能存在违法犯罪情形的，应当移送有关部门处理。

**Article 663** The People's Procuratorate shall review the accident investigation conclusions of a facility such as a detention center, a prison, or a compulsory medical institution, or the competent authority. Under any of the following circumstances, the People's Procuratorate shall investigate and verify:  
(1) The person under supervision or the person subjected to compulsory medical treatment, his or her legal representative, or near relative has an objection to the investigation conclusion, and the People's Procuratorate deems it necessary to investigate;  
(2) The People's Procuratorate has an objection to the investigation conclusion;  
(3) Other circumstances requiring investigation.  
The People's Procuratorate shall notify the custodial facility or the competent authority and the near relative of the person under supervision or the person subjected to compulsory medical treatment in writing of the conclusion of the investigation and verification. If it considers that the handling opinion of the custodial facility or the competent authority is improper, or there is a problem in supervision and law enforcement, it shall put forward a corrective opinion or a procuratorial suggestion; if it considers that there may be illegal or criminal circumstances, it shall transfer the matter to the relevant department for handling.

### 第十五章 案件管理

<a id="art-664"></a>第六百六十四条  人民检察院负责案件管理的部门对检察机关办理案件的受理、期限、程序、质量等进行管理、监督、预警。

**Article 664** The case management department of a People's Procuratorate shall manage, supervise and provide early warning regarding the acceptance, time limits, procedures, quality, etc., of cases handled by the procuratorial organ.

<a id="art-665"></a>第六百六十五条  人民检察院负责案件管理的部门发现本院办案活动具有下列情形之一的，应当及时提出纠正意见：  
（一）查封、扣押、冻结、保管、处理涉案财物不符合有关法律和规定的；  
（二）法律文书制作、使用不符合法律和有关规定的；  
（三）违反羁押期限、办案期限规定的；  
（四）侵害当事人、辩护人、诉讼代理人的诉讼权利的；  
（五）未依法对立案、侦查、审查逮捕、公诉、审判等诉讼活动以及执行活动中的违法行为履行法律监督职责的；  
（六）其他应当提出纠正意见的情形。  
情节轻微的，可以口头提示；情节较重的，应当发送案件流程监控通知书，提示办案部门及时查明情况并予以纠正；情节严重的，应当同时向检察长报告。  
办案部门收到案件流程监控通知书后，应当在十日以内将核查情况书面回复负责案件管理的部门。

**Article 665** If the case management department of a People's Procuratorate discovers that any of the following circumstances exists in the case-handling activities of this procuratorate, it shall promptly issue a corrective opinion:  
(1) The sealing, seizure, freezing, custody, or disposal of assets involved in the case does not conform to relevant laws and regulations;  
(2) The production or use of legal documents does not conform to laws and relevant regulations;  
(3) Violation of provisions on custody periods or case-handling periods;  
(4) Infringement upon the procedural rights of parties, defenders, or litigation agents;  
(5) Failure to perform the duty of legal supervision over illegal acts in litigation activities such as case filing, investigation, review of arrest, public prosecution, adjudication, and enforcement activities according to law;  
(6) Other circumstances where a corrective opinion shall be issued.  
If the circumstances are minor, an oral reminder may be given; if the circumstances are relatively serious, a case process monitoring notice shall be sent, reminding the handling department to promptly ascertain the situation and make corrections; if the circumstances are serious, the matter shall also be reported to the Chief Procurator at the same time.  
After receiving the case process monitoring notice, the handling department shall reply in writing to the case management department regarding the verification within ten days.

<a id="art-666"></a>第六百六十六条  人民检察院负责案件管理的部门对以本院名义制发法律文书实施监督管理。

**Article 666** The case management department of a People's Procuratorate shall exercise supervision and management over the production and issuance of legal documents in the name of this procuratorate.

<a id="art-667"></a>第六百六十七条  人民检察院办理的案件，办结后需要向其他单位移送案卷材料的，统一由负责案件管理的部门审核移送材料是否规范、齐备。负责案件管理的部门认为材料规范、齐备，符合移送条件的，应当立即由办案部门按照规定移送；认为材料不符合要求的，应当及时通知办案部门补送、更正。

**Article 667** For cases handled by a People's Procuratorate, if case file materials need to be transferred to another unit after completion, the case management department shall uniformly review whether the transfer materials are standardized and complete. If the case management department considers the materials standardized, complete, and meeting the conditions for transfer, the handling department shall immediately transfer them according to the provisions; if it considers the materials do not meet the requirements, it shall promptly notify the handling department to supplement or correct them.

<a id="art-668"></a>第六百六十八条  监察机关或者公安机关随案移送涉案财物及其孳息的，人民检察院负责案件管理的部门应当在受理案件时进行审查，并及时办理入库保管手续。

**Article 668** If a supervisory organ or a public security organ transfers assets involved in the case and their fruits along with the case, the case management department of the People's Procuratorate shall examine them at the time of case acceptance and promptly handle the procedures for storage in the warehouse.

<a id="art-669"></a>第六百六十九条  人民检察院负责案件管理的部门对扣押的涉案物品进行保管，并对查封、扣押、冻结、处理涉案财物工作进行监督管理。对违反规定的行为提出纠正意见；涉嫌违法违纪的，报告检察长。

**Article 669** The case management department of a People's Procuratorate shall keep the seized assets involved in the case, and exercise supervision and management over the sealing, seizure, freezing, and disposal of assets involved in the case. It shall issue corrective opinions on acts that violate regulations; if the act is suspected of being illegal or disciplinary, it shall report to the Chief Procurator.

<a id="art-670"></a>第六百七十条  人民检察院办案部门需要调用、移送、处理查封、扣押、冻结的涉案财物的，应当按照规定办理审批手续。审批手续齐全的，负责案件管理的部门应当办理出库手续。

**Article 670** If a handling department of a People's Procuratorate needs to requisition, transfer, or dispose of sealed, seized, or frozen assets involved in the case, it shall handle the approval procedures according to the provisions. If the approval procedures are complete, the case management department shall handle the release procedures.

### 第十六章 刑事司法协助

<a id="art-671"></a>第六百七十一条  人民检察院依据国际刑事司法协助法等有关法律和有关刑事司法协助条约进行刑事司法协助。

**Article 671** A People's Procuratorate shall provide international criminal judicial assistance in accordance with the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law and other relevant laws and relevant treaties on criminal judicial assistance.

<a id="art-672"></a>第六百七十二条  人民检察院刑事司法协助的范围包括刑事诉讼文书送达，调查取证，安排证人作证或者协助调查，查封、扣押、冻结涉案财物，返还违法所得及其他涉案财物，移管被判刑人以及其他协助。

**Article 672** The scope of criminal judicial assistance by a People's Procuratorate includes service of criminal procedural documents, investigation and evidence collection, arranging for witness testimony or assistance in investigation, sealing, seizure, freezing of assets involved in the case, return of illegal gains and other assets involved in the case, transfer of sentenced persons, and other assistance.

<a id="art-673"></a>第六百七十三条  最高人民检察院是检察机关开展国际刑事司法协助的主管机关，负责审核地方各级人民检察院向外国提出的刑事司法协助请求，审查处理对外联系机关转递的外国提出的刑事司法协助请求，审查决定是否批准执行外国的刑事司法协助请求，承担其他与国际刑事司法协助相关的工作。  
办理刑事司法协助相关案件的地方各级人民检察院应当向最高人民检察院层报需要向外国提出的刑事司法协助请求，执行最高人民检察院交办的外国提出的刑事司法协助请求。

**Article 673** The Supreme People's Procuratorate is the competent authority for procuratorial organs to carry out international criminal judicial assistance. It shall be responsible for reviewing requests for criminal judicial assistance submitted by local People's Procuratorates at all levels to foreign countries, examining and handling requests for criminal judicial assistance submitted by foreign countries forwarded by the foreign liaison authority, examining and deciding whether to approve the execution of requests for criminal judicial assistance from foreign countries, and undertaking other work related to international criminal judicial assistance.  
Local People's Procuratorates at all levels handling cases related to criminal judicial assistance shall report requests for criminal judicial assistance to be submitted to foreign countries level by level to the Supreme People's Procuratorate, and execute requests for criminal judicial assistance from foreign countries assigned by the Supreme People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-674"></a>第六百七十四条  地方各级人民检察院需要向外国请求刑事司法协助的，应当制作刑事司法协助请求书并附相关材料。经省级人民检察院审核同意后，报送最高人民检察院。  
刑事司法协助请求书应当依照相关刑事司法协助条约的规定制作；没有条约或者条约没有规定的，可以参照[国际刑事司法协助法第十三条](../../刑事/刑法及修正案/中华人民共和国国际刑事司法协助法.md#art-13)的规定制作。被请求方有特殊要求的，在不违反我国法律的基本原则的情况下，可以按照被请求方的特殊要求制作。

**Article 674** Where a local people's procuratorate at any level needs to request criminal judicial assistance from a foreign country, it shall prepare a written request for criminal judicial assistance and attach relevant materials. After review and approval by the provincial people's procuratorate, it shall be submitted to the Supreme People's Procuratorate.  
The written request for criminal judicial assistance shall be prepared in accordance with the provisions of the relevant criminal judicial assistance treaty; if there is no treaty or the treaty has no provisions, it may be prepared with reference to the provisions of Article 13 of the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law. If the requested party has special requirements, the request may be prepared in accordance with such special requirements, provided that the basic principles of the laws of China are not violated.

<a id="art-675"></a>第六百七十五条  最高人民检察院收到地方各级人民检察院刑事司法协助请求书及所附相关材料后，应当依照国际刑事司法协助法和有关条约进行审查。对符合规定、所附材料齐全的，最高人民检察院是对外联系机关的，应当及时向外国提出请求；不是对外联系机关的，应当通过对外联系机关向外国提出请求。对不符合规定或者材料不齐全的，应当退回提出请求的人民检察院或者要求其补充、修正。

**Article 675** After receiving the written request for criminal judicial assistance and the attached relevant materials from a local people's procuratorate at any level, the Supreme People's Procuratorate shall review the request in accordance with the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law and relevant treaties. Where the request complies with the provisions and the attached materials are complete, if the Supreme People's Procuratorate is the external liaison authority, it shall promptly submit the request to the foreign country; if it is not the external liaison authority, it shall submit the request to the foreign country through the external liaison authority. Where the request does not comply with the provisions or the materials are incomplete, the request shall be returned to the people's procuratorate that made the request, or the procuratorate shall be required to supplement or correct the materials.

<a id="art-676"></a>第六百七十六条  最高人民检察院收到外国提出的刑事司法协助请求后，应当对请求书及所附材料进行审查。对于请求书形式和内容符合要求的，应当按照职责分工，将请求书及所附材料转交有关主管机关或者省级人民检察院处理；对于请求书形式和内容不符合要求的，可以要求请求方补充材料或者重新提出请求。  
外国提出的刑事司法协助请求明显损害我国主权、安全和社会公共利益的，可以直接拒绝提供协助。

**Article 676** After receiving a request for criminal judicial assistance from a foreign country, the Supreme People's Procuratorate shall review the request and the attached materials. Where the form and content of the request meet the requirements, the request and the attached materials shall be forwarded in accordance with the division of functions and powers to the relevant competent authority or the provincial people's procuratorate for handling; where the form and content of the request do not meet the requirements, the requesting party may be required to supplement materials or submit a new request.  
Where a request for criminal judicial assistance made by a foreign country obviously undermines the sovereignty, security and social public interests of China, the provision of assistance may be directly refused.

<a id="art-677"></a>第六百七十七条  最高人民检察院在收到对外联系机关转交的刑事司法协助请求书及所附材料后，经审查，分别作出以下处理：  
（一）根据国际刑事司法协助法和刑事司法协助条约的规定，认为可以协助执行的，作出决定并安排有关省级人民检察院执行；  
（二）根据国际刑事司法协助法或者刑事司法协助条约的规定，认为应当全部或者部分拒绝协助的，将请求书及所附材料退回对外联系机关并说明理由；  
（三）对执行请求有保密要求或者有其他附加条件的，通过对外联系机关向外国提出，在外国接受条件并且作出书面保证后，决定附条件执行；  
（四）需要补充材料的，书面通过对外联系机关要求请求方在合理期限内提供。

**Article 677** After receiving the written request for criminal judicial assistance and the attached materials forwarded by the external liaison authority, the Supreme People's Procuratorate shall, upon review, handle the matter as follows:  
(1) Where it considers, in accordance with the provisions of the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law and the criminal judicial assistance treaty, that assistance may be executed, it shall make a decision and arrange for the relevant provincial people's procuratorate to execute it;  
(2) Where it considers, in accordance with the provisions of the International Criminal Judicial Assistance Law or the criminal judicial assistance treaty, that assistance shall be wholly or partially refused, it shall return the request and the attached materials to the external liaison authority and state the reasons;  
(3) Where there are confidentiality requirements or other additional conditions for the execution of the request, it shall raise them through the external liaison authority to the foreign country, and after the foreign country accepts the conditions and makes a written assurance, decide to execute conditionally;  
(4) Where supplementary materials are needed, it shall, in writing, request the requesting party through the external liaison authority to provide them within a reasonable time limit.

<a id="art-678"></a>第六百七十八条  有关省级人民检察院收到最高人民检察院交办的外国刑事司法协助请求后，应当依法执行，或者交由下级人民检察院执行。  
负责执行的人民检察院收到刑事司法协助请求书和所附材料后，应当立即安排执行，并将执行结果及有关材料报经省级人民检察院审查后，报送最高人民检察院。  
对于不能执行的，应当将刑事司法协助请求书和所附材料，连同不能执行的理由，通过省级人民检察院报送最高人民检察院。  
因请求书提供的地址不详或者材料不齐全，人民检察院难以执行该项请求的，应当立即通过最高人民检察院书面通知对外联系机关，要求请求方补充提供材料。

**Article 678** After receiving the criminal judicial assistance request from a foreign country assigned by the Supreme People's Procuratorate, the relevant provincial people's procuratorate shall execute it according to law, or assign it to a lower-level people's procuratorate for execution.  
Upon receiving the written request for criminal judicial assistance and the attached materials, the people's procuratorate responsible for execution shall promptly arrange execution, and shall report the results of execution and relevant materials to the provincial people's procuratorate for review, then submit them to the Supreme People's Procuratorate.  
Where execution is impossible, the written request for criminal judicial assistance and the attached materials, together with the reasons for impossibility, shall be submitted to the Supreme People's Procuratorate through the provincial people's procuratorate.  
Where the people's procuratorate finds it difficult to execute the request due to an unclear address provided in the request or incomplete materials, it shall promptly notify the external liaison authority in writing through the Supreme People's Procuratorate, requesting the requesting party to provide supplementary materials.

<a id="art-679"></a>第六百七十九条  最高人民检察院应当对执行结果进行审查。对于符合请求要求和有关规定的，通过对外联系机关转交或者转告请求方。

**Article 679** The Supreme People's Procuratorate shall review the results of execution. Where the results comply with the requirements of the request and relevant provisions, they shall be forwarded or communicated to the requesting party through the external liaison authority.

### 第十七章 附 则

<a id="art-680"></a>第六百八十条  人民检察院办理国家安全机关、海警机关、监狱移送的刑事案件以及对国家安全机关、海警机关、监狱立案、侦查活动的监督，适用本规则关于公安机关的规定。

**Article 680** When handling criminal cases transferred by state security organs, maritime police authorities, or prisons, and when supervising the case filing and investigation activities of state security organs, maritime police authorities, and prisons, the people's procuratorates shall apply the provisions of these Rules concerning the public security organs.

<a id="art-681"></a>第六百八十一条  军事检察院等专门人民检察院办理刑事案件，适用本规则和其他有关规定。

**Article 681** When handling criminal cases, the military procuratorates and other special people's procuratorates shall apply these Rules and other relevant provisions.

<a id="art-682"></a>第六百八十二条  本规则所称检察官，包括检察长、副检察长、检察委员会委员、检察员。  
本规则所称检察人员，包括检察官和检察官助理。

**Article 682** The term "procurator" as used in these Rules includes the Chief Procurator, Deputy Chief Procurators, members of the Procuratorial Committee, and procurators.  
The term "procuratorial personnel" as used in these Rules includes procurators and assistant procurators.

<a id="art-683"></a>第六百八十三条  本规则由最高人民检察院负责解释。

**Article 683** The Supreme People's Procuratorate shall be responsible for the interpretation of these Rules.

<a id="art-684"></a>第六百八十四条  本规则自2019年12月30日起施行。本规则施行后，《人民检察院刑事诉讼规则（试行）》（高检发释字〔2012〕2号）同时废止；最高人民检察院以前发布的司法解释和规范性文件与本规则不一致的，以本规则为准。  
主送：各省、自治区、直辖市人民检察院，解放军军事检察院，新疆  
生产建设兵团人民检察院。  
抄送：中央纪委国家监委机关，中央政法委，最高人民法院，公安部，  
国家安全部，司法部；  
本院领导同志、检委会专职委员；  
机关各内设机构、各直属事业单位。  
最高人民检察院办公厅                    2019年12月30日印发  
最高人民检察院  
高检发释字〔2019〕4号

**Article 684** These Rules shall come into force as of December 30, 2019. After these Rules come into force, the People's Procuratorate Criminal Procedure Rules (Trial) (Gao Jian Fa Shi Zi [2012] No. 2) shall be abolished simultaneously; if any judicial interpretations and normative documents previously issued by the Supreme People's Procuratorate are inconsistent with these Rules, these Rules shall prevail.  
Addressees: People's procuratorates of all provinces, autonomous regions, and municipalities directly under the Central Government; the People's Procuratorate of the People's Liberation Army; the People's Procuratorate of Xinjiang Production and Construction Corps.  
Copied to: Central Commission for Discipline Inspection and the National Supervisory Commission; Central Political and Legal Affairs Commission; Supreme People's Court; Ministry of Public Security; Ministry of State Security; Ministry of Justice; Leaders of this Procuratorate and full-time members of the Procuratorial Committee; All internal organs and directly affiliated institutions.  
Issued by the General Office of the Supreme People's Procuratorate on December 30, 2019  
Supreme People's Procuratorate  
Gao Jian Fa Shi Zi [2019] No. 4
